@SpringBootTest(
    classes = {ChecksApplication.class, GuidedPublicationReplayIT.RuntimeHooks.class},
    webEnvironment = SpringBootTest.WebEnvironment.RANDOM_PORT,
    properties = {
      "spring.main.banner-mode=off",
      "logging.level.root=WARN",
      "spring.jmx.enabled=false",
      "spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto=none",
      "studio.execution.dispatcher.enabled=false"
    })
@org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.actuate.observability.AutoConfigureObservability(
    metrics = true,
    tracing = false)
@ActiveProfiles("test")
@org.springframework.test.context.TestExecutionListeners(
    listeners = GuidedPublicationReplayIT.OwnedContextCapture.class,
    mergeMode =
        org.springframework.test.context.TestExecutionListeners.MergeMode.MERGE_WITH_DEFAULTS)
@org.springframework.test.annotation.DirtiesContext(
    classMode = org.springframework.test.annotation.DirtiesContext.ClassMode.AFTER_CLASS,
    hierarchyMode = org.springframework.test.annotation.DirtiesContext.HierarchyMode.CURRENT_LEVEL)
// The external Core HTTP fixture and two workers are class-owned and read the volatile identity
// below. Enforce serialized methods so no other test can change identity during an HTTP read.
@org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.Execution(org.junit.jupiter.api.parallel.ExecutionMode.SAME_THREAD)
@org.junit.jupiter.api.TestInstance(org.junit.jupiter.api.TestInstance.Lifecycle.PER_CLASS)
@org.junit.jupiter.api.TestMethodOrder(org.junit.jupiter.api.MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation.class)
class GuidedPublicationReplayIT {
  /**
   * STUDIO-OBS-014 AC-10 canary (ruling AR-2 e): every Studio audit event this class's real guided
   * and replay runs emit, captured from the audit logger itself. The OBS-007 secret-login case
   * registers its canaries in {@link #AUDIT_CANARIES}; {@code
   * auditStreamCarriesIdsAndCodesOnlyAcrossTheWholeClass} asserts none of them appears.
   */
  private static final ch.qos.logback.core.read.ListAppender<
          ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>
      AUDIT_EVENTS = auditCapture();

  private static final java.util.Set<String> AUDIT_CANARIES =
      java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();

  private static ch.qos.logback.core.read.ListAppender<ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>
      auditCapture() {
    var appender =
        new ch.qos.logback.core.read.ListAppender<ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>();
    appender.setName("AUDIT_EVENTS");
    return appender;
  }

  /**
   * (Re-)attaches {@link #AUDIT_EVENTS} to the audit logger. Spring Boot stops and resets logback
   * whenever an application context starts (this class's own and each nested replica), which
   * detaches and stops every appender and also drops logback context listeners; so the capture is
   * re-attached after each context start: before every test and right after each nested replica
   * starts (S3b: an attach-once capture stayed empty for the whole class).
   */
  static void ensureAuditCapture() {
    var logger =
        ((ch.qos.logback.classic.LoggerContext) org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getILoggerFactory())
            .getLogger(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.audit.StudioActionAudit.CATEGORY);
    if (!AUDIT_EVENTS.isStarted()) {
      AUDIT_EVENTS.start();
    }
    if (logger.getAppender(AUDIT_EVENTS.getName()) != AUDIT_EVENTS) {
      logger.addAppender(AUDIT_EVENTS);
    }
  }

  private java.util.Set<String> tallyBefore = java.util.Set.of();
  private int tallyAuditStart;

  @org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll
  void detachAuditCapture() throws Exception {
    com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.GuardDecisionTally.write(GuidedPublicationReplayIT.class);
    ((ch.qos.logback.classic.LoggerContext) org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getILoggerFactory())
        .getLogger(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.audit.StudioActionAudit.CATEGORY)
        .detachAppender(AUDIT_EVENTS);
  }

  static final GuidedContracts C = new GuidedContracts();
  static final ObjectMapper JSON = new ObjectMapper().findAndRegisterModules();
  static final String ONE = "cccccccc-cccc-4ccc-8ccc-cccccccccccc",
      TWO = "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa",
      THREE = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
  static final String COOKIE = "controlled-core-cookie",
      SERVICE = "controlled-runner-service",
      CALLBACK = "controlled-callback-service";
  static final String SAVED = "PRIVATE-SAVED-" + UUID.randomUUID();
  static final Path TEMP;
  /** Test-only, value-free observation of the ORIGINAL native schema validation and reply. */
  public static final class B1OutcomeCapture {
    public static final ThreadLocal<ObjectNode> ACTIVE = new ThreadLocal<>();
    private static final java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean INSTALLED = new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean();
    private static final java.util.Set<String> KNOWN = java.util.Set.of("schemaVersion", "sessionId", "workerId", "ownerFence", "scopeHash", "contextId", "contextRevision", "sourceRevision", "configurationRevision", "versions", "contract", "ledger", "availability", "errorMessage", "coordinate", "stepId", "ordinal", "idempotencyKey", "requestDigest", "operationHash", "generation", "documentId", "admission", "admissionId", "authorizationId", "admittedAt", "policyDecisionHash", "state", "dispatchCount", "outcomeCode", "assertionPassed", "executionEvidence", "evidenceHash", "authoredInputsHash", "preparationId", "attemptOrdinal", "kind", "creationId", "bootstrapId", "url", "origin", "observationHash", "bindingId", "producerAdmissionId", "properties", "definitions", "oneOf", "allOf", "anyOf", "items", "required", "type", "additionalProperties", "ordinaryOutcome", "outcome", "PrefixDriftCallback");
    private static String cohort;
    private static int events, bytes, errors;
    private static boolean errorsTruncated;
    public static synchronized void append(ObjectNode event) {
      try {
        Path target = TEMP.resolve("obs015-outcome-boundary-java.jsonl");
        if (events >= 200 || bytes >= 1040000) return;
        String line = events >= 199 ? "{\"truncated\":true,\"instrumentationError\":false}\n" : GuidedContracts.canonical(event) + "\n";
        int size = line.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length;
        if (bytes + size > 1040000) line = "{\"truncated\":true,\"instrumentationError\":false}\n";
        if (!Files.exists(target)) Files.createFile(target, java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")));
        Files.writeString(target, line, StandardCharsets.UTF_8, java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.APPEND);
        Files.setPosixFilePermissions(target, java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"));
        events++; bytes += line.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length;
      } catch (Throwable ignored) { /* Diagnostic loss never changes the original validation. */ }
    }
    public static String digest(String value) { return GuidedContracts.hash(com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.TextNode.valueOf(value)); }
    public static String safePointer(Object pointer) {
      if (pointer == null) return "UNAVAILABLE";
      StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();
      for (String part : pointer.toString().split("[/#.\\[\\]]")) {
        if (result.length() > 2048) return result + "/TRUNCATED";
        if (part.isEmpty()) continue;
        result.append('/').append(KNOWN.contains(part) || part.matches("[0-9]{1,6}") ? part : "sha256:" + digest(part));
      }
      return result.toString();
    }
    public static void shape(JsonNode node, String path, com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ArrayNode fields) {
      if (fields.size() >= 200) return;
      fields.addObject().put("path", path).put("type", node.isNumber() ? "NUMBER" : node.getNodeType().name());
      if (node.isObject()) node.fields().forEachRemaining(f -> shape(f.getValue(), path + "/" + (KNOWN.contains(f.getKey()) ? f.getKey() : "sha256:" + digest(f.getKey())), fields));
      else if (node.isArray()) for (int at = 0; at < node.size() && fields.size() < 200; at++) shape(node.get(at), path + "/" + at, fields);
    }
    public static boolean begin(String name, JsonNode body) {
      try {
        if (!name.equals("v3/bridge#/definitions/outcome") || ACTIVE.get() != null) return false;
        boolean client = java.util.Arrays.stream(Thread.currentThread().getStackTrace()).anyMatch(f -> f.getClassName().endsWith("GuidedRunnerClient") && f.getMethodName().equals("send"));
        if (!client) return false;
        String sessionHash = GuidedContracts.hash(body.path("sessionId"));
        synchronized (B1OutcomeCapture.class) { if (cohort == null) cohort = sessionHash; if (!cohort.equals(sessionHash)) return false; }
        String canonical = GuidedContracts.canonical(body);
        ObjectNode row = C.object().put("kind", "original-outcome-validation").put("canonicalBodySha256", GuidedContracts.hash(body))
            .put("canonicalBodyBytes", canonical.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length).put("sessionHash", sessionHash)
            .put("admissionHash", GuidedContracts.hash(body.path("ledger").path("admission").path("admissionId")))
            .put("expandedOutcomeSchemaHash", "UNAVAILABLE").put("instrumentationError", false).put("truncated", false);
        shape(body, "", row.putArray("shape"));
        for (String key : java.util.List.of("state", "outcomeCode")) {
          String value = body.path("ledger").path(key).asText();
          row.put(key, java.util.Set.of("ADMITTED", "DISPATCHING", "SUCCEEDED", "FAILED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME", "REVOKED", "OK", "PENDING", "TARGET_CHANGED", "POLICY_DENIED", "ASSERTION_FAILED", "TIMEOUT", "CANCELLED", "ACK_LOST").contains(value) ? value : "OTHER");
        }
        ACTIVE.set(row); return true;
      } catch (Throwable failure) { append(C.object().put("instrumentationError", true).put("stage", "begin").put("failureClass", failure.getClass().getName())); return false; }
    }
    public static Object getter(Object value, String... names) {
      for (String name : names) try { return value.getClass().getMethod(name).invoke(value); } catch (ReflectiveOperationException unavailable) { /* Try only named metadata getters, never getMessage/arguments. */ }
      return null;
    }
    public static void schema(Object schema, Object result) {
      try {
        ObjectNode parent = ACTIVE.get(); if (parent == null || !(result instanceof java.util.Set<?> messages) || messages.isEmpty()) return;
        synchronized (B1OutcomeCapture.class) {
          if (errors >= 64) { if (!errorsTruncated) append(C.object().put("truncated", true).put("kind", "validation-error-cap")); errorsTruncated = true; return; }
        }
        ObjectNode row = C.object().put("kind", "native-schema-validation").put("canonicalBodySha256", parent.path("canonicalBodySha256").asText())
            .put("valid", messages.isEmpty()).put("expandedOutcomeSchemaHash", "UNAVAILABLE").put("instrumentationError", false);
        Object node = getter(schema, "getSchemaNode");
        if (node instanceof JsonNode actual) row.put("observedNativeSchemaNodeHash", GuidedContracts.hash(actual));
        var list = row.putArray("errors");
        synchronized (B1OutcomeCapture.class) {
          for (Object message : messages) {
            if (errors >= 64) { row.put("truncated", true); break; }
            Object keyword = getter(message, "getType", "getCode"); String word = keyword == null ? "UNAVAILABLE" : keyword.toString();
            list.addObject().put("keyword", java.util.Set.of("type", "required", "additionalProperties", "oneOf", "allOf", "anyOf", "const", "enum", "pattern", "minLength", "maxLength", "minimum", "maximum", "uniqueItems", "$ref").contains(word) ? word : "sha256:" + digest(word))
                .put("instancePointer", safePointer(getter(message, "getInstanceLocation", "getPath")))
                .put("schemaPointer", safePointer(getter(message, "getSchemaLocation", "getSchemaPath")));
            errors++;
          }
        }
        append(row);
      } catch (Throwable failure) { append(C.object().put("instrumentationError", true).put("stage", "schema").put("failureClass", failure.getClass().getName())); }
    }
    public static void end(boolean entered, Throwable failure) {
      if (!entered) return;
      try { ObjectNode row = ACTIVE.get(); if (row != null) { row.put("accepted", failure == null).put("threw", failure != null).put("truncated", errorsTruncated || row.path("shape").size() >= 200); append(row); } }
      finally { ACTIVE.remove(); }
    }
    public static void install() {
      if (!INSTALLED.compareAndSet(false, true)) return;
      try {
        var instrumentation = net.bytebuddy.agent.ByteBuddyAgent.install();
        new net.bytebuddy.agent.builder.AgentBuilder.Default().disableClassFormatChanges()
            .with(net.bytebuddy.agent.builder.AgentBuilder.RedefinitionStrategy.RETRANSFORMATION)
            .with(new net.bytebuddy.agent.builder.AgentBuilder.Listener.Adapter() {
              @Override public void onError(String type, ClassLoader loader, net.bytebuddy.utility.JavaModule module, boolean loaded, Throwable failure) {
                append(C.object().put("instrumentationError", true).put("stage", "transform").put("typeHash", digest(type)));
              }
            })
            .type(net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.named("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedContracts"))
            .transform((builder, type, loader, module, domain) -> builder.visit(net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.to(B1OutcomeAdvice.class)
                .on(net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.named("validatedCanonical").and(net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.takesArguments(String.class, JsonNode.class)))))
            .type(net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.named("com.networknt.schema.JsonSchema"))
            .transform((builder, type, loader, module, domain) -> builder.visit(net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.to(B1SchemaAdvice.class)
                .on(net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.named("validate").and(net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.returns(java.util.Set.class)))))
            .installOn(instrumentation);
        append(C.object().put("kind", "observer-install").put("instrumentationError", false).put("expandedOutcomeSchemaHash", "UNAVAILABLE"));
      } catch (Throwable failure) { append(C.object().put("instrumentationError", true).put("stage", "install").put("failureClass", failure.getClass().getName())); }
    }
  }
  public static final class B1OutcomeAdvice {
    @net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.OnMethodEnter(suppress = Throwable.class)
    public static boolean enter(@net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.Argument(0) String name, @net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.Argument(1) JsonNode body) { return B1OutcomeCapture.begin(name, body); }
    @net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.OnMethodExit(onThrowable = Throwable.class, suppress = Throwable.class)
    public static void exit(@net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.Enter boolean entered, @net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.Thrown Throwable failure) { B1OutcomeCapture.end(entered, failure); }
  }
  public static final class B1SchemaAdvice {
    @net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.OnMethodExit(suppress = Throwable.class)
    public static void exit(@net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.This Object schema,
        @net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice.Return(typing = net.bytebuddy.implementation.bytecode.assign.Assigner.Typing.DYNAMIC) Object result) { B1OutcomeCapture.schema(schema, result); }
  }

  @DynamicPropertySource
  static void properties(DynamicPropertyRegistry r) {
    r.add("spring.datasource.driver-class-name", () -> "org.postgresql.Driver");
    r.add("spring.datasource.url", DB::getJdbcUrl);
    r.add("spring.datasource.username", DB::getUsername);
    r.add("spring.datasource.password", DB::getPassword);
    r.add("server.port", () -> BACKEND_PORT);
    r.add("management.endpoints.web.exposure.include", () -> "health,prometheus");
    r.add("checks.core-service.base-url", () -> ORIGIN);
    r.add("server.ssl.enabled", () -> true);
    r.add("server.ssl.key-store", () -> TEMP.resolve("tls.p12").toString());
    r.add("server.ssl.key-store-password", () -> "fixture");
    r.add("server.ssl.key-store-type", () -> "PKCS12");
    r.add("server.ssl.trust-store", () -> TEMP.resolve("trust.p12").toString());
    r.add("server.ssl.trust-store-password", () -> "fixture");
    r.add("server.ssl.client-auth", () -> "want");
    r.add("studio.guided-authoring.enabled", () -> true);
    r.add(
        "studio.guided-authoring.service-public-key",
        () -> Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(ISSUER.getPublic().getEncoded()));

  @TestConfiguration
  static class RuntimeHooks {
    @Bean
    @org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary
    GuidedSessionRepository observedOriginalSessionRepository(
        JdbcTemplate jdbc,
        GuidedContracts contracts,
        org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
      var transactions =
          new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate(manager) {
            @Override
            public <T> T execute(
                org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionCallback<T> callback) {
              try {
                C5Capture capture = c5Capture;
                C5ClaimSampler observer = capture == null ? null : capture.claimSampler;
                if (observer == null
                    || org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager
                        .isActualTransactionActive()
                    || !observer.observingCallbacks()) {
                  if (capture != null
                      && capture.claimSamplerSelected
                      && capture.pendingFinalizer()
                      && !org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager
                          .isActualTransactionActive()) {
                    boolean[] committedCandidate = {false};
                    T result =
                        super.execute(
                            status -> {
                              T value = callback.doInTransaction(status);
                              committedCandidate[0] =
                                  status.isNewTransaction() && !status.isRollbackOnly();
                              return value;
                            });
                    if (committedCandidate[0]
                        && !org.springframework.transaction.support
                            .TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive()
                        && result instanceof PublicationContracts.Attempt attempt
                        && attempt.status() == PublicationContracts.AttemptStatus.VALIDATING) {
                      capture.afterPublicationClaim(attempt.validationDeadline());
                    }
                    return result;
                  }
                  return super.execute(callback);
                }
                return super.execute(
                    status -> {
                      C5ClaimSampler.PgClaimBinding binding =
                          status.isNewTransaction() ? observer.bindPrequery(manager, jdbc) : null;
                      try {
                        return callback.doInTransaction(status);
                      } finally {
                        // Retire BEFORE original superclass commit/rollback, never just filter
                        // exit.
                        observer.retirePrequery(binding);
                      }
                    });
              } catch (GuidedSessionException original) {
                if (p4AdversarialPasswordSelection && P4_FIRST_GUIDED_REQUEST.get() != null) {
                  try {
                    int line = -1;
                    for (StackTraceElement frame : original.getStackTrace()) {
                      if (frame
                              .getClassName()
                              .equals(
                                  "com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.translation.GuidedProposalValidator")
                          && frame.getMethodName().equals("action")) {
                        line = frame.getLineNumber();
                        break;
                      }
                    }
                    boolean policyDenied = original.getMessage().equals("POLICY_DENIED");
                    P4_FIRST_GUARD.compareAndSet(
                        null,
                        C.object()
                            .put("policyDenied", policyDenied)
                            .put("status", original.status())
                            .put("validatorLine", line)
                            .put(
                                "validatorCountPredicate",
                                line == 363 && policyDenied && original.status() == 422)
                            .put(
                                "actualCallbackRollbackObserved",
                                !org.springframework.transaction.support
                                    .TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
                            .put("phaseOrdinal", 1)
                            .put(
                                "passwordCount",
                                P4_FIRST_GUIDED_REQUEST.get().path("passwordCount").asInt(-1)));
                  } catch (RuntimeException | AssertionError observerFailure) {
                    P4_WITNESS_FAILED.set(true);
                  }
                }
                throw original;
              }
            }
          };
      assertThat(transactions.getTransactionManager()).isSameAs(manager);
      return new GuidedSessionRepository(jdbc, transactions, contracts);
    }

    @Bean
    static org.springframework.beans.factory.config.BeanPostProcessor ownedSchedulerTaskTracking() {
      return new org.springframework.beans.factory.config.BeanPostProcessor() {
        @Override
        public Object postProcessBeforeInitialization(Object bean, String name) {
          if (bean
              instanceof
              org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.SimpleAsyncTaskScheduler scheduler) {
            scheduler.setTaskTerminationTimeout(60000);
          }
          return bean;
        }
      };
    }

    @Bean
    @org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary
    GuidedLlmGatewayClient actualCommittedModelBudgetDiagnostics(
        @Qualifier("guidedLlmGatewayClient") GuidedLlmGatewayClient actual, JdbcTemplate jdbc) {
      return (request, budget) -> {
        try {
          COMMITTED_MODEL_BUDGET_WITNESSES.add(committedModelBudgetWitness(jdbc, request));
        } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
          // Diagnostic failure must not replace the actual gateway result or skip its call.
          MODEL_BUDGET_WITNESS_FAILURES.incrementAndGet();
        }
        return actual.propose(request, budget);
      };
    }

    @Bean
    WebMvcConfigurer safeFinalizeDiagnostics(
        ObservedReplayProperties replay,
        GuidedPublicationProperties publication,
        ObservedReplayPins pins) {
      return new WebMvcConfigurer() {
        @Override
        public void extendHandlerExceptionResolvers(List<HandlerExceptionResolver> resolvers) {
          resolvers.add(
              0,
              (request, response, handler, failure) -> {
                String path = request.getRequestURI();
                boolean finalize =
                    path.startsWith("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/")
                        && path.endsWith("/finalize");
                boolean planning =
                    path.startsWith("/internal/v2/studio/guided-sessions/")
                        && List.of("plans", "dependency-admissions", "proposals")
                            .contains(path.substring(path.lastIndexOf('/') + 1));
                if ((finalize || planning) && failure instanceof GuidedSessionException guarded) {
                  persistFinalizeDiagnostic(guarded, replay, publication, pins);
                }
                // Observe metadata only; the existing production resolver still decides the
                // response.
                return null;
              });
        }
      };
    }

    @Bean
    @org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary
    RunnerDispatchClient actualDispatchDiagnostics(HttpRunnerDispatchClient actual) {
      return new RunnerDispatchClient() {
        @Override
        public DispatchOutcome send(RunJob job) {
          UUID id = job.executionId();
          try {
            DispatchOutcome result = actual.send(job);
            ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.compute(
                id,
                (key, prior) -> {
                  ObjectNode metadata = prior == null ? C.object() : prior.deepCopy();
                  metadata.set("sendOutcome", safeDispatchOutcome(result));
                  return metadata;
                });
            return result;
          } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
            ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.compute(
                id,
                (key, prior) -> {
                  ObjectNode metadata = prior == null ? C.object() : prior.deepCopy();
                  metadata.set("sendException", safeProbeFailure(failure));
                  return metadata;
                });
            throw failure;
          }
        }

        @Override
        public CancelOutcome cancel(UUID executionId) {
          CancelOutcome result = actual.cancel(executionId);
          ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.compute(
              executionId,
              (key, prior) -> {
                ObjectNode metadata = prior == null ? C.object() : prior.deepCopy();
                metadata.put("cancelOutcome", result.name());
                return metadata;
              });
          return result;
        }
      };
    }

    @Bean
    @org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary
    com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.StudioVaultProvider countedLoginVault() {
      return COUNTED_VAULT;
    }

    @Bean
    @org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary
    @SuppressWarnings("checkstyle:ParameterNumber")
    StudioDispatcher recordedObservedRefusals(
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioDispatchLedgerStatements statements,
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.UITestRevisionRepository revisions,
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.DispatchAuthorization authorization,
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.RunnerDispatchClient runner,
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.StudioExecutionProperties properties,
        ObjectMapper mapper,
        JdbcTemplate jdbc,
        org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate transactions) {
      return new com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioDispatcherFailureIT.RecordingDispatcher(
          statements,
          revisions,
          authorization,
          runner,
          properties,
          mapper,
          jdbc,
          transactions,
          new com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioDispatcherFailureIT.RecordingDispatcher
              .Observer() {
            public void record(
                JsonNode binding, RuntimeException failure, String code, java.time.Instant now) {
              C5Capture active = c5Capture;
              if (active != null) {
                active.recordPoolRefusal(binding, failure, code, now);
              }
            }

            public void failed() {
              C5Capture active = c5Capture;
              if (active != null && active.poolRefusalTarget != null) {
                active.failed = true;
              }
            }
          });
    }

    @Bean
    @org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary
    com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.StudioSecretUseAuthorization controlledLoginEntitlement() {
      return (principal, csi, environment, name) -> {
        P3_MAY_USE.add(new P3Entitlement(principal, csi, environment, name));
        return java.util.Set.of("fixture@example.invalid", "user-1").contains(principal)
                && csi.equals(primaryCsi)
                && environment.equals("fixture")
                && name.equals("PASS")
            || p3Entitled(principal, csi, environment, name);
      };
    }

    @Bean
    com.citi.put.checks.studio.internal.ProgressReportService.ReportCommitHook
        observedCommitFault() {
      return id -> {
        if (id.equals(preReadyId) && FAIL_TERMINAL_COMMIT.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
          TERMINAL_ROLLBACKS.incrementAndGet();
          throw new IllegalStateException("Controlled observed terminal commit rollback");
        }
      };
    }

    @Bean
    org.springframework.boot.web.servlet.FilterRegistrationBean<jakarta.servlet.Filter>
        observedReportCapture() {
      jakarta.servlet.Filter filter =
          (request, response, chain) -> {
            var http = (jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest) request;
            C5Capture activeC5 = c5Capture;
            if (activeC5 != null
                && activeC5.claimSamplerSelected
                && activeC5.selectFinalizer(http)) {
              try {
                chain.doFilter(request, response);
              } finally {
                activeC5.retireFinalizer();
              }
              return;
            }
            P4RevocationState c6 = p4Revocation;
            if (c6 != null
                && c6.c6Kind == 4
                && c6.guidedSession != null
                && http.getMethod().equals("POST")
                && http.getRequestURI()
                    .equals(
                        "/internal/v2/studio/guided-sessions/" + c6.guidedSession + "/outcomes")) {
              var wrapped =
                  new org.springframework.web.util.ContentCachingRequestWrapper(http, 262144);
              try {
                chain.doFilter(wrapped, response);
              } finally {
                p8CaptureInjectedCallback(
                    c6,
                    wrapped.getContentAsByteArray(),
                    ((jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse) response).getStatus());
              }
              return;
            }
            if (activeC5 != null
                && (activeC5.selectedLifecycle(http) || activeC5.selectedClaim(http))) {
              var wrapped =
                  new org.springframework.web.util.ContentCachingRequestWrapper(http, 262144);
              boolean claim = activeC5.selectedClaim(http);
              long ingress = System.nanoTime();
              C5ClaimSampler selectedSampler = claim ? activeC5.claimSampler : null;
              if (selectedSampler != null) {
                selectedSampler.enterCurrent();
              }
              try {
                chain.doFilter(wrapped, response);
              } finally {
                if (selectedSampler != null) {
                  selectedSampler.leaveCurrent();
                }
                int status = ((jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse) response).getStatus();
                if (claim) {
                  activeC5.recordClaim(
                      wrapped.getContentAsByteArray(), status, System.nanoTime() - ingress);
                } else {
                  activeC5.recordLifecycle(wrapped.getContentAsByteArray(), status);
                }
              }
              return;
            }
            UUID p3Execution =
                P3_EXECUTIONS.stream()
                    .filter(
                        id ->
                            http.getRequestURI()
                                .equals("/internal/studio/executions/" + id + "/progress"))
                    .findFirst()
                    .orElse(null);
            if (p3Execution != null) {
              var wrapped =
                  new org.springframework.web.util.ContentCachingRequestWrapper(http, 262144);
              try {
                chain.doFilter(wrapped, response);
              } finally {
                P6ChildCut cut = p6ChildCut;
                if (cut != null && cut.execution.equals(p3Execution)) {
                  cut.capture(
                      http,
                      wrapped.getContentAsByteArray(),
                      ((jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse) response).getStatus());
                }
              }
              if (((jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse) response).getStatus() >= 200
                  && ((jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse) response).getStatus() < 300
                  && wrapped.getContentAsByteArray().length > 0) {
                p3CaptureOrdinaryReport(p3Execution, wrapped.getContentAsByteArray());
              }
              return;
            }
            UUID target = preReadyId;
            boolean capture =
                target != null
                    && http.getRequestURI()
                        .equals("/internal/studio/executions/" + target + "/progress")
                    && http.getHeader("X-Observed-Negative-Probe") == null;
            if (!capture) {
              chain.doFilter(request, response);
              return;
            }
            if (HOLD_TERMINAL_RETRY.get() && TERMINAL_ROLLBACKS.get() > 0) {
              retryPaused.countDown();
              try {
                if (!retryRelease.await(10, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                  throw new jakarta.servlet.ServletException("Controlled retry barrier timed out");
                }
              } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                throw new jakarta.servlet.ServletException("Controlled retry barrier interrupted");
              }
            }
            var wrapped =
                new org.springframework.web.util.ContentCachingRequestWrapper(http, 262144);
            try {
              chain.doFilter(wrapped, response);
            } finally {
              if (wrapped.getContentAsByteArray().length > 0) {
                try {
                  RECEIVED_FAILURE_REPORTS.add(C.parseWire(wrapped.getContentAsByteArray()));
                } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
                  /* Original production parsing still owns the response. */
                }
              }
            }
          };
      var registration = new org.springframework.boot.web.servlet.FilterRegistrationBean<>(filter);
      registration.addUrlPatterns(
          "/internal/studio/executions/*", "/internal/v2/studio/guided-sessions/*");
      if ("true".equals(System.getenv("STUDIO_C5_CLAIM_DIAGNOSTIC"))) {
        registration.addUrlPatterns("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/*");
      }
      return registration;
    }
  }

  @LocalServerPort int port;
  @Autowired JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  @Autowired org.springframework.context.ConfigurableApplicationContext ownedApplicationContext;
  private org.springframework.test.context.TestContext ownedFrameworkContext;
  private boolean ownedApplicationQuiescent;

  public static final class OwnedContextCapture
      extends org.springframework.test.context.support.AbstractTestExecutionListener {
    private GuidedPublicationReplayIT ownedInstance;

    @Override
    public int getOrder() {
      return 3500;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepareTestInstance(org.springframework.test.context.TestContext testContext) {
      assertThat(testContext.getTestClass()).isEqualTo(GuidedPublicationReplayIT.class);
      var instance = (GuidedPublicationReplayIT) testContext.getTestInstance();
      assertThat(testContext.getApplicationContext()).isSameAs(instance.ownedApplicationContext);
      instance.ownedFrameworkContext = testContext;
      ownedInstance = instance;
    }

    @Override
    public void afterTestClass(org.springframework.test.context.TestContext testContext)
        throws Exception {
      try {
        assertThat(testContext.getTestClass()).isEqualTo(GuidedPublicationReplayIT.class);
        assertThat(ownedInstance).isNotNull();
        assertThat(ownedInstance.ownedFrameworkContext).isSameAs(testContext);
        assertThat(testContext.getApplicationContext())
            .isSameAs(ownedInstance.ownedApplicationContext);
        ownedInstance.stop();
      } finally {
        ownedInstance = null;
      }
    }
  }

  @Autowired GuidedSessionRepository sessions;
  @Autowired GuidedTranslationStore plans;
  @Autowired StudioDispatcher dispatcher;
  @Autowired StudioGatewayProperties gatewayProperties;
  @Autowired com.citi.put.checks.studio.scheduling.StudioSchedulingProperties scheduling;
  @Autowired com.citi.put.checks.studio.scheduling.StudioScheduleProcessor scheduleProcessor;

  private final Map<String, String> testOwnedSessions = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
  private Throwable failedTest;
  private boolean cleanupUnconfirmed;
  private int beforeFixtureModelCalls;
  private int beforeFixtureProviderReads;
  private int beforeFixtureProviderFailures;
  private int beforeFixtureModelKinds;

  @RegisterExtension
  final AfterTestExecutionCallback retainActualFailure =
      context -> failedTest = context.getExecutionException().orElse(null);

  @BeforeEach
  void beginOwnedSessionTracking() throws Exception {
    B1OutcomeCapture.install();
    assertThat(cleanupUnconfirmed)
        .as("prior failed-test actual worker teardown was not confirmed; inspect cleanup evidence")
        .isFalse();
    // Every test starts as the CSI owner; a test that fails after lowering the role must not leak
    // viewer/none authority into later tests (that masked root causes as FORBIDDEN_SCOPE).
    coreRole = "owner";
    ensureAuditCapture();
    tallyBefore = com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.GuardDecisionTally.sessions(jdbc);
    tallyAuditStart = AUDIT_EVENTS.list.size();
    assertThat(primaryCsi).as("a prior P3 case left its CSI unrestored").isEqualTo("CSI-123");
    refreshFixtureCredentials();
    testOwnedSessions.clear();
    failedTest = null;
    beforeFixtureModelCalls = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    beforeFixtureModelKinds = MODEL_KINDS.size();
    beforeFixtureProviderReads = VAULT.readCount();
    beforeFixtureProviderFailures = PROVIDER_FAILURES.get();
    assertThat(safeExecutionMetadata(UUID.randomUUID()))
        .as("read-only empty execution diagnostic query preflight")
        .isEmpty();
  }

  @AfterEach
  void cancelFailedTestOwnedSessions(TestInfo testInfo) throws Exception {
    try {
      // AC-4 tally (po-qa014-rulings a1): this class plants no guard refusal.
      com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.GuardDecisionTally.record(
          GuidedPublicationReplayIT.class,
          testInfo.getTestMethod().orElseThrow().getName(),
          jdbc,
          tallyBefore,
          List.copyOf(AUDIT_EVENTS.list.subList(tallyAuditStart, AUDIT_EVENTS.list.size())),
          java.util.Set.of());
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError tallyFailure) {
      if (failedTest == null) {
        throw tallyFailure;
      }
      failedTest.addSuppressed(tallyFailure);
    }
    try {
      persistActualBudgetEvidence(testInfo);
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError evidenceFailure) {
      if (failedTest == null) {
        throw evidenceFailure;
      }
      failedTest.addSuppressed(evidenceFailure);
    }
    if (failedTest == null) {
      return;
    }
    var evidence = C.object().put("testMethod", testInfo.getTestMethod().orElseThrow().getName());
    var cleanups = evidence.putArray("sessions");
    for (var owned : testOwnedSessions.entrySet()) {
      var cleanup = cleanups.addObject().put("sessionId", owned.getKey());
      try {
        refreshFixtureCredentials(); // Actual cancel/teardown acknowledgement needs a live token.
        var metadata = C.object();
        var before =
            sessions.transaction(
                () -> {
                  var locked = sessions.lock(owned.getKey());
                  try {
                    metadata.setAll(safeFailureMetadata(locked));
                  } catch (RuntimeException metadataFailure) {
                    metadata.put("captureFailure", true);
                    failedTest.addSuppressed(
                        new IllegalStateException("Safe failure metadata unavailable"));
                  }
                  return locked;
                });
        metadata.put("testMethod", testInfo.getTestMethod().orElseThrow().getName());
        try {
          Files.writeString(
              TEMP.resolve(
                  "failed-test-state-"
                      + testInfo.getTestMethod().orElseThrow().getName()
                      + "-"
                      + owned.getKey()
                      + ".json"),
              metadata.toPrettyString());
        } catch (IOException writeFailure) {
          failedTest.addSuppressed(
              new IllegalStateException("Safe failure metadata could not be saved"));
        }
        try {
          metadata.set("executions", safeExecutionMetadata(UUID.fromString(owned.getValue())));
        } catch (RuntimeException probeFailure) {
          metadata.set("executionProbeFailure", safeProbeFailure(probeFailure));
          failedTest.addSuppressed(
              new IllegalStateException("Optional execution diagnostic unavailable"));
        }
        try {
          Files.writeString(
              TEMP.resolve(
                  "failed-test-state-"
                      + testInfo.getTestMethod().orElseThrow().getName()
                      + "-"
                      + owned.getKey()
                      + ".json"),
              metadata.toPrettyString());
        } catch (IOException writeFailure) {
          failedTest.addSuppressed(
              new IllegalStateException("Enriched safe failure metadata could not be saved"));
        }
        assertThat(before.document().path("testId").asText()).isEqualTo(owned.getValue());
        assertThat(before.document().path("scope").path("principalId").asText())
            .isEqualTo("user-1");
        String state = before.document().path("state").asText();
        cleanup.put("beforeState", state);
        if (!List.of(
                "SUCCEEDED",
                "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT",
                "FAILED",
                "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
                "CANCELLED",
                "EXPIRED")
            .contains(state)) {
          var request =
              HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                      URI.create(
                          "https://localhost:"
                              + port
                              + "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/"
                              + owned.getKey()
                              + "/cancel"))
                  .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15))
                  .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE)
                  .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                  .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                  .POST(
                      HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(
                          C.object()
                              .put("schemaVersion", 3)
                              .put("reason", "userRequested")
                              .toString()))
                  .build();
          var response =
              HttpClient.newBuilder()
                  .sslContext(TLS)
                  .build()
                  .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
          cleanup.put("cancelHttpStatus", response.statusCode());
          assertThat(response.statusCode())
              .as("actual authenticated fixture cancellation")
              .isEqualTo(202);
          assertThat(C.parse(response.body()).path("state").asText()).isEqualTo("CANCELLED");
        }
        long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
        String teardown;
        do {
          teardown =
              sessions.transaction(
                  () -> sessions.lock(owned.getKey()).progress().path("teardownStatus").asText());
          if (teardown.equals("CONFIRMED")) {
            break;
          }
          Thread.sleep(30);
        } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
        cleanup.put("teardownStatus", teardown);
        assertThat(teardown).as("actual worker stop/close acknowledgement").isEqualTo("CONFIRMED");
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
        cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
        cleanup.put("cleanupFailureClass", failure.getClass().getName());
        failedTest.addSuppressed(failure);
        // Preserve the original test failure and block later bodies if actual teardown is unknown.
      }
    }
    evidence.put("allActualTeardownsConfirmed", !cleanupUnconfirmed);
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve(
            "failed-test-cleanup-" + testInfo.getTestMethod().orElseThrow().getName() + ".json"),
        evidence.toPrettyString());
  }

  private void persistActualBudgetEvidence(TestInfo testInfo) throws Exception {
    if (testOwnedSessions.isEmpty()) {
      return;
    }
    assertThat(gatewayProperties.maxOutputTokens()).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(4000);
    var evidence = C.object().put("transportOutputCeiling", gatewayProperties.maxOutputTokens());
    var scopedSessions = evidence.putArray("sessions");
    for (String id : testOwnedSessions.keySet()) {
      var scoped = scopedSessions.addObject();
      long sessionOrdinal = budgetSessionOrdinal(jdbc, id);
      scoped.put("sessionOrdinal", sessionOrdinal);
      JsonNode limits =
          sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("limits").deepCopy());
      assertThat(limits.path("maxModelOutputTokensTotal").asInt()).isEqualTo(32000);
      assertThat(limits.path("maxModelInputTokensTotal").asInt()).isEqualTo(262144);
      copyDiagnosticNumbers(
          limits,
          scoped.putObject("sessionLimits"),
          "maxModelOutputTokensTotal",
          "maxModelInputTokensTotal");
      var witnesses = scoped.putArray("beforeGatewaySend");
      COMMITTED_MODEL_BUDGET_WITNESSES.stream()
          .filter(witness -> witness.path("sessionOrdinal").asLong() == sessionOrdinal)
          .sorted(
              java.util.Comparator.comparingLong(
                  witness -> witness.path("attemptOrdinal").asLong()))
          .forEach(witnesses::add);
      long priorInput = 0, priorOutput = 0, priorProposals = 0;
      for (JsonNode witness : witnesses) {
        assertThat(witness.path("outsideCallerTransaction").asBoolean()).isTrue();
        assertThat(witness.path("committedCallingReservation").asBoolean()).isTrue();
        assertThat(witness.path("serviceReservationOutput").asLong()).isEqualTo(4000);
        assertThat(witness.path("persistedOutputCeiling").asLong()).isEqualTo(4000);
        assertThat(witness.path("actualGatewayOutputCeiling").asLong()).isEqualTo(4000);
        assertThat(witness.path("serviceReservationInput").asLong())
            .isEqualTo(witness.path("persistedInputCeiling").asLong());
        assertThat(witness.path("cumulativeInput").asLong())
            .isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(priorInput + witness.path("serviceReservationInput").asLong());
        assertThat(witness.path("cumulativeOutput").asLong())
            .isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(priorOutput + 4000);
        assertThat(witness.path("inputTotalLimit").asLong()).isEqualTo(262144);
        assertThat(witness.path("outputTotalLimit").asLong()).isEqualTo(32000);
        assertThat(witness.path("cumulativeInput").asLong()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(262144);
        assertThat(witness.path("cumulativeOutput").asLong()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(32000);
        assertThat(witness.path("cumulativeProposals").asLong()).isGreaterThan(priorProposals);
        priorProposals = witness.path("cumulativeProposals").asLong();
        priorInput = witness.path("cumulativeInput").asLong();
        priorOutput = witness.path("cumulativeOutput").asLong();
      }
      var attempts = scoped.putArray("attempts");
      for (var row :
          jdbc.queryForList(
              "SELECT a.request_id,a.attempt_number,a.status,a.identity->>'inputCeiling' AS"
                  + " input_ceiling, a.identity->>'outputCeiling' AS output_ceiling, (SELECT"
                  + " count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts earlier WHERE"
                  + " earlier.session_id=a.session_id AND (earlier.created_at,earlier.attempt_id)"
                  + " <= (a.created_at,a.attempt_id)) AS attempt_ordinal FROM"
                  + " studio_guided_model_attempts a WHERE a.session_id=? ORDER BY"
                  + " a.created_at,a.attempt_id",
              id)) {
        long inputCeiling = Long.parseLong(String.valueOf(row.get("input_ceiling")));
        long outputCeiling = Long.parseLong(String.valueOf(row.get("output_ceiling")));
        assertThat(outputCeiling).isEqualTo(4000);
        long attemptOrdinal = ((Number) row.get("attempt_ordinal")).longValue();
        var safe =
            attempts
                .addObject()
                .put("attemptOrdinal", attemptOrdinal)
                .put("persistedInputCeiling", inputCeiling)
                .put("persistedOutputCeiling", outputCeiling)
                .put("wireFound", false);
        for (String raw : MODEL_WIRE) {
          JsonNode wire = C.parse(raw);
          JsonNode request =
              C.parse(wire.path("contents").get(0).path("parts").get(0).path("text").asText());
          if (request.path("requestId").asText().equals(String.valueOf(row.get("request_id")))) {
            int actualOutput = wire.path("generationConfig").path("maxOutputTokens").asInt();
            int actualEncodedInput = raw.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length;
            assertThat(actualOutput).isEqualTo(4000);
            assertThat((long) actualEncodedInput).isLessThanOrEqualTo(inputCeiling);
            safe.put("wireFound", true)
                .put("actualWireOutputCeiling", actualOutput)
                .put("actualCompleteEncodedInputBytes", actualEncodedInput);
          }
        }
        if ("APPLIED".equals(row.get("status"))) {
          assertThat(witnesses.findValues("attemptOrdinal"))
              .filteredOn(ordinal -> ordinal.asLong() == attemptOrdinal)
              .hasSize(1);
          assertThat(safe.path("wireFound").asBoolean())
              .as("applied attempt has actual scoped controlled gateway wire evidence")
              .isTrue();
        }
      }
    }
    assertThat(MODEL_BUDGET_WITNESS_FAILURES.get()).isZero();
    requireNumericBudgetEvidence(evidence);
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve(
            "model-budget-evidence-" + testInfo.getTestMethod().orElseThrow().getName() + ".json"),
        evidence.toPrettyString());
  }

  private static long budgetSessionOrdinal(JdbcTemplate jdbc, String id) {
    return jdbc.queryForObject(
        "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_sessions earlier JOIN studio_guided_sessions scoped ON"
            + " scoped.session_id=? WHERE"
            + " ((earlier.document->>'createdAt')::timestamptz,earlier.session_id) <="
            + " ((scoped.document->>'createdAt')::timestamptz,scoped.session_id)",
        Long.class,
        id);
  }

  private static ObjectNode committedModelBudgetWitness(
      JdbcTemplate jdbc, GuidedModelRequest request) {
    String id = request.projection().document().path("sessionId").asText();
    var row =
        jdbc.queryForMap(
            "SELECT a.status,a.identity->>'inputCeiling' AS input_ceiling,"
                + " a.identity->>'outputCeiling' AS output_ceiling,"
                + " s.progress->'modelReservations'->a.reservation_key->>'input' AS"
                + " reservation_input,"
                + " s.progress->'modelReservations'->a.reservation_key->>'output' AS"
                + " reservation_output, s.document->'counters'->>'modelInputTokens' AS"
                + " cumulative_input, s.document->'counters'->>'modelOutputTokens' AS"
                + " cumulative_output, s.document->'counters'->>'modelProposals' AS"
                + " cumulative_proposals, s.document->'limits'->>'maxModelInputTokensTotal' AS"
                + " input_limit, s.document->'limits'->>'maxModelOutputTokensTotal' AS"
                + " output_limit, (SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts earlier WHERE"
                + " earlier.session_id=a.session_id AND (earlier.created_at,earlier.attempt_id) <="
                + " (a.created_at,a.attempt_id)) AS attempt_ordinal FROM"
                + " studio_guided_model_attempts a JOIN studio_guided_sessions s USING(session_id)"
                + " WHERE a.session_id=? AND a.request_id=? AND a.attempt_number=?",
            id,
            request.requestId(),
            request.attempt());
    var witness =
        C.object()
            .put("sessionOrdinal", budgetSessionOrdinal(jdbc, id))
            .put("attemptOrdinal", ((Number) row.get("attempt_ordinal")).longValue())
            .put("actualGatewayOutputCeiling", request.maxOutputTokens())
            .put(
                "outsideCallerTransaction",
                !org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager
                    .isActualTransactionActive())
            .put(
                "committedCallingReservation",
                "CALLING".equals(row.get("status"))
                    && row.get("reservation_input") != null
                    && row.get("reservation_output") != null);
    for (var field :
        Map.of(
                "persistedInputCeiling",
                "input_ceiling",
                "persistedOutputCeiling",
                "output_ceiling",
                "serviceReservationInput",
                "reservation_input",
                "serviceReservationOutput",
                "reservation_output",
                "cumulativeInput",
                "cumulative_input",
                "cumulativeOutput",
                "cumulative_output",
                "cumulativeProposals",
                "cumulative_proposals",
                "inputTotalLimit",
                "input_limit",
                "outputTotalLimit",
                "output_limit")
            .entrySet()) {
      witness.put(field.getKey(), Long.parseLong(String.valueOf(row.get(field.getValue()))));
    }
    requireNumericBudgetEvidence(witness);
    return witness;
  }

  private static void requireNumericBudgetEvidence(JsonNode value) {
    if (value.isContainerNode()) {
      value.forEach(GuidedPublicationReplayIT::requireNumericBudgetEvidence);
    } else {
      assertThat(value.isNumber() || value.isBoolean()).isTrue();
    }
  }

  private ObjectNode safeFailureMetadata(GuidedSessionRepository.Session locked) {
    JsonNode document = locked.document(), progress = locked.progress();
    String id = document.path("sessionId").asText();
    var out = C.object().put("schemaVersion", 1);
    out.put("publicationSnapshotPresent", progress.path("publicationSnapshotIdentity").isObject());
    out.put("publicationRevisionPresent", progress.path("publicationRevisionId").isTextual());
    putDiagnosticUuid(out, "sessionId", id);
    putDiagnosticUuid(out, "testId", document.path("testId").asText());
    out.put(
        "state",
        closedDiagnostic(
            document.path("state").asText(),
            "PREPARING",
            "OBSERVING",
            "PROPOSING",
            "ACTING",
            "AWAITING_CLARIFICATION",
            "FINALIZING",
            "SUCCEEDED",
            "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT",
            "FAILED",
            "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
            "CANCELLED",
            "EXPIRED"));
    out.put("errorCode", safeDiagnosticCode(progress.path("errorCode").asText()));
    out.put(
        "bootstrapPhase",
        closedDiagnostic(
            progress.path("translationBootstrap").path("phase").asText(),
            "SOURCE_PLAN",
            "PLAN_ACCEPTED",
            "MATERIALIZED",
            "ALLOCATED",
            "CONTEXT_PENDING",
            "READY"));
    out.put(
        "materializationPresent",
        Boolean.TRUE.equals(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM studio_guided_materializations WHERE session_id=?)",
                Boolean.class,
                id)));
    copyDiagnosticNumbers(
        document.path("counters"),
        out.putObject("counters"),
        "actions",
        "observations",
        "modelProposals",
        "modelInputTokens",
        "modelOutputTokens",
        "sanitizedEvidenceBytes");
    copyDiagnosticNumbers(
        progress,
        out.putObject("coordinate"),
        "stepIndex",
        "translationOrdinal",
        "generation",
        "contextRevision");
    int stepIndex = progress.path("stepIndex").asInt(-1);
    if (stepIndex >= 0 && stepIndex < document.path("sourceSteps").size()) {
      putDiagnosticUuid(
          out,
          "activeSourceStepId",
          document.path("sourceSteps").get(stepIndex).path("stepId").asText());
    }
    copyDiagnosticNumbers(
        progress.path("observation").path("coverage"),
        out.putObject("observationCounts"),
        "passwordControls",
        "withheldRegions");
    copyDiagnosticNumbers(
        progress.path("translationAcquisition"),
        out.putObject("acquisition"),
        "allowanceMs",
        "totalExtraPolls");
    copyDiagnosticNumbers(
        progress.path("translationAcquisition").path("active"),
        out.putObject("activeAcquisition"),
        "ordinal",
        "elapsedMs",
        "extraPolls");
    out.put("fixtureModelHttpCalls", MODEL_WIRE.size() - beforeFixtureModelCalls)
        .put("fixturePhysicalProviderReads", VAULT.readCount() - beforeFixtureProviderReads)
        .put("fixtureProviderFailures", PROVIDER_FAILURES.get() - beforeFixtureProviderFailures);
    var modelCounts = out.putObject("fixtureModelKinds");
    List<String> kinds = List.copyOf(MODEL_KINDS);
    for (String kind : List.of("SOURCE_PLAN", "NEXT_ACTION", "VERIFY_STEP")) {
      modelCounts.put(
          kind,
          kinds.subList(Math.min(beforeFixtureModelKinds, kinds.size()), kinds.size()).stream()
              .filter(kind::equals)
              .count());
    }
    if (plans.exists(id)) {
      JsonNode translation = plans.state(id);
      out.put(
          "continuation",
          closedDiagnostic(
              translation.path("continuation").asText(),
              "SOURCE_PLAN",
              "MATERIALIZE",
              "DEPENDENCIES",
              "NEXT",
              "REFRESH",
              "AWAITING_CLARIFICATION",
              "FINALIZING",
              "STOPPED"));
      copyDiagnosticNumbers(translation, out.putObject("translationCounters"), "planRevision");
      var declarations = out.putArray("declarations");
      for (JsonNode declared : translation.path("manifest").path("configurationReferences")) {
        var safe =
            declarations
                .addObject()
                .put(
                    "kind",
                    closedDiagnostic(
                        declared.path("kind").asText(),
                        "destination",
                        "nonsecretText",
                        "secretRef"));
        putDiagnosticUuid(safe, "stepId", declared.path("stepId").asText());
      }
      var obligations = out.putArray("obligations");
      for (JsonNode obligation : translation.path("plan").path("obligations")) {
        var safe =
            obligations
                .addObject()
                .put(
                    "operationKind",
                    closedDiagnostic(
                        obligation.path("operationKind").asText(),
                        "navigate",
                        "fill",
                        "click",
                        "read",
                        "assert",
                        "press",
                        "hover",
                        "check",
                        "uncheck",
                        "selectOption"));
        putDiagnosticUuid(safe, "stepId", obligation.path("stepId").asText());
        copyDiagnosticNumbers(obligation, safe, "occurrence", "referenceIndex");
      }
    }
    var actions = out.putArray("actions");
    for (var row :
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT document::text AS ledger,proposal->'operation'->>'kind' AS operation_kind"
                + " FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY step_id,ordinal",
            id)) {
      JsonNode action = C.parse(String.valueOf(row.get("ledger")));
      var safe =
          actions
              .addObject()
              .put(
                  "operationKind",
                  closedDiagnostic(
                      String.valueOf(row.get("operation_kind")),
                      "navigate",
                      "fill",
                      "click",
                      "read",
                      "assert",
                      "press",
                      "hover",
                      "check",
                      "uncheck",
                      "selectOption"))
              .put(
                  "state",
                  closedDiagnostic(
                      action.path("state").asText(),
                      "ADMITTED",
                      "DISPATCHING",
                      "SUCCEEDED",
                      "FAILED",
                      "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
                      "REVOKED"))
              .put("outcomeCode", safeDiagnosticCode(action.path("outcomeCode").asText()));
      putDiagnosticUuid(safe, "stepId", action.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText());
      putDiagnosticUuid(safe, "admissionId", action.path("admission").path("admissionId").asText());
      copyDiagnosticNumbers(action.path("coordinate"), safe, "ordinal");
      copyDiagnosticNumbers(action, safe, "dispatchCount", "ownerFence", "generation");
    }
    var associations = out.putArray("obligationAdmissions");
    jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT admission_id IS NOT NULL AS admitted,plan_revision,"
                + " (meaning->>'occurrence')::integer AS occurrence,"
                + " (proposal->'coordinate'->>'ordinal')::integer AS ordinal,"
                + " proposal->'operation'->>'kind' AS operation_kind FROM"
                + " studio_guided_obligation_admissions WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY created_at",
            id)
        .forEach(
            row -> {
              var association =
                  associations
                      .addObject()
                      .put("admitted", Boolean.TRUE.equals(row.get("admitted")))
                      .put(
                          "operationKind",
                          closedDiagnostic(
                              String.valueOf(row.get("operation_kind")),
                              "navigate",
                              "fill",
                              "read",
                              "click",
                              "assert",
                              "press",
                              "hover",
                              "check",
                              "uncheck",
                              "selectOption"));
              for (String field : List.of("plan_revision", "occurrence", "ordinal")) {
                Object number = row.get(field);
                if (number instanceof Number numeric) {
                  association.put(field.replace("_", ""), numeric.longValue());
                }
              }
            });
    var attempts = out.putArray("modelAttempts");
    jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT kind,status,attempt_number,input_tokens,output_tokens, safe_result->>'code' AS"
                + " provider_code,safe_result->>'status' AS response_status,"
                + " safe_result->>'schemaVersion' AS response_schema_version,"
                + " safe_result->'action'->'operation'->>'kind' AS response_operation_kind FROM"
                + " studio_guided_model_attempts WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY"
                + " created_at,attempt_number",
            id)
        .forEach(
            row -> {
              var safe =
                  attempts
                      .addObject()
                      .put(
                          "kind",
                          closedDiagnostic(
                              String.valueOf(row.get("kind")),
                              "SOURCE_PLAN",
                              "NEXT_ACTION",
                              "VERIFY_STEP"))
                      .put(
                          "status",
                          closedDiagnostic(
                              String.valueOf(row.get("status")),
                              "RESERVED",
                              "CALLING",
                              "APPLIED",
                              "REJECTED",
                              "SUPERSEDED",
                              "UNKNOWN_CALL"))
                      .put(
                          "providerCode",
                          closedDiagnostic(
                              String.valueOf(row.get("provider_code")),
                              "OK",
                              "INVALID_RESPONSE",
                              "TOO_LARGE",
                              "BUSY",
                              "TIMEOUT",
                              "TRANSPORT",
                              "DISABLED"));
              safe.put(
                  "responseStatus",
                  closedDiagnostic(
                      String.valueOf(row.get("response_status")),
                      "ACTION",
                      "CLARIFICATION",
                      "COMPLETE",
                      "UNSUPPORTED"));
              safe.put(
                  "responseOperationKind",
                  closedDiagnostic(
                      String.valueOf(row.get("response_operation_kind")),
                      "navigate",
                      "fill",
                      "read",
                      "click",
                      "assert",
                      "press",
                      "hover",
                      "check",
                      "uncheck",
                      "selectOption"));
              Object responseVersion = row.get("response_schema_version");
              if (responseVersion != null
                  && String.valueOf(responseVersion).matches("[0-9]{1,2}")) {
                safe.put(
                    "responseSchemaVersion", Integer.parseInt(String.valueOf(responseVersion)));
              }
              for (String key : List.of("attempt_number", "input_tokens", "output_tokens")) {
                Object value = row.get(key);
                if (value instanceof Number number && number.longValue() >= 0) {
                  safe.put(key.replace("_", ""), number.longValue());
                }
              }
            });
    return out;
  }

  private ArrayNode safeExecutionMetadata(UUID testId) {
    var executions = JSON.createArrayNode();
    jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT e.id,e.revision_id,e.status,e.error_code,e.snapshot_revision, l.state AS"
                + " ledger_state,l.attempt_id,l.last_runner_sequence,"
                + " e.observed_revision_binding->>'attemptId' AS accepted_attempt_id,"
                + " l.execution_id IS NOT NULL AS ledger_present, l.admission_id IS NOT NULL AS"
                + " admission_present, e.observed_admission_receipt IS NOT NULL AS"
                + " observed_receipt_present, e.browser_started_at IS NOT NULL AS browser_started,"
                + " e.video_key IS NOT NULL AS video_present, l.manifest_key IS NOT NULL AS"
                + " manifest_present, e.step_results IS NOT NULL AS step_results_present, CASE WHEN"
                + " e.ended_at IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE GREATEST(0, FLOOR(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM"
                + " e.ended_at-e.started_at)*1000))::bigint END AS elapsed_ms FROM"
                + " ui_test_executions e LEFT JOIN studio_dispatch_ledger l ON l.execution_id=e.id"
                + " AND l.execution_accepted_at=e.started_at WHERE e.ui_test_id=? ORDER BY"
                + " e.started_at,e.id",
            testId)
        .forEach(
            row -> {
              var safe = executions.addObject();
              ObjectNode actualSend = ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.get((UUID) row.get("id"));
              safe.put("actualDispatchClientInvoked", actualSend != null);
              if (actualSend != null) {
                safe.set("actualDispatchClient", actualSend.deepCopy());
              }
              for (String key : List.of("id", "revision_id", "attempt_id", "accepted_attempt_id")) {
                putDiagnosticUuid(safe, key, String.valueOf(row.get(key)));
              }
              safe.put(
                  "status",
                  closedDiagnostic(
                      String.valueOf(row.get("status")),
                      "PENDING",
                      "RUNNING",
                      "PASSED",
                      "FAILED",
                      "ERROR"));
              safe.put(
                  "ledgerState",
                  closedDiagnostic(
                      String.valueOf(row.get("ledger_state")),
                      "QUEUED",
                      "CLAIMED",
                      "DISPATCHING",
                      "ADMITTED",
                      "RUNNING",
                      "FINALIZING",
                      "DONE"));
              Object error = row.get("error_code");
              safe.put(
                  "errorCode",
                  error == null
                      ? "NONE"
                      : ExecutionErrorCode.values().contains(String.valueOf(error))
                          ? String.valueOf(error)
                          : "OTHER");
              for (String key :
                  List.of(
                      "ledger_present",
                      "admission_present",
                      "observed_receipt_present",
                      "browser_started",
                      "video_present",
                      "manifest_present",
                      "step_results_present")) {
                if (row.get(key) instanceof Boolean present) {
                  safe.put(key, present);
                }
              }
              for (String key :
                  List.of("snapshot_revision", "last_runner_sequence", "elapsed_ms")) {
                if (row.get(key) instanceof Number number && number.longValue() >= 0) {
                  safe.put(key, number.longValue());
                }
              }
              if (row.get("id") instanceof UUID executionId) {
                // R10-rev2-2: value-free IPC attribution written by the ordinary parent and child.
                safe.set("ipcDiagnostics", p10IpcDiagnostics(executionId));
              }
            });
    return executions;
  }

  private static ObjectNode safeDispatchOutcome(DispatchOutcome result) {
    var out = C.object().put("kind", result.kind().name()).put("retryable", result.retryable());
    String detail = result.detail();
    if (detail == null) {
      return out.put("reason", "NONE");
    }
    if (detail.equals("the run job could not be serialized")) {
      return out.put("reason", "SERIALIZATION_REFUSAL");
    }
    if (detail.equals(HttpRunnerDispatchClient.RUNNER_NOT_CONFIGURED)) {
      return out.put("reason", "RUNNER_NOT_CONFIGURED");
    }
    if (detail.equals(HttpRunnerDispatchClient.SERVICE_IDENTITY_NOT_CONFIGURED)) {
      return out.put("reason", "SERVICE_IDENTITY_NOT_CONFIGURED");
    }
    if (detail.equals("runner refused with 429")) {
      return out.put("reason", "HTTP_STATUS").put("classifiedHttpStatus", 429);
    }
    var status =
        java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("(?:runner|redirect) ([1-5][0-9]{2})").matcher(detail);
    if (status.matches()) {
      return out.put("reason", "HTTP_STATUS")
          .put("classifiedHttpStatus", Integer.parseInt(status.group(1)));
    }
    return out.put("reason", closedDiagnostic(detail, "timeout", "connection loss", "interrupted"));
  }

  private static ObjectNode safeProbeFailure(RuntimeException failure) {
    var out = C.object();
    var causes = out.putArray("causes");
    Throwable cursor = failure;
    for (int depth = 0; cursor != null && depth < 8; depth++, cursor = cursor.getCause()) {
      var cause = causes.addObject().put("class", cursor.getClass().getName());
      if (cursor instanceof SQLException sql) {
        cause.put(
            "sqlState",
            closedDiagnostic(
                sql.getSQLState(),
                "42703",
                "42P01",
                "42883",
                "22P02",
                "25P02",
                "57014",
                "08006",
                "23514",
                "40001",
                "55P03"));
      }
      var frames = cause.putArray("frames");
      StackTraceElement[] stack = cursor.getStackTrace();
      for (int index = 0; index < Math.min(stack.length, 8); index++) {
        StackTraceElement frame = stack[index];
        frames
            .addObject()
            .put("class", frame.getClassName())
            .put("method", frame.getMethodName())
            .put("line", frame.getLineNumber());
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  private static String closedDiagnostic(String value, String... allowed) {
    return value != null && List.of(allowed).contains(value) ? value : "OTHER";
  }

  private static String safeDiagnosticCode(String value) {
    return closedDiagnostic(
        value,
        "OK",
        "PENDING",
        "INVALID_CONTRACT",
        "UNAUTHENTICATED",
        "FORBIDDEN_SCOPE",
        "REVISION_CONFLICT",
        "STALE_GENERATION",
        "TARGET_MISMATCH",
        "AMBIGUOUS_TARGET",
        "MISSING_SOURCE",
        "BINDING_ORDER",
        "SENSITIVE_SOURCE",
        "ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED",
        "UNSUPPORTED_SURFACE",
        "BUDGET_EXCEEDED",
        "STALE_FENCE",
        "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
        "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
        "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT",
        "RUNTIME_INCOMPATIBLE",
        "ASSERTION_FAILED",
        "CANCELLED",
        "EXPIRED",
        "SECRET_UNAVAILABLE",
        "SECRET_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED",
        "SECRET_USE_REVOKED",
        "VAULT_UNAVAILABLE",
        "LOGIN_REQUIRED",
        "SECRET_TOO_LONG",
        "ACK_LOST",
        "TIMEOUT",
        "TARGET_CHANGED",
        "POLICY_DENIED",
        "REFERENCE_MISMATCH");
  }

  private static void putDiagnosticUuid(ObjectNode out, String key, String value) {
    if (value.matches(
        "[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}")) {
      out.put(key, UUID.fromString(value).toString());
    }
  }

  private static void copyDiagnosticNumbers(JsonNode from, ObjectNode to, String... keys) {
    for (String key : keys) {
      JsonNode value = from.path(key);
      if (value.isIntegralNumber() && value.canConvertToLong() && value.longValue() >= 0) {
        to.put(key, value.longValue());
      }
    }
  }

  private static final List<String> RETAINED_PROGRESS_KEYS =
      List.of(
          "stepIndex",
          "errorCode",
          "detail",
          "teardownStatus",
          "publicationRevisionId",
          "publicationRevisionDigest",
          "evidenceRef");

  private String retainedPublicationSnapshot(String sessionId) {
    StringBuilder snapshot = new StringBuilder();
    for (String table :
        List.of(
            "studio_guided_publication_bases",
            "studio_guided_publication_observations",
            "studio_guided_publication_preparations",
            "studio_guided_publication_admissions",
            "studio_guided_publication_attempts",
            "studio_guided_publications")) {
      snapshot
          .append(table)
          .append('=')
          .append(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(t) ORDER BY to_jsonb(t)::text),'[]')::text"
                      + " FROM "
                      + table
                      + " t WHERE session_id=?",
                  String.class,
                  sessionId))
          .append('\n');
    }
    return snapshot.toString();
  }
