  @Test
  void actualHttpExplicitFullLearnRefreshesStaleDraftIdentityCache() throws Exception {
    startWorkers(); seedGovernedPolicies("CSI-123");
    var stale = LEGACY_V20_STALE_CACHE; var seed = stale.publication();
    com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.LegacyV20ConfirmedBaseFixture.assertStaleCachePreservedAtHead(
        ownedApplicationContext.getBean(javax.sql.DataSource.class), stale);
    UUID test = seed.testId();
    JsonNode cached = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT authoring_revision_inputs::text FROM ui_tests WHERE id=?", String.class, test));
    assertThat(cached).isEqualTo(stale.oldCachedInputs()); assertThat(cached).isNotEqualTo(stale.expectedCurrentInputs());
    JsonNode draft = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test, null, 200, null);
    assertThat(draft.path("readiness").asText()).isEqualTo("NEEDS_RELEARN");
    assertThat(draft.path("firstChangedStepId").asText()).isEqualTo(seed.firstStepId().toString());
    assertThat(C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT authoring_revision_inputs::text FROM ui_tests WHERE id=?", String.class, test))).isEqualTo(cached);
    ObjectNode start = C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("testId", test.toString()).put("sourceRevision", draft.path("sourceRevision").asText())
        .put("configurationRevision", draft.path("configurationRevision").asText()).put("environmentId", "fixture").put("applicationId", "orders")
        .put("baseRevisionId", seed.baseRevisionId().toString()); start.set("versions", versions());
    String before = incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test);
    assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString()).path("messageCode").asText()).isEqualTo("BASE_IDENTITY_INCOMPLETE");
    assertThat(incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test)).isEqualTo(before);
    incrementalTenScenario = true;
    try {
      start.put("firstLearnStepId", seed.firstStepId().toString());
      String id = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString()).path("session").path("sessionId").asText();
      awaitFinalizing(id);
      JsonNode evidence = sessions.transaction(() -> ownedApplicationContext.getBean(GuidedPublicationEvidenceRepository.class).read(id).document());
      assertThat(evidence.path("snapshotEvidence").path("baseProof").isNull()).isTrue();
      assertThat(evidence.path("incrementalEvidence").path("switch").path("reason").asText()).isEqualTo("EMPTY_PREFIX");
      int calls = MODEL_WIRE.size();
      JsonNode receipt = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/finalize", C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3)
          .put("expectedSourceRevision", draft.path("sourceRevision").asText()).put("expectedConfigurationRevision", draft.path("configurationRevision").asText()), 200, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(calls);
      assertThat(C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT authoring_revision_inputs::text FROM ui_tests WHERE id=?", String.class, test))).isEqualTo(stale.expectedCurrentInputs());
      assertThat(C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT authoring_revision_inputs::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?::uuid", String.class, receipt.path("revisionId").asText()))).isEqualTo(stale.expectedCurrentInputs());
      assertThat(C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT \"references\"::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?::uuid", String.class, receipt.path("revisionId").asText())).path("authoredDeclarations"))
          .isEqualTo(stale.newDeclarations()).isNotEqualTo(stale.oldDeclarations());
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT to_jsonb(p)::text FROM studio_guided_publications p WHERE session_id=?", String.class, seed.sessionId())).isEqualTo(seed.immutableReceiptRowBytes());
      var counters = jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT source_revision,configuration_revision FROM ui_tests WHERE id=?", test);
      assertThat(((Number) counters.get("source_revision")).longValue()).isEqualTo(draft.path("sourceRevision").asLong());
      assertThat(((Number) counters.get("configuration_revision")).longValue()).isEqualTo(draft.path("configurationRevision").asLong());
      assertThat(C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT authoring::text FROM ui_tests WHERE id=?", String.class, test))).isEqualTo(stale.newAuthoring());
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT to_jsonb(r)::text FROM ui_test_revisions r WHERE id=?", String.class, seed.baseRevisionId())).isEqualTo(seed.immutableBaseRowBytes());
      JsonNode complete = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?::uuid", String.class, receipt.path("revisionId").asText()));
      assertThat(complete.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes")).hasSize(1);
      assertThat(complete.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom")).isEmpty();
      assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test, null, 200, null).path("readiness").asText()).isEqualTo("READY");
    } finally { incrementalTenScenario = false; }
  }

  @Test
  void historicalIncompleteBaseRequiresExplicitFirstStepAndPublishesFullLearnWithoutOldProof() throws Exception {
    startWorkers(); seedGovernedPolicies("CSI-123");
    com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.LegacyV20ConfirmedBaseFixture.assertPreservedAtHead(
        ownedApplicationContext.getBean(javax.sql.DataSource.class), LEGACY_V20_BASE);
    UUID test = LEGACY_V20_BASE.testId();
    JsonNode old = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test, null, 200, null);
    assertThat(old.path("readiness").asText()).isEqualTo("NEEDS_RELEARN");
    assertThat(old.path("firstChangedStepId").asText()).isEqualTo(LEGACY_V20_BASE.firstStepId().toString());
    // A supported ordinary draft edit gives a real current second position for the narrowed hint.
    // The historical revision remains untouched and incomplete; no V21 row is made incomplete.
    ObjectNode edit = C.object();
    for (String field : List.of("authoringSchemaVersion", "globalContext", "steps", "name", "description", "csiId", "environment", "variables", "configuration", "sourceRevision", "configurationRevision")) edit.set(field, old.path(field).deepCopy());
    String second = UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes("obs015-historical-current-second".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).toString();
    ((com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ArrayNode) edit.path("steps")).addObject().put("id", second).put("text", "Check Destination is visible.");
    var put = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("https://localhost:" + port + "/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test))
        .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE).header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        .PUT(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(edit.toString())).build();
    var edited = HttpClient.newBuilder().sslContext(TLS).build().send(put, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
    assertThat(edited.statusCode()).as(edited.body()).isEqualTo(200);
    JsonNode draft = C.parse(edited.body());
    ObjectNode start = C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("testId", test.toString()).put("sourceRevision", draft.path("sourceRevision").asText())
        .put("configurationRevision", draft.path("configurationRevision").asText()).put("environmentId", "fixture").put("applicationId", "orders")
        .put("baseRevisionId", LEGACY_V20_BASE.baseRevisionId().toString()); start.set("versions", versions());
    String before = incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test);
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size(), nativeNavigations = NAVIGATIONS.get(), effects = RECEIPTS.size();
    JsonNode omitted = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    assertThat(omitted.path("messageCode").asText()).isEqualTo("BASE_IDENTITY_INCOMPLETE");
    assertThat(incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test)).isEqualTo(before);
    JsonNode narrowed = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start.deepCopy().put("firstLearnStepId", second), 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    assertThat(narrowed.path("messageCode").asText()).isEqualTo("FIRST_LEARN_STEP_NARROWED");
    assertThat(incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test)).isEqualTo(before);
    assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(models); assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(nativeNavigations); assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
    incrementalTenScenario = true;
    try {
      start.put("firstLearnStepId", LEGACY_V20_BASE.firstStepId().toString());
      String id = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString()).path("session").path("sessionId").asText();
      awaitFinalizing(id);
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT reason FROM studio_guided_relearn_switches WHERE session_id=?", String.class, id)).isEqualTo("EMPTY_PREFIX");
      JsonNode evidence = sessions.transaction(() -> ownedApplicationContext.getBean(GuidedPublicationEvidenceRepository.class).read(id).document());
      assertThat(evidence.path("snapshotEvidence").path("baseProof").isNull()).isTrue();
      assertThat(evidence.path("incrementalEvidence").path("program").path("actions")).isEmpty();
      assertThat(evidence.path("incrementalEvidence").path("switch").path("retainedPrefixAdmissionIds")).isEmpty();
      int afterLearn = MODEL_WIRE.size(); assertThat(afterLearn).isGreaterThan(models);
      JsonNode published = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/finalize", C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3)
          .put("expectedSourceRevision", draft.path("sourceRevision").asText()).put("expectedConfigurationRevision", draft.path("configurationRevision").asText()), 200, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(afterLearn);
      JsonNode artifact = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?::uuid", String.class, published.path("revisionId").asText()));
      assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes")).hasSize(2);
      assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom")).isEmpty();
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT to_jsonb(r)::text FROM ui_test_revisions r WHERE id=?", String.class, LEGACY_V20_BASE.baseRevisionId())).isEqualTo(LEGACY_V20_BASE.immutableBaseRowBytes());
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT to_jsonb(p)::text FROM studio_guided_publications p WHERE session_id=?", String.class, LEGACY_V20_BASE.sessionId())).isEqualTo(LEGACY_V20_BASE.immutableReceiptRowBytes());
      assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test, null, 200, null).path("readiness").asText()).isEqualTo("READY");
      assertGuidedLearnRunRecorded(id, new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>());
    } finally { incrementalTenScenario = false; }
  }

  private void b1AssertTrustedCeilingStart(ObjectNode start, UUID test) throws Exception {
    ObjectNode narrowed = POLICY.deepCopy(); narrowed.putArray("allowedOrigins").add(SECRET_ORIGIN);
    ((ObjectNode) narrowed.path("applicationPolicy")).putArray("readGrants");
    C.validate("v3/replay-ipc#/definitions/TrustedPolicy", narrowed);
    String pins = GuidedContracts.hash(narrowed), catalog = GuidedContracts.hash(CATALOG);
    Map<String,Object> settings = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
    settings.put("spring.datasource.driver-class-name", "org.postgresql.Driver");
    settings.put("spring.datasource.url", DB.getJdbcUrl()); settings.put("spring.datasource.username", DB.getUsername()); settings.put("spring.datasource.password", DB.getPassword());
    settings.put("server.port", "0"); settings.put("server.ssl.enabled", "false"); settings.put("spring.jmx.enabled", "false"); settings.put("spring.main.banner-mode", "off"); settings.put("logging.level.root", "WARN");
    settings.put("checks.core-service.base-url", ORIGIN);
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.enabled", true);
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.service-public-key", Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(ISSUER.getPublic().getEncoded()));
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.service-issuer", "fixture-issuer");
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.enabled", true); settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.allow-authored-nonsecret", true);
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.applications", "orders"); settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.environments", "fixture,fixture-other");
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.worker-id", "worker-1"); settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.poll-ms", "3600000");
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.controller-origin", "https://localhost:" + port);
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.controller-token-file", TEMP.resolve("coordinator.jwt").toString());
    settings.put("studio.guided.publication.enabled", true); settings.put("studio.observed-replay.enabled", true);
    settings.put("studio.observed-replay.catalog-json", C.object().putArray("catalog").add(CATALOG).toString());
    settings.put("studio.observed-replay.policies-json", C.object().putArray("policies").add(narrowed).add(P3_OTHER_POLICY).toString());
    settings.put("studio.gateway.provider", "vertex"); settings.put("studio.gateway.endpoint", "http://127.0.0.1:" + PROVIDER.getAddress().getPort());
    settings.put("studio.gateway.project", "fixture"); settings.put("studio.gateway.location", "global"); settings.put("studio.gateway.model", "controlled"); settings.put("studio.gateway.credentials.mode", "none");
    settings.put("studio.execution.dispatcher.enabled", false); settings.put("studio.scheduling.poll-interval-ms", "3600000");
    try (var replica = new org.springframework.boot.builder.SpringApplicationBuilder(ChecksApplication.class).profiles("test")
        .initializers(context -> context.getEnvironment().getPropertySources().addFirst(new org.springframework.core.env.MapPropertySource("b1-trusted-ceiling", settings)))
        .run("--spring.profiles.active=test", "--checks.local-dev-auth.enabled=false")) {
      int replicaPort = ((org.springframework.boot.web.servlet.context.ServletWebServerApplicationContext) replica).getWebServer().getPort();
      ObjectNode scope = C.object().put("csiId", primaryCsi).put("applicationId", "orders").put("environmentId", "fixture");
      var actualPins = replica.getBean(ObservedReplayPins.class).policy(scope);
      assertThat(actualPins.path("allowedOrigins")).contains(C.object().textNode(SECRET_ORIGIN));
      assertThat(ownedApplicationContext.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.policy.ObservationPolicyService.class).activeForStart(scope).document().path("allowedOrigins"))
          .contains(C.object().textNode(ORIGIN));
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> replica.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.policy.ObservationPolicyService.class).activeForStart(scope))
          .isInstanceOf(GuidedSessionException.class).hasMessage("RUNTIME_INCOMPATIBLE");
      String before = b1ScopedSnapshot(test);
      int model = MODEL_WIRE.size(), legacy = LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get(), nav = NAVIGATIONS.get(), effects = RECEIPTS.size();
      var request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("http://localhost:" + replicaPort + "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions"))
          .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE).header("Content-Type", "application/json").header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
          .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(start.toString())).build();
      var response = HttpClient.newHttpClient().send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
      assertThat(response.statusCode()).as("actual start-time trusted ceiling recheck").isEqualTo(409);
      assertThat(C.parse(response.body()).path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("RUNTIME_INCOMPATIBLE");
      assertThat(b1ScopedSnapshot(test)).isEqualTo(before);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(model); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacy);
      assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(nav); assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
      assertThat(GuidedContracts.hash(narrowed)).isEqualTo(pins); assertThat(GuidedContracts.hash(CATALOG)).isEqualTo(catalog);
    } finally { ensureAuditCapture(); }
  }

  private void incrementalJoined(String variant) throws Exception {
    startWorkers();
    seedGovernedPolicies(primaryCsi);
    incrementalTenScenario = true;
    INCREMENTAL_MODEL_CONTEXTS.clear();
    try {
      var texts = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
      texts.add("Open " + URL + " and read the Order reference.");
      texts.add(variant.equals("same-pair-literal") ? "Enter that order reference twice in Destination (an authored alternative is literal-control)."
          : "Enter that order reference in Destination (an authored alternative is literal-control).");
      texts.add(variant.equals("assert-fail") ? "Check Order reference contains that order reference." : "Check Destination has that order reference.");
      texts.add("Check Destination is visible.");
      texts.add("Click Submit and check Confirmation appears.");
      for (int index = 5; index < 10; index++) texts.add("Check Confirmation is visible at checkpoint " + (index + 1) + ".");
      if (variant.startsWith("confirm-edit4")) {
        texts.set(1, "Enter that order reference in Destination then click Submit.");
        texts.set(2, "Check Confirmation is visible.");
        texts.set(3, "Check Confirmation is visible at checkpoint 4.");
        texts.set(4, "Check Confirmation is visible at checkpoint 5.");
      }
      var authored = new java.util.ArrayList<AuthoringStep>();
      for (int index = 0; index < 10; index++) authored.add(new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(index)), texts.get(index), null));
      var authoring = new AuthoringV2Request(2, null, authored);
      UUID test = UUID.randomUUID();
      jdbc.update("INSERT INTO ui_tests(id,name,csi_id,environment,browser,steps,variables,authoring,authoring_revision_inputs,created_by,created_at,updated_at)"
          + " VALUES (?,'Incremental ten',?,'fixture','chromium',?::jsonb,'[]'::jsonb,?::jsonb,?::jsonb,'fixture@example.invalid',now(),now())",
          test, primaryCsi, JSON.writeValueAsString(texts), JSON.writeValueAsString(authoring),
          JSON.writeValueAsString(AuthoringRevisionIdentity.create(authoring, Map.of())));
      assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test, null, 200, null).path("readiness").asText()).isEqualTo("UNLEARNED");
      String original = startLearning(test);
      awaitFinalizing(original);
      JsonNode base = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + original + "/finalize",
          C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("expectedSourceRevision", "1").put("expectedConfigurationRevision", "1"), 200, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      if (variant.equals("unused-declaration")) jdbc.update("INSERT INTO studio_schedules(id,test_id,csi_id,cron_expression,time_zone,execution_principal,created_by,updated_by)"
          + " VALUES (?,?,?,'0 * * * * *','UTC','user-1','fixture@example.invalid','fixture@example.invalid')", UUID.randomUUID(), test, primaryCsi);
      JsonNode draft = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test, null, 200, null);
      assertThat(draft.path("readiness").asText()).isEqualTo("READY");
      ObjectNode edit = C.object();
      for (String field : List.of("authoringSchemaVersion", "globalContext", "steps", "name", "description", "csiId", "environment", "variables", "configuration", "sourceRevision", "configurationRevision")) edit.set(field, draft.path(field).deepCopy());
      var currentSteps = (com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ArrayNode) edit.path("steps");
      int boundary;
      boolean explicitReady = variant.equals("ready6") || variant.startsWith("confirm-") && !variant.startsWith("confirm-edit4") || variant.equals("policy-prefix");
      switch (variant) {
        case "insert4" -> {
          ObjectNode inserted = C.object().put("id", UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes("obs015-inserted-four".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).toString())
              .put("text", "Check Destination is visible.");
          inserted.set("context", currentSteps.get(3).path("context").deepCopy());
          currentSteps.insert(3, inserted);
          boundary = 3;
        }
        case "delete4" -> { currentSteps.remove(3); boundary = 3; }
        case "reorder4" -> {
          JsonNode fourth = currentSteps.get(3).deepCopy(), fifth = currentSteps.get(4).deepCopy();
          currentSteps.set(3, fifth); currentSteps.set(4, fourth); boundary = 3;
        }
        case "deleted-consumers" -> {
          ((ObjectNode) currentSteps.get(1)).put("text", "Enter literal-control in Destination.");
          ((ObjectNode) currentSteps.get(2)).put("text", "Check Destination is visible."); boundary = 1;
        }
        case "append-input" -> {
          ((ObjectNode) currentSteps.get(3)).put("text", "Check Destination is visible. Check page title is Operations. The note unused-input is context only."); boundary = 3;
        }
        case "config" -> {
          ((ObjectNode) edit.path("configuration")).put("timeoutSeconds", 31); boundary = 0;
        }
        case "unused-declaration" -> {
          ((com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ArrayNode) edit.path("variables")).addObject().put("name", "UNUSED_DECLARATION").put("kind", "plain").put("value", "unused-change"); boundary = -2;
        }
        case "empty" -> { currentSteps.removeAll(); boundary = -1; }
        case "tail1", "tail7" -> {
          int retained = variant.equals("tail1") ? 1 : 7;
          while (currentSteps.size() > retained) currentSteps.remove(currentSteps.size() - 1);
          boundary = retained - 1;
        }
        case "append11" -> {
          ObjectNode appended = currentSteps.get(9).deepCopy();
          appended.put("id", UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes("obs015-appended-eleven".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).toString())
              .put("text", "Check Confirmation is visible at checkpoint 11.");
          currentSteps.add(appended); boundary = 10;
        }
        case "ready6", "confirm-prefix", "policy-prefix", "confirm-long-wait", "confirm-cancel", "confirm-expire", "confirm-source-change", "confirm-recover", "confirm-owner-loss", "confirm-race" -> boundary = 5;
        default -> {
          ((ObjectNode) currentSteps.get(3)).put("text", texts.get(3) + " Verify it remains visible."); boundary = 3;
        }
      }
      JsonNode edited = draft;
      if (!explicitReady) {
        var put = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("https://localhost:" + port + "/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test))
            .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE).header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString()).PUT(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(edit.toString())).build();
        var editedResponse = HttpClient.newBuilder().sslContext(TLS).build().send(put, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
        assertThat(editedResponse.statusCode()).as(editedResponse.body()).isEqualTo(200);
        edited = C.parse(editedResponse.body());
        if (boundary < 0) assertThat(edited.path("firstChangedStepId").isNull()).isTrue();
        else assertThat(edited.path("firstChangedStepId").asText()).isEqualTo(currentSteps.get(boundary).path("id").asText());
        if (!variant.equals("unused-declaration")) assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test + "/execute", C.object(), 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString())
            .path("error").asText()).isEqualTo("LEARN_REQUIRED");
      }
      if (variant.startsWith("confirm-") || variant.equals("policy-prefix")) {
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.ObservedPublicationFixture.ensurePolicy(jdbc, primaryCsi,
            POLICY.path("applicationId").asText(), POLICY.path("environment").asText(),
            java.util.stream.StreamSupport.stream(POLICY.path("allowedOrigins").spliterator(), false).map(JsonNode::asText).toList(),
            POLICY.path("applicationPolicy"), variant.equals("policy-prefix") ? "DENY" : null);
      }
      int beforeCalls = MODEL_WIRE.size(), beforeLegacy = LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get();
      int beforeNavigations = NAVIGATIONS.get(), beforeEffects = RECEIPTS.size();
      if (variant.equals("unused-declaration")) {
        assertThat(edited.path("readiness").asText()).isEqualTo("READY");
        assertThat(edited.path("firstChangedStepId").isNull()).isTrue();
        assertThat(edited.path("sourceRevision")).isEqualTo(draft.path("sourceRevision"));
        assertThat(edited.path("configurationRevision").asLong()).isEqualTo(draft.path("configurationRevision").asLong() + 1);
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT state FROM studio_schedules WHERE test_id=?", String.class, test)).isEqualTo("DISABLED");
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT requested_enabled FROM studio_schedules WHERE test_id=?", Boolean.class, test)).isFalse();
        UUID execution = UUID.fromString(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test + "/execute", C.object(), 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString()).path("executionId").asText());
        JsonNode binding = observedBinding(execution);
        assertThat(binding.path("revisionId").asText()).isEqualTo(base.path("revisionId").asText());
        var originalRevision = jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT source_revision,configuration_revision,revision_digest FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?::uuid", base.path("revisionId").asText());
        JsonNode acceptedConfiguration = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT configuration_snapshot::text FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?", String.class, execution));
        assertThat(acceptedConfiguration.path("sourceRevision").asLong()).isEqualTo(((Number) originalRevision.get("source_revision")).longValue());
        assertThat(acceptedConfiguration.path("configurationRevision").asLong()).isEqualTo(((Number) originalRevision.get("configuration_revision")).longValue());
        assertThat(binding.path("revisionDigest").asText()).isEqualTo(originalRevision.get("revision_digest"));
        assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
        long end = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos(); JsonNode replay;
        do { replay = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/executions/" + execution, null, 200, null); if (replay.path("streamComplete").asBoolean()) break; Thread.sleep(30); } while (System.nanoTime() < end);
        assertThat(replay.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("PASSED");
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(beforeCalls); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
        assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeNavigations + 1); assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(beforeEffects + 1);
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT state FROM studio_schedules WHERE test_id=?", String.class, test)).isEqualTo("DISABLED");
        return;
      }
      ObjectNode start = C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("testId", test.toString())
          .put("sourceRevision", edited.path("sourceRevision").asText()).put("configurationRevision", edited.path("configurationRevision").asText())
          .put("environmentId", "fixture").put("applicationId", "orders").put("baseRevisionId", base.path("revisionId").asText());
      start.set("versions", versions());
      if (explicitReady) start.put("firstLearnStepId", currentSteps.get(boundary).path("id").asText());
      if (boundary < 0) {
        String before = incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test);
        JsonNode refused = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 422, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
        assertThat(refused.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("INVALID_CONTRACT");
        assertThat(incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test)).isEqualTo(before);
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(beforeCalls);
        assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
        assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeNavigations);
        assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(beforeEffects);
        return;
      }
      assertIncrementalStartRefusalsLeaveNoWrites(start, currentSteps, boundary, test);
      if (b1TrustedCeiling) b1AssertTrustedCeilingStart(start, test);
      if (variant.equals("delete4")) {
        String beforeDeleted = b1ScopedSnapshot(test);
        int modelDeleted = MODEL_WIRE.size(), legacyDeleted = LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get(), navDeleted = NAVIGATIONS.get(), effectsDeleted = RECEIPTS.size();
        JsonNode deleted = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start.deepCopy().put("firstLearnStepId", INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(3)), 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
        assertThat(deleted.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("REVISION_CONFLICT");
        assertThat(deleted.path("messageCode").asText()).isEqualTo("FIRST_LEARN_STEP_UNKNOWN");
        assertThat(b1ScopedSnapshot(test)).isEqualTo(beforeDeleted);
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(modelDeleted); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacyDeleted);
        assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(navDeleted); assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effectsDeleted);
      }
      if (variant.equals("equal4")) start.put("firstLearnStepId", INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(3));
      if (variant.equals("before4at2")) {
        // The server still reports changed step four. An author may explicitly choose more work.
        assertThat(edited.path("firstChangedStepId").asText()).isEqualTo(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(3));
        start.put("firstLearnStepId", INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(1));
        boundary = 1;
      }
      incrementalDomVariant = variant;
      Files.writeString(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-mode.json"), C.object().put("mode", variant)
          .put("stepId", INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(variant.startsWith("confirm-") && !variant.startsWith("confirm-edit4") ? 4 : 1))
          .put("ordinal", variant.equals("same-pair-literal") || variant.startsWith("confirm-edit4") ? 1 : 0).toString());
      Files.writeString(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-events.jsonl"), "");
      String mixed = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString()).path("session").path("sessionId").asText();
      if (variant.equals("append-input")) assertPendingContextGuardsThenRelease(mixed);
      if (variant.startsWith("drift-auth")) assertActualDriftCallbackAuthorityThenRelease(mixed);
      JsonNode confirmedRequest = variant.startsWith("confirm-") ? confirmIncrementalPrefix(mixed, variant, test, beforeCalls, beforeLegacy, beforeEffects) : null;
      if (List.of("confirm-cancel", "confirm-expire", "confirm-source-change", "confirm-owner-loss", "confirm-race").contains(variant)) return;
      if (variant.equals("drift-plan-fail")) {
        awaitState(mixed, "FAILED");
        JsonNode result = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + mixed + "/result", null, 200, null);
        assertThat(result.path("relearnedFrom").path("stepId").asText()).isEqualTo(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(1));
        assertThat(result.path("relearnedFrom").path("driftSwitchStepId").asText()).isEqualTo(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(1));
        assertThat(result.path("learnedFromThisRun").asBoolean()).isFalse(); assertThat(result.path("assertionsPassed").asBoolean()).isFalse();
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts m JOIN studio_guided_relearn_switches s USING(session_id) WHERE m.session_id=? AND m.created_at<s.created_at", Integer.class, mixed)).isZero();
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isGreaterThan(beforeCalls); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
        assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(beforeEffects);
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE test_id=?", Integer.class, test)).isEqualTo(1);
        return;
      }
      if (List.of("assert-fail", "policy-prefix", "unknown2").contains(variant)) {
        assertIncrementalTerminalRefusal(mixed, variant, test, base, beforeCalls, beforeLegacy, beforeEffects);
        return;
      }
      awaitFinalizing(mixed);
      boolean drifted = List.of("drift2", "admitted-drift2", "admitted-literal2", "pre-literal2", "same-pair-literal", "drift-first", "drift-auth", "drift-auth-pre").contains(variant);
      if (drifted) {
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT initial_step_index FROM studio_guided_relearn_plans WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, mixed)).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT reason FROM studio_guided_relearn_switches WHERE session_id=?", String.class, mixed)).isEqualTo("DRIFT");
        int effective = variant.equals("drift-first") ? 0 : 1;
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT effective_step_index FROM studio_guided_relearn_switches WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, mixed)).isEqualTo(effective);
        boundary = effective;
      }
      assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).as("one actual mixed-session navigation").isEqualTo(beforeNavigations + 1);
      int expectedSubmit = variant.equals("tail1") ? 0 : 1;
      assertThat(RECEIPTS.size()).isEqualTo(beforeEffects + expectedSubmit);
      if (expectedSubmit == 1) {
        boolean literal = List.of("deleted-consumers", "admitted-literal2", "pre-literal2", "same-pair-literal").contains(variant);
        assertThat(RECEIPTS.getLast()).as("executed current binding or authored replacement bytes").isEqualTo(literal ? "literal-control" : VALUES.getLast());
        assertThat(RECEIPTS.getLast()).isNotEqualTo(RECEIPTS.get(beforeEffects - 1));
      }
      var requests = INCREMENTAL_MODEL_CONTEXTS.stream().filter(request -> request.path("context").path("sessionId").asText().equals(mixed)).toList();
      assertThat(requests).isNotEmpty();
      for (JsonNode request : requests) {
        JsonNode context = request.path("context");
        if (request.path("kind").asText().equals("SOURCE_PLAN")) {
          assertThat(context.path("learningWindow").path("firstLearnStepId").asText()).isEqualTo(currentSteps.get(boundary).path("id").asText());
          assertThat(context.path("learningWindow").path("learnableStepIds")).hasSize(currentSteps.size() - boundary);
        } else {
          var suffixIds = new java.util.HashSet<String>();
          for (int index = boundary; index < currentSteps.size(); index++) suffixIds.add(currentSteps.get(index).path("id").asText());
          assertThat(suffixIds).contains(context.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText());
        }
        assertThat(context.has("authoringSnapshot")).isFalse();
        assertThat(context.has("snapshotEvidence")).isFalse();
      }
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isGreaterThan(beforeCalls);
      assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts m JOIN studio_guided_relearn_switches s USING(session_id) WHERE m.session_id=? AND m.created_at<s.created_at", Integer.class, mixed)).isZero();
      var actions = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.effectiveActions(mixed));
      if (List.of("admitted-drift2", "admitted-literal2", "same-pair-literal", "drift-auth").contains(variant)) assertIncrementalSpentAdmissionHasOneFreshNativeReplacement(mixed);
      assertThat(actions.stream().map(action -> action.path("executionEvidence").path("contextId").asText()).distinct().toList()).hasSize(1);
      assertThat(actions.stream().allMatch(action -> action.path("dispatchCount").asInt() == 1)).isTrue();
      if (variant.equals("append-input") || List.of("deleted-consumers", "admitted-literal2", "pre-literal2", "same-pair-literal").contains(variant)) assertIncrementalAppendAckHistory(mixed);
      assertIncrementalImmutableSourceAndPlanHistory(mixed, base.path("revisionId").asText());
      int callsAtFinalize = MODEL_WIRE.size(), effectsAtFinalize = RECEIPTS.size(), navigationsAtFinalize = NAVIGATIONS.get();
      JsonNode merged = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + mixed + "/finalize",
          C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("expectedSourceRevision", edited.path("sourceRevision").asText())
              .put("expectedConfigurationRevision", edited.path("configurationRevision").asText()), 200, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(callsAtFinalize);
      assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effectsAtFinalize);
      assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(navigationsAtFinalize);
      JsonNode artifact = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?", String.class, UUID.fromString(merged.path("revisionId").asText())));
      assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("sourceSteps")).hasSize(currentSteps.size());
      assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes")).hasSize(currentSteps.size());
      assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom")).hasSize(boundary);
      JsonNode baseArtifact = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?", String.class, UUID.fromString(base.path("revisionId").asText())));
      for (int index = 0; index < currentSteps.size(); index++) {
        String stepId = currentSteps.get(index).path("id").asText();
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("sourceSteps").get(index).path("stepId").asText()).isEqualTo(stepId);
        if (index < boundary) {
          assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom").path(stepId).path("revisionId").asText()).isEqualTo(base.path("revisionId").asText());
          assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(stepId))
              .isEqualTo(baseArtifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(stepId));
        } else {
          assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom").has(stepId)).isFalse();
          if (variant.equals("before4at2") && index < 3)
            assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(stepId))
                .isEqualTo(baseArtifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(stepId));
          else if (!explicitReady && !drifted && baseArtifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").has(stepId))
            assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(stepId))
                .isNotEqualTo(baseArtifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(stepId));
        }
      }
      if (variant.equals("append-input")) {
        assertThat(artifact.path("inputRecipes")).hasSize(2);
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_materialization_inputs WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, mixed)).isEqualTo(3);
        JsonNode raw = sessions.transaction(() -> ownedApplicationContext.getBean(GuidedPublicationEvidenceRepository.class).read(mixed).document());
        assertThat(raw.path("materializationEvidence").path("entries")).hasSize(3);
        for (JsonNode entry : raw.path("materializationEvidence").path("entries")) assertThat(entry.path("metadata").has("value")).isFalse();
      }
      if (List.of("deleted-consumers", "admitted-literal2", "pre-literal2", "same-pair-literal").contains(variant)) {
        JsonNode binding = artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("bindings").get(0);
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("bindings")).hasSize(1);
        if (variant.equals("same-pair-literal")) assertThat(binding.path("consumers")).hasSize(1);
        else assertThat(binding.path("consumers")).isEmpty();
        assertThat(binding.path("producer").path("stepId").asText()).isEqualTo(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(0));
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("incremental").path("retainedPrefixAdmissionIds"))
            .contains(binding.path("provenance").path("producerAdmissionId"));
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_producers WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, mixed)).isEqualTo(1);
        if (variant.equals("admitted-literal2") || variant.equals("same-pair-literal")) {
          assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_consumer_edges WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, mixed)).isEqualTo(variant.equals("same-pair-literal") ? 2 : 1);
          assertThat(C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT document::text FROM studio_guided_allocations WHERE session_id=?", String.class, mixed)).path("consumers")).hasSize(2);
        }
      }
      if (variant.equals("drift-first")) {
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom")).isEmpty();
        assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_allocations WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, mixed)).isEqualTo(2);
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("bindings")).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("incremental").path("retainedPrefixAdmissionIds")).hasSize(1);
        JsonNode raw = sessions.transaction(() -> ownedApplicationContext.getBean(GuidedPublicationEvidenceRepository.class).read(mixed).document());
        assertThat(raw.path("snapshotEvidence").path("baseProof").isObject()).isTrue();
        assertThat(raw.path("incrementalEvidence").path("program").path("sourceStepIds")).hasSize(3);
      }
      if (confirmedRequest != null) {
        JsonNode admission = actions.stream().filter(action -> action.path("coordinate").equals(confirmedRequest.path("coordinate"))).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(admission.path("evidenceHash")).isNotEqualTo(confirmedRequest.path("evidenceHash"));
        assertThat(admission.path("dispatchCount").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
      }
      assertIncrementalRecordingSpansBothHalves(mixed, actions, boundary);
      int readsBeforeOrdinary = INCREMENTAL_ORDINARY_READS.get();
      JsonNode accepted = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test + "/execute", C.object(), 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      UUID execution = UUID.fromString(accepted.path("executionId").asText());
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
      long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
      JsonNode snapshot;
      do {
        snapshot = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/executions/" + execution, null, 200, null);
        if (snapshot.path("streamComplete").asBoolean()) break;
        Thread.sleep(30);
      } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
      assertThat(snapshot.path("status").asText()).as("joined incremental ordinary replay logs %s", TEMP).isEqualTo("PASSED");
      assertThat(snapshot.path("video").path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("AVAILABLE");
      assertThat(INCREMENTAL_ORDINARY_READS.get() - readsBeforeOrdinary).as("actual ordinary child recaptures each executable read").isEqualTo(variant.equals("tail1") ? 0 : 1);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(callsAtFinalize);
      assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
      if (expectedSubmit == 1) {
        boolean literal = List.of("deleted-consumers", "admitted-literal2", "pre-literal2", "same-pair-literal").contains(variant);
        assertThat(RECEIPTS.getLast()).isEqualTo(literal ? "literal-control" : VALUES.getLast());
        if (!literal) assertThat(RECEIPTS.getLast()).isNotEqualTo(RECEIPTS.get(RECEIPTS.size() - 2));
        if (literal) {
          int reads = INCREMENTAL_ORDINARY_READS.get();
          String previousPageValue = VALUES.getLast();
          UUID second = UUID.fromString(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test + "/execute", C.object(), 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString()).path("executionId").asText());
          assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
          JsonNode replay;
          long secondDeadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
          do {
            replay = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/executions/" + second, null, 200, null);
            if (replay.path("streamComplete").asBoolean()) break;
            Thread.sleep(30);
          } while (System.nanoTime() < secondDeadline);
          assertThat(replay.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("PASSED");
          assertThat(replay.path("video").path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("AVAILABLE");
          assertThat(INCREMENTAL_ORDINARY_READS.get() - reads).isEqualTo(1);
          assertThat(VALUES.getLast()).isNotEqualTo(previousPageValue);
          assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(callsAtFinalize); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
          assertThat(RECEIPTS.getLast()).isEqualTo("literal-control");
          assertThat(observedBinding(second).path("revisionId").asText()).isEqualTo(merged.path("revisionId").asText());
        }
      }
    } finally {
      INCREMENTAL_ACK_RELEASE.values().forEach(java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch::countDown);
      INCREMENTAL_ACK_RELEASE.clear(); INCREMENTAL_ACK_ENTERED.clear(); INCREMENTAL_DRIFT_CAPTURE.clear();
      incrementalTenScenario = false;
      incrementalDomVariant = null;
      Files.writeString(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-mode.json"), "{}");
      seedGovernedPolicies(primaryCsi);
    }
  }

  private void assertIncrementalTerminalRefusal(String id, String variant, UUID test, JsonNode base,
      int model, int legacy, int effects) throws Exception {
    String expected = variant.equals("unknown2") ? "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME" : "FAILED";
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
    String state;
    do {
      state = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("state").asText());
      if (List.of("FAILED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED", "FINALIZING").contains(state)) break;
      Thread.sleep(25);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    assertThat(state).as("prefix refusal retains factual terminal outcome").isEqualTo(expected);
    assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(model);
    assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacy);
    assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_no_invocation_receipts WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_relearn_switches WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE test_id=?", Integer.class, test)).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT confirmed_revision_id::text FROM ui_tests WHERE id=?", String.class, test)).isEqualTo(base.path("revisionId").asText());
    JsonNode result = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/result", null, 200, null);
    assertThat(result.path("relearnedFrom").isNull()).as("no learning boundary was entered").isTrue();
    var ledger = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.actions(id));
    if (variant.equals("assert-fail")) {
      assertThat(ledger.stream().filter(action -> action.path("outcomeCode").asText().equals("ASSERTION_FAILED")
          && action.path("assertionPassed").isBoolean() && !action.path("assertionPassed").asBoolean()).toList()).hasSize(1);
    } else if (variant.equals("unknown2")) {
      var unknown = ledger.stream().filter(action -> action.path("state").asText().equals("UNKNOWN_OUTCOME")).toList();
      assertThat(unknown).hasSize(1);
      assertThat(unknown.getFirst().path("attemptOrdinal").asInt()).isZero();
      var events = incrementalNativeEvents(id, unknown.getFirst().path("admission").path("admissionId").asText());
      assertThat(events.stream().filter(event -> event.path("phase").asText().equals("NATIVE_COMPLETED")).toList()).hasSize(1);
      assertThat(ledger.stream().anyMatch(action -> action.path("attemptOrdinal").asInt() == 1)).isFalse();
    } else {
      assertThat(result.toString()).contains("POLICY_DENIED");
      assertThat(ledger.stream().filter(action -> action.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText().equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(4))).toList()).isEmpty();
    }
  }

  private List<JsonNode> incrementalNativeEvents(String session, String admission) throws Exception {
    var events = new java.util.ArrayList<JsonNode>();
    for (String line : Files.readAllLines(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-events.jsonl"))) {
      if (!line.isBlank()) {
        JsonNode event = C.parse(line);
        if (event.path("sessionId").asText().equals(session) && event.path("admissionId").asText().equals(admission)) events.add(event);
      }
    }
    return events;
  }

  private void assertIncrementalSpentAdmissionHasOneFreshNativeReplacement(String id) throws Exception {
    JsonNode receipt = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT document::text FROM studio_guided_no_invocation_receipts WHERE session_id=?", String.class, id));
    assertThat(receipt.path("phase").asText()).isEqualTo("ADMITTED_PRE_INVOCATION");
    assertThat(receipt.path("nativeInvocationStarted").asBoolean(true)).isFalse();
    var same = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.actions(id)).stream()
        .filter(action -> action.path("coordinate").equals(receipt.path("coordinate"))).toList();
    assertThat(same).hasSize(2);
    JsonNode spent = same.stream().filter(action -> action.path("attemptOrdinal").asInt() == 0).findFirst().orElseThrow();
    JsonNode replacement = same.stream().filter(action -> action.path("attemptOrdinal").asInt() == 1).findFirst().orElseThrow();
    assertThat(spent.path("state").asText()).isEqualTo("FAILED");
    assertThat(spent.path("outcomeCode").asText()).isEqualTo("TARGET_CHANGED");
    assertThat(spent.path("admission").path("admissionId")).isEqualTo(receipt.path("admissionId"));
    assertThat(replacement.path("state").asText()).isEqualTo("SUCCEEDED");
    assertThat(spent.path("admission").path("admissionId")).isNotEqualTo(replacement.path("admission").path("admissionId"));
    assertThat(spent.path("dispatchCount").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(replacement.path("dispatchCount").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
    var firstEvents = incrementalNativeEvents(id, spent.path("admission").path("admissionId").asText());
    assertThat(firstEvents.stream().filter(event -> event.path("phase").asText().equals("DISPATCH_ENTER")).toList()).hasSize(1);
    assertThat(firstEvents.stream().filter(event -> event.path("phase").asText().startsWith("NATIVE_")).toList()).isEmpty();
    var replacementEvents = incrementalNativeEvents(id, replacement.path("admission").path("admissionId").asText());
    assertThat(replacementEvents.stream().filter(event -> event.path("phase").asText().equals("NATIVE_COMPLETED")).toList()).hasSize(1);
  }

  /** Public refusal path plus byte snapshots of the actual PostgreSQL authority rows. */
  private void assertIncrementalStartRefusalsLeaveNoWrites(ObjectNode start, JsonNode steps, int boundary, UUID test) throws Exception {
    var attacks = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, ObjectNode>();
    attacks.put("BASE_REVISION_MISMATCH", start.deepCopy().put("baseRevisionId", UUID.randomUUID().toString()));
    attacks.put("FIRST_LEARN_STEP_UNKNOWN", start.deepCopy().put("firstLearnStepId", UUID.randomUUID().toString()));
    // READY may explicitly select any existing step; a changed draft may never skip its first change.
    if (!start.has("firstLearnStepId") && boundary + 1 < steps.size())
      attacks.put("FIRST_LEARN_STEP_NARROWED", start.deepCopy().put("firstLearnStepId", steps.get(boundary + 1).path("id").asText()));
    if (boundary > 0) {
      JsonNode previous = new com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.policy.ObservationPolicyRepository(jdbc, C)
          .active(primaryCsi, "orders", "fixture").orElseThrow().document();
      ObjectNode withoutPrefixRead = POLICY.path("applicationPolicy").deepCopy();
      withoutPrefixRead.putArray("readGrants");
      try {
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.ObservedPublicationFixture.ensurePolicy(jdbc, primaryCsi,
            "orders", "fixture", List.of(SECRET_ORIGIN), withoutPrefixRead, previous.path("sideEffectStance").asText());
        String before = incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test);
        int model = MODEL_WIRE.size(), legacy = LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get(), navigation = NAVIGATIONS.get(), effects = RECEIPTS.size();
        JsonNode refused = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 403, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
        assertThat(refused.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED");
        assertThat(refused.path("messageCode").asText()).isEqualTo("PREFIX_ORIGIN_NOT_GOVERNED");
        assertThat(incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test)).isEqualTo(before);
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(model); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacy);
        assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(navigation); assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
      } finally {
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.ObservedPublicationFixture.ensurePolicy(jdbc, primaryCsi,
            "orders", "fixture", java.util.stream.StreamSupport.stream(previous.path("allowedOrigins").spliterator(), false)
                .map(JsonNode::asText).toList(), previous.path("applicationPolicy"), previous.path("sideEffectStance").asText());
      }
    }
    for (var attack : attacks.entrySet()) {
      String before = incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test);
      int model = MODEL_WIRE.size(), legacy = LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get(), navigation = NAVIGATIONS.get(), effects = RECEIPTS.size();
      JsonNode refused = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", attack.getValue(), 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      assertThat(refused.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("REVISION_CONFLICT");
      assertThat(refused.path("messageCode").asText()).isEqualTo(attack.getKey());
      assertThat(incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(test)).as("refusal preserves all source/session/base/budget/revision bytes").isEqualTo(before);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(model);
      assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacy);
      assertThat(NAVIGATIONS.get()).isEqualTo(navigation);
      assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
    }
  }

  private String incrementalAuthoritySnapshot(UUID test) {
    ObjectNode snapshot = C.object();
    for (String table : List.of("studio_guided_sessions", "studio_guided_publication_bases", "studio_guided_source_manifests",
        "studio_guided_translation_state", "studio_guided_relearn_plans", "studio_guided_model_attempts", "studio_guided_session_policies")) {
      snapshot.set(table, C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(t) ORDER BY to_jsonb(t)::text),'[]'::jsonb)::text FROM " + table + " t", String.class)));
    }
    snapshot.set("test", C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT to_jsonb(t)::text FROM ui_tests t WHERE id=?", String.class, test)));
    snapshot.set("revisions", C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(r) ORDER BY id),'[]'::jsonb)::text FROM ui_test_revisions r WHERE test_id=?", String.class, test)));
    snapshot.set("dailyBudget", C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(t) ORDER BY budget_day),'[]'::jsonb)::text FROM studio_guided_csi_token_days t WHERE csi_id=?", String.class, primaryCsi)));
    return GuidedContracts.canonical(snapshot);
  }

  private JsonNode confirmIncrementalPrefix(String id, String variant, UUID test, int model, int legacy, int effects) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      String state = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("state").asText());
      if (state.equals("AWAITING_CLARIFICATION")) break;
      assertThat(List.of("FAILED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME", "EXPIRED", "CANCELLED", "FINALIZING")).doesNotContain(state);
      Thread.sleep(25);
    }
    JsonNode waiting = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id, null, 200, null);
    assertThat(waiting.path("session").path("state").asText()).isEqualTo("AWAITING_CLARIFICATION");
    assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(model);
    assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacy);
    assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_relearn_switches WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    JsonNode question = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarification", null, 200, null);
    assertThat(question.path("reason").asText()).isEqualTo("SIDE_EFFECT_CONFIRMATION");
    assertThat(question.path("promptCode").asText()).isEqualTo("CONFIRM_ACTION");
    assertThat(question.path("choices")).hasSize(1);
    assertThat(question.path("choices").get(0).path("labelSource").asText()).isEqualTo("AUTHORED");
    assertThat(question.toString()).doesNotContain("prefixRequestDigest", "authoringSnapshot", "authoredDeclarations");
    JsonNode progress = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).progress().deepCopy());
    JsonNode request = C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT document::text FROM studio_guided_prefix_requests WHERE session_id=? AND request_digest=?",
        String.class, id, progress.path("sideEffectConfirmation").path("prefixRequestDigest").asText()));
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND step_id=? AND ordinal=?",
        Integer.class, id, request.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText(), request.path("coordinate").path("ordinal").asInt())).isZero();
    ObjectNode answer = C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("clarificationId", question.path("clarificationId").asText())
        .put("generation", question.path("generation").asInt()).put("choiceId", question.path("choices").get(0).path("choiceId").asText());
    // Another session/base confirmation cannot authorise this current question.
    JsonNode wrong = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify",
        answer.deepCopy().put("clarificationId", UUID.randomUUID().toString()), 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    assertThat(wrong.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("STALE_GENERATION");
    assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
    for (ObjectNode bad : List.of(answer.deepCopy().put("generation", question.path("generation").asInt() + 1),
        answer.deepCopy().put("choiceId", UUID.randomUUID().toString()))) {
      String before = incrementalPendingSnapshot(id);
      publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", bad,
          bad.path("generation").equals(answer.path("generation")) ? 422 : 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(before);
    }
    String otherCookie = p3CoreCookie("other-current-author", "other-current-author@example.invalid");
    try {
      String before = incrementalPendingSnapshot(id);
      p3Call(otherCookie, "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", answer, 403);
      assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(before);
    } finally { P3_CORE.remove("put_session=" + otherCookie); }
    JsonNode current = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().deepCopy());
    JsonNode target = null;
    for (JsonNode candidate : progress.path("observation").path("nodes"))
      if (candidate.path("name").asText().equals("Submit")) target = candidate;
    assertThat(target).isNotNull();
    ObjectNode direct = C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("sessionId", id).put("scopeHash", current.path("scopeHash").asText())
        .put("generation", progress.path("generation").asInt()).put("evidenceHash", progress.path("evidenceHash").asText());
    direct.set("coordinate", request.path("coordinate")); direct.set("executionEvidence", progress.path("executionEvidence"));
    direct.put("contextRevision", request.path("contextRevision").asInt()).put("authoredInputsHash", request.path("authoredInputsHash").asText());
    direct.putObject("target").put("nodeId", target.path("nodeId").asText())
        .put("documentId", progress.path("documentId").asText()).put("generation", progress.path("generation").asInt())
        .putObject("locator").put("kind", "role").put("role", target.path("role").asText()).put("name", "Submit").put("exact", true);
    direct.putObject("operation").put("kind", "click");
    String beforeDirect = incrementalPendingSnapshot(id);
    JsonNode refusedDirect = incrementalControllerProbe(id, current.path("owner").path("fence").asLong(), direct, 409, "admissions");
    assertThat(refusedDirect.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("STALE_GENERATION");
    assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarification", null, 200, null)).isEqualTo(question);
    assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(beforeDirect);
    ObjectNode command = C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("sessionId", id)
        .put("expectedSourceRevision", current.path("scope").path("sourceRevision").asText())
        .put("expectedConfigurationRevision", current.path("scope").path("configurationRevision").asText())
        .put("expectedPlanRevision", plans.state(id).path("planRevision").asLong());
    String pending = incrementalPendingSnapshot(id);
    incrementalControllerProbe(id, current.path("owner").path("fence").asLong() + 1, command, 409);
    assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(pending);
    if (variant.equals("confirm-recover")) {
      var replacement = new com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.translation.GuidedTranslationCoordinator(
          plans, sessions, C, URI.create("https://localhost:" + port), TEMP.resolve("coordinator.jwt"));
      replacement.continueRuns(); replacement.continueRuns();
      assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarification", null, 200, null)).isEqualTo(question);
      assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(pending);
    }
    if (variant.equals("confirm-long-wait")) {
      JsonNode originalPreparation = incrementalPreparationEvent(id, request.path("evidenceHash").asText());
      Instant originalDeadline = Instant.parse(originalPreparation.path("expiresAt").asText());
      while (!Instant.now().isAfter(originalDeadline.plusMillis(20))) Thread.sleep(100);
      JsonNode renewed = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("owner").deepCopy());
      assertThat(renewed.path("workerId")).isEqualTo(current.path("owner").path("workerId"));
      assertThat(renewed.path("fence")).isEqualTo(current.path("owner").path("fence"));
      assertThat(Instant.parse(renewed.path("leaseExpiresAt").asText())).isAfter(originalDeadline);
      assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(pending);
    }
    if (variant.equals("confirm-source-change")) {
      JsonNode draft = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test, null, 200, null);
      ObjectNode changed = C.object();
      for (String field : List.of("authoringSchemaVersion", "globalContext", "steps", "name", "description", "csiId", "environment", "variables", "configuration", "sourceRevision", "configurationRevision")) changed.set(field, draft.path(field).deepCopy());
      ((ObjectNode) changed.path("steps").get(9)).put("text", "Check Confirmation still visible after a source edit.");
      var put = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("https://localhost:" + port + "/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test))
          .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE).header("Content-Type", "application/json")
          .PUT(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(changed.toString())).build();
      assertThat(HttpClient.newBuilder().sslContext(TLS).build().send(put, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()).statusCode()).isEqualTo(200);
      publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", answer, 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    }
    if (variant.equals("confirm-owner-loss")) {
      var controller = com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedInternalIdentityFixture.controller();
      ObjectNode context = ownedApplicationContext.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService.class)
          .runnerContext(controller, id, current.path("owner").path("fence").asLong());
      // Fault the actual current owner through its authenticated stop path. The real runtime
      // invalidates its grant, closes its original browser and sends its owning-worker lifecycle.
      ownedApplicationContext.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.bridge.GuidedDispatchService.class).stop(context, "LEASE_LOST");
      awaitState(id, "FAILED");
      publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", answer, 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    }
    if (variant.equals("confirm-expire")) {
      // Actual original lifetime elapses. No SQL timestamp rewrite, clock substitution or new lease.
      Instant expires = Instant.parse(question.path("expiresAt").asText());
      long renewCredentialsAt = System.nanoTime();
      while (!Instant.now().isAfter(expires.plusMillis(20))) {
        if (System.nanoTime() >= renewCredentialsAt) {
          refreshFixtureCredentials(); renewCredentialsAt = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos();
        }
        Thread.sleep(250);
      }
      ownedApplicationContext.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService.class).expire();
      publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", answer, 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    } else if (variant.equals("confirm-cancel") || variant.equals("confirm-source-change")) {
      publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/cancel", C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("reason", "userRequested"), 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", answer, 409, UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    }
    if (List.of("confirm-cancel", "confirm-expire", "confirm-source-change", "confirm-owner-loss").contains(variant)) {
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(model); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacy); assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND step_id=? AND ordinal=?",
          Integer.class, id, request.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText(), request.path("coordinate").path("ordinal").asInt())).isZero();
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_relearn_switches WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
      assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/result", null, 200, null).path("relearnedFrom").isNull()).isTrue();
      assertThat(Files.readAllLines(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-events.jsonl")).stream().filter(line -> !line.isBlank()).map(C::parse)
          .filter(event -> event.path("sessionId").asText().equals(id) && event.path("phase").asText().equals("NATIVE_ENTER")
              && event.path("stepId").equals(request.path("coordinate").path("stepId")) && event.path("ordinal").equals(request.path("coordinate").path("ordinal"))).toList()).isEmpty();
      return request;
    }
    assertThat(Files.readAllLines(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-events.jsonl")).stream().map(C::parse)
        .filter(event -> event.path("sessionId").asText().equals(id) && event.path("phase").asText().equals("NATIVE_ENTER")
              && event.path("stepId").equals(request.path("coordinate").path("stepId")) && event.path("ordinal").equals(request.path("coordinate").path("ordinal"))).toList())
        .as("protected action stays native-zero during the human wait; earlier navigation was lawful").isEmpty();
    if (variant.equals("confirm-race")) {
      var startRace = new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
      try (var pool = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
        var yes = pool.submit(() -> { startRace.await(); return incrementalPublicRaceRequest(id, "clarify", answer); });
        var no = pool.submit(() -> { startRace.await(); return incrementalPublicRaceRequest(id, "cancel", C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("reason", "userRequested")); });
        startRace.countDown();
        assertThat(yes.get(35, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS).statusCode()).isIn(202, 409);
        assertThat(no.get(35, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS).statusCode()).isEqualTo(202);
      }
      long until = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(30).toNanos(); String outcome;
      do { outcome = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("state").asText());
        if (List.of("CANCELLED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME", "FAILED").contains(outcome)) break; Thread.sleep(20);
      } while (System.nanoTime() < until);
      assertThat(outcome).isIn("CANCELLED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME", "FAILED");
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND step_id=? AND ordinal=?", Integer.class,
          id, request.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText(), request.path("coordinate").path("ordinal").asInt())).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(Files.readAllLines(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-events.jsonl")).stream().filter(line -> !line.isBlank()).map(C::parse)
          .filter(event -> event.path("sessionId").asText().equals(id) && event.path("phase").asText().equals("NATIVE_ENTER")
              && event.path("stepId").equals(request.path("coordinate").path("stepId")) && event.path("ordinal").equals(request.path("coordinate").path("ordinal"))).count()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE test_id=?", Integer.class, test)).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/result", null, 200, null).path("learnedFromThisRun").asBoolean()).isFalse();
      return request;
    }
    String answerKey = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    JsonNode accepted = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", answer, 202, answerKey);
    assertThat(publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/clarify", answer, 202, answerKey)).isEqualTo(accepted);
    return request;
  }

  private void assertActualDriftCallbackAuthorityThenRelease(String id) throws Exception {
    long until = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
    while (!INCREMENTAL_DRIFT_CAPTURE.containsKey(id) && System.nanoTime() < until) Thread.sleep(10);
    assertThat(INCREMENTAL_DRIFT_CAPTURE).containsKey(id);
    ObjectNode actual = INCREMENTAL_DRIFT_CAPTURE.get(id); JsonNode receipt = actual.path("receipt");
    long fence = receipt.path("ownerFence").asLong();
    assertThat(receipt.path("nativeInvocationStarted").asBoolean(true)).isFalse();
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_no_invocation_receipts WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_relearn_switches WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    String before = incrementalPendingSnapshot(id), raw = incrementalImmutableSnapshot(id);
    String worker = token("owning-worker", "worker-1", "studio-guided-controller");
    try {
      assertThat(incrementalDriftProbe(id, fence, actual, token("owning-worker", "other-worker", "studio-guided-controller"), TLS).statusCode()).isEqualTo(403);
      assertThat(incrementalDriftProbe(id, fence + 1, actual, worker, TLS).statusCode()).isEqualTo(403);
      assertThat(incrementalDriftProbe(id, fence, actual, "invalid", TLS).statusCode()).isEqualTo(401);
      var trustStore = KeyStore.getInstance("PKCS12");
      try (var input = Files.newInputStream(TEMP.resolve("trust.p12"))) { trustStore.load(input, "fixture".toCharArray()); }
      var trusts = TrustManagerFactory.getInstance(TrustManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm()); trusts.init(trustStore);
      var noCertificate = SSLContext.getInstance("TLS"); noCertificate.init(null, trusts.getTrustManagers(), null);
      assertThat(incrementalDriftProbe(id, fence, actual, worker, noCertificate).statusCode()).isEqualTo(401);
      for (String field : List.of("scopeHash", "authoredInputsHash", "contextRevision", "requestDigest", "operationHash", "workerId", "prefixProgramHash", "evidenceHash", "sourceRevision")) {
        ObjectNode forged = actual.deepCopy(); ObjectNode changed = (ObjectNode) forged.path("receipt");
        if (field.equals("contextRevision")) changed.put(field, receipt.path(field).asInt() + 1);
        else if (field.equals("workerId")) changed.put(field, "foreign-worker");
        else if (field.equals("sourceRevision")) changed.put(field, "999");
        else changed.put(field, "0".repeat(64));
        changed.remove("receiptHash"); changed.put("receiptHash", GuidedContracts.hash(changed));
        var rejected = incrementalDriftProbe(id, fence, forged, worker, TLS);
        assertThat(rejected.statusCode()).as("authentic transport cannot authorize mismatched %s", field).isIn(403, 409, 422);
        assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(before);
        assertThat(incrementalImmutableSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(raw);
      }
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts WHERE session_id=?", Integer.class, id)).isZero();
    } finally { INCREMENTAL_ACK_RELEASE.get(id).countDown(); }
  }

  private HttpResponse<String> incrementalDriftProbe(String id, long fence, JsonNode body, String bearer, SSLContext tls) throws Exception {
    var request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("https://localhost:" + port + "/internal/v2/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/outcomes"))
        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10)).header("Authorization", "Bearer " + bearer).header("X-Owner-Fence", Long.toString(fence))
        .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString()).header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(body.toString())).build();
    return HttpClient.newBuilder().sslContext(tls).build().send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
  }

  private void assertPendingContextGuardsThenRelease(String id) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
    while (!INCREMENTAL_ACK_ENTERED.contains(id) && System.nanoTime() < deadline) Thread.sleep(10);
    assertThat(INCREMENTAL_ACK_ENTERED).as("actual runner accepted version2 on the original browser before returning ACK").contains(id);
    var service = ownedApplicationContext.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService.class);
    var materialization = new com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.bridge.GuidedMaterializationService(jdbc, C);
    var controller = com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedInternalIdentityFixture.controller();
    JsonNode current = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().deepCopy());
    long fence = current.path("owner").path("fence").asLong();
    int calls = MODEL_WIRE.size(), legacy = LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get(), effects = RECEIPTS.size();
    String before = incrementalPendingSnapshot(id), versions = incrementalImmutableSnapshot(id);
    try {
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_materialization_deliveries WHERE session_id=? AND status='PENDING'", Integer.class, id)).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.reserveModel(controller, id, fence, "pending-context-direct-reserve", 1, 1))
          .isInstanceOf(GuidedSessionException.class).hasMessage("REVISION_CONFLICT");
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> sessions.transaction(() -> plans.reserveAttempt(current, "pending-context-attempt", 1, "NEXT_ACTION", "0".repeat(64),
          plans.state(id).path("planRevision").asLong(), 1, 1, "fixture", Instant.now().plusSeconds(10))))
          .isInstanceOf(GuidedSessionException.class).hasMessage("REVISION_CONFLICT");
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> sessions.transaction(() -> { sessions.lock(id); materialization.bump(id); return null; }))
          .isInstanceOf(GuidedSessionException.class).hasMessage("REVISION_CONFLICT");
      ObjectNode old = (ObjectNode) C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT proposal::text FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY step_id,ordinal DESC LIMIT 1", String.class, id));
      ((ObjectNode) old.path("coordinate")).put("stepId", INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(3)).put("ordinal", 0);
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.admit(controller, id, fence, "pending-context-stale-action", old))
          .isInstanceOf(GuidedSessionException.class).hasMessage("STALE_GENERATION");
      assertThat(incrementalPendingSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(before);
      assertThat(incrementalImmutableSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(versions);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(calls); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(legacy); assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(effects);
    } finally { INCREMENTAL_ACK_RELEASE.get(id).countDown(); }
  }

  private void assertIncrementalAppendAckHistory(String id) throws Exception {
    var versions = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT context_revision,context_id,authored_inputs_hash,previous_inputs_hash,input_count"
        + " FROM studio_guided_materialization_versions WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY context_revision", id);
    assertThat(versions).hasSize(2);
    assertThat(versions.get(0).get("context_id")).isEqualTo(versions.get(1).get("context_id"));
    assertThat(versions.get(1).get("previous_inputs_hash")).isEqualTo(versions.get(0).get("authored_inputs_hash"));
    assertThat(((Number) versions.get(1).get("input_count")).intValue()).isGreaterThan(((Number) versions.get(0).get("input_count")).intValue());
    var delivery = jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT status,ack::text,context_hash,context_metadata::text FROM studio_guided_materialization_deliveries WHERE session_id=?", id);
    assertThat(delivery.get("status")).isEqualTo("ACKNOWLEDGED");
    JsonNode ack = C.parse((String) delivery.get("ack"));
    ObjectNode issued = (ObjectNode) C.parse((String) delivery.get("context_metadata"));
    issued.set("authoredInputs", C.parse(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT authored_inputs::text FROM studio_guided_materialization_snapshots WHERE session_id=? AND context_revision=2", String.class, id)));
    assertThat(GuidedContracts.hash(issued)).isEqualTo(delivery.get("context_hash"));
    assertThat(ack.path("contextHash").asText()).isEqualTo(delivery.get("context_hash"));
    assertThat(ack.path("contextRevision").asInt()).isEqualTo(2);
    assertThat(issued.path("prefixProgram").isObject()).isTrue();
    assertThat(GuidedContracts.hash(issued.path("prefixProgram"))).isEqualTo(issued.path("prefixProgramHash").asText());
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND (document->>'contextRevision')::integer=1", Integer.class, id)).isPositive();
    assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND (document->>'contextRevision')::integer=2", Integer.class, id)).isPositive();
    var materialization = new com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.bridge.GuidedMaterializationService(jdbc, C);
    String before = incrementalImmutableSnapshot(id);
    for (ObjectNode forged : List.of(((ObjectNode) ack).deepCopy().put("contextRevision", 1),
        ((ObjectNode) ack).deepCopy().put("contextHash", "0".repeat(64)),
        ((ObjectNode) ack).deepCopy().put("authoredInputsHash", versions.get(0).get("authored_inputs_hash").toString()),
        ((ObjectNode) ack).deepCopy().put("ownerFence", ack.path("ownerFence").asInt() + 1))) {
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> sessions.transaction(() -> { materialization.acknowledge(sessions.lock(id).document(), forged); return null; }))
          .isInstanceOf(GuidedSessionException.class);
      assertThat(incrementalImmutableSnapshot(id)).isEqualTo(before);
    }
    try (var pool = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
      var first = pool.submit(() -> sessions.transaction(() -> { materialization.acknowledge(sessions.lock(id).document(), ack); return null; }));
      var second = pool.submit(() -> sessions.transaction(() -> { materialization.acknowledge(sessions.lock(id).document(), ack); return null; }));
