      assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isZero();
      assertThat(PROVIDER_FAILURES.get()).isEqualTo(providerFailures);
      ObjectNode metrics =
          C.object()
              .put("humanDelayMs", 1250)
              .put("modelDelayMs", 1250)
              .put("sourceStepTimeoutMs", 1000)
              .put("learningSubmits", 1)
              .put("finalizationEffects", 0)
              .put("freshRecordedReplays", 2)
              .put("replayModelCalls", 0)
              .put("bindingReadRetries", 0)
              .put("artifactHash", artifactHash);
      metrics.set("originalQuestionCapture", capture);
      metrics.set("preparationReceipt", witnessReceipt);
      metrics.set(
          "chosenAndUnchosenReceipts",
          JSON.valueToTree(CHOICE_RECEIPTS.subList(choiceReceipts, CHOICE_RECEIPTS.size())));
      metrics.set(
          "nativeInvocations",
          JSON.valueToTree(REPLAY_INVOCATIONS.subList(invocations, REPLAY_INVOCATIONS.size())));
      Files.writeString(TEMP.resolve("observed-choice-evidence.json"), metrics.toPrettyString());
    } finally {
      observedChoicePage = false;
      askObservedChoice = false;
      beforeObservedQuestion = null;
    }
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(8)
  void originalQuestionDriftAndSqlCaptureRefusalRollbackAndCommitUsageWithoutAnotherCall()
      throws Exception {
    observedChoicePage = true;
    try {
      for (String mode : List.of("observation-body-drift", "sql-capture")) {
        int calls = MODEL_WIRE.size(), receipts = RECEIPTS.size();
        askObservedChoice = true;
        if (mode.equals("observation-body-drift")) {
          beforeObservedQuestion =
              context ->
                  sessions.transaction(
                      () -> {
                        var current = sessions.lock(context.path("sessionId").asText());
                        // Preserve the reserved attempt identity so this reaches actual question
                        // capture. The acknowledged hash must reject a changed sanitized body.
                        current
                            .progress()
                            .withObject("/observation")
                            .put("scopeHash", "0".repeat(64));
                        sessions.save(current);
                        return null;
                      });
        } else {
          beforeObservedQuestion = null;
          jdbc.execute(
              "CREATE FUNCTION fixture_reject_question_capture() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql"
                  + " AS $$ BEGIN IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM studio_guided_clarification_contexts"
                  + " WHERE session_id=NEW.session_id) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'injected original"
                  + " question capture failure'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $$");
          jdbc.execute(
              "CREATE TRIGGER fixture_reject_question_capture BEFORE INSERT ON"
                  + " studio_guided_publication_observations FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION"
                  + " fixture_reject_question_capture()");
        }
        String id = startLearning(createSource(TEXT));
        try {
          awaitState(id, "FAILED");
          JsonNode result =
              publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/result", null, 200, null);
          assertThat(result.path("errorCode").asText()).isEqualTo("INVALID_CONTRACT");
          assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(calls + 4);
          assertThat(
                  jdbc.queryForObject(
                      "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts WHERE session_id=? AND"
                          + " status='REJECTED' AND safe_result IS NULL AND input_tokens=20 AND"
                          + " output_tokens=40",
                      Integer.class,
                      id))
              .isEqualTo(1);
          assertThat(
                  jdbc.queryForObject(
                      "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_clarification_contexts WHERE"
                          + " session_id=?",
                      Integer.class,
                      id))
              .isZero();
          assertThat(
                  jdbc.queryForObject(
                      "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_publication_preparations WHERE"
                          + " session_id=? AND observed_choice_receipt IS NOT NULL",
                      Integer.class,
                      id))
              .isZero();
          assertThat(
                  jdbc.queryForObject(
                      "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_publication_observations WHERE"
                          + " session_id=?",
                      Integer.class,
                      id))
              .isEqualTo(1);
          assertThat(
                  jdbc.queryForObject(
                      "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_publications WHERE session_id=?",
                      Integer.class,
                      id))
              .isZero();
          JsonNode counters =
              sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("counters").deepCopy());
          assertThat(counters.path("modelProposals").asInt()).isEqualTo(4);
          assertThat(counters.path("modelInputTokens").asLong()).isPositive();
          assertThat(counters.path("modelOutputTokens").asLong()).isPositive();
          Thread.sleep(1100);
          assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(calls + 4);
          assertThat(RECEIPTS).hasSize(receipts);
          assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isZero();
          ObjectNode metrics =
              C.object()
                  .put("mode", mode)
                  .put("modelCalls", 4)
                  .put("rejectedAttemptInputTokens", 20)
                  .put("rejectedAttemptOutputTokens", 40)
                  .put("rolledBackContexts", 0)
                  .put("rolledBackQuestionObservations", 0)
                  .put("publishedRows", 0)
                  .put("laterGatewayCalls", 0)
                  .put("publicErrorCode", "INVALID_CONTRACT");
          metrics.set("committedCounters", counters);
          Files.writeString(
              TEMP.resolve("question-refusal-" + mode + ".json"), metrics.toPrettyString());
        } finally {
          beforeObservedQuestion = null;
          if (mode.equals("sql-capture")) {
            jdbc.execute(
                "DROP TRIGGER fixture_reject_question_capture ON"
                    + " studio_guided_publication_observations");
            jdbc.execute("DROP FUNCTION fixture_reject_question_capture()");
          }
        }
      }
    } finally {
      observedChoicePage = false;
      askObservedChoice = false;
      beforeObservedQuestion = null;
    }
  }

  private void awaitState(String id, String expected) throws Exception {
    long end = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(30).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < end) {
      String state =
          sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("state").asText());
      if (state.equals(expected)) {
        return;
      }
      if (List.of("FAILED", "EXPIRED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME", "CANCELLED").contains(state)) {
        throw new AssertionError("Expected " + expected + " but got " + state + "; logs " + TEMP);
      }
      Thread.sleep(20);
    }
    throw new AssertionError("Expected " + expected + "; logs " + TEMP);
  }

  private HttpResponse<String> observedAdmissionHttp(String execution, String body)
      throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request =
        HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                URI.create(
                    "https://localhost:"
                        + port
                        + "/internal/studio/executions/"
                        + execution
                        + "/observed-admission"))
            .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + CALLBACK)
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .POST(
                body == null
                    ? HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.noBody()
                    : HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(body))
            .build();
    return HttpClient.newBuilder()
        .sslContext(TLS)
        .build()
        .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
  }

  @Test
  void actualIncrementalSecretPrefixRecapturesRotatedValueWithScopedAuditAndOrdinaryReplay() throws Exception {
    p3Case(() -> {
      p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
      p3Grant(primaryCsi, "fixture");
      UUID test = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", true);
      VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", test);
      String first = p3ExpectedPassword;
      p3Seed(scope, first);
      String original = p3Start(test, "fixture");
      awaitFinalizing(original);
      P3Publication base = p3Publish(original);
      String rotated = "P15-PREFIX-ROTATED-" + UUID.randomUUID();
      p3ExpectedPassword = rotated;
      p3Seed(scope, rotated);
      P3_RECEIPTS.clear(); P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
      int beforeModels = MODEL_WIRE.size(), beforeLegacy = LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get();
      int beforeReads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
      ObjectNode start = p3StartCommand(test, "fixture").put("baseRevisionId", base.revision().toString()).put("firstLearnStepId", THREE);
      String mixed = p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 202).path("session").path("sessionId").asText();
      awaitFinalizing(mixed);
      assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS") - beforeReads).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_model_attempts m JOIN studio_guided_relearn_switches s USING(session_id)"
          + " WHERE m.session_id=? AND m.created_at<s.created_at", Integer.class, mixed)).isZero();
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isGreaterThan(beforeModels);
      assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).hasSize(1);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isTrue();
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().password().equals(rotated)).as("current-session password fill uses fresh Vault value").isTrue();
      assertThat(P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS).hasSize(1);
      p3AssertUsed(mixed, null); p3AssertAuditScope(mixed, null, scope);
      var raw = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.actions(mixed));
      assertThat(raw.stream().map(action -> action.path("executionEvidence").path("contextId").asText()).distinct().toList()).hasSize(1);
      var secret = raw.stream().filter(action -> action.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
          && action.path("coordinate").path("ordinal").asInt() == 1).findFirst().orElseThrow();
      assertThat(secret.path("state").asText()).isEqualTo("SUCCEEDED");
      assertThat(secret.path("dispatchCount").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
      int finalizedModels = MODEL_WIRE.size(), finalizedReads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size();
      P3Publication merged = p3Publish(mixed);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(finalizedModels); assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads).hasSize(finalizedReads);
      JsonNode artifact = C.parse(merged.artifact());
      assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom")).hasSize(2);
      for (String step : List.of(ONE, TWO)) {
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("reusedFrom").path(step).path("revisionId").asText()).isEqualTo(base.revision().toString());
        assertThat(artifact.path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(step))
            .isEqualTo(C.parse(base.artifact()).path("learnedPlan").path("stepSnapshotHashes").path(step));
      }
      var sinks = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, byte[]>();
      assertGuidedLearnRunRecorded(mixed, sinks);
      assertThat(merged.artifact()).doesNotContain(first, rotated);
      UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
      p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, merged);
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
      JsonNode report = p3AwaitExecution(execution, "PASSED");
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE).hasSize(finalizedModels); assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isEqualTo(beforeLegacy);
      assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS") - beforeReads).isEqualTo(2);
      p3AssertUsed(null, execution); p3AssertAuditScope(null, execution, scope);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).hasSize(2);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getLast().password().equals(rotated)).isTrue();
      assertThat(p3DecodeVideo(execution, report)).isPositive();
      p3Scan(List.of(first, rotated));
      for (String value : List.of(first, rotated)) assertJoinedSinkAbsent(sinks, value);
      p3WriteEvidence("incremental-secret-prefix", C.object().put("prefixValueReads", 1).put("ordinaryValueReads", 1)
          .put("prefixModelCalls", 0).put("suffixModelCalls", finalizedModels - beforeModels)
          .put("baseRevisionId", base.revision().toString()).put("revisionId", merged.revision().toString())
          .put("sameGuidedBrowser", true).put("currentSessionAuditJoined", true));
    });
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(9)
  void actualSecretLoginPublishesValueFreeAndTwoOrdinaryRunsResolveFreshWithoutModel()
      throws Exception {
    coreRole = "owner";
    String canary = "JOINED-LOGIN-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    var fixtureHttp = HttpClient.newHttpClient();
    fixtureHttp.send(
        HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create(SECRET_ORIGIN + "/__reset"))
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.noBody())
            .build(),
        HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.discarding());
    fixtureHttp.send(
        HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create(SECRET_ORIGIN + "/__rotate"))
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(canary))
            .build(),
        HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.discarding());
    UUID testId =
        createSource(
            List.of(
                "Open " + SECRET_ORIGIN + "/login.",
                "Enter <var USER> in User and <secret PASS> as password.",
                "Click Sign in, check Home, open Reports and check Reports appears."));
    jdbc.update(
        ("UPDATE ui_tests SET variables='[{\"name\":\"USER\",\"kind\":\"plain\","
            + "\"value\":\"fixture-user\"},{\"name\":\"PASS\",\"kind\":\"secretRef\"}]'::jsonb"
            + " WHERE id=?"),
        testId);
    var vaultScope =
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", testId);
    VAULT.rotate(
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET,
        vaultScope,
        "PASS",
        canary);
    VAULT.bindOrigins(
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET,
        vaultScope,
        "PASS",
        SECRET_ORIGIN);
    int baselineReads =
        COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, vaultScope, "PASS");
    int baselineModels = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    String rotatedCanary = "ROTATED-LOGIN-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    var canaries = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
    canaries.add(canary);
    canaries.add(
        rotatedCanary); // Both actual values are known to the original stream matcher at opening.
    // AC-10 canary: the secret values, the authored saved-input literal, the authored step text and
    // the page labels the run clicks must never appear in an audit event (labels only as a hash).
    AUDIT_CANARIES.addAll(
        List.of(canary, rotatedCanary, "fixture-user", "Sign in", "Reports", "Click Sign in"));
    var fixtureReceiptIds = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
    var videoKeys = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
    var videoHashes = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
    var attemptIds = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
    var decodedVideos = JSON.createArrayNode();
    var sinkBytes = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, byte[]>();
    var workerIdentity = joinedWorkerIdentity();
    C5Capture capture = new C5Capture(canaries, false);
    c5Capture = capture;
    Throwable c5PrimaryFailure = null;
    secretLoginScenario = true;
    s2TraceBinding = new S2TraceBinding(vaultScope, capture);
    try {
      capture.beginS2(++p3CaptureCaseOrdinal);
      capture.writeProfileValues();
      String sessionId = startLearning(testId);
      awaitFinalizing(sessionId);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, vaultScope, "PASS")
                  - baselineReads)
          .as("one committed guided password dispatch")
          .isEqualTo(1);
      var learned =
          C.parse(
              fixtureHttp
                  .send(
                      HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create(SECRET_ORIGIN + "/__receipts"))
                          .GET()
                          .build(),
                      HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString())
                  .body());
      assertThat(learned.size()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(learned.get(0).path("accepted").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(learned.get(0).path("username").asText()).isEqualTo("fixture-user");
      fixtureReceiptIds.add(learned.get(0).path("id").asText());
      assertThat(learned.get(0).path("password").asText().equals(canary)).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE session_id=?"
                      + " AND secret_id='PASS' AND row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome='USED'",
                  Integer.class,
                  sessionId))
          .isEqualTo(1);
      int learningModels = MODEL_WIRE.size();
      assertThat(learningModels - baselineModels).isPositive();
      var finalize =
          C.object()
              .put("schemaVersion", 3)
              .put("expectedSourceRevision", "1")
              .put("expectedConfigurationRevision", "1");
      int beforePublicationReads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size();
      JsonNode publication =
          publicCall(
              "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + sessionId + "/finalize",
              finalize,
              200,
              UUID.randomUUID().toString());
      UUID revisionId = UUID.fromString(publication.path("revisionId").asText());
      String artifact =
          jdbc.queryForObject(
              "SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?", String.class, revisionId);
      String artifactHash =
          jdbc.queryForObject(
              "SELECT artifact_hash FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?", String.class, revisionId);
      assertThat(independentArtifactHash(C.parse(artifact))).isEqualTo(artifactHash);
      assertThat(publication.path("artifactHash").asText()).isEqualTo(artifactHash);
      assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size()).isEqualTo(beforePublicationReads);
      sinkBytes.put("publication", publication.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      sinkBytes.put("artifact", artifact.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, vaultScope, "PASS")
                  - baselineReads)
          .as("publication performs zero value reads")
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(learningModels);
      // STUDIO-OBS-013 AC-6 / TEST-001: the guided learn run itself is recorded (AR-QA TEST001).
      ObjectNode guidedRecording = assertGuidedLearnRunRecorded(sessionId, sinkBytes);
      String guidedVideoKey = guidedRecording.path("videoKey").asText();
      var executions = new java.util.ArrayList<UUID>();
      for (int run = 0; run < 2; run++) {
        String actual = run == 0 ? canary : rotatedCanary;
        if (run == 1) {
          capture.writeProfileValues();
          VAULT.rotate(
              com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET,
              vaultScope,
              "PASS",
              actual);
          VAULT.bindOrigins(
              com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET,
              vaultScope,
              "PASS",
              SECRET_ORIGIN);
          fixtureHttp.send(
              HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create(SECRET_ORIGIN + "/__rotate"))
                  .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(actual))
                  .build(),
              HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.discarding());
        }
        UUID execution =
            UUID.fromString(
                publicCall(
                        "/api/v1/studio/tests/" + testId + "/execute",
                        C.object(),
                        202,
                        UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                    .path("executionId")
                    .asText());
        executions.add(execution);
        capture.watchExecution(execution);
        JsonNode binding = observedBinding(execution);
        assertThat(binding.path("artifactHash").asText()).isEqualTo(artifactHash);
        assertThat(binding.path("revisionId").asText()).isEqualTo(revisionId.toString());
        attemptIds.add(binding.path("attemptId").asText());
        assertThat(attemptIds).doesNotHaveDuplicates();
        assertThat(
                COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                        StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, vaultScope, "PASS")
                    - baselineReads)
            .as("acceptance remains names-only")
            .isEqualTo(run + 1);
        assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
        long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
        String status = "";
        while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
          status =
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT status FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?", String.class, execution);
          if (List.of("PASSED", "FAILED", "ERROR").contains(status)) {
            break;
          }
          Thread.sleep(50);
        }
        assertThat(status)
            .as("actual compiled child login, evidence at %s", TEMP)
            .isEqualTo("PASSED");
        assertThat(
                COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                        StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, vaultScope, "PASS")
                    - baselineReads)
            .isEqualTo(run + 2);
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(learningModels);
        assertThat(LEGACY_MODEL_CALLS.get()).isZero();
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?",
                    String.class,
                    revisionId))
            .isEqualTo(artifact);
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT artifact_hash FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?",
                    String.class,
                    revisionId))
            .isEqualTo(artifactHash);
        JsonNode receipts =
            C.parse(
                fixtureHttp
                    .send(
                        HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create(SECRET_ORIGIN + "/__receipts"))
                            .GET()
                            .build(),
                        HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString())
                    .body());
        assertThat(receipts.size()).isEqualTo(run + 2);
        assertThat(receipts.get(run + 1).path("accepted").asBoolean()).isTrue();
        assertThat(receipts.get(run + 1).path("username").asText()).isEqualTo("fixture-user");
        fixtureReceiptIds.add(receipts.get(run + 1).path("id").asText());
        assertThat(fixtureReceiptIds).doesNotHaveDuplicates();
        assertThat(receipts.get(run + 1).path("password").asText().equals(actual)).isTrue();
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    ("SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=? AND"
                        + " row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome='USED'"),
                    Integer.class,
                    execution))
            .isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_observed_secret_read_entries WHERE execution_id=?",
                    Integer.class,
                    execution))
            .isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    ("SELECT browser_started_at IS NOT NULL AND video_key IS NOT NULL AND"
                        + " observed_admission_receipt IS NOT NULL FROM ui_test_executions WHERE"
                        + " id=?"),
                    Boolean.class,
                    execution))
            .isTrue();
        JsonNode snapshot = awaitJoinedVideo(execution);
        sinkBytes.put("snapshot-" + run, snapshot.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        String videoKey =
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT video_key FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?", String.class, execution);
        Path video = TEMP.resolve("objects").resolve(videoKey).normalize();
        assertThat(video.startsWith(TEMP.resolve("objects"))).isTrue();
        assertThat(Files.isRegularFile(video, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)).isTrue();
        assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(video)).isFalse();
        byte[] videoBytes = Files.readAllBytes(video);
        assertThat(videoBytes.length).isPositive();
        HttpResponse<byte[]> served =
            HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .sslContext(TLS)
                .build()
                .send(
                    HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                            URI.create(
                                "https://localhost:"
                                    + port
                                    + "/api/v1/studio/executions/"
                                    + execution
                                    + "/video"))
                        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15))
                        .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE)
                        .GET()
                        .build(),
                    HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofByteArray());
        assertThat(served.statusCode()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(java.util.Arrays.equals(served.body(), videoBytes)).isTrue();
        videoKeys.add(videoKey);
        videoHashes.add(
            HexFormat.of().formatHex(MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(videoBytes)));
        decodedVideos.add(decodeJoinedVideo(video, execution));
        sinkBytes.put("native-video-" + run, videoBytes);
        collectJoinedSinks(sinkBytes);
        for (String value : canaries) {
          assertJoinedSinkAbsent(sinkBytes, value);
          assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.stream().anyMatch(wire -> wire.contains(value))).isFalse();
          for (String file : List.of("guided.log", "ordinary.log")) {
            assertThat(Files.readString(TEMP.resolve(file)).contains(value))
                .as("redacted actual %s", file)
                .isFalse();
          }
        }
      }
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  ("SELECT count(DISTINCT vault_version) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE"
                      + " execution_id IN (?,?) AND row_kind='RELEASE' AND"
                      + " decision_stage='DISPATCH'"),
                  Integer.class,
                  executions.get(0),
                  executions.get(1)))
          .isEqualTo(2);
      assertThat(executions).doesNotHaveDuplicates();
      assertThat(videoKeys).doesNotHaveDuplicates();
      assertThat(videoKeys).doesNotContain(guidedVideoKey);
      assertThat(videoHashes).doesNotHaveDuplicates();
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                  StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.VARIABLE, vaultScope, "USER"))
          .as("plain USER comes from accepted configuration, not Vault")
          .isZero();
      var secretReads =
          COUNTED_VAULT.reads.stream()
              .filter(
                  read ->
                      read.key.equals(
                          com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.FakeStudioVaultProvider.key(
                              StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, vaultScope, "PASS")))
              .toList();
      assertThat(secretReads).hasSize(3);
      for (OwnedVaultRead read : secretReads) {
        assertThat(read.value != null && allErased(read.value))
            .as("actual delivered provider array erased")
            .isTrue();
      }
      assertThat(secretReads.stream().map(read -> read.version).distinct().count()).isEqualTo(2);
      assertThat(joinedWorkerIdentity()).isEqualTo(workerIdentity);
      var evidence =
          C.object()
              .put(
                  "scope",
                  "actual Core HTTP/PostgreSQL/counted Vault/compiled worker/full Chromium")
              .put("guidedValueReads", 1)
              .put("publicationValueReads", 0)
              .put("ordinaryValueReads", 2)
              .put("learningModelCalls", learningModels - baselineModels)
              .put("publicationReplayModelCalls", 0)
              .put("revisionId", revisionId.toString())
              .put("rotated", true)
              .put("artifactHash", artifactHash)
              .put("plainUserVaultReads", 0)
              .put("nativeVideosDecoded", decodedVideos.size())
              .put("fixturePasswordReceipts", "TEST_ONLY_ORACLE_EXCLUDED_FROM_PRODUCT_SINKS")
              .put(
                  "remaining",
                  "TRACE/SSE/metrics/profile-before-cleanup/all fault sinks and actual service"
                      + " consume/read/send/recovery remain unproved");
      evidence.set("workerIdentity", workerIdentity);
      evidence.set("guidedRecording", guidedRecording);
      evidence.set("nativeVideos", decodedVideos);
      evidence.set("videoKeys", JSON.valueToTree(videoKeys));
      evidence.set("executionIds", JSON.valueToTree(executions));
      evidence.set("attemptIds", JSON.valueToTree(attemptIds));
      evidence.set("videoHashes", JSON.valueToTree(videoHashes));
      evidence.set("fixtureReceiptIds", JSON.valueToTree(fixtureReceiptIds));
      evidence.set(
          "scannedSinkByteCounts",
          JSON.valueToTree(
              sinkBytes.entrySet().stream()
                  .collect(
                      java.util.stream.Collectors.toMap(
                          Map.Entry::getKey, e -> e.getValue().length))));
      evidence.put("imageProvenance", "NOT_ASSERTED_CONTROLLED_LOCAL_CATALOG");
      collectJoinedSinks(sinkBytes);
      for (String value : canaries) {
        assertJoinedSinkAbsent(sinkBytes, value);
      }
      capture.writeNumericEvidence();
      Files.writeString(
          TEMP.resolve("secret-login-joined-evidence.json"), evidence.toPrettyString());
    } catch (Exception | Error original) {
      c5PrimaryFailure = original;
      throw original;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, this::p3CleanupOwnedResources);
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::awaitS2Completion);
      if (failures.isEmpty() && !cleanupUnconfirmed) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p3Scan(canaries));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::writeNumericEvidence);
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::close);
        if (!cleanupUnconfirmed) {
          c5Capture = null;
          secretLoginScenario = false;
          s2TraceBinding = null;
          p3OwnedGuidedCookies.clear();
          p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.clear();
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              failures,
              () ->
                  Files.writeString(
                      TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"),
                      "{\"active\":false}",
                      StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
      } else {
        cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
        failures.add(
            new IllegalStateException("S2 actual secret-bearing owner completion unconfirmed"));
        // Keep the original capture, TRACE and patterns over unconfirmed secret-bearing work.
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        if (c5PrimaryFailure != null) {
          failures.forEach(c5PrimaryFailure::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ── STUDIO-OBS-013 TEST-001 (AR-QA ar-qa-sec001-test001.json): the guided learn run's own
  // recording, joined: real backend, PostgreSQL, guided worker on Chromium, filesystem store. ──

  /** The replica sets no grace, so it judges the window with the record's own default. */
  private static final Duration GUIDED_SETTLE_GRACE =
      com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.evidence.GuidedRecordingProperties.DEFAULT_SETTLE_GRACE;

  /** The guided manifest disclosure (AC-5), as the runner writes it. */
  private static final String GUIDED_DISCLOSURE =
      "Password fills are rendered as masked dots (password-only rule). A page that later"
          + " displays a secret in plain text is not protected by this recording.";

  /** Polls the result (bounded, inside settle-grace) until the video is no longer PROCESSING. */
  private JsonNode awaitGuidedVideoSettled(String sessionId) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
    JsonNode result;
    do {
      result =
          publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + sessionId + "/result", null, 200, null);
      if (!result.path("evidence").path("video").path("status").asText().equals("PROCESSING")) {
        break;
      }
      Thread.sleep(100);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    C.validate("v3/result", result);
    return result;
  }

  private HttpResponse<byte[]> guidedVideoRead(String sessionId, String range, boolean cookie)
      throws Exception {
    var builder =
        HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                URI.create(
                    "https://localhost:"
                        + port
                        + "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/"
                        + sessionId
                        + "/video"))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15))
            .GET();
    if (cookie) {
      builder.header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE);
    }
    if (range != null) {
      builder.header("Range", range);
    }
    return HttpClient.newBuilder()
        .sslContext(TLS)
        .build()
        .send(builder.build(), HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofByteArray());
  }

  /** Exactly one V20 fact for the session. */
  private Map<String, Object> guidedRecordingRow(String sessionId) {
    var rows =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT state, partial, video_key, manifest_key, timings::text AS timings,"
                + " expires_at IS NOT NULL AS has_expiry FROM studio_guided_session_recordings"
                + " WHERE session_id=?",
            sessionId);
    assertThat(rows).as("exactly one V20 recording fact").hasSize(1);
    return rows.get(0);
  }

  private String guidedRecordingRowState(String sessionId) {
    var rows =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT state FROM studio_guided_session_recordings WHERE session_id=?",
            String.class,
            sessionId);
    return rows.isEmpty() ? null : rows.get(0);
  }

  /**
   * What a replica with no recording store must publish, from the stored fact and the DB-clock
   * window (AR-1 R1 table with servesRecordings false).
   */
  private String replicaExpectedVideoStatus(String sessionId) {
    Map<String, Object> row =
        jdbc.queryForMap(
            "SELECT s.state AS session_state, r.state AS recording_state,"
                + " clock_timestamp() < s.expires_at + make_interval(secs => ?) AS within"
                + " FROM studio_guided_sessions s LEFT JOIN studio_guided_session_recordings r"
                + " ON r.session_id = s.session_id WHERE s.session_id=?",
            (double) GUIDED_SETTLE_GRACE.toSeconds(),
            sessionId);
    Object recording = row.get("recording_state");
    if (recording == null) {
      boolean terminal =
          List.of(
                  "SUCCEEDED",
                  "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT",
                  "FAILED",
                  "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
                  "CANCELLED",
                  "EXPIRED")
              .contains(String.valueOf(row.get("session_state")));
      return !terminal || Boolean.TRUE.equals(row.get("within")) ? "PROCESSING" : "UNAVAILABLE";
    }
    return switch (String.valueOf(recording)) {
      case "AVAILABLE", "UNAVAILABLE" -> "UNAVAILABLE";
      case "FAILED" -> "FAILED";
      default -> throw new AssertionError("unexpected stored recording state " + recording);
    };
  }

  private static int guidedTimingKindRank(String kind) {
    int rank = List.of("prefixReplay", "step", "clarificationWait", "action").indexOf(kind);
    assertThat(rank).as("timing kind %s", kind).isNotNegative();
    return rank;
  }

  /** Independent R2 order check: startMs, kind rank, ordinal (null first), stepId code points. */
  private static int guidedTimingOrder(JsonNode a, JsonNode b) {
    int byStart = Long.compare(a.path("startMs").asLong(), b.path("startMs").asLong());
    if (byStart != 0) {
      return byStart;
    }
    int byKind =
        Integer.compare(
            guidedTimingKindRank(a.path("kind").asText()),
            guidedTimingKindRank(b.path("kind").asText()));
    if (byKind != 0) {
      return byKind;
    }
    long ordinalA = a.path("ordinal").isNull() ? -1 : a.path("ordinal").asLong();
    long ordinalB = b.path("ordinal").isNull() ? -1 : b.path("ordinal").asLong();
    if (ordinalA != ordinalB) {
      return Long.compare(ordinalA, ordinalB);
    }
    int byStep = java.util.Arrays.compare(
        a.path("stepId").asText().codePoints().toArray(),
        b.path("stepId").asText().codePoints().toArray());
    return byStep != 0 ? byStep : java.util.Arrays.compare(
        a.path("admissionId").asText("").codePoints().toArray(),
        b.path("admissionId").asText("").codePoints().toArray());
  }

  /**
   * AC-6 happy half on the OBS-007 login learn run: AVAILABLE, the V20 fact, the stored WebM, an
   * authenticated 206, offsets equal to the dispatched ledger, a clean manifest. Adds the guided
   * bytes to {@code sinks} for the method's canary scan. Returns the evidence record.
   */
  private ObjectNode assertGuidedLearnRunRecorded(String sessionId, Map<String, byte[]> sinks)
      throws Exception {
    JsonNode result = awaitGuidedVideoSettled(sessionId);
    JsonNode video = result.path("evidence").path("video");
    assertThat(video.path("status").asText()).as(video.toString()).isEqualTo("AVAILABLE");
    assertThat(video.path("partial").isBoolean() && !video.path("partial").booleanValue())
        .as("partial is false")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(video.path("error").isNull()).isTrue();
    assertThat(video.path("expiresAt").isTextual()).isTrue();
    assertThat(result.path("evidence").path("ref").asText()).isEqualTo(sessionId);
    assertThat(result.path("replayVerified").asBoolean()).isFalse();

    // V20 fact; the backend's key derivation agrees with what the runner uploaded under.
    Map<String, Object> row = guidedRecordingRow(sessionId);
    assertThat(row.get("state")).isEqualTo("AVAILABLE");
    assertThat(row.get("partial")).isEqualTo(false);
    assertThat(row.get("has_expiry")).isEqualTo(true);
    String csi =
        jdbc.queryForObject(
            "SELECT document->'scope'->>'csiId' FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE session_id=?",
            String.class,
            sessionId);
    var keys =
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.evidence.GuidedRecordingKeys.derive(csi, sessionId)
            .orElseThrow();
    String videoKey = String.valueOf(row.get("video_key"));
    String manifestKey = String.valueOf(row.get("manifest_key"));
    assertThat(videoKey).isEqualTo(keys.videoKey());
    assertThat(manifestKey).isEqualTo(keys.manifestKey());

    // The stored object.
    Path objects = TEMP.resolve("objects");
    Path file = objects.resolve(videoKey).normalize();
    assertThat(file.startsWith(objects)).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(file)).isFalse();
    byte[] bytes = Files.readAllBytes(file);
    assertThat(bytes.length).as("larger than the 1 KiB range read").isGreaterThan(1024);
    assertThat(HexFormat.of().formatHex(bytes, 0, 4)).isEqualTo("1a45dfa3");
    ObjectNode decoded = decodeJoinedVideo(file, UUID.fromString(sessionId));

    // Authenticated range read, and the unauthenticated refusal.
    HttpResponse<byte[]> ranged = guidedVideoRead(sessionId, "bytes=0-1023", true);
    assertThat(ranged.statusCode()).isEqualTo(206);
    assertThat(ranged.headers().firstValue("Content-Range"))
        .contains("bytes 0-1023/" + bytes.length);
    assertThat(ranged.body()).isEqualTo(java.util.Arrays.copyOfRange(bytes, 0, 1024));
    HttpResponse<byte[]> anonymous = guidedVideoRead(sessionId, "bytes=0-1023", false);
    assertThat(anonymous.statusCode()).isEqualTo(401);
    String exposed =
        result
            + "|"
            + ranged.headers().map()
            + "|"
            + anonymous.headers().map()
            + "|"
            + new String(anonymous.body(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    for (String forbidden :
        List.of(videoKey, manifestKey, "put-checks-studio-evidence", objects.toString())) {
      assertThat(exposed).as("public surface names %s", forbidden).doesNotContain(forbidden);
    }

    // Offsets: one action item per dispatched admission, one step item per step, sorted, bounded.
    JsonNode timings = result.path("actionTimings");
    assertThat(GuidedContracts.canonical(C.parse(String.valueOf(row.get("timings")))))
        .as("V20 timings are served verbatim")
        .isEqualTo(GuidedContracts.canonical(timings));
    long maxMs =
        jdbc.queryForObject(
            "SELECT (document->'limits'->>'maxSessionSeconds')::bigint * 1000"
                + " FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE session_id=?",
            Long.class,
            sessionId);
    var ledger = new java.util.HashMap<String, String>();
    var ordinals = new java.util.HashMap<String, Integer>();
    for (var action :
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT admission_id, step_id, ordinal FROM studio_guided_actions a"
                + " WHERE session_id=? AND (document->>'dispatchCount')::integer = 1"
                + " AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM studio_guided_no_invocation_receipts r WHERE r.session_id=a.session_id"
                + " AND r.admission_id=a.admission_id AND r.document->>'nativeInvocationStarted'='false')",
            sessionId)) {
      ledger.put(String.valueOf(action.get("admission_id")), String.valueOf(action.get("step_id")));
      ordinals.put(
          String.valueOf(action.get("admission_id")), ((Number) action.get("ordinal")).intValue());
    }
    assertThat(ledger).as("the learn run dispatched actions").isNotEmpty();
    var actionItems = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, JsonNode>();
    var stepItems = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, JsonNode>();
    JsonNode previous = null;
    for (JsonNode item : timings) {
      long start = item.path("startMs").asLong();
      assertThat(item.path("startMs").isIntegralNumber() && start >= 0 && start <= maxMs)
          .as("startMs bounded: %s", item)
          .isTrue();
      if (!item.path("endMs").isNull()) {
        assertThat(item.path("endMs").asLong()).isBetween(start, maxMs);
      }
      if (previous != null) {
        assertThat(guidedTimingOrder(previous, item)).as("R2 order").isNegative();
      }
      previous = item;
      String kind = item.path("kind").asText();
      if (kind.equals("action")) {
        String admission = item.path("admissionId").asText();
        assertThat(actionItems.put(admission, item)).as("one item per admission").isNull();
        assertThat(item.path("stepId").asText()).isEqualTo(ledger.get(admission));
        assertThat(item.path("ordinal").asInt()).isEqualTo(ordinals.get(admission));
      } else {
        assertThat(kind).as("OBS-013 emits step and action only").isEqualTo("step");
        assertThat(stepItems.put(item.path("stepId").asText(), item)).isNull();
      }
    }
    assertThat(actionItems.keySet())
        .as("happy path: every dispatched action has its reading (AR-1 auto-decision 5)")
        .containsExactlyInAnyOrderElementsOf(ledger.keySet());
    assertThat(stepItems.keySet()).containsExactlyInAnyOrderElementsOf(Set.copyOf(ledger.values()));
    for (var step : stepItems.entrySet()) {
      long min =
          actionItems.values().stream()
              .filter(item -> item.path("stepId").asText().equals(step.getKey()))
              .mapToLong(item -> item.path("startMs").asLong())
              .min()
              .orElseThrow();
      assertThat(step.getValue().path("startMs").asLong()).isEqualTo(min);
    }

    // Manifest: the runner's audit record next to the video, runner vocabulary (STORED).
    Path manifest = objects.resolve(manifestKey).normalize();
    assertThat(manifest.startsWith(objects)).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.isRegularFile(manifest, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)).isTrue();
    byte[] manifestBytes = Files.readAllBytes(manifest);
    JsonNode manifestBody = C.parse(new String(manifestBytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("kind").asText()).isEqualTo("guidedRecording");
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("sessionId").asText()).isEqualTo(sessionId);
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("evidenceState").asText()).isEqualTo("STORED");
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("evidencePartial").asBoolean()).isFalse();
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("videoKey").asText()).isEqualTo(videoKey);
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("manifestKey").asText()).isEqualTo(manifestKey);
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("disclosure").asText()).isEqualTo(GUIDED_DISCLOSURE);
    var servedActions = C.parse("[]");
    for (JsonNode item : timings) {
      if (item.path("kind").asText().equals("action")) {
        ((com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ArrayNode) servedActions).add(item);
      }
    }
    assertThat(GuidedContracts.canonical(manifestBody.path("timings")))
        .isEqualTo(GuidedContracts.canonical(servedActions));

    sinks.put("guided-result", result.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    sinks.put(
        "guided-v20-timings", String.valueOf(row.get("timings")).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    sinks.put("guided-manifest", manifestBytes);
    sinks.put("guided-native-video", bytes);
    sinks.put("guided-video-range", ranged.body());

    ObjectNode evidence =
        C.object()
            .put("sessionId", sessionId)
            .put("videoKey", videoKey)
            .put("videoBytes", bytes.length)
            .put("actionItems", actionItems.size())
            .put("stepItems", stepItems.size())
            .put("rangeStatus", ranged.statusCode());
    evidence.set("decoded", decoded);
    return evidence;
  }

  /**
   * AC-6 fault half: a recorder-side fault mid-session (the live WebM removed from the recorder's
   * directory after the first dispatched action, as runner recorderFileLost) settles UNAVAILABLE,
   * the session still finishes, and no video object exists. The store is the same one the happy
   * path proves AVAILABLE with, so UNAVAILABLE here can only come from the injected fault.
   *
   * <p>Hosted on the class's plain (non-secret) learn fixture, not the secret-login one: the S2
   * operation observer in the guided worker is single-use per worker process (its drain restores
   * the wrapped RevRuntime prototype methods), and the secret-login test is its one case. A second
   * secret-bearing S2 case here left the login test's teardown without its completion receipt.
   * Whether the run logs in is irrelevant to the recorder fault under test.
   */
  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(9)
  void guidedRecorderFaultSettlesUnavailableAndTheSessionStillFinishes() throws Exception {
    UUID testId = createSource(TEXT);
    long launchesBefore = p3LaunchCounter().path("count").asLong();
    String sessionId = startLearning(testId);

    // Inject: after the first dispatched action, remove the live recording file. Playwright's
    // recorder writes the WebM from an ffmpeg child that creates the output only once it has
    // probed the first screencast frame, so the file appears asynchronously after page creation
    // (S4b: under host load the directory was still empty at the first dispatch). The trigger is
    // therefore the product signal itself, the live .webm present in recordVideo.dir, while the
    // session is still before FINALIZING. A session that reaches FINALIZING with no live file
    // fails here; it is not retried or skipped.
    Path recorderDirectory = null;
    int removed = 0;
    String stateAtInjection = null;
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(90).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      JsonNode launches = p3LaunchCounter();
      Integer dispatched =
          jdbc.queryForObject(
              "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=?"
                  + " AND (document->>'dispatchCount')::integer = 1",
              Integer.class,
              sessionId);
      String state =
          jdbc.queryForObject(
              "SELECT state FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE session_id=?",
              String.class,
              sessionId);
      assertThat(state)
          .as("the recorder produced a live file before the recording was closed; logs %s", TEMP)
          .isNotIn(
              "FINALIZING",
              "SUCCEEDED",
              "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT",
              "FAILED",
              "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
              "CANCELLED",
              "EXPIRED");
      if (launches.path("count").asLong() > launchesBefore
          && launches.path("lastRecordVideoDir").isTextual()
          && dispatched != null
          && dispatched > 0) {
        Path directory = Path.of(launches.path("lastRecordVideoDir").asText());
        java.util.List<Path> live;
        try (var files = Files.list(directory)) {
          live =
              files
                  .filter(
                      file ->
                          file.getFileName().toString().endsWith(".webm")
                              && Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS))
                  .toList();
        }
        if (!live.isEmpty()) {
          stateAtInjection = state;
          recorderDirectory = directory;
          for (Path recorded : live) {
            Files.delete(recorded);
            removed++;
          }
          break;
        }
      }
      Thread.sleep(20);
    }
    assertThat(recorderDirectory)
        .as("guided recorder live file observed mid-session; logs %s", TEMP)
        .isNotNull();
    assertThat(removed).as("the live recording file was removed").isPositive();
    assertThat(stateAtInjection).isNotNull();

    // The session still finishes.
    awaitFinalizing(sessionId);
    publicCall(
        "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + sessionId + "/finalize",
        C.object()
            .put("schemaVersion", 3)
            .put("expectedSourceRevision", "1")
            .put("expectedConfigurationRevision", "1"),
        200,
        UUID.randomUUID().toString());

    JsonNode result = awaitGuidedVideoSettled(sessionId);
    JsonNode video = result.path("evidence").path("video");
    assertThat(video.path("status").asText()).as(video.toString()).isEqualTo("UNAVAILABLE");
    assertThat(video.path("error").isNull()).isTrue();
    assertThat(video.path("expiresAt").isNull()).isTrue();
    assertThat(video.path("partial").isBoolean() && !video.path("partial").booleanValue()).isTrue();
    assertThat(result.path("actionTimings").isArray()).isTrue();
    assertThat(result.path("actionTimings")).isEmpty();

    Map<String, Object> row = guidedRecordingRow(sessionId);
    assertThat(row.get("state")).isEqualTo("UNAVAILABLE");
    assertThat(row.get("partial")).isEqualTo(false);
    assertThat(row.get("has_expiry")).isEqualTo(false);
    assertThat(C.parse(String.valueOf(row.get("timings"))).isArray()).isTrue();
    assertThat(C.parse(String.valueOf(row.get("timings")))).isEmpty();

    // No fake video: the object is absent, the manifest says UNAVAILABLE, the route says 404.
    Path objects = TEMP.resolve("objects");
    Path file = objects.resolve(String.valueOf(row.get("video_key"))).normalize();
    assertThat(file.startsWith(objects)).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.notExists(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)).isTrue();
    Path manifest = objects.resolve(String.valueOf(row.get("manifest_key"))).normalize();
    assertThat(manifest.startsWith(objects)).isTrue();
    byte[] manifestBytes = Files.readAllBytes(manifest);
    JsonNode manifestBody = C.parse(new String(manifestBytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("evidenceState").asText()).isEqualTo("UNAVAILABLE");
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("timings").isArray()).isTrue();
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("timings")).isEmpty();
    assertThat(manifestBody.path("disclosure").asText()).isEqualTo(GUIDED_DISCLOSURE);
    HttpResponse<byte[]> refused = guidedVideoRead(sessionId, null, true);
    assertThat(refused.statusCode()).isEqualTo(404);
    assertThat(C.parse(new String(refused.body(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).path("error").asText())
        .isEqualTo("VIDEO_UNAVAILABLE");
  }

  /** Test-only accounting at the existing provider seam; it never resolves an extra value. */
  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(10)
  void actualCoreMayUseDenialStartsNoSessionAndNoBrowser() throws Exception {
    p3Case(
        () -> {
          p3Mode = P3Mode.REGISTRATION;
          UUID positive = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", false);
          String warm = p3Start(positive, "fixture");
          awaitFinalizing(warm);
          p3Publish(warm);
          JsonNode registered = p3LaunchCounter();
          assertThat(registered.path("registered").asBoolean()).isTrue();
          assertThat(registered.path("count").asLong()).isPositive();
          p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
          UUID denied = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", true);
          VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", denied);
          p3Seed(scope, "P3-DENIED-" + UUID.randomUUID());
          String deniedCookie = p3CoreCookie("p3-denied", "p3-denied@example.invalid");
          int calls = MODEL_WIRE.size(),
              reads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size(),
              core = CORE_CALLS.get();
          long launches = p3LaunchCounter().path("count").asLong();
          JsonNode refusal =
              p3Call(
                  deniedCookie,
                  "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions",
                  p3StartCommand(denied, "fixture"),
                  403);
          assertThat(refusal.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("SECRET_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED");
          assertThat(CORE_CALLS.get()).isGreaterThan(core);
          assertThat(
                  P3_MAY_USE.stream()
                      .anyMatch(
                          call ->
                              call.principal().equals("p3-denied")
                                  && call.csi().equals(primaryCsi)
                                  && call.environment().equals("fixture")
                                  && call.name().equals("PASS")))
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(
                  jdbc.queryForObject(
                      "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE test_id=?",
                      Long.class,
                      denied.toString()))
              .isZero();
          assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(calls);
          assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size()).isEqualTo(reads);
          assertThat(p3LaunchCounter().path("count").asLong()).isEqualTo(launches);
          p3WriteEvidence(
              "core-start-denial",
              C.object()
                  .put("coreCalls", CORE_CALLS.get() - core)
                  .put("valueReads", COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size() - reads)
                  .put("newLaunchCalls", p3LaunchCounter().path("count").asLong() - launches)
                  .put("registeredPositiveLaunchCount", registered.path("count").asLong())
                  .put(
                      "sessions",
                      jdbc.queryForObject(
                          "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE test_id=?",
                          Long.class,
                          denied.toString())));
        });
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(11)
  void actualCoreGuidedSameSecretIdStaysWithinPersistedScope() throws Exception {
    p3Case(
        () -> {
          List<VaultScope> scopes = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
          List<String> canaries = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
          int ordinal = 0;
          for (var axis :
              List.of(
                  Map.entry(primaryCsi, "fixture"),
                  Map.entry("CSI-456", "fixture"),
                  Map.entry(primaryCsi, "fixture-other"),
                  Map.entry(primaryCsi, "fixture"))) {
            p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
            p3Grant(axis.getKey(), axis.getValue());
            UUID test = p3CreateSource(axis.getKey(), axis.getValue(), true);
            VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(axis.getKey(), axis.getValue(), test);
            String value = "P3-SCOPED-" + UUID.randomUUID();
            canaries.add(value);
            scopes.add(scope);
            p3ExpectedPassword = value;
            P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
            P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
            p3Seed(scope, value);
            int read =
                COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
            List<Integer> foreignReads =
                scopes.stream()
                    .map(
                        other ->
                            COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                                StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, other, "PASS"))
                    .toList();
            String id = p3Start(test, axis.getValue());
            awaitFinalizing(id);
            assertThat(
                    COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                        - read)
                .isEqualTo(1);
            assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).hasSize(1);
            assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isTrue();
            assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().password().equals(value)).isTrue();
            assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().username().equals("p3-user")).isTrue();
            for (int i = 0; i < scopes.size() - 1; i++) {
              assertThat(
                      COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                          StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scopes.get(i), "PASS"))
                  .isEqualTo(foreignReads.get(i));
            }
            assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.lists.stream().anyMatch(scope::equals)).isTrue();
            p3AssertUsed(id, null);
            p3AssertAuditScope(id, null, scope);
            p3Publish(id);
            p3Scan(canaries);
            // Missing test scope is a real names-only refusal, not a value read or a malformed
            // policy.
            UUID missing = p3CreateSource(axis.getKey(), axis.getValue(), true);
            int values = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size(), calls = MODEL_WIRE.size();
            JsonNode refusal =
                p3Call(
                    p3Cookie,
                    "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions",
                    p3StartCommand(missing, axis.getValue()),
                    404);
            assertThat(refusal.path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("SECRET_UNAVAILABLE");
            assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size()).isEqualTo(values);
            assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(calls);
            assertThat(
                    jdbc.queryForObject(
                        "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE test_id=?",
                        Long.class,
                        missing.toString()))
                .isZero();
            p3WriteEvidence(
                "guided-scope-" + (++ordinal),
                C.object()
                    .put("ordinal", ordinal)
                    .put(
                        "ownReads",
                        COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                                StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                            - read)
                    .put("foreignReads", 0)
                    .put("acceptedPosts", P3_RECEIPTS.stream().filter(P3Receipt::accepted).count())
                    .put("missingScopeValueReads", COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size() - values)
                    .put(
                        "namesLookupObserved",
                        COUNTED_VAULT.lists.stream().anyMatch(scope::equals)));
          }
        });
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(12)
  void actualCoreOrdinarySameSecretIdStaysWithinAcceptedScope() throws Exception {
    p3Case(
        () -> {
          List<VaultScope> scopes = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
          List<String> canaries = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
          List<P3ScopeReplay> staged = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
          int ordinal = 0;
          for (var axis :
              List.of(
                  Map.entry(primaryCsi, "fixture"),
                  Map.entry("CSI-456", "fixture"),
                  Map.entry(primaryCsi, "fixture-other"),
                  Map.entry(primaryCsi, "fixture"))) {
            P3ScopeReplay prepared = p3PrepareScopeReplay(axis);
            staged.add(prepared);
            scopes.add(prepared.scope());
            canaries.add(prepared.value());
            if (staged.size() == 1) {
              continue; // Stage the two existing cross-CSI positives before accepting either run.
            }
            boolean concurrent = staged.size() == 2;
            List<P3ScopeReplay> batch = concurrent ? List.copyOf(staged) : List.of(prepared);
            if (concurrent) {
              p3ConcurrentScopeReplays(batch);
            }
            for (P3ScopeReplay current : batch) {
              UUID test = current.test();
              VaultScope scope = current.scope();
              String value = current.value();
              P3Publication published = current.published();
              p3ExpectedPassword = value;
              int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
              List<Integer> beforeReads =
                  scopes.stream()
                      .map(
                          other ->
                              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                                  StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, other, "PASS"))
                      .toList();
              if (!concurrent) {
                P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
                P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
                UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
                p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, published);
                assertThat(
                        COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                            StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS"))
                    .isEqualTo(current.ownReads());
                assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
                p3AssertScopeReplay(current, execution, p3AwaitExecution(execution, "PASSED"));
                assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.size()).isEqualTo(1);
                assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isTrue();
                assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().password().equals(value)).isTrue();
                assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().username().equals("p3-user")).isTrue();
                for (int i = 0; i < scopes.size() - 1; i++) {
                  assertThat(
                          COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                              StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scopes.get(i), "PASS"))
                      .isEqualTo(beforeReads.get(i));
                }
                assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
              }
              // Genuine accepted scope remains fixed when its own entry vanishes at the actual
              // read.
              P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
              P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
              p3Seed(scope, value);
              UUID unavailable = p3Execute(test);
              p3AssertBinding(unavailable, scope, published);
              int reads =
                  COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
              P3_FAIL_READ.set(scope);
              assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isZero();
              JsonNode refused = p3AwaitExecution(unavailable, "ERROR");
              assertThat(refused.path("errorCode").asText()).isEqualTo("REFERENCE_UNAVAILABLE");
              assertThat(
                      COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                              StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                          - reads)
                  .isEqualTo(1);
              assertThat(P3_FAIL_READ.get()).isNull();
              assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).isEmpty();
              for (int i = 0; i < scopes.size(); i++) {
                if (scopes.get(i).equals(scope)) {
                  continue;
                }
                assertThat(
                        COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                            StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scopes.get(i), "PASS"))
                    .isEqualTo(beforeReads.get(i));
              }
              assertThat(ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.containsKey(unavailable)).isFalse();
              assertThat(
                      jdbc.queryForObject(
                          "SELECT browser_started_at IS NULL AND observed_admission_receipt IS NULL"
                              + " FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?",
                          Boolean.class,
                          unavailable))
                  .isTrue();
              assertThat(
                      jdbc.queryForObject(
                          "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=?"
                              + " AND row_kind='RELEASE' AND outcome='UNAVAILABLE'",
                          Long.class,
                          unavailable))
                  .isEqualTo(1);
              p3AssertAuditScope(null, unavailable, scope);
              p3Seed(scope, value);
              String denied =
                  p3CoreCookie("p3-denied-ordinary", "p3-denied-ordinary@example.invalid");
              long accepted =
                  jdbc.queryForObject(
                      "SELECT count(*) FROM ui_test_executions WHERE ui_test_id=?",
                      Long.class,
                      test);
              int deniedReads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size();
              JsonNode noPermission =
                  p3Call(denied, "/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test + "/execute", C.object(), 400);
              assertThat(noPermission.path("error").asText()).isEqualTo("UNKNOWN_REFERENCE");
              assertThat(
                      jdbc.queryForObject(
                          "SELECT count(*) FROM ui_test_executions WHERE ui_test_id=?",
                          Long.class,
                          test))
                  .isEqualTo(accepted);
              assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size()).isEqualTo(deniedReads);
              assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
              p3Scan(canaries);
              p3WriteEvidence(
                  "ordinary-scope-" + (++ordinal),
                  C.object()
                      .put("ordinal", ordinal)
                      .put("successfulOwnRead", reads - current.ownReads())
                      .put("foreignReads", 0)
                      .put(
                          "missingOwnRead",
                          COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                                  StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                              - reads)
                      .put(
                          "missingOwnSends",
                          ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.containsKey(unavailable) ? 1 : 0)
                      .put("deniedPrincipalReads", COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size() - deniedReads)
                      .put(
                          "artifactUnchanged",
                          jdbc.queryForObject(
                                  "SELECT artifact_hash FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?",
                                  String.class,
                                  published.revision())
                              .equals(published.artifactHash())));
            }
          }
        });
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(19)
  void syntheticSinkScannerDetectsIndependentNonAsciiEncodedControls() {
    String value = "é😀\"\\A z+";
    // Independent literal vectors: C3 A9 / F0 9F 98 80 / 22 / 5C / 41 / 20 / 7A / 2B.
    // Expectations never call the scanner's encoders.
    List<String> encodedLeaks =
        List.of(
            "%C3%A9%F0%9F%98%80%22%5C%41%20%7A%2B",
            "%c3%a9%f0%9f%98%80%22%5c%41%20%7a%2b",
            "%C3%A9%F0%9F%98%80%22%5CA+z%2B",
            "%C3%A9%F0%9F%98%80%22%5CA%20z%2B",
            "c3a9f09f9880225c41207a2b",
            "C3A9F09F9880225C41207A2B",
            "é😀\\\"\\\\A z+",
            "\"é😀\\\"\\\\A z+\"",
            "\\u00e9\\ud83d\\ude00\\u0022\\u005c\\u0041\\u0020\\u007a\\u002b",
            "\\u00E9\\uD83D\\uDE00\\u0022\\u005C\\u0041\\u0020\\u007A\\u002B");
    for (String leak : encodedLeaks) {
      assertThatThrownBy(
              () ->
                  assertJoinedSinkAbsent(
                      Map.of("synthetic-control", leak.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)), value))
          .isInstanceOf(AssertionError.class);
    }
    assertJoinedSinkAbsent(
        Map.of(
            "synthetic-unrelated",
            "%C3%A8%F0%9F%98%80%22%5CA%20z%2B".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)),
        value);
    // Extend this existing independent support case, never add another Core/browser case.
    List<byte[]> byteLeaks = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    for (String leak : encodedLeaks) {
      byteLeaks.add(leak.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }
    byteLeaks.add(HexFormat.of().parseHex("c3a9f09f9880225c41207a2b")); // Literal UTF8.
    byteLeaks.add(HexFormat.of().parseHex("e9003dd800de22005c00410020007a002b00")); // UTF16LE.
    byteLeaks.add(HexFormat.of().parseHex("00e9d83dde000022005c00410020007a002b")); // UTF16BE.
    byteLeaks.add("w6nwn5iAIlxBIHor".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)); // Independent base64.
    // encodeURIComponent and form-space replacement coincide for this independent input.
    byteLeaks.add("%C3%A9%F0%9F%98%80%22%5CA%20z%2B".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    assertThat(byteLeaks.size()).isEqualTo(15);
    assertThat(canaryByteVariants(value).size()).isEqualTo(15);
    for (byte[] leak : byteLeaks) {
      for (int split = 0; split <= leak.length; split++) {
        C5CanaryStream scanner = new C5CanaryStream(List.of(value));
        try {
          byte[] padded = new byte[leak.length + 6];
          java.util.Arrays.fill(padded, (byte) '~');
          System.arraycopy(leak, 0, padded, 3, leak.length);
          scanner.write(padded, 3, split);
          scanner.write(padded, 3 + split, leak.length - split);
          scanner.closeInput();
          assertThat(scanner.encodedBytes)
              .as("every original split/offset byte consumed")
              .isEqualTo(leak.length);
          assertThat(scanner.writeCalls).isEqualTo(2);
          assertThat(scanner.hits)
              .as("independent canary detected across every boundary")
              .isPositive();
          assertThatThrownBy(() -> scanner.assertClean(true)).isInstanceOf(AssertionError.class);
        } finally {
          scanner.closeInput();
          scanner.wipe();
        }
      }
      C5CanaryStream integerScanner = new C5CanaryStream(List.of(value));
      try {
        for (byte octet : leak) {
          integerScanner.write(Byte.toUnsignedInt(octet) | 0x100);
        }
        integerScanner.closeInput();
        assertThat(integerScanner.encodedBytes).isEqualTo(leak.length);
        assertThat(integerScanner.writeCalls).isEqualTo(leak.length);
        assertThat(integerScanner.hits).as("int writes retain low-eight-bit matching").isPositive();
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> integerScanner.assertClean(true))
            .isInstanceOf(AssertionError.class);
      } finally {
        integerScanner.closeInput();
        integerScanner.wipe();
      }
    }
    C5CanaryStream unrelated = new C5CanaryStream(List.of(value));
    try {
      byte[] bytes = "%C3%A8%F0%9F%98%80%22%5CA%20z%2B".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      for (int offset = 0; offset < bytes.length; offset += 3) {
        unrelated.write(bytes, offset, Math.min(3, bytes.length - offset));
      }
      unrelated.closeInput();
      unrelated.assertClean(true);
    } finally {
      unrelated.closeInput();
      unrelated.wipe();
    }
    C5CanaryStream overlap = new C5CanaryStream(List.of("abab"));
    try {
      byte[] prefix = "ab".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      overlap.write(prefix, 0, prefix.length);
      overlap.write('~'); // A non-start byte must clear a live partial match.
      overlap.write(prefix, 0, prefix.length);
      assertThat(overlap.hits).as("broken prefix is not a full match").isZero();
      overlap.write(prefix, 0, prefix.length);
      overlap.closeInput();
      assertThat(overlap.encodedBytes).isEqualTo(7);
      assertThat(overlap.hits).as("overlapping prefix still finds the real suffix").isPositive();
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> overlap.assertClean(true)).isInstanceOf(AssertionError.class);
    } finally {
      overlap.closeInput();
      overlap.wipe();
    }
    C5CanaryStream volume = new C5CanaryStream(List.of(value));
    try {
      byte[] chunk = new byte[65536];
      java.util.Arrays.fill(chunk, (byte) '~');
      for (int index = 0; index < 2049; index++) {
        volume.write(chunk, 0, chunk.length);
      }
      volume.closeInput();
      assertThat(volume.encodedBytes)
          .as("actual bytes exceed historical aggregate cap")
          .isEqualTo(2049L * 65536)
          .isGreaterThan(128L * 1024 * 1024);
      assertThat(volume.writeCalls).isEqualTo(2049);
      volume.assertClean(true);
    } finally {
      volume.closeInput();
      volume.wipe();
    }
    C5CanaryStream invalid = new C5CanaryStream(List.of(value));
    try {
      invalid.write(new byte[] {'~'}, 1, 1);
      invalid.write('~');
      invalid.closeInput();
      invalid.write('~');
      assertThat(invalid.encodedBytes).isEqualTo(2);
      assertThat(invalid.invalidWrites).isEqualTo(2);
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> invalid.assertClean(true)).isInstanceOf(AssertionError.class);
    } finally {
      invalid.closeInput();
      invalid.wipe();
    }
    C5CanaryStream notClosed = new C5CanaryStream(List.of(value));
    try {
      notClosed.write('~');
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> notClosed.assertClean(true)).isInstanceOf(AssertionError.class);
      notClosed.closeInput();
      notClosed.assertClean(true);
    } finally {
      notClosed.closeInput();
      notClosed.wipe();
    }
    C5CanaryStream overflow = new C5CanaryStream(List.of(value));
    try {
      overflow.encodedBytes = Long.MAX_VALUE;
      overflow.write('~');
      overflow.closeInput();
      assertThat(overflow.encodedBytes).isEqualTo(Long.MAX_VALUE);
      assertThat(overflow.failed).isTrue();
      assertThatThrownBy(() -> overflow.assertClean(true)).isInstanceOf(AssertionError.class);
    } finally {
      overflow.closeInput();
      overflow.wipe();
    }
  }

  /** Observations only; neither the PID header nor this handle grants execution authority. */
  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(37)
  void actualOrdinaryOwnedCompiledChildLossWhileSolePasswordPrimitivePending() throws Exception {
    String value = "C11-MIDFILL-" + UUID.randomUUID() + "-+/界";
    C5Capture capture = new C5Capture(List.of(value), false);
    c5Capture = capture;
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      capture.writeProfileValues();
      p3Case(() -> p9MidfillChildCut(value, capture));
    } catch (Exception | Error failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      if (!cleanupUnconfirmed) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p3Scan(List.of(value)));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::writeNumericEvidence);
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::close);
        if (!cleanupUnconfirmed) {
          c5Capture = null;
        }
      } else {
        failures.add(new IllegalStateException("Midfill terminated producer drain unconfirmed"));
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        if (primary != null) {
          failures.forEach(primary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private void p9MidfillChildCut(String value, C5Capture capture) throws Exception {
    assertThat(p4Revocation).isNull();
    assertThat(p6ChildCut).isNull();
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant("CSI-456", "fixture-other");
    UUID test = p3CreateSource("CSI-456", "fixture-other", true);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of("CSI-456", "fixture-other", test);
    p3ExpectedPassword = value;
    p3Seed(scope, value);
    P3Publication publication = p4PositiveNativeControl(test);
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    P4RevocationState state =
        new P4RevocationState(P4_REVOCATION_CASE.incrementAndGet(), 2, test, jdbc);
    state.c6Kind =
        2; // Reuse the original ordinary8s-stall/pending-probe fixture; no public cancel.
    state.c6Capture = capture;
    state.c6Phase = p4StartObserverPhase(1);
    p4Revocation = state;
    p4WriteRevocationMode(state);
    state.execution = p3Execute(test);
    p4WriteRevocationMode(state);
    ObjectNode mode =
        (ObjectNode) C.parse(Files.readString(TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json")));
    mode.put("childCut", true); // Existing actual fork/Ready observation; no new transport field.
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"), mode.toString(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    P6ChildCut cut = new P6ChildCut(state.execution, scope, observedBinding(state.execution), true);
    p6ChildCut = cut;
    var retained = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, byte[]>();
    Throwable primary = null;
    boolean terminated = false;
    try {
      p3AssertBinding(state.execution, scope, publication);
      capture.watchExecution(state.execution, p3Cookie);
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(cut.positive.await(25, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      assertThat(cut.failed).isFalse();
      assertThat(state.c6Pending.await(15, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      synchronized (state) {
        assertThat(state.failed).isFalse();
        assertThat(state.c6FillEntries).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(state.c6PrimitiveAt).isPositive();
        assertThat(state.active).containsOnlyKeys(state.inFlightOrdinal);
        assertThat(state.active.get(state.inFlightOrdinal)).isEqualTo(1);
      }
      var liveOwner = p6Owner(state.execution);
      assertThat("RUNNING".equals(liveOwner.get("state"))).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              String.valueOf(liveOwner.get("attempt_id"))
                  .equals(cut.report.path("attemptId").asText()))
