          .isTrue();
      assertThat(
              String.valueOf(liveOwner.get("admission_id"))
                  .equals(cut.report.path("admissionId").asText()))
          .isTrue();
      JsonNode admitted = C.parse((String) liveOwner.get("receipt"));
      assertThat(admitted.path("admissionId").equals(cut.report.path("admissionId"))).isTrue();
      var releases =
          jdbc.queryForList(
              "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=? AND row_kind='RELEASE'"
                  + " AND decision_stage='DISPATCH' AND outcome='RESOLVED' AND secret_id='PASS'",
              state.execution);
      assertThat(releases.size()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(p4AuditOwnerIdentity(state, releases.getFirst())).isTrue();
      p6VerifyChild(cut);
      cut.childTree =
          java.util.stream.Stream.concat(
                  java.util.stream.Stream.of(cut.child), cut.child.descendants())
              .map(GuidedPublicationReplayIT::p6SnapshotPid)
              .toList();
      assertThat(cut.childTree.size()).isBetween(2, 128);
      cut.signalOrder =
          cut.childTree.stream()
              .sorted(
                  java.util.Comparator.comparingInt((P6OwnedPid member) -> p6Depth(member, cut))
                      .reversed())
              .toList();
      // Retain actual owned file bytes before parent cleanup can delete them. No SSE wait occurs
      // here.
      p9RetainOwnedFiles(retained);
      assertThat(retained.isEmpty()).isFalse();
      assertJoinedSinkAbsent(retained, value);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - reads)
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=?"
                      + " AND row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome='USED'",
                  Integer.class,
                  state.execution))
          .isZero();
      p8WritePendingProbe(state, 2);
      assertThat(state.c6CutPending.await(2, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      synchronized (state) {
        assertThat(state.failed).isFalse();
        assertThat(state.active).containsOnlyKeys(state.inFlightOrdinal);
        assertThat(state.active.get(state.inFlightOrdinal)).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(state.c6NativeClose || state.reasonProved || state.restored || state.closed)
            .isFalse();
        state.c6CutAt = System.nanoTime();
        assertThat(state.c6CutAt - state.c6PendingAt)
            .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofMillis(250).toNanos());
      }
      p6SameProcess(cut.child, cut.childStarted);
      cut.childCutStarted = true;
      p6Signal(
          "-KILL",
          "-" + cut.child.pid()); // First fatal signal is the verified compiled-fork group.
      assertThat(System.nanoTime() - state.c6PendingAt)
          .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofMillis(250).toNanos());
      assertThat(cut.parent.isAlive()).isTrue();
      JsonNode outcome = p3AwaitExecution(state.execution, "ERROR");
      JsonNode finalReport = p3RunnerReport(state.execution);
      assertThat(outcome.path("errorCode").isNull()).isTrue();
      assertThat(finalReport.path("outcome").path("errorCode").asText()).isEqualTo("RUN_ERROR");
      assertThat(finalReport.path("secretUse").has("PASS")).isFalse();
      // The original parent snapshot contains observed steps only; no child result invents future
      // ones.
      assertThat(finalReport.path("steps").isArray()).isTrue();
      for (JsonNode step : finalReport.path("steps")) {
        assertThat(step.path("stepIndex").asInt(-1)).isBetween(0, 1);
      }
      // p3RunnerReport above is the authentic matching FINAL; no unscoped callback flag is
      // authority.
      p3AssertBinding(state.execution, scope, publication);
      p3CleanupOrdinary(
          state.execution, p3Cookie, System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos());
      p9DrainRetainedChild(cut);
      terminated = true;
      p8AssertUnknownUsageLineage(state);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - reads)
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).isEmpty();
      ObjectNode profileWitness = p9PostClearProfileWitness(state);
      p3Scan(List.of(value));
      assertJoinedSinkAbsent(retained, value);
      // Whatever original manifest/evidence disposition exists is scanned, never deleted or
      // fabricated.
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "c11-midfill-process",
          C.object()
              .put("actualReadyStarted", true)
              .put("soleCdpPending", true)
              .put("compiledChildTerminated", true)
              .put("capturedDescendantsGone", true)
              .put("parentAlive", cut.parent.isAlive())
              .put("authenticScopedFinalObserved", true)
              .put("knownUsedBeforeCut", false)
              .put("physicalReadDelta", 1)
              .put("unknownUsageCount", 1)
              .put("normalSdkCloseObserved", state.c6NativeClose)
              .put("normalDescriptorRestoreObserved", state.restored)
              .put("normalChildSealObserved", false)
              .put("terminatedProducerDrain", true)
              .put("retainedFiles", retained.size())
              .put("retainedFilesPreCutOnly", true)
              .put(
                  "postFaultScratchProfileObserved",
                  profileWitness.path("postFaultScratchProfileObserved").booleanValue())
              .put(
                  "postFaultProfileSampleOrdinal",
                  profileWitness.path("profileSampleOrdinal").asInt())
              .put("originalQuiesceObserved", profileWitness.path("quiesceObserved").booleanValue())
              .put("originalQuiescentTrue", profileWitness.path("quiescentTrue").booleanValue())
              .put("fullFaultSinkClosureClaimed", false));
    } catch (Exception | Error failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      if (cut.childCutStarted) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p9DrainRetainedChild(cut));
        // Preserve primary errors; this genuine authority cleanup is not the fault outcome oracle.
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () ->
                p3CleanupOrdinary(
                    state.execution,
                    p3Cookie,
                    System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos()));
        if (failures.isEmpty()) {
          terminated = true;
          p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p3Scan(List.of(value)));
          p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> assertJoinedSinkAbsent(retained, value));
          // A dead producer cannot consume its fixture controls. Retire only this actual terminated
          // scope.
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              failures, () -> Files.deleteIfExists(TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer.json")));
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              failures,
              () ->
                  Files.writeString(
                      TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"),
                      "{\"active\":false}",
                      StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
          if (failures.isEmpty()) {
            p4Revocation = null;
            p6ChildCut = null;
          }
        }
        if (!terminated || !failures.isEmpty()) {
          cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
        }
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        if (primary != null) {
          failures.forEach(primary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static ObjectNode p9PostClearProfileWitness(P4RevocationState state) throws Exception {
    Path file = TEMP.resolve("p3-c11-midfill-process.json");
    assertThat(
            Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
                && !Files.isSymbolicLink(file))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.size(file)).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1024);
    assertThat(Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file))
        .isEqualTo(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"));
    ObjectNode witness = (ObjectNode) C.parse(Files.readString(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    requireNumericBudgetEvidence(witness);
    assertThat(witness.size()).isEqualTo(9);
    assertThat(
            witness.path("case").asInt(-1) == state.ordinal
                && witness.path("epoch").asInt(-1) == state.ordinal)
        .isTrue();
    for (String field :
        List.of(
            "postFaultScratchProfileObserved",
            "quiesceObserved",
            "quiescentTrue",
            "readyAllocationMatched",
            "profileInodeMatched",
            "observerFailed")) {
      assertThat(witness.path(field).isBoolean()).isTrue();
    }
    assertThat(witness.path("observerFailed").booleanValue()).isFalse();
    assertThat(
            witness.path("quiesceObserved").booleanValue()
                && witness.path("quiescentTrue").booleanValue()
                && witness.path("readyAllocationMatched").booleanValue()
                && witness.path("profileInodeMatched").booleanValue()
                && witness.path("postFaultScratchProfileObserved").booleanValue())
        .isTrue();
    int ordinal = p4Number(witness, "profileSampleOrdinal");
    List<String> rows = Files.readAllLines(TEMP.resolve("c5-profile-counts.jsonl"));
    assertThat(ordinal).isBetween(1, rows.size());
    JsonNode sample = C.parse(rows.get(ordinal - 1));
    assertThat(sample.path("failed").isBoolean() && !sample.path("failed").booleanValue()).isTrue();
    assertThat(sample.path("hits").asLong(-1)).isZero();
    assertThat(sample.path("files").asLong(-1)).isPositive();
    return witness;
  }

  private static void p9DrainRetainedChild(P6ChildCut cut) throws Exception {
    var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
    for (P6OwnedPid member : cut.signalOrder) {
      p3AttemptTeardown(
          failures,
          () -> {
            if (!member.handle().isAlive()) {
              return;
            }
            p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
            P6ProcessStatus actual = p6Status(member.handle());
            if ("X".equals(actual.state()) || actual.state().startsWith("Z")) {
              return;
            }
            assertThat(actual.group() == member.group()).isTrue();
            p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
            p6Signal("-KILL", Long.toString(member.handle().pid()));
          });
    }
    p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p6AwaitProcesses(cut.childTree, true));
    throwTeardownFailures(failures);
  }

  private static void p9RetainOwnedFiles(Map<String, byte[]> retained) throws Exception {
    long bytes = 0;
    for (String directory : List.of("journal", "scratch", "objects")) {
      Path root = TEMP.resolve(directory);
      if (!Files.isDirectory(root, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
        continue;
      }
      assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(root)).isFalse();
      try (var paths = Files.walk(root)) {
        for (Path file :
            paths
                .filter(path -> Files.isRegularFile(path, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS))
                .toList()) {
          assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(file)).isFalse();
          assertThat(file.toRealPath().startsWith(root.toRealPath())).isTrue();
          long size = Files.size(file);
          assertThat(size).isLessThanOrEqualTo(67108864);
          bytes += size;
          assertThat(bytes).isLessThanOrEqualTo(268435456);
          retained.put("retained-" + TEMP.relativize(file), Files.readAllBytes(file));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static volatile P6ChildCut p6ChildCut;

  private static final class P6ChildCut {
    final UUID execution;
    final VaultScope scope;
    final JsonNode binding;
    final Process parent;
    final Instant parentStarted;
    final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch positive = new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
    final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch signInEntered =
        new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
    final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch releaseSignIn =
        new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
    final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch signInExited =
        new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
    final AtomicInteger signInCalls = new AtomicInteger();
    volatile boolean failed, finalSeen;
    volatile long childPid;
    volatile JsonNode ready, report;
    ProcessHandle child;
    Instant childStarted;
    List<P6OwnedPid> childTree = List.of(), signalOrder = List.of();
    boolean stopIssued, childCutStarted, parentKilled, recovered, restarted;
    boolean midfill;

    P6ChildCut(UUID execution, VaultScope scope, JsonNode binding) {
      this.execution = execution;
      this.scope = scope;
      this.binding = binding.deepCopy();
      parent = ordinaryWorker;
      parentStarted = parent.info().startInstant().orElseThrow();
    }

    P6ChildCut(UUID execution, VaultScope scope, JsonNode binding, boolean midfill) {
      this(execution, scope, binding);
      this.midfill = midfill;
    }

    synchronized void capture(
        jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest request, byte[] bytes, int status) {
      if (midfill) {
        captureMidfill(request, bytes, status);
        return;
      }
      try {
        if (bytes.length > 262144) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Child cut callback bound");
        }
        JsonNode body = C.parse(new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        if ("FINAL".equals(body.path("kind").asText())) {
          finalSeen = true;
        }
        if (status < 200
            || status >= 300
            || !"PROGRESS".equals(body.path("kind").asText())
            || !"USED".equals(body.path("secretUse").path("PASS").asText())) {
          return;
        }
        if (!("Bearer " + CALLBACK).equals(request.getHeader("Authorization"))
            || !execution.toString().equals(body.path("executionId").asText())
            || !binding.path("attemptId").equals(body.path("attemptId"))
            || !"observed-fixture".equals(body.path("runnerInstanceId").asText())
            || "1".equals(request.getHeader("X-Fixture-Child-Observer-Failed"))) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Child cut accepted callback identity");
        }
        String pid = request.getHeader("X-Fixture-Child-Pid");
        String encoded = request.getHeader("X-Fixture-Child-Ready");
        if (pid == null
            || !pid.matches("[1-9][0-9]{0,12}")
            || encoded == null
            || encoded.length() > 4096) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Child cut actual Ready observation missing");
        }
        JsonNode actualReady =
            C.parse(new String(Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(encoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        if (!"ready".equals(actualReady.path("kind").asText())
            || actualReady.path("protocolVersion").asInt() != 3
            || !body.path("executionId").equals(actualReady.path("runId"))
            || !body.path("attemptId").equals(actualReady.path("attemptId"))
            || !body.path("admissionId").equals(actualReady.path("admissionId"))
            || !body.path("browserStartedAt").equals(actualReady.path("browserStartedAt"))
            || !GuidedContracts.hash(binding.path("requiredProfile"))
                .equals(actualReady.path("profileHash").asText())
            || !binding.path("requiredBuild").equals(actualReady.path("build"))) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Child cut actual Ready binding differs");
        }
        if (report == null) {
          childPid = Long.parseLong(pid);
          ready = actualReady.deepCopy();
          report = body.deepCopy();
          positive.countDown();
        } else if (childPid != Long.parseLong(pid) || !ready.equals(actualReady)) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Child cut actual Ready identity changed");
        }
      } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
        failed = true;
        positive.countDown();
      }
    }

    private void captureMidfill(
        jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest request, byte[] bytes, int status) {
      try {
        if (bytes.length > 262144) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Midfill callback bound");
        }
        JsonNode body = C.parse(new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        if (status < 200 || status >= 300 || !"STARTED".equals(body.path("kind").asText())) {
          return;
        }
        if (!("Bearer " + CALLBACK).equals(request.getHeader("Authorization"))
            || !execution.toString().equals(body.path("executionId").asText())
            || !binding.path("attemptId").equals(body.path("attemptId"))
            || !"observed-fixture".equals(body.path("runnerInstanceId").asText())
            || "1".equals(request.getHeader("X-Fixture-Child-Observer-Failed"))) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Midfill authenticated callback identity");
        }
        String pid = request.getHeader("X-Fixture-Child-Pid");
        String encoded = request.getHeader("X-Fixture-Child-Ready");
        if (pid == null
            || !pid.matches("[1-9][0-9]{0,12}")
            || encoded == null
            || encoded.length() > 4096) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Midfill actual Ready observation missing");
        }
        JsonNode observed =
            C.parse(new String(Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(encoded), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        if (!"ready".equals(observed.path("kind").asText())
            || observed.path("protocolVersion").asInt() != 3
            || !body.path("executionId").equals(observed.path("runId"))
            || !body.path("attemptId").equals(observed.path("attemptId"))
            || !body.path("admissionId").equals(observed.path("admissionId"))
            || !body.path("browserStartedAt").equals(observed.path("browserStartedAt"))
            || !GuidedContracts.hash(binding.path("requiredProfile"))
                .equals(observed.path("profileHash").asText())
            || !binding.path("requiredBuild").equals(observed.path("build"))) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Midfill actual Ready binding differs");
        }
        if (report == null) {
          childPid = Long.parseLong(pid);
          ready = observed.deepCopy();
          report = body.deepCopy();
          positive.countDown();
        } else if (childPid != Long.parseLong(pid) || !ready.equals(observed)) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Midfill Ready identity changed");
        }
      } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
        failed = true;
        positive.countDown();
      }
    }
  }

  private record P6OwnedPid(ProcessHandle handle, Instant started, long parent, long group) {}

  private record P6ProcessStatus(long parent, long group, String state) {}

  private void p6CommittedChildCut() throws Exception {
    assertThat(Files.isExecutable(Path.of("/bin/kill"))).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.isExecutable(Path.of("/bin/ps"))).isTrue();
    assertThat(
            ownedApplicationContext
                .getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.StudioExecutionProperties.class)
                .runDeadlineOrDefault()
                .toMillis())
        .as("unchanged production deadline within accepted case budget")
        .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(600).toMillis());
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant(primaryCsi, "fixture");
    UUID test = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", true);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", test);
    String value = p3ExpectedPassword;
    p3Seed(scope, value);
    String guided = p3Start(test, "fixture");
    awaitFinalizing(guided);
    P3Publication publication = p3Publish(guided);
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
    p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, publication);
    var cut = new P6ChildCut(execution, scope, observedBinding(execution));
    p6ChildCut = cut;
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"),
        C.object()
            .put("active", false)
            .put("childCut", true)
            .put("executionId", execution.toString())
            .toString(),
        StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(cut.positive.await(30, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      assertThat(cut.failed).as("actual Ready/accepted progress observation valid").isFalse();
      assertThat(cut.signInEntered.await(10, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      assertThat(cut.signInCalls.get()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.size() == 1 && P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isTrue();
      var owner = p6Owner(execution);
      JsonNode receipt = C.parse((String) owner.get("receipt"));
      assertThat("RUNNING".equals(owner.get("state"))).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              String.valueOf(owner.get("attempt_id")).equals(cut.report.path("attemptId").asText()))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(
              String.valueOf(owner.get("admission_id"))
                  .equals(cut.report.path("admissionId").asText()))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(receipt.path("admissionId").equals(cut.report.path("admissionId"))).isTrue();
      Instant originalDeadline = ((java.sql.Timestamp) owner.get("run_deadline_at")).toInstant();
      assertThat(originalDeadline.equals(Instant.parse(receipt.path("runDeadlineAt").asText())))
          .as("original authoritative admission deadline retained")
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(originalDeadline.isAfter(Instant.now())).isTrue();
      assertThat(((Number) owner.get("last_runner_sequence")).longValue())
          .isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(cut.report.path("runnerSequence").asLong());
      JsonNode artifact = C.parse(publication.artifact());
      int consumer = -1;
      for (int index = 0; index < artifact.path("steps").size(); index++) {
        if (TWO.equals(artifact.path("steps").get(index).path("stepId").asText())) {
          assertThat(consumer).isEqualTo(-1);
          consumer = index;
        }
      }
      boolean pass = false;
      for (JsonNode step : cut.report.path("steps")) {
        pass |=
            step.path("stepIndex").asInt(-1) == consumer
                && "PASS".equals(step.path("status").asText());
      }
      assertThat(consumer >= 0 && pass).as("actual secret source step completed PASS").isTrue();
      Map<String, Object> used = p6Usage(execution);
      Map<String, Object> release =
          jdbc.queryForMap(
              "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE id=? AND release_occurred_at=?",
              used.get("release_id"),
              used.get("release_occurred_at"));
      assertThat("USED".equals(used.get("usage_outcome"))).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              "RELEASE".equals(release.get("row_kind"))
                  && "DISPATCH".equals(release.get("decision_stage"))
                  && "RESOLVED".equals(release.get("outcome")))
          .isTrue();
      p4CompareAuditScope(release, used);
      p3AssertAuditScope(null, execution, scope);
      assertThat(String.valueOf(owner.get("attempt_id")).equals(release.get("attempt_id")))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(execution.equals(release.get("execution_id"))).isTrue();
      p6VerifyChild(cut);
      p6AssertNoFinalManifest(cut);
      p6SameProcess(cut.parent.toHandle(), cut.parentStarted);
      cut.stopIssued = true;
      p6Signal("-STOP", Long.toString(cut.parent.pid()));
      long stoppedBy = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(5).toNanos();
      while (System.nanoTime() < stoppedBy
          && !p6Status(cut.parent.toHandle()).state().startsWith("T")) {
        Thread.sleep(10);
      }
      p6SameProcess(cut.parent.toHandle(), cut.parentStarted);
      assertThat(p6Status(cut.parent.toHandle()).state().startsWith("T"))
          .as("own parent actually STOPPED before child cut")
          .isTrue();
      p6SameProcess(cut.child, cut.childStarted);
      assertThat(p6Status(cut.child).group() == cut.child.pid()).isTrue();
      cut.childTree =
          java.util.stream.Stream.concat(
                  java.util.stream.Stream.of(cut.child), cut.child.descendants())
              .map(GuidedPublicationReplayIT::p6SnapshotPid)
              .toList();
      assertThat(cut.childTree.size()).isGreaterThan(1);
      p6KillOwnedTree(cut);
      p6AwaitProcesses(cut.childTree, false);
      // Z acknowledges execution termination only. The stopped owner cannot yet reap its child.
      p6SameProcess(cut.parent.toHandle(), cut.parentStarted);
      assertThat(p6Status(cut.parent.toHandle()).state().startsWith("T")).isTrue();
      cut.parentKilled = true;
      cut.parent.destroyForcibly();
      assertThat(cut.parent.waitFor(10, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      p6AwaitProcesses(cut.childTree, true);
      assertThat(cut.parent.isAlive()).isFalse();
      cut.releaseSignIn.countDown();
      assertThat(cut.signInExited.await(10, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      assertThat(cut.failed).isFalse();
      p6AssertNoFinalManifest(cut);
      assertThat("RUNNING".equals(p6Owner(execution).get("state"))).isTrue();
      var reaper =
          ownedApplicationContext.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.recovery.RunningReaper.class);
      reaper.reapOnce();
      assertThat("RUNNING".equals(p6Owner(execution).get("state"))).isTrue();
      p6WaitOriginalDeadline(cut);
      p6AssertNoFinalManifest(cut);
      reaper.reapOnce();
      var terminal = p6Owner(execution);
      assertThat(
              "DONE".equals(terminal.get("state"))
                  && "ERROR".equals(terminal.get("status"))
                  && "STALE".equals(terminal.get("error_code")))
          .isTrue();
      cut.recovered = true;
      assertThat(used.equals(p6Usage(execution)))
          .as("positive USED not relabeled after crash")
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(
              release.equals(
                  jdbc.queryForMap(
                      "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE id=? AND release_occurred_at=?",
                      release.get("id"),
                      release.get("release_occurred_at"))))
          .isTrue();
      reaper.reapOnce();
      assertThat(terminal.equals(p6Owner(execution))).isTrue();
      assertThat(used.equals(p6Usage(execution))).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - reads)
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
      assertThat(cut.signInCalls.get()).isEqualTo(1);
      p3Scan(List.of(value));
      startOrdinaryWorker(true);
      cut.restarted = true;
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "committed-child-parent-crash",
          C.object()
              .put("acceptedPositiveProgress", true)
              .put("actualReadyChildBound", true)
              .put("childExecutionTerminated", true)
              .put("parentKilled", true)
              .put("allOwnedPidsGone", true)
              .put("actualFinalObserved", false)
              .put("actualManifestObserved", false)
              .put("positiveUsageUnchanged", true)
              .put("originalDeadlineUsed", true)
              .put("valueReads", 1)
              .put("modelCalls", 0));
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      cut.releaseSignIn.countDown();
      if (cut.stopIssued && cut.parent.isAlive()) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              p6SameProcess(cut.parent.toHandle(), cut.parentStarted);
              p6Signal("-CONT", Long.toString(cut.parent.pid()));
            });
      }
      if (primary != null && cut.childCutStarted) {
        // Captured descendants remain ours after reparenting; live PPID is not their authority.
        // Verify original start identity and unchanged group before every independent cleanup
        // signal.
        for (P6OwnedPid member : cut.signalOrder) {
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              failures,
              () -> {
                if (!member.handle().isAlive()) {
                  return;
                }
                p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
                P6ProcessStatus status = p6Status(member.handle());
                if ("X".equals(status.state()) || status.state().startsWith("Z")) {
                  return; // The later genuine-gone check still owns closure.
                }
                assertThat(status.group() == member.group()).isTrue();
                p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
                p6Signal(
                    "-KILL",
                    member.group() == member.handle().pid()
                        ? "-" + member.handle().pid()
                        : Long.toString(member.handle().pid()));
              });
        }
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p6AwaitProcesses(cut.childTree, true));
      }
      if (cut.parentKilled) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> stopOwnedProcessTree(cut.parent));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p6AwaitProcesses(cut.childTree, true));
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              assertThat(cut.signInExited.await(10, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS))
                  .isTrue();
            });
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              if (!cut.recovered) {
                p6WaitOriginalDeadline(cut);
                ownedApplicationContext
                    .getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.recovery.RunningReaper.class)
                    .reapOnce();
                assertThat("DONE".equals(p6Owner(execution).get("state"))).isTrue();
                cut.recovered = true;
              }
            });
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p3Scan(List.of(value)));
        if (failures.isEmpty() && cut.recovered && !cut.restarted) {
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              failures,
              () -> {
                startOrdinaryWorker(true);
                cut.restarted = true;
              });
        }
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
        if (primary != null) {
          failures.forEach(primary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          var failure = new IllegalStateException("Owned child crash cleanup unconfirmed");
          failures.forEach(failure::addSuppressed);
          throw failure;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private Map<String, Object> p6Owner(UUID execution) {
    return jdbc.queryForMap(
        "SELECT e.status,e.error_code,e.snapshot_revision,e.step_results::text AS step_results,"
            + "e.observed_admission_receipt::text AS receipt,l.state,l.attempt_id,l.admission_id,"
            + "l.last_runner_sequence,l.run_deadline_at FROM studio_dispatch_ledger l"
            + " JOIN ui_test_executions e ON e.id=l.execution_id"
            + " AND e.started_at=l.execution_accepted_at WHERE l.execution_id=?",
        execution);
  }

  private Map<String, Object> p6Usage(UUID execution) {
    var rows =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=? AND secret_id='PASS'"
                + " AND row_kind='USAGE'",
            execution);
    assertThat(rows.size()).as("one actual terminal summary for this released secret").isEqualTo(1);
    return java.util.Collections.unmodifiableMap(new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>(rows.getFirst()));
  }

  private void p6AssertNoFinalManifest(P6ChildCut cut) {
    assertThat(cut.finalSeen || P3_FINAL_REPORTS.containsKey(cut.execution)).isFalse();
    var store =
        ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
            com.citi.put.checks.studio.recovery.ResultManifestStore.class);
    assertThat(store.isAvailable()).as("real local manifest adapter available").isTrue();
    var identity =
        new com.citi.put.checks.studio.recovery.ResultManifestStore.ExecutionIdentity(
            cut.scope.csiId(),
            UUID.fromString(cut.scope.testId()),
            cut.execution,
            UUID.fromString(cut.binding.path("attemptId").asText()));
    assertThat(
            store
                .read(com.citi.put.checks.studio.recovery.ManifestKeys.manifestKey(identity))
                .isEmpty())
        .as("no authentic manifest was produced by the physically cut parent")
        .isTrue();
  }

  private void p6WaitOriginalDeadline(P6ChildCut cut) throws Exception {
    Instant deadline =
        ((java.sql.Timestamp) p6Owner(cut.execution).get("run_deadline_at")).toInstant();
    long remaining = Duration.between(Instant.now(), deadline).toMillis();
    assertThat(remaining)
        .as("original accepted run deadline within case budget")
        .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(620).toMillis());
    while (Instant.now().isBefore(deadline)) {
      Thread.sleep(
          Math.max(1, Math.min(1000, Duration.between(Instant.now(), deadline).toMillis())));
    }
  }

  private static P6OwnedPid p6SnapshotPid(ProcessHandle handle) {
    Instant started = handle.info().startInstant().orElseThrow();
    p6SameProcess(handle, started);
    P6ProcessStatus status = p6Status(handle);
    p6SameProcess(handle, started);
    return new P6OwnedPid(handle, started, status.parent(), status.group());
  }

  private static int p6Depth(P6OwnedPid member, P6ChildCut cut) {
    var byPid =
        cut.childTree.stream()
            .collect(
                java.util.stream.Collectors.toMap(
                    pid -> pid.handle().pid(), java.util.function.Function.identity()));
    int depth = 0;
    P6OwnedPid cursor = member;
    while (cursor.handle().pid() != cut.child.pid()) {
      assertThat(++depth).as("captured ancestry bounded").isLessThanOrEqualTo(128);
      cursor = byPid.get(cursor.parent());
      assertThat(cursor != null)
          .as("each captured process belongs to real child ancestry")
          .isTrue();
    }
    return depth;
  }

  private static void p6KillOwnedTree(P6ChildCut cut) throws Exception {
    // Java must retain start identities. The JS helper cannot consume them while its owner is
    // STOPPED.
    // Mirror run/tree.ts deepest-first enumeration, including Playwright's separate browser groups.
    assertThat(cut.childTree.size()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(128);
    var ordered =
        cut.childTree.stream()
            .sorted(
                java.util.Comparator.comparingInt((P6OwnedPid member) -> p6Depth(member, cut))
                    .reversed())
            .toList();
    cut.signalOrder = ordered;
    for (P6OwnedPid member : ordered) {
      if (!member.handle().isAlive()) {
        continue;
      }
      p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
      P6ProcessStatus status = p6Status(member.handle());
      if ("X".equals(status.state()) || status.state().startsWith("Z")) {
        continue; // Termination is intermediate; the later genuine-gone check still owns drainage.
      }
      assertThat(status.parent() == member.parent() && status.group() == member.group()).isTrue();
      p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
      cut.childCutStarted = true; // Set before the first actual tree signal attempt can fail.
      p6Signal(
          "-KILL",
          member.group() == member.handle().pid()
              ? "-" + member.handle().pid()
              : Long.toString(member.handle().pid()));
    }
  }

  private static void p6SameProcess(ProcessHandle handle, Instant started) {
    assertThat(handle.isAlive() && handle.info().startInstant().map(started::equals).orElse(false))
        .as("verified owned process identity retained")
        .isTrue();
  }

  private static void p6VerifyChild(P6ChildCut cut) {
    p6SameProcess(cut.parent.toHandle(), cut.parentStarted);
    cut.child = ProcessHandle.of(cut.childPid).orElseThrow();
    cut.childStarted = cut.child.info().startInstant().orElseThrow();
    assertThat(cut.parent.descendants().anyMatch(handle -> handle.pid() == cut.childPid)).isTrue();
    assertThat(
            cut.child
                .info()
                .commandLine()
                .map(command -> command.contains(TEMP.resolve("recorded-child.mjs").toString()))
                .orElse(false))
        .as("actual compiled recorded child module")
        .isTrue();
    P6ProcessStatus status = p6Status(cut.child);
    assertThat(status.parent() == cut.parent.pid() && status.group() == cut.childPid).isTrue();
  }

  private static P6ProcessStatus p6Status(ProcessHandle handle) {
    Process ps;
    try {
      ps =
          new ProcessBuilder(
                  "/bin/ps", "-p", Long.toString(handle.pid()), "-o", "ppid=,pgid=,stat=")
              .redirectErrorStream(true)
              .start();
    } catch (IOException failure) {
      throw new IllegalStateException("Owned process OS-state probe unavailable", failure);
    }
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      assertThat(ps.waitFor(5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      if (ps.exitValue() != 0 && !handle.isAlive()) {
        return new P6ProcessStatus(-1, -1, "X");
      }
      assertThat(ps.exitValue()).isZero();
      String[] parts =
          new String(ps.getInputStream().readNBytes(1024), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
              .trim()
              .split("\\s+");
      assertThat(parts.length).isEqualTo(3);
      return new P6ProcessStatus(Long.parseLong(parts[0]), Long.parseLong(parts[1]), parts[2]);
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      primary = failure;
      if (failure instanceof InterruptedException) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
      }
      throw new IllegalStateException("Owned process OS-state observation unavailable", failure);
    } finally {
      try {
        p6CloseCommand(ps);
      } catch (AssertionError cleanup) {
        if (primary != null) {
          primary.addSuppressed(cleanup);
        } else {
          throw cleanup;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static void p6Signal(String signal, String ownedTarget) throws Exception {
    assertThat(List.of("-STOP", "-CONT", "-KILL", "-TERM").contains(signal)).isTrue();
    assertThat(ownedTarget.matches("-?[1-9][0-9]{0,12}")).isTrue();
    Process command =
        new ProcessBuilder("/bin/kill", signal, "--", ownedTarget)
            .redirectErrorStream(true)
            .start();
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      assertThat(command.waitFor(5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      assertThat(command.exitValue()).as("actual signal delivered to verified owner").isZero();
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      primary = failure;
      if (failure instanceof InterruptedException) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
      }
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      try {
        p6CloseCommand(command);
      } catch (AssertionError cleanup) {
        if (primary != null) {
          primary.addSuppressed(cleanup);
        } else {
          throw cleanup;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static void p6CloseCommand(Process command) {
    boolean interrupted = Thread.interrupted();
    try {
      if (command.isAlive()) {
        command.destroyForcibly();
      }
      long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(5).toNanos();
      while (command.isAlive() && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
        try {
          command.waitFor(20, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException interruption) {
          interrupted = true;
        }
      }
      assertThat(command.isAlive()).as("owned OS probe/signal process genuinely joined").isFalse();
    } finally {
      if (interrupted) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
      }
    }
  }

  private static void p6AwaitProcesses(List<P6OwnedPid> owned, boolean gone) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos();
    do {
      boolean complete = true;
      for (P6OwnedPid pid : owned) {
        if (pid.handle().isAlive()) {
          var started = pid.handle().info().startInstant();
          if (!pid.handle().isAlive()) {
            continue;
          }
          assertThat(started.map(pid.started()::equals).orElse(false)).isTrue();
          if (gone) {
            complete = false;
          } else {
            String state = p6Status(pid.handle()).state();
            if (!state.startsWith("Z") && !"X".equals(state)) {
              complete = false;
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if (complete) {
        return;
      }
      Thread.sleep(20);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    throw new IllegalStateException(
        gone
            ? "Owned processes not genuinely reaped"
            : "Owned child group not execution-terminated");
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(27)
  void actualPoolDefiniteRefusalThenFreshSecretAttemptResolvesRotatedValue() throws Exception {
    p7CapturedFaultAndRetry(true);
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(28)
  void actualScopedVaultOutageThenFreshSecretAttemptResolvesRotatedValue() throws Exception {
    p7CapturedFaultAndRetry(false);
  }

  private static final java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<VaultScope> P7_OUTAGE_SCOPE =
      new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>();

  private void p7CapturedFaultAndRetry(boolean capacity) throws Exception {
    var canaries = List.of("C4-OLD-" + UUID.randomUUID(), "C4-FRESH-" + UUID.randomUUID());
    C5Capture capture = new C5Capture(canaries, false);
    c5Capture = capture;
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      capture.writeProfileValues();
      p3Case(() -> p7FaultAndRetry(capacity, canaries, capture));
      capture.c4WindowsExecuted =
          true; // Genuine whole workflow and original owner cleanup returned.
    } catch (Exception | Error failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      P7_OUTAGE_SCOPE.set(null);
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      if (!cleanupUnconfirmed) {
        // The same capture remains active until p3Case has genuinely drained its owners.
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p3Scan(canaries));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::writeNumericEvidence);
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::close);
        c5Capture = null;
      } else {
        // No timeout-as-drain: retain the original quarantine over unreaped producers.
        failures.add(new IllegalStateException("C4 actual owner closure remains unconfirmed"));
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        if (primary != null) {
          failures.forEach(primary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private void p7FaultAndRetry(boolean capacity, List<String> canaries, C5Capture capture)
      throws Exception {
    UUID blockerTest = null;
    P3Publication blockerPublication = null;
    if (capacity) {
      p3Mode = P3Mode.REGISTRATION;
      blockerTest = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", false);
      String holderLearning = p3Start(blockerTest, "fixture");
      capture.selectedGuidedSession = holderLearning;
      awaitFinalizing(holderLearning);
      blockerPublication = p3Publish(holderLearning);
    }
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant(primaryCsi, "fixture");
    UUID test = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", true);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", test);
    p3ExpectedPassword = canaries.getFirst();
    p3Seed(scope, p3ExpectedPassword);
    String learning = p3Start(test, "fixture");
    capture.selectedGuidedSession = learning;
    awaitFinalizing(learning);
    P3Publication publication = p3Publish(learning);
    p3CleanupOwnedResources();
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size(), attempted = VAULT.readCount();
    int successful =
        COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    boolean paused = false;
    Throwable workflowPrimary = null;
    try {
      UUID blocker = null;
      if (capacity) {
        Files.deleteIfExists(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-check-entered"));
        Files.writeString(
            TEMP.resolve("initial-context-state.json"), "{\"pause\":true,\"fail\":false}");
        paused = true;
        blocker = p3Execute(blockerTest);
      }
      capture.armTrace();
      UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
      p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, publication);
      capture.watchExecution(execution);
      JsonNode first = observedBinding(execution).deepCopy();
      String attempt = first.path("attemptId").asText();
      if (capacity) {
        capture.poolRefusalTarget = first.deepCopy();
        var recording =
            (com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioDispatcherFailureIT.RecordingDispatcher)
                dispatcher;
        assertThat(
                recording.sameGraph(
                    ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
                        com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioDispatchLedgerStatements.class),
                    ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
                        com.citi.put.checks.studio.UITestRevisionRepository.class),
                    ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
                        com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.RunnerDispatchClient.class),
                    ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
                        com.citi.put.checks.studio.StudioExecutionProperties.class),
                    ownedApplicationContext.getBean(ObjectMapper.class),
                    ownedApplicationContext.getBean(JdbcTemplate.class),
                    ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
                        org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate.class)))
            .as("same actual original Spring dependency instances")
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(
                ownedApplicationContext.getBean("studioDispatcher").getClass()
                    == StudioDispatcher.class)
            .as("original component remains separate; scheduled polling disabled")
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(
                ownedApplicationContext
                    .getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.StudioExecutionProperties.class)
                    .dispatcherOrDefault()
                    .enabledOrDefault())
            .isFalse();
      }
      if (capacity) {
        p3AssertBinding(
            blocker, VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", blockerTest), blockerPublication);
        assertThat(dispatcher.dispatch(blocker)).isTrue();
        long until = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(15).toNanos();
        while (!Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-check-entered"))
            && System.nanoTime() < until) {
          Thread.sleep(20);
        }
        assertThat(Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-check-entered"))).isTrue();
        assertThat("ADMITTED".equals(p6Owner(blocker).get("state"))).isTrue();
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT browser_started_at IS NULL FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?",
                    Boolean.class,
                    blocker))
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(VAULT.readCount()).as("nonsecret actual browser holder").isEqualTo(attempted);
      }
      if (!capacity) {
        assertThat(P7_OUTAGE_SCOPE.compareAndSet(null, scope)).isTrue();
      }
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(capacity ? 1 : 0);
      if (capacity) {
        var original = ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.get(execution);
        assertThat(original != null).isTrue();
        JsonNode originalRefusal = original.path("sendOutcome");
        assertThat(
                "DEFINITE_REFUSAL".equals(originalRefusal.path("kind").asText())
                    && originalRefusal.path("retryable").asBoolean()
                    && originalRefusal.path("classifiedHttpStatus").asInt() == 429)
            .isTrue();
        Files.writeString(
            TEMP.resolve("initial-context-state.json"), "{\"pause\":false,\"fail\":false}");
        paused = false;
      }
      assertThat(VAULT.readCount() - attempted).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - successful)
          .isEqualTo(capacity ? 1 : 0);
      var owner =
          jdbc.queryForMap(
              "SELECT l.state,l.attempt_id,l.admission_id,l.definite_refusal_attempt_id,"
                  + "l.zero_send_decision_attempt_id,e.observed_admission_receipt,e.browser_started_at,e.error_code"
                  + " FROM studio_dispatch_ledger l JOIN ui_test_executions e ON"
                  + " e.id=l.execution_id AND e.started_at=l.execution_accepted_at WHERE"
                  + " l.execution_id=?",
              execution);
      assertThat("QUEUED".equals(owner.get("state"))).isTrue();
      for (String field :
          List.of(
              "attempt_id",
              "admission_id",
              "observed_admission_receipt",
              "browser_started_at",
              "error_code")) {
        assertThat(owner.get(field) == null).as("actual first fault is not admitted").isTrue();
      }
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.isEmpty() && P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.isEmpty()).isTrue();
      assertThat(P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()).isZero();
      List<Map<String, Object>> oldAudit = p7AttemptAudit(execution, attempt);
      assertThat(oldAudit.size()).isEqualTo(2);
      var oldRelease =
          oldAudit.stream()
              .filter(row -> "RELEASE".equals(row.get("row_kind")))
              .findFirst()
              .orElseThrow();
      var oldUsage =
          oldAudit.stream()
              .filter(row -> "USAGE".equals(row.get("row_kind")))
              .findFirst()
              .orElseThrow();
      assertThat("REFUSED".equals(oldUsage.get("usage_outcome"))).isTrue();
      assertThat(oldRelease.get("id").equals(oldUsage.get("release_id"))).isTrue();
      p4CompareAuditScope(oldRelease, oldUsage);
      p3AssertAuditScope(null, execution, scope);
      if (capacity) {
        var actual = ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.get(execution);
        assertThat(actual != null).isTrue();
        JsonNode refusal = actual.path("sendOutcome").deepCopy();
        assertThat(
                "DEFINITE_REFUSAL".equals(refusal.path("kind").asText())
                    && refusal.path("retryable").asBoolean()
                    && refusal.path("classifiedHttpStatus").asInt() == 429)
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(attempt.equals(String.valueOf(owner.get("definite_refusal_attempt_id"))))
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(owner.get("zero_send_decision_attempt_id") == null).isTrue();
        assertThat(
                "RESOLVED".equals(oldRelease.get("outcome"))
                    && oldRelease.get("vault_version") != null)
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_observed_no_send_decisions WHERE execution_id=?"
                        + " AND attempt_id=?",
                    Integer.class,
                    execution,
                    UUID.fromString(attempt)))
            .isZero();
        p3AwaitExecution(blocker, "PASSED");
        // Only the genuine holder is cleaned; global cleanup would cancel the queued retry owner.
        p3CleanupOrdinary(blocker, p3Cookie, System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos());
        Thread.sleep(
            Duration.ofSeconds(5).toMillis()); // Original configured Retry-After pacing only.
      } else {
        assertThat(P7_OUTAGE_SCOPE.get() == null).isTrue();
        assertThat(ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.containsKey(execution)).isFalse();
        assertThat(owner.get("definite_refusal_attempt_id") == null).isTrue();
        assertThat(attempt.equals(String.valueOf(owner.get("zero_send_decision_attempt_id"))))
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(
                "VAULT_UNAVAILABLE".equals(oldRelease.get("outcome"))
                    && oldRelease.get("vault_version") == null)
            .isTrue();
        var receipt =
            jdbc.queryForMap(
                "SELECT disposition,not_entered_ids::text AS not_entered FROM"
                    + " studio_observed_no_send_decisions WHERE execution_id=? AND attempt_id=?",
                execution,
                UUID.fromString(attempt));
        assertThat("REQUEUED".equals(receipt.get("disposition"))).isTrue();
        JsonNode notEntered = C.parse((String) receipt.get("not_entered"));
        assertThat(notEntered.isArray()).isTrue();
        assertThat(notEntered.isEmpty()).isTrue();
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_observed_secret_read_entries WHERE execution_id=?"
                        + " AND attempt_id=? AND secret_id='PASS'",
                    Integer.class,
                    execution,
                    UUID.fromString(attempt)))
            .isEqualTo(1);
      }
      p3ExpectedPassword = canaries.getLast();
      p3Seed(scope, p3ExpectedPassword);
      capture.writeProfileValues();
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
      JsonNode snapshot = p3AwaitExecution(execution, "PASSED");
      JsonNode second = observedBinding(execution);
      String freshAttempt = second.path("attemptId").asText();
      assertThat(attempt.equals(freshAttempt)).isFalse();
      assertThat(
              first
                  .path("replayGrant")
                  .path("grantId")
                  .equals(second.path("replayGrant").path("grantId")))
          .isFalse();
      var before = ((ObjectNode) first).deepCopy();
      var after = ((ObjectNode) second).deepCopy();
      before.remove(List.of("attemptId", "replayGrant"));
      after.remove(List.of("attemptId", "replayGrant"));
      assertThat(before.equals(after)).isTrue();
      var grant = second.path("replayGrant");
      assertThat(grant.path("attemptId").asText().equals(freshAttempt)).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              Duration.between(
                      Instant.parse(grant.path("issuedAt").asText()),
                      Instant.parse(grant.path("expiresAt").asText()))
                  .toMillis())
          .isBetween(1L, 30000L);
      assertThat(
              ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.get(execution).path("sendOutcome").path("kind").asText())
          .isEqualTo("ACCEPTED");
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT definite_refusal_attempt_id IS NULL AND zero_send_decision_attempt_id IS"
                      + " NULL FROM studio_dispatch_ledger WHERE execution_id=?",
                  Boolean.class,
                  execution))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(VAULT.readCount() - attempted).isEqualTo(2);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - successful)
          .isEqualTo(capacity ? 2 : 1);
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.size()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(
              P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()
                  && P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().password().equals(canaries.getLast()))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()).isPositive();
      p3AssertUsed(null, execution);
      var freshAudit = p7AttemptAudit(execution, freshAttempt);
      assertThat(freshAudit.size()).isEqualTo(2);
      var freshRelease =
          freshAudit.stream()
              .filter(row -> "RELEASE".equals(row.get("row_kind")))
              .findFirst()
              .orElseThrow();
      assertThat("RESOLVED".equals(freshRelease.get("outcome"))).isTrue();
      if (capacity) {
        assertThat(oldRelease.get("vault_version").equals(freshRelease.get("vault_version")))
            .isFalse();
      }
      assertThat(oldAudit.equals(p7AttemptAudit(execution, attempt))).isTrue();
      p3AssertAuditScope(null, execution, scope);
      assertThat(p3DecodeVideo(execution, snapshot)).isPositive();
      p3CleanupOwnedResources();
      p3Scan(canaries);
      p3WriteEvidence(
          capacity ? "c4-real-429-fresh-retry" : "c4-real-outage-fresh-retry",
          C.object()
              .put("actualFirstStatus", capacity ? 429 : 0)
              .put("firstRunnerClientInvoked", capacity)
              .put("firstValueReturned", capacity)
              .put("providerAttempts", 2)
              .put("successfulValues", capacity ? 2 : 1)
              .put("freshAttemptGrant", true)
              .put("firstAuditImmutable", true)
              .put("rotatedNativeValueAccepted", true)
              .put("nativeVideoDecoded", true)
              .put("modelCallsDuringOrdinaryPasses", 0)
              .put("sameActiveC5Window", true));
    } catch (Exception | Error failure) {
      workflowPrimary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      P7_OUTAGE_SCOPE.set(null);
      if (paused) {
        try {
          Files.writeString(
              TEMP.resolve("initial-context-state.json"), "{\"pause\":false,\"fail\":false}");
        } catch (Exception | Error cleanup) {
          if (workflowPrimary != null) {
            workflowPrimary.addSuppressed(cleanup);
          } else {
            throw cleanup;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * STUDIO-OBS-012A ruling R8 (joined): the c6-kill Vault-outage strand, reproduced as a real
   * fault.
   *
   * <p>A secret-bearing published run is held before Ready by the existing initial-context pause
   * seam. The owned ordinary runner parent is then actually stopped (SIGSTOP) for longer than one
   * secret control window and continued, which is the host-sleep stall of the incident. The runner
   * fails closed on the missed window and stops the run itself. Nobody recorded a cancel, so its
   * FINAL must be the conformant pre-Ready shape (outer RUN_ERROR, reason CANCELLED) that the
   * backend accepts on its own, without any reaper: no reapOnce is called and the scheduled
   * processors are suppressed in this context.
   */
  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(38)
  void actualSecretControlWindowLossBeforeReadyReachesTerminalError() throws Exception {
    String canary = "R8-SELFSTOP-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    p3Case(() -> p8PreReadySelfStop(canary));
    p3Scan(List.of(canary));
  }

  private void p8PreReadySelfStop(String canary) throws Exception {
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant(primaryCsi, "fixture");
    UUID test = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", true);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", test);
    p3ExpectedPassword = canary;
    p3Seed(scope, canary);
    String learning = p3Start(test, "fixture");
    awaitFinalizing(learning);
    P3Publication publication = p3Publish(learning);
    p3CleanupOwnedResources();
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    long logStart = Files.size(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log"));
    Process parent = ordinaryWorker;
    Instant parentStarted = parent.toHandle().info().startInstant().orElseThrow();
    boolean paused = false;
    boolean stopped = false;
    Throwable workflowPrimary = null;
    try {
      Files.deleteIfExists(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-check-entered"));
      Files.writeString(
          TEMP.resolve("initial-context-state.json"), "{\"pause\":true,\"fail\":false}");
      paused = true;
      UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
      p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, publication);
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
      long entered = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(15).toNanos();
      while (!Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-check-entered"))
          && System.nanoTime() < entered) {
        Thread.sleep(20);
      }
      assertThat(Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-check-entered"))).isTrue();
      assertThat("ADMITTED".equals(p6Owner(execution).get("state"))).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT browser_started_at IS NULL FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?",
                  Boolean.class,
                  execution))
          .as("held before Ready")
          .isTrue();

      // The stall: the owned parent is genuinely stopped across more than one 1 s secret control
      // window. Timers and sockets in the parent stand still, exactly as in a host sleep.
      p6SameProcess(parent.toHandle(), parentStarted);
      stopped = true;
      p6Signal("-STOP", Long.toString(parent.pid()));
      long stoppedBy = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(5).toNanos();
      while (System.nanoTime() < stoppedBy
          && !p6Status(parent.toHandle()).state().startsWith("T")) {
        Thread.sleep(10);
      }
      assertThat(p6Status(parent.toHandle()).state().startsWith("T"))
          .as("own ordinary parent actually STOPPED")
          .isTrue();
      Thread.sleep(3_000);
      p6SameProcess(parent.toHandle(), parentStarted);
      p6Signal("-CONT", Long.toString(parent.pid()));
      stopped = false;

      List<JsonNode> runnerLines = List.of();
      long selfStopBy = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(15).toNanos();
      while (System.nanoTime() < selfStopBy) {
        runnerLines = p8RunnerLines(logStart, execution);
        if (runnerLines.stream()
            .anyMatch(line -> "run.cancel_requested".equals(line.path("event").asText()))) {
          break;
        }
        Thread.sleep(20);
      }
      Files.writeString(
          TEMP.resolve("initial-context-state.json"), "{\"pause\":false,\"fail\":false}");
      paused = false;

      JsonNode snapshot = p3AwaitExecution(execution, "ERROR");
      JsonNode finalReport = p3RunnerReport(execution);
      assertThat(finalReport.path("browserStartedAt").isNull()).isTrue();
      assertThat(finalReport.path("outcome").path("result").asText()).isEqualTo("ERROR");
      assertThat(finalReport.path("outcome").path("errorCode").asText())
          .as("a self-stop the API never recorded is a run fault, not the runner CANCELLED")
          .isEqualTo("RUN_ERROR");
      assertThat(finalReport.path("outcome").path("errorMessage").asText())
          .isIn(
              "INVALID_CONTRACT",
              "PROFILE_MISMATCH",
              "POLICY_REFUSED",
              "AUTHORIZATION_EXPIRED",
              "REFERENCE_MISMATCH",
              "PASSWORD_CONTROL_READ_REFUSED",
              "TARGET_DRIFT",
              "TIMEOUT",
              "CANCELLED",
              "INTERNAL_ERROR");
      var row =
          jdbc.queryForMap(
              "SELECT e.status,e.error_code,e.browser_started_at,l.state,l.cancel_requested_at"
                  + " FROM ui_test_executions e JOIN studio_dispatch_ledger l"
                  + " ON l.execution_id=e.id AND l.execution_accepted_at=e.started_at"
                  + " WHERE e.id=?",
              execution);
      assertThat(row.get("status")).isEqualTo("ERROR");
      assertThat(row.get("error_code")).as("unmapped cancellation, no invented cause").isNull();
      assertThat(row.get("browser_started_at")).isNull();
      assertThat(row.get("cancel_requested_at")).as("nobody recorded a cancel").isNull();
      assertThat(row.get("state")).as("the FINAL itself left ADMITTED").isNotEqualTo("ADMITTED");
      assertThat(snapshot.path("stepResults").size()).isPositive();
      for (JsonNode step : snapshot.path("stepResults")) {
        assertThat(step.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("SKIPPED");
        assertThat(step.path("offsetMs").isNull()).isTrue();
        assertThat(step.path("durationMs").isNull()).isTrue();
      }
      assertThat(snapshot.path("startedAt").isNull()).isTrue();

      // Read the runner's own log after the terminal settled.
      runnerLines = p8RunnerLines(logStart, execution);
      List<JsonNode> stops =
          runnerLines.stream()
              .filter(line -> "run.cancel_requested".equals(line.path("event").asText()))
              .toList();
      assertThat(stops).hasSize(1);
      assertThat(stops.getFirst().path("source").asText()).isEqualTo("control-window");
      assertThat(stops.getFirst().path("recorded").asBoolean(true)).isFalse();
      assertThat(
              runnerLines.stream()
                  .filter(line -> "report.terminal_delivered".equals(line.path("event").asText()))
                  .count())
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(runnerLines)
          .noneMatch(line -> "report.terminal_rejected".equals(line.path("event").asText()))
          .noneMatch(line -> "run.browser_started".equals(line.path("event").asText()));

      // No secret value was typed or sent: one dispatch resolution, no login receipt, no use.
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - reads)
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).isEmpty();
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=?"
                      + " AND row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome='USED'",
                  Long.class,
                  execution))
          .isZero();
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "r8-preready-control-window-selfstop",
          C.object()
              .put("parentStoppedMs", 3_000)
              .put("selfStopRecorded", false)
              .put("terminalDelivered", true)
              .put("terminalRejected", false)
              .put("errorCodeNull", true)
              .put("browserStartedAtNull", true)
              .put("reaperInvoked", false)
              .put("secretReads", 1)
              .put("loginReceipts", 0)
              .put("modelCalls", 0));
    } catch (Exception | Error failure) {
      workflowPrimary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      if (stopped && parent.isAlive()) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              p6SameProcess(parent.toHandle(), parentStarted);
              p6Signal("-CONT", Long.toString(parent.pid()));
            });
      }
      if (paused) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () ->
                Files.writeString(
                    TEMP.resolve("initial-context-state.json"),
                    "{\"pause\":false,\"fail\":false}"));
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        if (workflowPrimary != null) {
          failures.forEach(workflowPrimary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /** The ordinary runner's JSON log lines for one execution, written since {@code start}. */
  private static List<JsonNode> p8RunnerLines(long start, UUID execution) throws IOException {
    byte[] all = Files.readAllBytes(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log"));
    String text =
        new String(
            all,
            (int) Math.min(start, all.length),
            all.length - (int) Math.min(start, all.length),
            StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    List<JsonNode> lines = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    for (String line : text.split("\n")) {
      if (!line.startsWith("{") || !line.contains(execution.toString())) {
        continue;
      }
      try {
        JsonNode parsed = JSON.readTree(line);
        if (execution.toString().equals(parsed.path("executionId").asText())) {
          lines.add(parsed);
        }
      } catch (IOException notJson) {
        // Child output relayed through the parent is not a structured runner line.
      }
    }
    return lines;
  }

  private List<Map<String, Object>> p7AttemptAudit(UUID execution, String attempt) {
    return jdbc.queryForList(
        "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=? AND attempt_id=?"
            + " AND secret_id='PASS' ORDER BY row_kind,id",
        execution,
        attempt);
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(33)
  void actualCoreGuidedControlValuePasswordRequestRefusesBeforeRead() throws Exception {
    p14Captured(P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE, false);
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(34)
  void actualCorePublishedControlValuePasswordRequestRefusesBeforeRead() throws Exception {
    p14Captured(P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE, true);
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(35)
  void actualCoreGuidedHasValuePasswordRequestRefusesBeforeRead() throws Exception {
    p14Captured(P14Recipe.HAS_VALUE, false);
  }

  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(36)
  void actualCorePublishedHasValuePasswordRequestAndReusedTextCanaryAssertion() throws Exception {
    // Two separately labeled ordinary runs share ONE genuine hasValue publication.
    p14Captured(P14Recipe.HAS_VALUE, true);
  }

  private void p14Captured(P14Recipe recipe, boolean published) throws Exception {
    String canary = "C14-PAGE-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    C5Capture capture = new C5Capture(List.of(canary), false);
    capture.cacheNonemptyRequired = published;
    c5Capture = capture;
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      capture.writeProfileValues();
      p3Case(
          () -> {
            p14Recipe = recipe;
            p14Page = published ? P14Page.TEXT_MATCH : P14Page.PASSWORD;
            p14Expected = "C14-EXPECTED-" + UUID.randomUUID();
            p14Canary = canary;
            p14Run(recipe, published, capture);
          });
    } catch (Exception | Error failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      if (!cleanupUnconfirmed) {
        // Original owner drainage has returned before resetting the actual served document.
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p3Scan(List.of(canary)));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::writeNumericEvidence);
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::close);
        p14Recipe = P14Recipe.NONE;
        p14Page = P14Page.TEXT_MATCH;
        p14Expected = null;
        p14Canary = null;
        c5Capture = null;
      } else {
        failures.add(new IllegalStateException("C14 original owner closure remains unconfirmed"));
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        if (primary != null) {
          failures.forEach(primary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private void p14Run(P14Recipe recipe, boolean published, C5Capture capture) throws Exception {
    UUID test = p14CreateSource(recipe);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", test);
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size();
    if (!published) {
      capture.armTrace();
    }
    int receiptBaseline = RECEIPTS.size();
    String session = p3Start(test, "fixture");
    capture.selectedGuidedSession = session;
    if (!published) {
      ObjectNode unavailable = p14AwaitAcquisitionWindow(session, recipe);
      // The unchanged30s wait starts only after the real target acquisition window exists.
      // Neither the window deadline, product allowance, owner lease nor poll caps are changed.
      awaitState(session, "FAILED");
      ObjectNode terminal = p14AcquisitionSnapshot(session);
      p14AssertAcquisitionIdentity(terminal, recipe);
      JsonNode firstActive =
          unavailable.path("progress").path("translationAcquisition").path("active");
      JsonNode lastMarker = terminal.path("progress").path("translationAcquisition");
      JsonNode lastActive = lastMarker.path("active");
      for (String field :
          List.of(
              "stepId",
              "stepIndex",
              "planRevision",
              "planHash",
              "obligationId",
              "targetIntentHash",
              "workerId",
              "ownerFence")) {
        assertThat(firstActive.path(field).equals(lastActive.path(field)))
            .as("same authentic Field acquisition identity through terminal")
            .isTrue();
      }
      for (String field : List.of("windowId", "openedAt", "deadline", "reservedMs")) {
        assertThat(
                firstActive
                    .path("window")
                    .path(field)
                    .equals(lastActive.path("window").path(field)))
            .as("same bounded original acquisition window")
            .isTrue();
      }
      assertThat(unavailable.path("materialization").equals(terminal.path("materialization")))
          .as("original immutable materialization survives actual stop")
          .isTrue();
      JsonNode firstDocument = unavailable.path("document"),
          lastDocument = terminal.path("document");
      assertThat(firstDocument.path("scope").equals(lastDocument.path("scope"))).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              firstDocument
                  .path("owner")
                  .path("workerId")
                  .equals(lastDocument.path("owner").path("workerId")))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(
              firstDocument
                  .path("owner")
                  .path("fence")
                  .equals(lastDocument.path("owner").path("fence")))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(terminal.path("progress").path("errorCode").asText().equals("BUDGET_EXCEEDED"))
          .as("actual terminal acquisition refusal, never cancellation or generic error")
          .isTrue();
      JsonNode result =
          p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + session + "/result", null, 200);
      assertThat(result.path("errorCode").asText().equals("BUDGET_EXCEEDED")).isTrue();
      boolean deadlineReached =
          !Instant.parse(lastActive.path("window").path("deadline").asText())
              .isAfter(Instant.now());
      boolean stepPollCapReached = lastActive.path("extraPolls").asInt() == 32;
      boolean sessionPollCapReached = lastMarker.path("totalExtraPolls").asInt() == 200;
      assertThat(deadlineReached || stepPollCapReached || sessionPollCapReached)
          .as("exact original acquisition deadline or acquisition poll cap")
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(lastActive.path("extraPolls").asInt()).isBetween(1, 32);
      assertThat(lastMarker.path("totalExtraPolls").asInt()).isBetween(1, 200);
      JsonNode counts = lastDocument.path("counters"), limits = lastDocument.path("limits");
      assertThat(counts.path("actions").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(counts.path("modelProposals").asInt()).isEqualTo(2);
      for (String field : List.of("modelProposals", "modelInputTokens", "modelOutputTokens")) {
        assertThat(counts.path(field).equals(firstDocument.path("counters").path(field)))
            .as("no Field inference or later model budget exhaustion")
            .isTrue();
      }
      assertThat(counts.path("actions").asLong()).isLessThan(limits.path("maxActions").asLong());
      assertThat(counts.path("modelProposals").asLong())
          .isLessThan(limits.path("maxModelProposals").asLong());
      assertThat(counts.path("modelInputTokens").asLong())
          .isLessThan(limits.path("maxModelInputTokensTotal").asLong());
      assertThat(counts.path("modelOutputTokens").asLong())
          .isLessThan(limits.path("maxModelOutputTokensTotal").asLong());
      assertThat(counts.path("observations").asLong())
          .isLessThan(limits.path("maxObservations").asLong());
      assertThat(
              limits.path("maxSanitizedEvidenceBytesTotal").asLong()
                  - counts.path("sanitizedEvidenceBytes").asLong())
          .isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(limits.path("maxObservationBytes").asLong());
      assertThat(
              P3_INVOCATIONS.stream()
                  .anyMatch(kind -> kind.equals("read") || kind.equals("assert")))
          .as("no requested password operation completed; acquisition precedes admission")
          .isFalse();
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=?"
                      + " AND step_id IN (?,?) AND admission_id IS NOT NULL",
                  Long.class,
                  session,
                  TWO,
                  THREE))
          .isZero();
      assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size()).isEqualTo(reads);
      p3AwaitTeardown(session);
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "c14-guided-" + recipe.ordinal(),
          C.object()
              .put("operator", recipe.ordinal())
              .put("path", 1)
              .put("passwordControls", 1)
              .put("nonterminalAcquisitionUnavailableSupport", true)
              .put("sameOriginalWindowTerminalBudgetRefusal", true)
              .put("originalAllowanceMs", lastMarker.path("allowanceMs").asLong())
              .put("originalHelperWaitMs", 30000)
              .put("deadlineReached", deadlineReached)
              .put("stepPollCapReached", stepPollCapReached)
              .put("sessionPollCapReached", sessionPollCapReached)
              .put("extraPolls", lastActive.path("extraPolls").asInt())
              .put("requestedPasswordOperations", 0)
              .put("vaultValueReads", 0));
    } else {
      awaitFinalizing(session);
      // Positive learning uses authenticated native outcomes and binding/effect lineage,
      // not the ordinary-only exported-session invocation observer.
      p14AssertPositiveLearning(session, recipe, receiptBaseline);
      P3Publication publication = p3Publish(session);
      capture.armTrace();
      int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
      p14Page = P14Page.PASSWORD;
      UUID passwordRun = p14Ordinary(test, scope, publication, capture, "TARGET_DRIFT", "ERROR");
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "c14-published-" + recipe.ordinal(),
          C.object()
              .put("operator", recipe.ordinal())
              .put("path", 2)
              .put("authenticTextPublication", true)
              .put("actualSameHintPasswordMutation", true)
              .put("firstGuardTargetDrift", true)
              .put("requestedPasswordOperations", 0)
              .put("vaultValueReads", 0)
              .put("ordinaryModelCalls", 0));
      // Close the genuine password-refusal owner BEFORE reusing the same immutable publication.
      p3CleanupOrdinary(
          passwordRun, p3Cookie, System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos());
      if (recipe == P14Recipe.HAS_VALUE) {
        p14Page = P14Page.TEXT_CANARY;
        UUID textRun = p14Ordinary(test, scope, publication, capture, "ASSERTION_FAILED", "FAILED");
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
        p3CleanupOrdinary(textRun, p3Cookie, System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos());
        p3WriteEvidence(
