            "c14-text-canary",
            C.object()
                .put("operator", recipe.ordinal())
                .put("path", 2)
                .put("sameAuthenticHasValuePublication", true)
                .put("actualTextAssertionFalse", true)
                .put("firstGuardAssertionFailed", true)
                .put("vaultValueReads", 0)
                .put("ordinaryModelCalls", 0)
                .put("separateVaultSecretCredit", false));
      }
    }
    assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size()).isEqualTo(reads);
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE test_id=?", Long.class, test))
        .isZero();
    p3Scan(List.of(p14Canary));
  }

  private void p14AssertPositiveLearning(String id, P14Recipe recipe, int receiptBaseline) {
    sessions.transaction(
        () -> {
          var current = sessions.lock(id);
          JsonNode document = current.document(), scope = document.path("scope");
          assertThat(document.path("state").asText().equals("FINALIZING")).isTrue();
          assertThat(document.path("sessionId").asText().equals(id)).isTrue();
          JsonNode plan = plans.state(id).path("plan");
          var materials =
              jdbc.queryForList(
                  "SELECT scope_hash,context_id,context_revision,worker_id,owner_fence,source_revision,"
                      // Keep the constant in bounded chunks across formatter reflow.
                      + "configuration_revision,authored_inputs_hash,authored_inputs::text"
                      + " AS authored_inputs,live FROM studio_guided_materializations WHERE"
                      + " session_id=?",
                  id);
          assertThat(materials.size()).isEqualTo(1);
          var material = materials.getFirst();
          assertThat(Boolean.TRUE.equals(material.get("live"))).isTrue();
          assertThat(document.path("scopeHash").asText().equals(material.get("scope_hash")))
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(scope.path("sourceRevision").asText().equals(material.get("source_revision")))
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(
                  scope
                      .path("configurationRevision")
                      .asText()
                      .equals(material.get("configuration_revision")))
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(
                  document
                      .path("owner")
                      .path("workerId")
                      .asText()
                      .equals(material.get("worker_id")))
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(
                  document.path("owner").path("fence").asLong()
                      == ((Number) material.get("owner_fence")).longValue())
              .isTrue();
          JsonNode inputs = C.parse((String) material.get("authored_inputs"));
          assertThat(C.hash(inputs).equals(material.get("authored_inputs_hash"))).isTrue();
          var manifests =
              jdbc.queryForList(
                  "SELECT manifest::text,manifest_hash FROM studio_guided_source_manifests WHERE"
                      + " session_id=?",
                  id);
          assertThat(manifests.size()).isEqualTo(1);
          JsonNode manifest = C.parse((String) manifests.getFirst().get("manifest"));
          assertThat(C.hash(manifest).equals(manifests.getFirst().get("manifest_hash"))).isTrue();
          for (String field : List.of("sourceRevision", "configurationRevision")) {
            assertThat(manifest.path(field).equals(scope.path(field))).isTrue();
          }
          var rows =
              jdbc.queryForList(
                  ("SELECT document::text AS ledger,proposal::text AS proposal FROM "
                      + "studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=?"),
                  id);
          assertThat(rows.size()).isEqualTo(recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE ? 5 : 3);
          for (var row : rows) {
            JsonNode ledger = C.parse((String) row.get("ledger")),
                proposal = C.parse((String) row.get("proposal"));
            assertThat(
                    ledger.path("sessionId").asText().equals(id)
                        && proposal.path("sessionId").asText().equals(id))
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    ledger.path("state").asText().equals("SUCCEEDED")
                        && ledger.path("outcomeCode").asText().equals("OK")
                        && ledger.path("dispatchCount").asInt() == 1)
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    ledger.path("ownerFence").asLong()
                        == ((Number) material.get("owner_fence")).longValue())
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(ledger.path("coordinate").equals(proposal.path("coordinate"))).isTrue();
            assertThat(proposal.path("scopeHash").asText().equals(material.get("scope_hash")))
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    proposal
                        .path("authoredInputsHash")
                        .asText()
                        .equals(material.get("authored_inputs_hash")))
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    proposal.path("contextRevision").asLong()
                        == ((Number) material.get("context_revision")).longValue())
                .isTrue();
            if (proposal.path("operation").path("kind").asText().equals("assert")) {
              assertThat(
                      ledger.path("assertionPassed").isBoolean()
                          && ledger.path("assertionPassed").booleanValue())
                  .isTrue();
            }
          }
          p14CompletedAction(rows, ONE, 0, "navigate");
          ObjectNode field =
              p14CompletedAction(
                  rows, TWO, 0, recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE ? "read" : "assert");
          JsonNode operation = field.path("proposal").path("operation");
          JsonNode fieldObligation = null;
          for (JsonNode obligation : plan.path("obligations")) {
            if (obligation.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
                && obligation.path("occurrence").asInt(-1) == 0) {
              assertThat(fieldObligation == null).isTrue();
              fieldObligation = obligation;
            }
          }
          assertThat(
                  fieldObligation != null
                      && fieldObligation.path("targetIntent").asText().equals("Field")
                      && fieldObligation
                          .path("operationKind")
                          .asText()
                          .equals(operation.path("kind").asText()))
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(
                  field
                      .path("proposal")
                      .path("executionEvidence")
                      .path("origin")
                      .asText()
                      .equals(ORIGIN))
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(
                  field
                          .path("proposal")
                          .path("target")
                          .path("locator")
                          .path("name")
                          .asText()
                          .equals("Field")
                      && field
                          .path("proposal")
                          .path("target")
                          .path("locator")
                          .path("role")
                          .asText()
                          .equals("textbox"))
              .isTrue();
          if (recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE) {
            JsonNode readRecipe = operation.path("recipe");
            assertThat(
                    readRecipe.path("kind").asText().equals("controlValue")
                        && readRecipe.path("classification").asText().equals("nonsecretApproved")
                        && readRecipe.path("controlType").asText().equals("text")
                        && readRecipe.path("policyRuleId").asText().equals("business-read"))
                .isTrue();
            String binding = operation.path("bindingId").asText();
            String readAdmission =
                field.path("ledger").path("admission").path("admissionId").asText();
            var allocations =
                jdbc.queryForList(
                    ("SELECT document::text FROM studio_guided_allocations WHERE "
                        + "session_id=? AND binding_id=? AND need_id='c14-field'"),
                    String.class,
                    id,
                    binding);
            assertThat(allocations.size()).isEqualTo(1);
            JsonNode allocation = C.parse(allocations.getFirst());
            assertThat(
                    allocation.path("bindingId").asText().equals(binding)
                        && allocation.path("producerStepId").asText().equals(TWO))
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(allocation.path("consumers").size()).isEqualTo(1);
            assertThat(
                    allocation.path("consumers").get(0).path("stepId").asText().equals(THREE)
                        && allocation
                            .path("consumers")
                            .get(0)
                            .path("argumentSlot")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("fill.value"))
                .isTrue();
            JsonNode need = null;
            for (JsonNode declared : plan.path("needs")) {
              if (declared.path("needId").asText().equals("c14-field")) {
                assertThat(need == null).isTrue();
                need = declared;
              }
            }
            assertThat(
                    need != null
                        && need.path("producerStepId").asText().equals(TWO)
                        && need.path("consumers").equals(allocation.path("consumers")))
                .isTrue();
            var producers =
                jdbc.queryForList(
                    ("SELECT document::text FROM studio_guided_producers WHERE "
                        + "session_id=? AND binding_id=? AND admission_id=?"),
                    String.class,
                    id,
                    binding,
                    readAdmission);
            assertThat(producers.size()).isEqualTo(1);
            JsonNode producer = C.parse(producers.getFirst()),
                availability = producer.path("availability");
            assertThat(
                    producer.path("bindingId").asText().equals(binding)
                        && producer.path("producerAdmissionId").asText().equals(readAdmission)
                        && producer
                            .path("coordinate")
                            .equals(field.path("proposal").path("coordinate"))
                        && producer.path("recipe").equals(readRecipe)
                        && producer
                            .path("origin")
                            .equals(
                                field.path("proposal").path("executionEvidence").path("origin")))
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    availability.path("bindingId").asText().equals(binding)
                        && availability.path("producerAdmissionId").asText().equals(readAdmission)
                        && availability
                            .path("contextId")
                            .asText()
                            .equals(material.get("context_id"))
                        && availability.path("workerId").asText().equals(material.get("worker_id"))
                        && availability.path("ownerFence").asLong()
                            == ((Number) material.get("owner_fence")).longValue())
                .isTrue();
            ObjectNode fill = p14CompletedAction(rows, THREE, 0, "fill");
            String fillAdmission =
                fill.path("ledger").path("admission").path("admissionId").asText();
            assertThat(
                    fill.path("proposal")
                            .path("target")
                            .path("locator")
                            .path("name")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("Destination")
                        && fill.path("proposal")
                            .path("target")
                            .path("locator")
                            .path("role")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("textbox")
                        && fill.path("proposal")
                            .path("operation")
                            .path("value")
                            .path("kind")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("binding")
                        && fill.path("proposal")
                            .path("operation")
                            .path("value")
                            .path("bindingId")
                            .asText()
                            .equals(binding))
                .isTrue();
            var edges =
                jdbc.queryForList(
                    ("SELECT document::text FROM studio_guided_consumer_edges WHERE "
                            + "session_id=? AND consumer_admission_id=?")
                        + " AND argument_slot='fill.value' AND binding_id=? AND"
                        + " producer_admission_id=?",
                    String.class,
                    id,
                    fillAdmission,
                    binding,
                    readAdmission);
            assertThat(edges.size()).isEqualTo(1);
            JsonNode edge = C.parse(edges.getFirst());
            assertThat(
                    edge.path("bindingId").asText().equals(binding)
                        && edge.path("producerAdmissionId").asText().equals(readAdmission)
                        && edge.path("consumerAdmissionId").asText().equals(fillAdmission)
                        && edge.path("argumentSlot").asText().equals("fill.value")
                        && edge.path("coordinate").equals(fill.path("proposal").path("coordinate"))
                        && edge.path("destinationOrigin").equals(producer.path("origin"))
                        && edge.path("destinationOrigin")
                            .equals(fill.path("proposal").path("executionEvidence").path("origin"))
                        && edge.path("transferPolicy").asText().equals("sameOrigin"))
                .isTrue();
            ObjectNode click = p14CompletedAction(rows, THREE, 1, "click");
            assertThat(
                    click
                        .path("proposal")
                        .path("target")
                        .path("locator")
                        .path("name")
                        .asText()
                        .equals("Submit"))
                .isTrue();
            ObjectNode confirmed = p14CompletedAction(rows, THREE, 2, "assert");
            assertThat(
                    confirmed
                            .path("proposal")
                            .path("operation")
                            .path("predicate")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("visible")
                        && confirmed
                            .path("proposal")
                            .path("target")
                            .path("locator")
                            .path("name")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("Confirmation"))
                .isTrue();
          } else {
            assertThat(
                    operation.path("predicate").asText().equals("hasValue")
                        && operation
                            .path("expected")
                            .path("kind")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("authoredLiteral"))
                .isTrue();
            String inputId = operation.path("expected").path("sourceInputId").asText();
            JsonNode expected = null;
            for (JsonNode input : inputs.path("inputs")) {
              if (input.path("inputId").asText().equals(inputId)) {
                assertThat(expected == null).isTrue();
                expected = input;
              }
            }
            assertThat(
                    expected != null
                        && expected.path("kind").asText().equals("nonsecretText")
                        && expected.path("sourceStepId").asText().equals(TWO)
                        && expected.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("assert.expected")
                        && expected.path("value").asText().equals(p14Expected))
                .isTrue();
            JsonNode declared = null;
            for (JsonNode reference : manifest.path("configurationReferences")) {
              if (reference
                  .path("referenceId")
                  .equals(expected.path("provenance").path("referenceId"))) {
                assertThat(declared == null).isTrue();
                declared = reference;
              }
            }
            int referenceIndex = fieldObligation.path("referenceIndex").asInt(-1);
            assertThat(referenceIndex >= 0 && referenceIndex < plan.path("sourceReferences").size())
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    plan.path("sourceReferences")
                        .get(referenceIndex)
                        .path("referenceId")
                        .equals(expected.path("provenance").path("referenceId")))
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    declared != null
                        && declared.path("referenceName").asText().equals("EXPECTED")
                        && declared.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
                        && declared.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("assert.expected")
                        && declared.path("kind").asText().equals("nonsecretText")
                        && expected
                            .path("provenance")
                            .path("referenceKind")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("configuration"))
                .isTrue();
            ObjectNode complete = p14CompletedAction(rows, THREE, 0, "assert");
            assertThat(
                    complete
                            .path("proposal")
                            .path("target")
                            .path("locator")
                            .path("name")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("Complete")
                        && complete
                            .path("proposal")
                            .path("operation")
                            .path("predicate")
                            .asText()
                            .equals("visible"))
                .isTrue();
            assertThat(
                    jdbc.queryForObject(
                        "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_producers WHERE session_id=?",
                        Integer.class,
                        id))
                .isZero();
          }
          return null;
        });
    if (recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE) {
      // Existing intrinsic page receipt only; no extra request, fabricated body or wait padding.
      assertThat(RECEIPTS.size()).isEqualTo(receiptBaseline + 1);
      assertThat(RECEIPTS.get(receiptBaseline).equals(p14Expected))
          .as("actual TEXT read value reached the intrinsic binding consumer")
          .isTrue();
    } else {
      assertThat(RECEIPTS.size()).isEqualTo(receiptBaseline);
    }
  }

  private ObjectNode p14CompletedAction(
      List<Map<String, Object>> rows, String step, int ordinal, String operation) {
    ObjectNode selected = null;
    for (var row : rows) {
      JsonNode ledger = C.parse((String) row.get("ledger")),
          proposal = C.parse((String) row.get("proposal"));
      if (ledger.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText().equals(step)
          && ledger.path("coordinate").path("ordinal").asInt(-1) == ordinal) {
        assertThat(selected == null).as("unique actual authored-coordinate action").isTrue();
        assertThat(proposal.path("operation").path("kind").asText().equals(operation)).isTrue();
        selected = C.object();
        selected.set("ledger", ledger);
        selected.set("proposal", proposal);
      }
    }
    assertThat(selected != null)
        .as("actual completed action at original authored coordinate")
        .isTrue();
    return selected;
  }

  private UUID p14Ordinary(
      UUID test,
      VaultScope scope,
      P3Publication publication,
      C5Capture capture,
      String firstGuard,
      String status)
      throws Exception {
    int invocations = P3_INVOCATIONS.size();
    UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
    p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, publication);
    capture.watchExecution(execution);
    assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
    JsonNode current = p3AwaitExecution(execution, status);
    JsonNode report = p3RunnerReport(execution);
    // TARGET_DRIFT is an actual inner reason under RUN_ERROR; do not relabel the wire enum.
    assertThat(report.path("outcome").path("errorMessage").asText().equals(firstGuard))
        .as("exact first inner production guard")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(report.path("steps").size()).isEqualTo(3);
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(0).path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("PASS");
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(1).path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("FAIL");
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(1).path("failure").asText().equals(firstGuard))
        .as("exact Field failure guard")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(2).path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("SKIPPED");
    if (firstGuard.equals("TARGET_DRIFT")) {
      assertThat(
              P3_INVOCATIONS.subList(invocations, P3_INVOCATIONS.size()).stream()
                  .anyMatch(
                      kind ->
                          kind.equals("read")
                              || kind.equals("assert")
                              || kind.equals("fill")
                              || kind.equals("click")))
          .isFalse();
    }
    assertThat(p3DecodeVideo(execution, current)).isPositive();
    return execution;
  }

  private UUID p14CreateSource(P14Recipe recipe) throws Exception {
    List<String> texts =
        recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE
            ? List.of(
                "Open " + URL + ".",
                "Read Field value.",
                "Enter captured Field value in Destination, click Submit and check Confirmation.")
            : List.of(
                "Open " + URL + ".",
                "Check Field value equals <var EXPECTED>.",
                "Check Complete heading remains visible.");
    var authoring =
        new AuthoringV2Request(
            2,
            null,
            List.of(
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(ONE), texts.get(0), null),
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(TWO), texts.get(1), null),
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(THREE), texts.get(2), null)));
    UUID test = UUID.randomUUID();
    String variables =
        recipe == P14Recipe.HAS_VALUE
            ? JSON.createArrayNode()
                .add(
                    C.object()
                        .put("name", "EXPECTED")
                        .put("kind", "plain")
                        .put("value", p14Expected))
                .toString()
            : "[]";
    jdbc.update(
        "INSERT INTO ui_tests(id,name,csi_id,environment,browser,steps,variables,authoring,"
            + "authoring_revision_inputs,created_by,created_at,updated_at)"
            + " VALUES (?,'C14 controlled Field',?,'fixture','chromium',?::jsonb,?::jsonb,"
            + "?::jsonb,?::jsonb,'fixture@example.invalid',now(),now())",
        test,
        primaryCsi,
        JSON.writeValueAsString(texts),
        variables,
        JSON.writeValueAsString(authoring),
        JSON.writeValueAsString(AuthoringRevisionIdentity.create(authoring, Map.of())));
    return test;
  }

  private static String p14Markup() {
    boolean password = p14Page == P14Page.PASSWORD;
    String actual =
        p14Page == P14Page.PASSWORD ? "" : p14Page == P14Page.TEXT_MATCH ? p14Expected : p14Canary;
    String consumer =
        p14Recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE && !password
            ? "<label for='destination'>Destination</label><textarea"
                + " id='destination'></textarea><output id='done' aria-label='Confirmation'"
                + " hidden>Submitted</output><button"
                + " id='submit'>Submit</button><script>document.getElementById('submit').onclick=()=>{"
                + "document.getElementById('done').hidden=false;fetch('/receipt',{method:'POST',"
                + "body:document.getElementById('destination').value});};</script>"
            : "";
    return "<!doctype html><html><head><title>Operations</title></head><body>"
        + "<main aria-label='Operations'><input aria-label='Field' type='"
        + (password ? "password" : "text")
        + "' value='"
        + actual
        + "'><h1>Complete</h1>"
        + consumer
        + "</main></body></html>";
  }

  private static void p14PasswordObservation(JsonNode observation) {
    assertThat(observation.path("coverage").path("passwordControls").asInt(-1)).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(observation.path("coverage").path("complete").asBoolean()).isFalse();
    assertThat(observation.path("coverage").path("truncated").asBoolean()).isFalse();
    assertThat(observation.path("coverage").path("reason").asText()).isEqualTo("sensitivity");
    assertThat(observation.path("coverage").path("withheldRegions").asInt()).isPositive();
    for (JsonNode node : observation.path("nodes")) {
      assertThat(node.path("name").asText().equals("Field")).isFalse();
      for (JsonNode locator : node.path("locatorHints")) {
        assertThat(
                locator.path("name").asText().equals("Field")
                    || locator.path("value").asText().equals("Field"))
            .isFalse();
      }
    }
  }

  private ObjectNode p14AcquisitionSnapshot(String session) {
    return sessions.transaction(
        () -> {
          var current = sessions.lock(session);
          ObjectNode snapshot = C.object();
          snapshot.set("document", current.document().deepCopy());
          snapshot.set("progress", current.progress().deepCopy());
          snapshot.set(
              "translation", plans.exists(session) ? plans.state(session).deepCopy() : C.object());
          var materials =
              jdbc.queryForList(
                  "SELECT authored_inputs::text FROM studio_guided_materializations WHERE"
                      + " session_id=?",
                  String.class,
                  session);
          snapshot.set(
              "materialization", materials.isEmpty() ? C.object() : C.parse(materials.getFirst()));
          snapshot.set("actions", JSON.valueToTree(sessions.actions(session)));
          return snapshot;
        });
  }

  private ObjectNode p14AwaitAcquisitionWindow(String session, P14Recipe recipe) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(30).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      ObjectNode current = p14AcquisitionSnapshot(session);
      String state = current.path("document").path("state").asText();
      assertThat(List.of("FAILED", "EXPIRED", "CANCELLED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME").contains(state))
          .as("genuine Field acquisition prerequisite before any terminal substitute")
          .isFalse();
      JsonNode active = current.path("progress").path("translationAcquisition").path("active");
      if (active.path("window").isObject()
          && active.path("extraPolls").asInt() > 0
          && active.path("pending").isNull()) {
        p14AssertAcquisitionIdentity(current, recipe);
        assertThat(
                Instant.parse(active.path("window").path("deadline").asText())
                    .isAfter(Instant.now()))
            .as("real acquisition window is still live at phase anchor")
            .isTrue();
        return current;
      }
      Thread.sleep(20);
    }
    throw new AssertionError("C14 authentic target acquisition window was not observed");
  }

  private void p14AssertAcquisitionIdentity(JsonNode snapshot, P14Recipe recipe) {
    JsonNode document = snapshot.path("document"), progress = snapshot.path("progress");
    JsonNode translation = snapshot.path("translation"), plan = translation.path("plan");
    JsonNode marker = progress.path("translationAcquisition"), active = marker.path("active");
    assertThat(marker.path("policy").asText()).isEqualTo("passive-target-1");
    assertThat(marker.path("allowanceMs").asLong()).isEqualTo(30000);
    assertThat(marker.path("sourceRevision").equals(document.path("scope").path("sourceRevision")))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(
            marker
                .path("configurationRevision")
                .equals(document.path("scope").path("configurationRevision")))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(active.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)).isTrue();
    assertThat(active.path("stepIndex").asInt(-1)).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(
            active.path("planRevision").asText().equals(translation.path("planRevision").asText()))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(active.path("planHash").asText().equals(C.hash(plan))).isTrue();
    assertThat(active.path("workerId").equals(document.path("owner").path("workerId"))).isTrue();
    assertThat(active.path("ownerFence").equals(document.path("owner").path("fence"))).isTrue();
    JsonNode accepted = null;
    for (JsonNode obligation : plan.path("obligations")) {
      if (obligation.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
          && obligation.path("occurrence").asInt(-1) == 0) {
        assertThat(accepted == null).as("unique genuine accepted Field obligation").isTrue();
        accepted = obligation;
      }
    }
    assertThat(accepted != null).isTrue();
    assertThat(accepted.path("targetIntent").asText()).isEqualTo("Field");
    assertThat(accepted.path("operationKind").asText())
        .isEqualTo(recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE ? "read" : "assert");
    if (recipe == P14Recipe.HAS_VALUE) {
      assertThat(accepted.path("predicate").asText()).isEqualTo("hasValue");
    }
    assertThat(active.path("obligationId").equals(accepted.path("obligationId"))).isTrue();
    JsonNode targetIdentity =
        accepted.has("selectedLocator")
            ? JSON.createArrayNode()
                .add(accepted.path("targetIntent"))
                .add(accepted.path("selectedLocator"))
            : accepted.path("targetIntent");
    assertThat(active.path("targetIntentHash").asText().equals(C.hash(targetIdentity))).isTrue();
    assertThat(
            snapshot.path("materialization").isObject()
                && !snapshot.path("materialization").isEmpty())
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(snapshot.path("actions").size()).isEqualTo(1);
    JsonNode navigation = snapshot.path("actions").get(0);
    assertThat(navigation.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText().equals(ONE)).isTrue();
    assertThat(navigation.path("state").asText()).isEqualTo("SUCCEEDED");
    assertThat(navigation.path("dispatchCount").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
    p14PasswordObservation(progress.path("observation"));
  }

  private static ObjectNode p14Model(ObjectNode result, JsonNode context, String kind) {
    if (kind.equals("SOURCE_PLAN")) {
      var references = result.putArray("sourceReferences");
      references
          .addObject()
          .put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan")
          .put("sourceRevision", "1")
          .put("stepId", ONE)
          .put("startUtf16", 5)
          .put("endUtf16", 5 + URL.length())
          .put("kind", "destination")
          .put("intendedSlot", "navigate.destination");
      var obligations = result.putArray("obligations");
      obligations.add(obligation(ONE, 0, "navigate", "page", 0, null));
      var needs = result.putArray("needs");
      if (p14Recipe == P14Recipe.CONTROL_VALUE) {
        obligations.add(obligation(TWO, 0, "read", "Field", null, "c14-field"));
        obligations.add(obligation(THREE, 0, "fill", "Destination", null, "c14-field"));
        obligations.add(obligation(THREE, 1, "click", "Submit", null, null));
        obligations.add(
            obligation(THREE, 2, "assert", "Confirmation", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
        needs
            .addObject()
            .put("needId", "c14-field")
            .put("producerStepId", TWO)
            .putArray("consumers")
            .addObject()
            .put("stepId", THREE)
            .put("argumentSlot", "fill.value");
      } else {
        JsonNode declaring = null;
        for (JsonNode configured : context.path("configurationReferences")) {
          if (configured.path("referenceName").asText().equals("EXPECTED")
              && configured.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
              && configured.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("assert.expected")) {
            assertThat(declaring == null).isTrue();
            declaring = configured;
          }
        }
        assertThat(declaring != null).as("authentic plain EXPECTED declaring step/slot").isTrue();
        var reference =
            ((ObjectNode) declaring)
                .deepCopy()
                .put("referenceKind", "configuration")
                .put("configurationRevision", "1");
        reference.remove("referenceName");
        references.add(reference);
        obligations.add(
            obligation(TWO, 0, "assert", "Field", 1, null).put("predicate", "hasValue"));
        obligations.add(
            obligation(THREE, 0, "assert", "Complete", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
      }
      return result;
    }
    JsonNode obligation = context.path("obligations").get(0);
    String op = obligation.path("operationKind").asText();
    String name = obligation.path("targetIntent").asText();
    if (op.equals("navigate")) {
      result
          .put("status", "ACTION")
          .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
          .putNull("question");
      result
          .putObject("action")
          .putObject("operation")
          .put("kind", "navigate")
          .put("destinationInputId", context.path("inputs").get(0).path("inputId").asText());
      result.withObject("/action").putObject("target").put("kind", "page");
      return result;
    }
    JsonNode node = null;
    for (JsonNode candidate : context.path("observation").path("nodes")) {
      if (candidate.path("name").asText().equals(name)) {
        if (op.equals("fill")
            && name.equals("Destination")
            && !(candidate.path("role").asText().equals("textbox")
                && candidate.path("elementType").asText().equals("textarea"))) {
          continue;
        }
        assertThat(node == null).as("unique real C14 exported target").isTrue();
        node = candidate;
      }
    }
    if (node == null) {
      assertThat(name.equals("Field") && p14Page == P14Page.PASSWORD).isTrue();
      p14PasswordObservation(context.path("observation"));
      result.put("status", "UNSUPPORTED").put("reasonCode", "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE");
      return result;
    }
    if (name.equals("Field")) {
      assertThat(p14Page == P14Page.TEXT_MATCH).isTrue();
      assertThat(node.path("role").asText()).isEqualTo("textbox");
    }
    result
        .put("status", "ACTION")
        .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
        .putNull("question");
    var action = result.putObject("action");
    var operation = action.putObject("operation").put("kind", op);
    action
        .putObject("target")
        .put("nodeId", node.path("nodeId").asText())
        .putObject("locator")
        .put("kind", "role")
        .put("role", node.path("role").asText())
        .put("name", name)
        .put("exact", true);
    if (op.equals("read")) {
      operation
          .put("bindingId", context.path("needs").get(0).path("bindingId").asText())
          .putObject("recipe")
          .put("kind", "controlValue")
          .put("classification", "nonsecretApproved")
          .put("controlType", "text")
          .put("policyRuleId", "business-read");
    } else if (op.equals("fill")) {
      operation
          .putObject("value")
          .put("kind", "binding")
          .put("bindingId", context.path("needs").get(0).path("bindingId").asText());
    } else if (op.equals("assert")) {
      if (name.equals("Field")) {
        operation
            .put("predicate", "hasValue")
            .putObject("expected")
            .put("kind", "authoredLiteral")
            .put(
                "sourceInputId",
                context
                    .path("inputs")
                    .get(obligation.path("referenceIndex").asInt())
                    .path("inputId")
                    .asText());
      } else {
        operation.put("predicate", "visible").putNull("expected");
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  private String p3Cookie;
  private final Map<String, String> p3OwnedGuidedCookies = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
  private final Map<UUID, String> p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
  private long p3NextCleanupGetAt;
  private int p3CaptureCaseOrdinal;
  private int p3CsiOrdinal;
  private int p4PublicationValueReads;

  private record P3Publication(UUID revision, String artifactHash, String artifact) {}

  private record P3ScopeReplay(
      UUID test, VaultScope scope, String value, P3Publication published, int ownReads) {
    @Override
    public String toString() {
      return "P3ScopeReplay[<private>]";
    }
  }

  private static final class P3ConcurrentHold {
    final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch[] entered = {
      new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1), new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1)
    };
    final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch[] exited = {
      new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1), new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1)
    };
    final java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch release = new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
    final AtomicInteger calls = new AtomicInteger();
    volatile boolean failed;

    void hold(boolean accepted, int ordinal) {
      if (ordinal >= 2) {
        failed = true;
        return;
      }
      entered[ordinal].countDown();
      if (!accepted) {
        failed = true;
        return;
      }
      try {
        if (!release.await(20, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
          failed = true;
        }
      } catch (InterruptedException failure) {
        failed = true;
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
      }
    }
  }

  private static volatile P3ConcurrentHold p3ConcurrentHold;

  private P3ScopeReplay p3PrepareScopeReplay(Map.Entry<String, String> axis) throws Exception {
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant(axis.getKey(), axis.getValue());
    UUID test = p3CreateSource(axis.getKey(), axis.getValue(), true);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(axis.getKey(), axis.getValue(), test);
    String value = "P3-ORDINARY-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    p3ExpectedPassword = value;
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    p3Seed(scope, value);
    String id = p3Start(test, axis.getValue());
    awaitFinalizing(id);
    P3Publication published = p3Publish(id);
    return new P3ScopeReplay(
        test,
        scope,
        value,
        published,
        COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS"));
  }

  private void p3AssertScopeReplay(P3ScopeReplay current, UUID execution, JsonNode report)
      throws Exception {
    assertThat(
            COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, current.scope(), "PASS"))
        .isEqualTo(current.ownReads() + 1);
    p3AssertBinding(execution, current.scope(), current.published());
    p3AssertUsed(null, execution);
    p3AssertAuditScope(null, execution, current.scope());
    p3DecodeVideo(execution, report);
  }

  private void p3ConcurrentScopeReplays(List<P3ScopeReplay> pair) throws Exception {
    assertThat(pair.size()).isEqualTo(2);
    assertThat(pair.get(0).scope().csiId().equals(pair.get(1).scope().csiId())).isFalse();
    assertThat(pair.get(0).value().equals(pair.get(1).value())).isFalse();
    assertThat(p3ConcurrentHold).isNull();
    long logStart;
    var hold = new P3ConcurrentHold();
    List<UUID> executions = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    List<Long> terminalDeadlines = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    List<JsonNode> receipts = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    List<P6OwnedPid> owned = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    Throwable primary = null;
    Process parent = null;
    try {
      assertThat(ordinaryWorker.children().count()).isZero();
      stopOwnedProcessTree(ordinaryWorker);
      logStart = Files.size(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log"));
      long startupDeadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
      startOrdinaryWorker(true, 2); // Real startup precedes either original authorization window.
      p3AwaitParentStarted(logStart, 2, startupDeadline);
      parent = ordinaryWorker;
      Instant parentStarted = parent.info().startInstant().orElseThrow();
      assertThat(p3ParentEvents(logStart, receipts, true)[0]).isEqualTo(2);
      P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
      P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
      p3ConcurrentHold = hold;
      List<P6OwnedPid> children = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
      for (int index = 0; index < pair.size(); index++) {
        P3ScopeReplay current = pair.get(index);
        p3ExpectedPassword = current.value();
        UUID execution = p3Execute(current.test());
        executions.add(execution);
        p3AssertBinding(execution, current.scope(), current.published());
        assertThat(
                COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(
                    StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, current.scope(), "PASS"))
            .isEqualTo(current.ownReads());
        assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
        terminalDeadlines.add(System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos());
        assertThat(hold.entered[index].await(15, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
        assertThat(hold.failed).isFalse();
        List<P6OwnedPid> currentChildren = p3OrdinaryChildren(parent);
        assertThat(currentChildren.size()).isEqualTo(index + 1);
        for (P6OwnedPid prior : children) {
          p6SameProcess(prior.handle(), prior.started());
          assertThat(
                  currentChildren.stream()
                      .anyMatch(
                          next ->
                              next.handle().pid() == prior.handle().pid()
                                  && next.started().equals(prior.started())))
              .isTrue();
        }
        for (P6OwnedPid child : currentChildren) {
          if (owned.stream()
              .noneMatch(
                  prior ->
                      prior.handle().pid() == child.handle().pid()
                          && prior.started().equals(child.started()))) {
            owned.add(child);
            for (ProcessHandle descendant : child.handle().descendants().limit(257).toList()) {
              owned.add(p6SnapshotPid(descendant));
            }
          }
        }
        assertThat(owned.size()).isBetween(1, 256);
        children = currentChildren;
        JsonNode receipt =
            C.parse(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT observed_admission_receipt::text FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?",
                    String.class,
                    execution));
        assertThat(receipt.path("kind").asText()).isEqualTo("observed");
        assertThat(
                receipt.path("request").path("executionId").asText().equals(execution.toString()))
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(
                receipt.path("admissionId").isTextual()
                    && receipt
                        .path("admissionId")
                        .equals(receipt.path("request").path("admissionId")))
            .isTrue();
        receipts.add(receipt);
      }
      assertThat(owned.size()).isBetween(2, 256);
      p3ScanOwnedChildArguments(children, pair.stream().map(P3ScopeReplay::value).toList());
      assertThat(hold.calls.get()).isEqualTo(2);
      assertThat(hold.release.getCount()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(hold.failed).isFalse();
      for (P6OwnedPid child : children) {
        p6SameProcess(child.handle(), child.started());
      }
      p6SameProcess(parent.toHandle(), parentStarted);
      p6Signal("-TERM", Long.toString(parent.pid()));
      long drainingDeadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(2).toNanos();
      while (p3ParentEvents(logStart, receipts, true)[1] == 0
          && System.nanoTime() < drainingDeadline) {
        Thread.sleep(10);
      }
      assertThat(p3ParentEvents(logStart, receipts, true)[1]).isEqualTo(1);
      hold.release.countDown();
      List<JsonNode> reports = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
      for (int index = 0; index < pair.size(); index++) {
        reports.add(
            p3AwaitExecution(executions.get(index), "PASSED", terminalDeadlines.get(index)));
      }
      for (int index = 0; index < pair.size(); index++) {
        p3AssertScopeReplay(pair.get(index), executions.get(index), reports.get(index));
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.size()).isEqualTo(2);
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.get(index).accepted()).isTrue();
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.get(index).password().equals(pair.get(index).value())).isTrue();
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.get(index).username().equals("p3-user")).isTrue();
      }
      assertThat(parent.waitFor(20, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
      assertThat(parent.exitValue()).isZero();
      p6AwaitProcesses(owned, true);
      assertThat(hold.failed).isFalse();
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
      int[] events = p3ParentEvents(logStart, receipts, true);
      assertThat(events).containsExactly(2, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1);
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "ordinary-concurrent-drain",
          C.object()
              .put("distinctCsiScopes", 2)
              .put("simultaneousOwnedChildren", 2)
              .put("actualOwnReceipts", 2)
              .put("privateEnvArgvScans", 2)
              .put("canaryVariants", 30)
              .put("canaryHits", 0)
              .put("actualDrainingInFlight", 2)
              .put("gracefulDrainCompleted", true)
              .put("parentWipeEvents", 2)
              .put("ownedProcessCount", owned.size())
              .put("ownedProcessesGone", true));
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      hold.release.countDown();
      for (int index = 0; index < Math.min(2, hold.calls.get()); index++) {
        var exited = hold.exited[index];
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> assertThat(exited.await(5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue());
      }
      Process actualParent = parent == null ? ordinaryWorker : parent;
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> stopOwnedProcessTree(actualParent));
      if (!owned.isEmpty()) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p6AwaitProcesses(owned, true));
      }
      if (failures.isEmpty()) {
        p3ConcurrentHold = null;
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              long restoredLogStart = Files.size(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log"));
              long startupDeadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
              startOrdinaryWorker(true);
              p3AwaitParentStarted(restoredLogStart, 1, startupDeadline);
            });
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
        if (primary != null) {
          failures.forEach(primary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static List<P6OwnedPid> p3OrdinaryChildren(Process parent) {
    Instant started = parent.info().startInstant().orElseThrow();
    p6SameProcess(parent.toHandle(), started);
    var direct = parent.children().limit(65).toList();
    assertThat(direct.size()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(64);
    var children =
        direct.stream()
            .filter(
                child ->
                    child
                        .info()
                        .commandLine()
                        .map(
                            command ->
                                command.contains(TEMP.resolve("recorded-child.mjs").toString()))
                        .orElse(false))
            .toList();
    assertThat(children.size()).isBetween(1, 2);
    var owned = new java.util.ArrayList<P6OwnedPid>();
    for (ProcessHandle child : children) {
      assertThat(
              child
                  .info()
                  .commandLine()
                  .map(command -> command.contains(TEMP.resolve("recorded-child.mjs").toString()))
                  .orElse(false))
          .as("actual direct compiled ordinary child")
          .isTrue();
      P6OwnedPid actual = p6SnapshotPid(child);
      assertThat(actual.parent() == parent.pid() && actual.group() == child.pid()).isTrue();
      owned.add(actual);
    }
    p6SameProcess(parent.toHandle(), started);
    return List.copyOf(owned);
  }

  private static void p3ScanOwnedChildArguments(List<P6OwnedPid> children, List<String> values)
      throws Exception {
    for (P6OwnedPid child : children) {
      p6SameProcess(child.handle(), child.started());
      C5CanaryStream scan = new C5CanaryStream(values);
      byte[] arguments = null, environment = null;
      Process probe = null;
      try {
        String[] actualArguments = child.handle().info().arguments().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(actualArguments.length).isBetween(1, 32);
        arguments = String.join("\0", actualArguments).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        assertThat(arguments.length).isBetween(1, 65536);
        probe =
            new ProcessBuilder(
                    "/bin/ps", "eww", "-p", Long.toString(child.handle().pid()), "-o", "command=")
                .redirectErrorStream(true)
                .start();
        assertThat(probe.waitFor(5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
        assertThat(probe.exitValue()).isZero();
        environment = probe.getInputStream().readNBytes(65537);
        assertThat(environment.length).isBetween(1, 65536);
        assertThat(
                containsBytes(
                    environment,
                    "PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)))
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(containsBytes(environment, "TMPDIR=".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))).isTrue();
        p6SameProcess(child.handle(), child.started());
        scan.write(arguments, 0, arguments.length);
        scan.write(environment, 0, environment.length);
        scan.closeInput();
        scan.assertClean(true);
      } finally {
        if (arguments != null) {
          java.util.Arrays.fill(arguments, (byte) 0);
        }
        if (environment != null) {
          java.util.Arrays.fill(environment, (byte) 0);
        }
        scan.wipe();
        if (probe != null) {
          p6CloseCommand(probe);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static void p3AwaitParentStarted(long offset, int poolSize, long deadline)
      throws Exception {
    int observed;
    do {
      observed = p3ParentEvents(offset, List.of(), false)[0];
      if (observed != 0) {
        break;
      }
      Thread.sleep(10);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    assertThat(System.nanoTime() <= deadline).as("original parent readiness deadline").isTrue();
    assertThat(observed)
        .as("actual parent startup within original readiness budget")
        .isEqualTo(poolSize);
  }

  private static int[] p3ParentEvents(long offset, List<JsonNode> receipts, boolean concurrentDrain)
      throws Exception {
    assertThat(receipts.size()).isBetween(0, 2);
    int[] counts = new int[6]; // pool size, drain2, completed, fault, and exact A/B parent wipes.
    byte[] bytes = null;
    try (var input = Files.newInputStream(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log"))) {
      input.skipNBytes(offset);
      bytes = input.readNBytes(2097153);
      assertThat(bytes.length).isLessThanOrEqualTo(2097152);
      String text = new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      int complete = text.lastIndexOf('\n');
      if (complete < 0) {
        return counts;
      }
      for (String line : text.substring(0, complete).split("\n")) {
        if (!line.startsWith("{")
            || !(line.contains("runner.started")
                || line.contains("runner.draining")
                || line.contains("runner.drained")
                || line.contains("runner.drain_deadline")
                || line.contains("runner.drain_killed")
                || line.contains("run.values_wiped"))) {
          continue;
        }
        JsonNode event;
        try {
          event = JSON.readTree(line);
        } catch (IOException invalid) {
          throw new IllegalStateException("C2 parent event shape unavailable");
        }
        String name = event.path("event").asText();
        // Startup has no receipt; C6's nonempty receipt selection grades only its wipe.
        if (!concurrentDrain
            && !(receipts.isEmpty()
                ? name.equals("runner.started")
                : name.equals("run.values_wiped"))) {
          continue;
        }
        switch (name) {
          case "runner.started" -> {
            assertThat(counts[0]).isZero();
            counts[0] = event.path("poolSize").asInt(-1);
          }
          case "runner.draining" -> {
            assertThat(event.path("inFlight").asInt(-1)).isEqualTo(2);
            counts[1]++;
          }
          case "runner.drained" -> {
            assertThat(event.path("completed").isBoolean()).isTrue();
            if (event.path("completed").asBoolean()) {
              counts[2]++;
            } else {
              counts[3]++;
            }
          }
          case "runner.drain_deadline", "runner.drain_killed" -> counts[3]++;
          case "run.values_wiped" -> {
            int matched = 0;
            for (int index = 0; index < receipts.size(); index++) {
              JsonNode receipt = receipts.get(index);
              JsonNode request = receipt.path("request");
              assertThat(
                      receipt.path("admissionId").isTextual()
                          && receipt.path("admissionId").equals(request.path("admissionId")))
                  .isTrue();
              if (List.of("executionId", "attemptId", "admissionId").stream()
                  .allMatch(
                      key ->
                          event.path(key).isTextual()
                              && event.path(key).equals(request.path(key)))) {
                counts[4 + index]++;
                matched++;
              }
            }
            assertThat(matched).isEqualTo(1);
          }
          default -> {
            // Other parent events do not contribute to this fixed observation.
          }
        }
      }
      return counts;
    } finally {
      if (bytes != null) {
        java.util.Arrays.fill(bytes, (byte) 0);
      }
    }
  }

  @FunctionalInterface
  private interface P3Action {
    void run() throws Exception;
  }

  /** The approved governed policy of both environments this class learns in, for one CSI. */
  private void seedGovernedPolicies(String csi) {
    for (JsonNode trusted : List.of(POLICY, P3_OTHER_POLICY)) {
      var origins = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
      trusted.path("allowedOrigins").forEach(origin -> origins.add(origin.asText()));
      com.citi.put.checks.studio.support.ObservedPublicationFixture.ensurePolicy(
          jdbc,
          csi,
          trusted.path("applicationId").asText(),
          trusted.path("environment").asText(),
          origins,
          trusted.path("applicationPolicy"),
          "ALLOW");
    }
  }

  /**
   * The fixture Core now answers a different CSI ownership for {@link #COOKIE}; drop the BFF's
   * per-cookie identity cache entry (60 s TTL) so the next request re-reads Core, as it would after
   * a real ownership change (S3b: a stale entry refused the SSE watch with 403).
   */
  @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  private void forgetCachedCoreIdentity() {
    ((com.github.benmanes.caffeine.cache.Cache<String, com.citi.put.checks.security.UserIdentity>)
            ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
                "identityCache", com.github.benmanes.caffeine.cache.Cache.class))
        .invalidate(COOKIE);
  }

  private void p3Case(P3Action operation) throws Exception {
    assertThat(cleanupUnconfirmed)
        .as("prior actual P3 owner cleanup remains unconfirmed")
        .isFalse();
    Throwable primary = null;
    p3OwnedGuidedCookies.clear();
    p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.clear();
    p3CaptureCaseOrdinal++;
    // A fresh CSI per case (see primaryCsi): set before the case's Core identity is minted.
    primaryCsi = "CSI-P3-" + (++p3CsiOrdinal);
    forgetCachedCoreIdentity();
    P3_INVOCATIONS.clear();
    p3Cookie = p3CoreCookie("user-1", "fixture@example.invalid");
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    p3ReportsProtected = false;
    p3ExpectedPassword = "P3-EXPECTED-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    jdbc.update(
        "INSERT INTO users(id,sso_id,name,email,created_at) VALUES"
            + " (?,'user-1','Fixture','fixture@example.invalid',now()) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
        UUID.randomUUID());
    for (String csi : List.of(primaryCsi, "CSI-456")) {
      jdbc.update(
          "INSERT INTO csi_systems(id,name,business_unit,criticality,owner_email,environment)"
              + " VALUES (?, 'Orders','Ops','HIGH','fixture@example.invalid','Production') ON"
              + " CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
          csi);
    }
    seedGovernedPolicies(primaryCsi);
    try {
      if (guidedWorker == null || ordinaryWorker == null) {
        startWorkers();
      }
      operation.run();
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      boolean confirmed = false;
      try {
        p3CleanupOwnedResources();
        confirmed = true;
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError cleanup) {
        cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
        if (primary != null) {
          primary.addSuppressed(cleanup);
        } else {
          throw cleanup;
        }
      } finally {
        if (confirmed) {
          p4PasswordControls = -1;
          p4AdversarialPasswordSelection = false;
          P4_FIRST_GUIDED_REQUEST.set(null);
          P4_FIRST_GUARD.set(null);
          p3Mode = null;
          p3ExpectedPassword = null;
          p3ReportsProtected = false;
          P3_FAIL_READ.set(null);
          P3_CORE.clear();
          P3_PERMITTED.clear();
          P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
          P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
          p3OwnedGuidedCookies.clear();
          p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.clear();
          primaryCsi = "CSI-123";
          forgetCachedCoreIdentity();
        }
        // Unconfirmed actual owners retain fixture authority; the sticky guard blocks new cases.
      }
    }
  }

  private static void p3CaptureOrdinaryReport(UUID execution, byte[] bytes) {
    int phase = 1;
    try {
      if (bytes.length > 262144) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("P3 callback witness bound");
      }
      String text =
          StandardCharsets.UTF_8
              .newDecoder()
              .onMalformedInput(java.nio.charset.CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
              .onUnmappableCharacter(java.nio.charset.CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
              .decode(java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes))
              .toString();
      phase = 2;
      // Strict duplicates/trailing tokens remain; ordinary timing numbers are not canonical-v1
      // integers.
      JsonNode actual = C.parse(text);
      long[] numeric = p3ReportNumericCounts(actual, 0);
      phase = 3;
      new com.citi.put.checks.studio.internal.StrictInternalBodyReader(JSON)
          .read(bytes, com.citi.put.checks.studio.internal.dto.RunnerReport.class);
      boolean finalReport = actual.path("kind").asText().equals("FINAL");
      P3_CAPTURE_DIAGNOSTICS.compute(
          execution,
          (id, previous) -> {
            ObjectNode metrics = previous == null ? C.object() : previous.deepCopy();
            metrics.put("captureSuccesses", metrics.path("captureSuccesses").asLong() + 1);
            if (finalReport) {
              metrics
                  .put("finalSuccesses", metrics.path("finalSuccesses").asLong() + 1)
                  .put("finalBytes", bytes.length)
                  .put("finalFloatLexemes", numeric[0])
                  .put("finalFractionalValues", numeric[1])
                  .put("guidedIntegerParserWouldRefuse", numeric[0] > 0);
            }
            return metrics;
          });
      if (finalReport) {
        P3_FINAL_REPORTS.put(execution, actual);
      }
    } catch (Exception failure) {
      final int failedPhase = phase;
      P3_CAPTURE_DIAGNOSTICS.compute(
          execution,
          (id, previous) -> {
            ObjectNode metrics = previous == null ? C.object() : previous.deepCopy();
            return metrics
                .put("captureFailures", metrics.path("captureFailures").asLong() + 1)
                .put("lastFailurePhase", failedPhase)
                .put("lastFailureBytes", bytes.length);
          });
      // Metadata failure never changes the original controller response or creates a FINAL.
    }
  }

  private static long[] p3ReportNumericCounts(JsonNode node, int depth) {
    int structuralDepth = depth + (node.isContainerNode() ? 1 : 0);
    if (structuralDepth > 64) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("P3 callback witness depth");
    }
    long floats = 0, fractions = 0;
    if (node.isNumber()) {
      double number = node.doubleValue();
      if (!Double.isFinite(number)) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("P3 nonfinite callback number");
      }
      if (node.isFloatingPointNumber()) {
        floats = 1;
        fractions = number != Math.rint(number) ? 1 : 0;
      }
    }
    if (node.isContainerNode()) {
      for (JsonNode child : node) {
        long[] counts = p3ReportNumericCounts(child, structuralDepth);
        floats += counts[0];
        fractions += counts[1];
      }
    }
    return new long[] {floats, fractions};
  }

  private void p3WriteCaptureDiagnostics(UUID execution) throws Exception {
    int ordinal = new java.util.ArrayList<>(p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.keySet()).indexOf(execution) + 1;
    assertThat(ordinal).isPositive();
    ObjectNode stored = P3_CAPTURE_DIAGNOSTICS.get(execution);
    var metrics = stored == null ? C.object() : stored.deepCopy();
    metrics
        .put("executionOrdinal", ordinal)
        .put("caseOrdinal", p3CaptureCaseOrdinal)
        .put("finalObserved", P3_FINAL_REPORTS.containsKey(execution));
    p3WriteEvidence("capture-" + p3CaptureCaseOrdinal + "-" + ordinal, metrics);
  }

  private static Duration p3CleanupRequestTimeout(long deadline) {
    long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
    if (remaining <= 0) {
      throw new IllegalStateException("Actual ordinary cleanup deadline exhausted");
    }
    return Duration.ofNanos(Math.min(Duration.ofSeconds(15).toNanos(), remaining));
  }

  private JsonNode p3CleanupSnapshot(UUID execution, String cookie, long deadline)
      throws Exception {
    for (; ; ) {
      long now = System.nanoTime();
      long delay = Math.max(0, p3NextCleanupGetAt - now);
      long remaining = deadline - now;
      if (remaining <= delay) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Actual ordinary cleanup wait exceeds deadline");
      }
      if (delay > 0) {
        java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.sleep(delay);
      }
      long requestStarted = System.nanoTime();
      p3NextCleanupGetAt = requestStarted + Duration.ofSeconds(1).toNanos();
      var request =
          HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                  URI.create(
                      "https://localhost:" + port + "/api/v1/studio/executions/" + execution))
              .timeout(p3CleanupRequestTimeout(deadline))
              .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + cookie)
              .GET()
              .build();
      var response =
          HttpClient.newBuilder()
              .sslContext(TLS)
              .build()
              .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
      if (response.statusCode() == 429) {
        assertThat(C.parse(response.body()).path("error").path("code").asText())
            .isEqualTo("RATE_LIMITED");
        String actualRetry =
            response
                .headers()
                .firstValue("Retry-After")
                .orElseThrow(
                    () -> new IllegalStateException("Actual rate response lacks Retry-After"));
        assertThat(actualRetry.matches("[1-9][0-9]{0,8}")).isTrue();
        long seconds = Long.parseLong(actualRetry);
        long retryNanos = Duration.ofSeconds(seconds).toNanos();
        now = System.nanoTime();
        if (retryNanos >= deadline - now) {
          throw new IllegalStateException("Actual Retry-After exceeds cleanup deadline");
        }
        p3NextCleanupGetAt = Math.max(p3NextCleanupGetAt, now + retryNanos);
        continue;
      }
      assertThat(response.statusCode())
          .as("actual bounded ordinary cleanup snapshot")
          .isEqualTo(200);
      if (System.nanoTime() >= deadline) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Actual cleanup response arrived past deadline");
      }
      return C.parse(response.body());
    }
  }

  private void p3CleanupOwnedResources() throws Exception {
    refreshFixtureCredentials(); // Actual owner teardown acknowledgement needs a live token.
    var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
    long ordinaryDeadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos();
    p3NextCleanupGetAt = System.nanoTime();
    for (var owned : new java.util.ArrayList<>(p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.entrySet())) {
      try {
        p3CleanupOrdinary(owned.getKey(), owned.getValue(), ordinaryDeadline);
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
        failures.add(failure);
      } finally {
        try {
          p3WriteCaptureDiagnostics(owned.getKey());
        } catch (Exception | AssertionError diagnosticsFailure) {
          failures.add(diagnosticsFailure);
        }
      }
    }
    for (var owned : new java.util.ArrayList<>(p3OwnedGuidedCookies.entrySet())) {
      try {
        String id = owned.getKey();
        String state =
            sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("state").asText());
        if (!List.of(
                "SUCCEEDED",
                "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT",
                "FAILED",
                "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
                "CANCELLED",
                "EXPIRED")
            .contains(state)) {
          p3Call(
              owned.getValue(),
              "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/cancel",
              C.object().put("schemaVersion", 3).put("reason", "userRequested"),
              202);
        }
        p3AwaitTeardown(id);
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
        failures.add(failure);
      }
    }
    if (failures.isEmpty() && Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer.json"))) {
      try {
        p4AssertObserverPhaseCompleted(P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get());
        Files.writeString(
            TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer.json"),
            "{\"active\":false,\"phase\":0}",
            StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      } catch (IOException | AssertionError failure) {
        failures.add(failure);
      }
    }
    if (failures.isEmpty() && p4Revocation != null) {
      try {
        p4AwaitRevocationObserverCompletion(p4Revocation);
        p4SealPrivateProducersAfterOwnerCleanup(p4Revocation);
        Files.writeString(
            TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"), "{\"active\":false}", StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        p4Revocation = null;
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
        failures.add(failure);
      }
    }
    if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
      var unconfirmed = new IllegalStateException("Actual owned P3 cleanup remains unconfirmed");
      failures.forEach(unconfirmed::addSuppressed);
      throw unconfirmed;
    }
  }

  private void p3CleanupOrdinary(UUID execution, String cookie, long deadline) throws Exception {
    P6ChildCut cut = p6ChildCut;
    if (cut != null && cut.execution.equals(execution) && cut.parentKilled && cut.recovered) {
      assertThat(cut.parent.isAlive()).isFalse();
      p6AwaitProcesses(cut.childTree, true);
      assertThat(cut.signInExited.getCount()).isZero();
      assertThat("DONE".equals(p6Owner(execution).get("state")) && cut.restarted).isTrue();
      return;
    }
    String status =
        jdbc.queryForObject(
            "SELECT status FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?", String.class, execution);
    if (!List.of("PASSED", "FAILED", "ERROR").contains(status)) {
      var request =
          HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                  URI.create(
                      "https://localhost:"
                          + port
                          + "/api/v1/studio/executions/"
                          + execution
                          + "/cancel"))
              .timeout(p3CleanupRequestTimeout(deadline))
              .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + cookie)
              .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
              .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
              .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString("{}"))
              .build();
      var cancelled =
          HttpClient.newBuilder()
              .sslContext(TLS)
              .build()
              .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
      if (cancelled.statusCode() == 409) {
        String code = C.parse(cancelled.body()).path("error").asText();
        assertThat(code).isIn("EXECUTION_TERMINAL", "CANCEL_ALREADY_REQUESTED");
      } else {
        assertThat(cancelled.statusCode()).as("genuine owned ordinary cancellation").isEqualTo(202);
      }
    }
    do {
      JsonNode snapshot = p3CleanupSnapshot(execution, cookie, deadline);
      var actual =
          jdbc.queryForMap(
              "SELECT l.state,l.attempt_id,l.admission_id,e.observed_admission_receipt FROM"
                  + " studio_dispatch_ledger l JOIN ui_test_executions e ON e.id=l.execution_id AND"
                  + " e.started_at=l.execution_accepted_at WHERE l.execution_id=?",
              execution);
      boolean terminal =
          List.of("PASSED", "FAILED", "ERROR").contains(snapshot.path("status").asText());
      if (terminal
          && snapshot.path("streamComplete").asBoolean()
          && "DONE".equals(actual.get("state"))) {
        boolean noTransportEntry =
            actual.get("attempt_id") == null
                && actual.get("admission_id") == null
                && actual.get("observed_admission_receipt") == null
                && !ACTUAL_DISPATCH_DIAGNOSTICS.containsKey(execution);
        if (noTransportEntry) {
          // Genuine pre-send DONE fences future dispatch; no attempted runner resource exists.
          p3WriteCaptureDiagnostics(execution);
          return;
        }
        if (P3_FINAL_REPORTS.containsKey(execution)) {
          JsonNode finalReport = p3RunnerReport(execution);
          assertThat(
                  List.of("PASSED", "FAILED", "ERROR")
                      .contains(finalReport.path("outcome").path("result").asText()))
              .isTrue();
          // The real runner wipes/closes owned resources before delivering this authenticated
          // FINAL.
          return;
        }
      }
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    throw new IllegalStateException("Actual ordinary terminal/resource acknowledgement missing");
  }

  private void p4AssertRepositoryGraph() {
    var factory = ownedApplicationContext.getBeanFactory();
    assertThat(sessions).isSameAs(factory.getBean("observedOriginalSessionRepository"));
    assertThat(sessions).isSameAs(factory.getBean(GuidedSessionRepository.class));
    assertThat(factory.getDependentBeans("guidedSessionRepository")).isEmpty();
    assertThat(
            java.util.Arrays.asList(factory.getDependentBeans("observedOriginalSessionRepository")))
        .contains(
            "guidedSessionService",
            "guidedTranslationService",
            "guidedTranslationCoordinator",
            "guidedPlanPublisher",
            "publicationResultProjector");
    assertThat(jdbc).isSameAs(factory.getBean(JdbcTemplate.class));
    assertThat(jdbc.getDataSource()).isSameAs(factory.getBean(javax.sql.DataSource.class));
    var manager = factory.getBean(org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager.class);
    assertThat(manager).isInstanceOf(org.springframework.orm.jpa.JpaTransactionManager.class);
    assertThat(((org.springframework.orm.jpa.JpaTransactionManager) manager).getDataSource())
        .isSameAs(jdbc.getDataSource());
  }

  private static boolean p4RevocationUsed(P4RevocationState state) {
    String ownerColumn = state.path == 1 ? "session_id" : "execution_id";
    Object owner = state.path == 1 ? state.guidedSession : state.execution;
    var usages =
        state.actualJdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT u.* FROM studio_secret_use_audit u JOIN studio_secret_use_audit r"
                + " ON r.id=u.release_id AND r.release_occurred_at=u.release_occurred_at"
                + " WHERE u."
                + ownerColumn
                + "=? AND u.secret_id='PASS' AND u.row_kind='USAGE'"
                + " AND u.usage_outcome='USED' AND r.row_kind='RELEASE'"
                + " AND r.decision_stage='DISPATCH' AND r.outcome='RESOLVED'",
            owner);
    if (usages.isEmpty()) {
      return false;
    }
    p4AuditRequire(usages.size() == 1, "Actual positive usage is not unique");
    Map<String, Object> usage = usages.getFirst();
    var releases =
        state.actualJdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE id=? AND release_occurred_at=?",
            usage.get("release_id"),
            usage.get("release_occurred_at"));
    p4AuditRequire(releases.size() == 1, "Actual positive release is not unique");
    Map<String, Object> release = releases.getFirst();
    p4AuditRequire(
        java.util.Objects.equals(release.get("id"), release.get("release_id"))
            && java.util.Objects.equals(release.get("id"), usage.get("release_id"))
            && release.get("affected_release_id") == null
            && usage.get("affected_release_id") == null,
        "Actual release usage links do not match");
    p4CompareAuditScope(release, usage);
    p4AuditRequire("RESOLVED".equals(usage.get("outcome")), "Positive usage outcome changed");
    if (!p4AuditOwnerIdentity(state, release)) {
      return false;
    }
    state.usedBefore = java.util.Collections.unmodifiableMap(new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>(usage));
    state.releaseBefore =
        java.util.Collections.unmodifiableMap(new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>(release));
    return true;
  }

  private static void p4AuditRequire(boolean condition, String code) {
    if (!condition) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException(code);
    }
  }

  private static void p4CompareAuditScope(
      Map<String, Object> release, Map<String, Object> related) {
    for (String field :
        List.of(
            "path",
            "attempt_id",
            "release_seq",
            "execution_id",
            "session_id",
            "admission_id",
            "principal_id",
            "csi_id",
            "environment",
            "test_id",
            "secret_id",
            "vault_version",
            "coordinate_step_id",
            "coordinate_ordinal",
            "source_reference_id",
            "release_occurred_at")) {
      p4AuditRequire(
          java.util.Objects.equals(release.get(field), related.get(field)),
          "Actual immutable audit scope differs: " + field);
    }
  }

  private static boolean p4AuditOwnerIdentity(
      P4RevocationState state, Map<String, Object> release) {
    p4AuditRequire(
        "CSI-456".equals(release.get("csi_id"))
            && "fixture-other".equals(release.get("environment"))
            && state.test.equals(release.get("test_id"))
            && "PASS".equals(release.get("secret_id"))
            && release.get("vault_version") != null,
        "Actual audit selection scope differs");
    if (state.path == 1) {
      var owners =
          state.actualJdbc.queryForList(
              "SELECT g.document::text AS action,g.proposal::text AS proposal,s.document::text AS"
                  + " session, m.authored_inputs::text AS inputs FROM studio_guided_actions g JOIN"
                  + " studio_guided_sessions s ON s.session_id=g.session_id JOIN"
                  + " studio_guided_materializations m ON m.session_id=g.session_id WHERE"
                  + " g.session_id=? AND g.admission_id=?",
              state.guidedSession,
              release.get("admission_id"));
      p4AuditRequire(owners.size() == 1, "Actual consumed admission identity differs");
      var owner = owners.getFirst();
      JsonNode action = C.parse((String) owner.get("action")),
          proposal = C.parse((String) owner.get("proposal")),
          session = C.parse((String) owner.get("session"));
      p4AuditRequire(
          "GUIDED".equals(release.get("path"))
              && state.guidedSession.equals(release.get("session_id"))
              && release.get("execution_id") == null
              && action.path("dispatchCount").asInt() == 1
              && action
                  .path("admission")
                  .path("admissionId")
                  .asText()
                  .equals(release.get("admission_id"))
              && (state.guidedSession + ":" + action.path("ownerFence").asText())
                  .equals(release.get("attempt_id"))
              && action.path("ownerFence").equals(session.path("owner").path("fence"))
              && action.path("coordinate").path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
              && action.path("coordinate").path("ordinal").asInt(-1) == 1
              && TWO.equals(release.get("coordinate_step_id"))
              && ((Number) release.get("coordinate_ordinal")).intValue() == 1
              && proposal.path("coordinate").equals(action.path("coordinate"))
              && "fill".equals(proposal.path("operation").path("kind").asText())
              && "secretRef".equals(proposal.path("operation").path("value").path("kind").asText())
              && session
                  .path("scope")
                  .path("principalId")
                  .asText()
                  .equals(release.get("principal_id")),
          "Actual guided release action fence coordinate differs");
      boolean actualInput = false;
      for (JsonNode input : C.parse((String) owner.get("inputs")).path("inputs")) {
        actualInput |=
            input
