                    .path("inputId")
                    .equals(proposal.path("operation").path("value").path("sourceInputId"))
                && input.path("kind").asText().equals("secretRef")
                && input.path("secretId").asText().equals("PASS")
                && input.path("sourceStepId").asText().equals(TWO)
                && input.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("fill.value")
                && input
                    .path("provenance")
                    .path("referenceId")
                    .asText()
                    .equals(String.valueOf(release.get("source_reference_id")));
      }
      p4AuditRequire(actualInput, "Actual released secret materialization identity differs");
    } else {
      var owners =
          state.actualJdbc.queryForList(
              "SELECT l.attempt_id,l.admission_id,e.observed_admission_receipt::text AS receipt,"
                  + " e.observed_revision_binding::text AS binding FROM studio_dispatch_ledger l"
                  + " JOIN ui_test_executions e ON e.id=l.execution_id"
                  + " AND e.started_at=l.execution_accepted_at WHERE l.execution_id=?",
              state.execution);
      p4AuditRequire(owners.size() == 1, "Actual observed owner identity differs");
      var owner = owners.getFirst();
      if (owner.get("admission_id") == null || owner.get("receipt") == null) {
        return false;
      }
      JsonNode binding = C.parse((String) owner.get("binding")),
          receipt = C.parse((String) owner.get("receipt"));
      p4AuditRequire(
          "OBSERVED_REPLAY".equals(release.get("path"))
              && state.execution.equals(release.get("execution_id"))
              && String.valueOf(owner.get("attempt_id")).equals(release.get("attempt_id"))
              && binding.path("attemptId").asText().equals(release.get("attempt_id"))
              && binding.path("principalId").asText().equals(release.get("principal_id"))
              && receipt
                  .path("request")
                  .path("attemptId")
                  .asText()
                  .equals(release.get("attempt_id"))
              && receipt
                  .path("admissionId")
                  .asText()
                  .equals(String.valueOf(owner.get("admission_id")))
              && release.get("session_id") == null
              && release.get("admission_id") == null
              && release.get("coordinate_step_id") == null
              && release.get("coordinate_ordinal") == null,
          "Actual observed release attempt admission binding differs");
    }
    return true;
  }

  private void p4AssertAuditLineageAfterCleanup(P4RevocationState state) {
    assertThat(state.usedBefore != null && state.releaseBefore != null).isTrue();
    var usage =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE id=? AND release_occurred_at=?",
            state.usedBefore.get("id"),
            state.usedBefore.get("release_occurred_at"));
    var release =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE id=? AND release_occurred_at=?",
            state.releaseBefore.get("id"),
            state.releaseBefore.get("release_occurred_at"));
    assertThat(usage.size()).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(release.size()).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(state.usedBefore.equals(usage.getFirst()))
        .as("SAME preflip USED identity and immutable row")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(state.releaseBefore.equals(release.getFirst()))
        .as("SAME original resolved release")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(p4AuditOwnerIdentity(state, release.getFirst())).isTrue();
    String column = state.path == 1 ? "session_id" : "execution_id";
    Object owner = state.path == 1 ? state.guidedSession : state.execution;
    var rechecks =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                + column
                + "=? AND secret_id='PASS' AND decision_stage='LIVE_RECHECK'",
            owner);
    assertThat(rechecks.size())
        .as("ALL selected secret rechecks, regardless of outcome")
        .isEqualTo(1);
    var recheck = rechecks.getFirst();
    assertThat(
            "REVOKED_MID_RUN".equals(recheck.get("outcome"))
                && "RELEASE".equals(recheck.get("row_kind"))
                && state.releaseBefore.get("id").equals(recheck.get("affected_release_id"))
                && recheck.get("id").equals(recheck.get("release_id")))
        .isTrue();
    p4CompareAuditScope(state.releaseBefore, recheck);
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                    + column
                    + "=? AND secret_id='PASS' AND row_kind='USAGE'",
                Long.class,
                owner))
        .isEqualTo(1);
  }

  private static void p4RevocationNotice(com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange exchange)
      throws IOException {
    boolean accepted = false, flipped = false;
    int sequence = 0;
    int timingSite = 100;
    P4RevocationState state = p4Revocation;
    try {
      byte[] bytes = exchange.getRequestBody().readNBytes(1025);
      if (!exchange.getRequestMethod().equals("POST") || bytes.length > 1024 || state == null) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid scalar revocation observer");
      }
      JsonNode body = C.parse(new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      if (!body.isObject()
          || body.size() != 9
          || p4Number(body, "case") != state.ordinal
          || !body.path("ok").isBoolean()) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid scalar observer identity");
      }
      int producer = p4Number(body, "producer"),
          event = p4Number(body, "event"),
          ordinal = p4Number(body, "ordinal"),
          operation = p4Number(body, "operation"),
          reason = p4Number(body, "reason");
      sequence = p4Number(body, "seq");
      synchronized (state) {
        if (producer < 1
            || producer > 3
            || (state.path == 1 ? producer != 1 : producer == 1)
            || sequence != state.sequences.getOrDefault(producer, 0) + 1) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid scalar observer sequence");
        }
        state.sequences.put(producer, sequence);
        if (state.c6Kind != 0 && event == 10) {
          timingSite = 110;
          if (state.c6Timing != null) {
            state.c6Timing.mark(5);
          }
          if (!state.started
              || state.c6PrimitiveAt == 0
              || state.c6FillEntries != 1
              || state.c6PendingSamples >= (state.c6Kind == 1 ? 1 : 2)
              || !body.path("ok").booleanValue()
              || operation != 1
              || state.active.size() != 1
              || !Integer.valueOf(1).equals(state.active.get(ordinal))) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "C6 original sole CDP primitive promise is not pending");
          }
          state.inFlightOrdinal = ordinal;
          state.c6DeadlineMarginMs = p4Number(body, "deltaMicros");
          state.c6PendingAt = System.nanoTime();
          if (state.c6Timing != null) {
            state.c6Timing.mark(6);
          }
          if (++state.c6PendingSamples == 1) {
            state.c6Pending.countDown();
          } else {
            state.c6CutPending.countDown();
          }
        } else if (state.c6Kind != 0 && event == 11) {
          if (ordinal != 1 || reason != 1 || state.c6RawCalls != 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 raw scalar observation mismatch");
          }
          state.c6RawCalls++;
          state.c6LiteralHits = operation;
          state.c6EncodedHits = p4Number(body, "deltaMicros");
        } else if (state.c6Kind != 0 && event == 13) {
          if (!body.path("ok").booleanValue() || state.c6NativeClose || ordinal != state.c6Phase) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 original SDK close not confirmed");
          }
          state.c6NativeClose = true;
          state.c6FenceAvailable = operation == 1;
          if (!state.c6FenceAvailable) {
            P8_C6_CLOSED_UNKNOWN.add(state.c6Phase);
          }
        } else if (event == 1) {
          if (state.started || ordinal != 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unexpected selected owner");
          }
          state.started = true;
        } else if (event == 2) {
          timingSite = 112;
          if (operation == 1 && state.c6Timing != null) {
            state.c6Timing.nativeNotice(true, ordinal, false);
          }
          if (state.c6Kind != 0 && state.c6CutAt != 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 unexpected later native entry after cut");
          }
          if (operation == 1 && state.c6Kind != 0) {
            state.c6FillEntries++;
          }
          if (!state.started
              || state.flippedAt != 0
              || state.active.putIfAbsent(ordinal, operation) != null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Native entry outside original authority");
          }
          if (operation == 1 && state.c6Timing != null) {
            state.c6Timing.nativeNotice(true, ordinal, true);
          }
          if (operation == 1 && state.c6Kind > 0 && state.c6Kind < 4) {
            if (state.c6PrimitiveAt != 0 || state.c6FillEntries != 1) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("Duplicate selected sole CDP entry");
            }
            state.c6PrimitiveAt = System.nanoTime();
            timingSite = 240;
            p8WritePendingProbe(state, 1);
            if (state.c6Timing != null) {
              state.c6Timing.mark(4);
            }
          }
        } else if (event == 3) {
          timingSite = 113;
          if (operation == 1 && state.c6Timing != null) {
            state.c6Timing.nativeNotice(false, ordinal, false);
          }
          Integer entered = state.active.remove(ordinal);
          if (entered == null || entered != operation) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unmatched original native settlement");
          }
          // CDP fulfillment is transport settlement, never proof that the password was written.
          if (state.c6Kind == 0 && operation == 1 && body.path("ok").booleanValue()) {
            state.filled = true;
          }
          if (state.flippedAt != 0 && ordinal != state.inFlightOrdinal) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unidentified residual primitive");
          }
          if (state.flippedAt != 0 && ordinal == state.inFlightOrdinal) {
            state.residualSettlements++;
            state.residualFulfilled = body.path("ok").booleanValue();
            if (operation != 2 || state.residualSettlements != 1) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                  "Duplicate or mismatched identified click settlement");
            }
          }
          if (operation == 1 && state.c6Timing != null) {
            state.c6Timing.nativeNotice(false, ordinal, true);
          }
        } else if (event == 4) {
          if (!body.path("ok").booleanValue() || (state.path == 2 && producer != 2)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid real complete acknowledgement");
          }
          if (state.c6Kind == 0
              && state.flippedAt == 0
              && state.started
              && state.filled
              && state.signInEntered
              && state.active.size() == 1
              && state.active.containsValue(2)
              && p4RevocationUsed(state)) {
            state.inFlightOrdinal = state.active.keySet().iterator().next();
            if (state.inFlightOrdinal != 3) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unexpected original Sign in invocation ordinal");
            }
            state.flippedAt = System.nanoTime();
            state.revoked.set(true);
            for (String actor : List.of("user-1", "fixture@example.invalid")) {
              if (!P3_PERMITTED.remove(
                  new P3Entitlement(actor, "CSI-456", "fixture-other", "PASS"))) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Missing genuine granted tuple");
              }
            }
            flipped = true;
          }
        } else if (event == 5) {
          if (state.c6Kind != 0) {
            if (!state.started || ordinal != 1 || reason < 1 || reason > 6) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 original stopping observation mismatch");
            }
            state.cutoffAt = System.nanoTime();
            state.reasonProved = true;
            state.cutoff.countDown();
          } else if (!state.started
              || state.flippedAt == 0
              || ordinal != 1
              || (state.path == 2 && (producer != 3 || reason != 4))) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unproved original authority cutoff");
          }
          if (state.c6Kind == 0 && state.cutoffAt == 0) {
            state.cutoffAt = System.nanoTime();
            state.cutoff.countDown();
          }
          if ((state.path == 1 && reason == 1) || (state.path == 2 && reason == 4)) {
            state.reasonProved = true;
          }
        } else if (event == 6) {
          state.restored = body.path("ok").booleanValue();
          if (!state.restored) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Native descriptor restoration incomplete");
          }
        } else if (event == 8) {
          if (state.flippedAt == 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unmatched real ACK bracket");
          }
          state.ackBracketMicros = p4Number(body, "deltaMicros");
        } else if (event == 9) {
          if (state.path != 1 || !body.path("ok").booleanValue()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unproved original owner closure");
          }
          state.closed = true;
        } else {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown scalar observer phase");
        }
        if (state.c6Kind != 0) {
          state.notifyAll();
        }
      }
      accepted = true;
    } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
      p8TimingRefusal(state, timingSite, timingSite >= 110);
      if (state != null) {
        state.failed = true;
      }
    } catch (IOException failure) {
      p8TimingRefusal(state, timingSite, timingSite >= 110);
      if (state != null) {
        state.failed = true;
      }
      throw failure;
    }
    byte[] response =
        C.object()
            .put("accepted", accepted)
            .put("flipped", flipped)
            .put("seq", sequence)
            .toString()
            .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    try {
      exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", "application/json");
      exchange.sendResponseHeaders(accepted ? 200 : 409, response.length);
      exchange.getResponseBody().write(response);
    } catch (IOException failure) {
      p8TimingRefusal(state, 114);
      if (state != null) {
        state.failed = true;
      }
      throw failure;
    }
  }

  private static void p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(Path root) throws IOException {
    if (Files.isSymbolicLink(root)
        || !Files.isDirectory(root, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
        || !root.toRealPath().getParent().equals(TEMP.toRealPath())
        || !Files.getPosixFilePermissions(root, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
            .equals(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rwx------"))
        || !Files.getOwner(root, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
            .equals(Files.getOwner(TEMP, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS))) {
      throw new IOException("Private observer directory confinement failed");
    }
  }

  private void p4WriteRevocationMode(P4RevocationState state) throws IOException {
    Path directory = TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-producers");
    if (!Files.exists(directory, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
      Files.createDirectory(
          directory,
          java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
              java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rwx------")));
    }
    p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(directory);
    ObjectNode mode =
        C.object()
            .put("active", true)
            .put("case", state.ordinal)
            .put("epoch", state.ordinal)
            .put("path", state.path);
    if (state.c6Kind != 0) {
      mode.put("c6", state.c6Kind);
    }
    if (state.execution != null) {
      mode.put("executionId", state.execution.toString());
      mode.put(
          "controlUrl",
          "https://localhost:"
              + port
              + "/internal/studio/executions/"
              + state.execution
              + "/control");
    }
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"), mode.toString(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
  }

  private void p4AwaitRevocationObserverCompletion(P4RevocationState state) throws Exception {
    if (state.c6Kind != 0) {
      // C6 never manufactures the legacy successful click/authority flip or residual ACK.
      p8AwaitC6Closed(state);
      return;
    }
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos();
    boolean complete;
    do {
      synchronized (state) {
        complete =
            state.active.isEmpty()
                && state.reasonProved
                && state.restored
                && state.residualSettlements == 1
                && state.residualFulfilled != null
                && state.ackBracketMicros > 0
                && (state.path == 2 || state.closed);
      }
      if (complete) {
        break;
      }
      Thread.sleep(20);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    assertThat(complete).as("all required original scalar witnesses settled").isTrue();
    assertThat(state.failed).as("no lost malformed or failed observer").isFalse();
    assertThat(Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-observer-failed"))).isFalse();
  }

  private void p4SealPrivateProducersAfterOwnerCleanup(P4RevocationState state) throws Exception {
    if (state.c6Kind == 3) {
      assertThat(state.c6BrowserTreeGone && state.c6NativeClose).isTrue();
      if (state.c6ProducerSealed) {
        p8AssertManagedNativeState(state);
        return; // Existing command already proved managed output closure, not raw SDK sealing.
      }
    }
    Path root = TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-producers");
    p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
    List<Integer> producers = state.path == 1 ? List.of(1) : List.of(2, 3);
    for (int producer : producers) {
      if (producer == 3) {
        continue; // Real nonreturning child exit publishes its exact synchronous final sample.
      }
      ObjectNode command =
          C.object()
              .put("case", state.ordinal)
              .put("producer", producer)
              .put("epoch", state.ordinal)
              .put("seal", true);
      Path target = root.resolve(state.ordinal + "-" + producer + "-seal.json");
      Path temporary = root.resolve(state.ordinal + "-" + producer + "-seal.tmp");
      p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
      Files.createFile(
          temporary,
          java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
              java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")));
      Files.writeString(
          temporary,
          command.toString(),
          StandardCharsets.UTF_8,
          java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.WRITE);
      p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
      Files.move(temporary, target, java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE);
    }
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos();
    for (int producer : producers) {
      Path file = root.resolve(state.ordinal + "-" + producer + ".json");
      JsonNode finalSample = null;
      do {
        p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
        if (Files.exists(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
          if (Files.isSymbolicLink(file)
              || !Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
              || Files.size(file) > 1024
              || !file.toRealPath().getParent().equals(root.toRealPath())
              || !Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file)
                  .equals(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"))) {
            throw new IOException("Private producer sample confinement failed");
          }
          p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
          JsonNode sample = C.parse(Files.readString(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
          if (!sample.isObject()
              || sample.size()
                  != (state.c6Kind == 3 && producer == 1 ? 19 : state.c6Kind == 0 ? 10 : 13)
                      // R10-rev2 I2: producer 2 also carries the control-read numbers.
                      + (producer == 2 ? P10_PRODUCER2_READ_KEYS.size() : 0)
              || sample.path("case").asInt(-1) != state.ordinal
              || sample.path("producer").asInt(-1) != producer
              || sample.path("epoch").asInt(-1) != state.ordinal
              || sample.path("role").asInt(-1) != producer
              || !sample.path("failed").isBoolean()
              || !sample.path("sealed").isBoolean()) {
            throw new IOException("Private producer sample identity failed");
          }
          if (state.c6Kind == 3 && producer == 1) {
            for (String field :
                List.of("highPending", "cdpCreationPending", "nativeActive", "restoreAttempt")) {
              assertThat(p4Number(sample, field)).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1000000);
            }
            assertThat(p4Number(sample, "busy")).isBetween(0, 2);
            assertThat(p4Number(sample, "restoreRefusal")).isBetween(0, 5);
          }
          boolean managedLoss = state.c6Kind == 3 && producer == 1;
          if (managedLoss
              ? state.c6NativeClose
                  && state.restored
                  && state.closed
                  && !sample.path("sealed").booleanValue()
                  && p4Number(sample, "busy") == 0
                  && p4Number(sample, "restoreAttempt") > 0
                  && p4Number(sample, "restoreRefusal") == 0
                  && p4Number(sample, "cdpCreationPending") == 0
                  && p4Number(sample, "originalPending") == 0
                  && p4Number(sample, "pending") == p4Number(sample, "highPending")
                  && p4Number(sample, "sent") == p4Number(sample, "acked")
              : sample.path("sealed").booleanValue()) {
            finalSample = sample;
            break;
          }
        }
        Thread.sleep(20);
      } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
      assertThat(finalSample != null)
          .as("actual producer final ACK/failure processing sample")
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(finalSample.path("failed").booleanValue()).isFalse();
      if (state.c6Kind == 3 && producer == 1) {
        assertThat(finalSample.path("sealed").booleanValue()).isFalse();
        assertThat(p4Number(finalSample, "pending"))
            .isEqualTo(p4Number(finalSample, "highPending"));
        assertThat(p4Number(finalSample, "nativeActive")).isEqualTo(state.active.size());
      } else {
        assertThat(p4Number(finalSample, "pending")).isZero();
      }
      assertThat(p4Number(finalSample, "originalPending")).isZero();
      assertThat(p4Number(finalSample, "sent")).isPositive();
      assertThat(p4Number(finalSample, "acked")).isEqualTo(p4Number(finalSample, "sent"));
      assertThat(p4Number(finalSample, "sent")).isEqualTo(state.sequences.get(producer));
    }
    assertThat(state.failed).isFalse();
    assertThat(Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-observer-failed"))).isFalse();
    // Preserve numeric final records in the owned evidence directory; remove only seal commands.
    for (int producer : producers) {
      p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
      Files.deleteIfExists(root.resolve(state.ordinal + "-" + producer + "-seal.json"));
    }
  }

  private void p4RevocationPair(int path) throws Exception {
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant("CSI-456", "fixture-other");
    UUID test = p3CreateSource("CSI-456", "fixture-other", true);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of("CSI-456", "fixture-other", test);
    String value = "P4-REVOKE-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    p3ExpectedPassword = value;
    p3Seed(scope, value);
    P3Publication publication = null;
    if (path == 2) {
      String control = p3Start(test, "fixture-other");
      awaitFinalizing(control);
      publication = p3Publish(control);
      p3CleanupOwnedResources();
      p3OwnedGuidedCookies.clear();
      P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
      P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
      P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    }
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    P4RevocationState state =
        new P4RevocationState(P4_REVOCATION_CASE.incrementAndGet(), path, test, jdbc);
    p4Revocation = state;
    p4WriteRevocationMode(state);
    try {
      if (path == 1) {
        state.guidedSession = p3Start(test, "fixture-other");
      } else {
        state.execution = p3Execute(test);
        p4WriteRevocationMode(state);
        p3AssertBinding(state.execution, scope, publication);
        assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
      }
      assertThat(state.cutoff.await(20, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS))
          .as("original cutoff ingress after genuine ACK and exact tuple flip")
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(state.cutoffAt - state.flippedAt)
          .isPositive()
          .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofMillis(2000).toNanos());
      assertThat(state.filled).isTrue();
      assertThat(state.signInEntered).isTrue();
      state.releaseSignIn.countDown();
      Object revokedAt;
      if (path == 1) {
        awaitState(state.guidedSession, "CANCELLED");
        p3AwaitTeardown(state.guidedSession);
        revokedAt =
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT revoked_at FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE session_id=?",
                Object.class,
                state.guidedSession);
        p3AssertUsed(state.guidedSession, null);
        assertThat(
                sessions.transaction(
                    () -> sessions.lock(state.guidedSession).progress().path("errorCode").asText()))
            .isEqualTo("SECRET_USE_REVOKED");
      } else {
        JsonNode result = p3AwaitExecution(state.execution, "ERROR");
        assertThat(result.path("errorCode").asText()).isEqualTo("AUTHORIZATION_REVOKED");
        assertThat(p3RunnerReport(state.execution).path("outcome").path("errorCode").asText())
            .isEqualTo("CANCELLED");
        revokedAt =
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT revoked_at FROM studio_dispatch_ledger WHERE execution_id=?",
                Object.class,
                state.execution);
        assertThat(
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT cancel_requested_at FROM studio_dispatch_ledger WHERE execution_id=?",
                    Object.class,
                    state.execution))
            .isNull();
        assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
        p3AssertBinding(state.execution, scope, publication);
        p3AssertUsed(null, state.execution);
      }
      assertThat(revokedAt).isNotNull();
      String ownerColumn = path == 1 ? "session_id" : "execution_id";
      Object owner = path == 1 ? state.guidedSession : state.execution;
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                      + ownerColumn
                      + "=? AND secret_id='PASS' AND row_kind='RELEASE' AND outcome='RESOLVED'",
                  Long.class,
                  owner))
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                      + ownerColumn
                      + "=? AND secret_id='PASS' AND decision_stage='LIVE_RECHECK' AND"
                      + " outcome='REVOKED_MID_RUN'",
                  Long.class,
                  owner))
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - reads)
          .isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).hasSize(1);
      assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isTrue();
      assertThat(P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()).isZero();
      p3AssertAuditScope(state.guidedSession, state.execution, scope);
      p3CleanupOwnedResources();
      p4AwaitRevocationObserverCompletion(state);
      p4AssertAuditLineageAfterCleanup(state);
      String table = path == 1 ? "studio_guided_sessions" : "studio_dispatch_ledger";
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT revoked_at FROM " + table + " WHERE " + ownerColumn + "=?",
                  Object.class,
                  owner))
          .isEqualTo(revokedAt);
      p3Scan(List.of(value));
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "revocation-first-pair-" + path,
          C.object()
              .put("path", path)
              .put("cutoffNanosIncludingQueues", state.cutoffAt - state.flippedAt)
              .put("ackBracketMicrosSameNodeClock", state.ackBracketMicros)
              .put("identifiedInFlightOrdinal", state.inFlightOrdinal)
              .put("freshSecretReads", 1)
              .put("residualSettlementCount", state.residualSettlements)
              .put("residualFulfilled", state.residualFulfilled)
              .put("residualRejected", !state.residualFulfilled)
              .put("acceptedPostDistinctFromPromiseSettlement", true)
              .put("priorPositiveUsagePreserved", true)
              .put("actualLiveRecheckRows", 1)
              .put("nativeEntryOnlyNotDomWrite", true)
              .put("originalOwnerCleanupConfirmed", true));
    } finally {
      state.releaseSignIn.countDown();
    }
  }

  private int p4StartObserverPhase(int count) throws Exception {
    if (P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get() > 0) {
      p4AssertObserverPhaseCompleted(P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get());
    }
    assertThat(p3OwnedGuidedCookies).isEmpty();
    assertThat(p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies).isEmpty();
    P4_EFFECT_REGISTRATIONS.set(0);
    P4_PASSWORD_WRITES.set(0);
    P4_NATIVE_SUBMITS.set(0);
    p4PasswordControls = count;
    int phase = P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.incrementAndGet();
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer.json"),
        C.object().put("active", true).put("phase", phase).toString(),
        StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    return phase;
  }

  private boolean p4OriginalCloseSettled(int phase) throws IOException {
    Path receipt = TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer-settled");
    if (!Files.exists(receipt)) {
      return false;
    }
    List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(receipt, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    for (String line : lines) {
      if (!line.matches("[1-9][0-9]{0,8}")) {
        P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
        return false;
      }
    }
    return lines.contains(Integer.toString(phase));
  }

  private void p4AssertObserverPhaseCompleted(int phase) throws IOException {
    P4RevocationState selected = p4Revocation;
    if (selected != null && selected.c6Kind != 0 && selected.c6Phase == phase) {
      long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos();
      synchronized (selected) {
        while (!selected.c6NativeClose && !selected.failed && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
          long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
          if (remaining <= 0) {
            break;
          }
          try {
            java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.timedWait(selected, remaining);
          } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new IOException("C6 close witness interrupted");
          }
        }
      }
      assertThat(selected.c6NativeClose).as("original SDK close was actually received").isTrue();
    }
    if (P8_C6_CLOSED_UNKNOWN.contains(phase)) {
      // Confirmed original close with an unavailable realm fence is UNKNOWN effects.
      // This branch supplies no completed fence, no zero-write or no-effect evidence.
      assertThat(P4_CLOSED_PHASES.contains(phase)).isFalse();
      return;
    }
    assertThat(p4OriginalCloseSettled(phase))
        .as("original SDK close settled after selected fence ACK completion")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.get())
        .as("all actual notification ACKs stayed valid")
        .isFalse();
    assertThat(Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer-failed")))
        .as("original public SDK fence stayed available")
        .isFalse();
    assertThat(P4_CLOSED_PHASES.contains(phase))
        .as("actual original before-close fence ACK")
        .isTrue();
    List<P4DocumentWitness> documents =
        P4_DOCUMENTS.values().stream().filter(witness -> witness.phase == phase).toList();
    assertThat(documents).isNotEmpty();
    for (P4DocumentWitness witness : documents) {
      synchronized (witness) {
        assertThat(witness.fenced && witness.fenceCompleted)
            .as("actual final fence ACK and correlated completion receipt")
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(witness.acknowledged.size()).isEqualTo(witness.events.size());
        assertThat(witness.events.containsValue("registered")).isTrue();
      }
    }
  }

  private void p4AssertRegisteredAndNoPasswordEffects() throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos();
    while (!p4OriginalCloseSettled(P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get()) && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      Thread.sleep(20);
    }
    p4AssertObserverPhaseCompleted(P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get());
    assertThat(P4_EFFECT_REGISTRATIONS.get()).isPositive();
    assertThat(P4_PASSWORD_WRITES.get()).isZero();
    assertThat(P4_NATIVE_SUBMITS.get()).isZero();
    assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).isEmpty();
    assertThat(P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()).isZero();
  }

  private static final class P8PendingTiming {
    private static final String[] POINTS = {
      "WaitBegin",
      "WaitExit",
      "NativeEntryIngress",
      "FocusAccepted",
      "Probe1WriteComplete",
      "PendingIngress",
      "PendingAccepted",
      "NativeSettleIngress"
    };
    final long origin = System.nanoTime();
    final long[] times = new long[8];
    final boolean[] observed = new boolean[8];
    boolean selected, waitResultObserved, waitResult, nativeEntryAccepted, nativeSettleAccepted;
    int nativeOrdinal, refusalOrdinal;
    boolean refusalScopeKnown, diagnosticFailed;
    long refusalNanos;
    boolean provisionalFocusLossObserved;
    ObjectNode early;

    synchronized void mark(int point) {
      if (!observed[point]) {
        observed[point] = true;
        times[point] = System.nanoTime() - origin;
      }
    }

    synchronized void nativeNotice(boolean entry, int ordinal, boolean accepted) {
      if (entry && !observed[2]) {
        nativeOrdinal = ordinal;
        mark(2);
      }
      if (ordinal != nativeOrdinal || !observed[2]) {
        return;
      }
      if (entry) {
        nativeEntryAccepted |= accepted;
      } else {
        mark(7);
        nativeSettleAccepted |= accepted;
      }
    }

    synchronized void refuse(int site, boolean scopeKnown) {
      if (refusalOrdinal == 0) {
        refusalOrdinal = site;
        refusalNanos = System.nanoTime() - origin;
        refusalScopeKnown = scopeKnown;
      }
    }

    synchronized void waitExit(boolean returned, boolean result) {
      mark(1);
      waitResultObserved = returned;
      waitResult = returned && result;
      early = snapshot(); // Memory only before the original assertion/exception exits.
    }

    synchronized ObjectNode snapshot() {
      ObjectNode out =
          C.object()
              .put("diagSelectedStateObserved", selected)
              .put("diagSameJavaMonotonicClock", true)
              .put("diagIngressNotNativeApiClock", true)
              .put("diagWaitResultObserved", waitResultObserved)
              .put("diagWaitResult", waitResult)
              .put("diagNativeOrdinalObserved", observed[2])
              .put("diagNativeOrdinal", nativeOrdinal)
              .put("diagNativeEntryAccepted", nativeEntryAccepted)
              .put("diagNativeSettleAccepted", nativeSettleAccepted)
              .put("diagFirstRefusalObserved", refusalOrdinal != 0)
              .put("diagFirstRefusalOrdinal", refusalOrdinal)
              .put("diagFirstRefusalElapsedNanos", refusalNanos)
              .put("diagFirstRefusalScopeKnown", refusalScopeKnown)
              .put("diagProvisionalFocusLossObserved", provisionalFocusLossObserved)
              .put("diagObservationFailed", diagnosticFailed);
      for (int i = 0; i < POINTS.length; i++) {
        out.put("diag" + POINTS[i] + "Observed", observed[i]);
        out.put("diag" + POINTS[i] + "ElapsedNanos", times[i]);
      }
      return out;
    }
  }

  private static void p8TimingRefusal(P4RevocationState state, int site) {
    if (state != null && state.c6Timing != null) {
      state.c6Timing.refuse(site, false);
    }
  }

  private static void p8TimingRefusal(P4RevocationState state, int site, boolean scopeKnown) {
    if (state != null && state.c6Timing != null) {
      state.c6Timing.refuse(site, scopeKnown);
    }
  }

  private static boolean p8CaptureFirstPendingWait(P4RevocationState state)
      throws InterruptedException {
    boolean returned = false, result = false;
    state.c6Timing.mark(0);
    try {
      result = state.c6Pending.await(15, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS);
      returned = true;
      return result;
    } finally {
      try {
        state.c6Timing.waitExit(returned, result);
      } catch (Throwable diagnosticFailure) {
        // Preserve the original await result/exception/interrupt; retain no raw throwable.
        state.c6Timing.diagnosticFailed = true;
      }
    }
  }

  private static void p8WritePendingTiming(int kind, C5Capture capture, boolean quarantine)
      throws Exception {
    P8PendingTiming timing = capture.c6Timing;
    ObjectNode record = capture.c6Acceptance == null ? C.object() : capture.c6Acceptance.deepCopy();
    ObjectNode late = timing.snapshot();
    record.setAll(late);
    record
        .put("diagSnapshotPartialNotClosureWitness", true)
        .put("diagQuarantineRetained", quarantine)
        .put("diagEarlySnapshotObserved", timing.early != null);
    if (timing.early != null) {
      for (var fields = timing.early.fields(); fields.hasNext(); ) {
        var field = fields.next();
        record.set("early" + field.getKey(), field.getValue().deepCopy());
      }
    }
    p3WriteEvidence("c6-" + kind, record); // Only original numeric receipt, only case-finally.
  }

  // destroyForcibly() also answers false when the pinned process exited and was reaped after the
  // caller's identity check: Chromium children exit by themselves once the primary is cut. A zombie
  // still accepts SIGKILL; only a reaped pid refuses. Release is still proven by p6AwaitProcesses.
  private static boolean p8KillRefusedWhileAlive(ProcessHandle pinned) {
    return !pinned.destroyForcibly() && pinned.isAlive();
  }

  // R10-rev2 I3: in C6 kinds 1-2 a runner 'control-window' self-stop (it missed a secret control
  // window by itself) fails as a precondition, with the I2 control-read numbers and the R10-rev2-2
  // IPC diagnostic in the description. Nothing is retried or relaxed.
  private static void p10AssertNoControlWindowSelfStop(P4RevocationState state, long logStart)
      throws IOException {
    long selfStops =
        p8RunnerLines(logStart, state.execution).stream()
            .filter(
                line ->
                    "run.cancel_requested".equals(line.path("event").asText())
                        && "control-window".equals(line.path("source").asText()))
            .count();
    if (selfStops == 0) {
      return;
    }
    assertThat(selfStops)
        .as(
            "R10-rev2 I3 precondition: runner control-window self-stop in C6 kind %s;"
                + " I2 control reads %s; IPC diagnostic %s",
            state.c6Kind, p10ControlReadNumbers(state), p10IpcDiagnostics(state.execution))
        .isZero();
  }

  // The producer-2 sample's numeric control-read keys for this case (I2), or a fixed marker.
  private static String p10ControlReadNumbers(P4RevocationState state) {
    try {
      Path file = TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-producers").resolve(state.ordinal + "-2.json");
      if (!Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
          || Files.size(file) > 1024) {
        return "{\"sample\":\"ABSENT\"}";
      }
      JsonNode sample = JSON.readTree(Files.readString(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      ObjectNode numbers = C.object();
      for (String key : P10_PRODUCER2_READ_KEYS) {
        JsonNode value = sample.path(key);
        if (value.isIntegralNumber()) {
          numbers.put(key, value.longValue());
        } else if (value.isBoolean()) {
          numbers.put(key, value.booleanValue());
        }
      }
      return numbers.toString();
    } catch (IOException | RuntimeException unreadable) {
      return "{\"sample\":\"UNREADABLE\"}";
    }
  }

  // R10b: what a failed C6 pending wait saw. Fixed codes, booleans and numbers only.
  private String p10PendingWaitState(UUID execution) {
    ObjectNode seen = C.object();
    try {
      var row =
          jdbc.queryForMap(
              "SELECT e.status,l.state AS ledger_state,"
                  + " e.browser_started_at IS NOT NULL AS browser_started"
                  + " FROM ui_test_executions e LEFT JOIN studio_dispatch_ledger l"
                  + " ON l.execution_id=e.id AND l.execution_accepted_at=e.started_at"
                  + " WHERE e.id=?",
              execution);
      seen.put(
          "status",
          closedDiagnostic(
              String.valueOf(row.get("status")),
              "PENDING",
              "RUNNING",
              "PASSED",
              "FAILED",
              "ERROR"));
      seen.put(
          "ledgerState",
          closedDiagnostic(
              String.valueOf(row.get("ledger_state")),
              "QUEUED",
              "CLAIMED",
              "DISPATCHING",
              "ADMITTED",
              "RUNNING",
              "FINALIZING",
              "DONE"));
      if (row.get("browser_started") instanceof Boolean started) {
        seen.put("browserStartedAt", started ? "SET" : "NULL");
      }
    } catch (RuntimeException unavailable) {
      seen.put("executionRow", "UNAVAILABLE");
    }
    JsonNode report = P3_FINAL_REPORTS.get(execution);
    if (report == null) {
      seen.put("final", "NOT_RECEIVED");
    } else {
      seen.put("finalErrorCode", p10Code(report.path("outcome").path("errorCode")));
      seen.put("finalErrorMessage", p10Code(report.path("outcome").path("errorMessage")));
    }
    seen.set("ipcDiagnostics", p10IpcDiagnostics(execution));
    return seen.toString();
  }

  private static String p10Code(JsonNode value) {
    if (value == null || value.isNull() || value.isMissingNode()) {
      return "NULL";
    }
    return value.isTextual() && value.asText().matches("[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,63}")
        ? value.asText()
        : "TEXT";
  }

  // R10-rev2-2: parent and child IPC attribution lines for one execution, re-validated field by
  // field (numbers, booleans and fixed codes only). Joined to the child by pid and execution.
  private static ArrayNode p10IpcDiagnostics(UUID execution) {
    ArrayNode out = JSON.createArrayNode();
    String id = execution.toString();
    try {
      List<JsonNode> children = p10DiagnosticLines(P10_CHILD_IPC_DIAGNOSTICS);
      for (JsonNode parent : p10DiagnosticLines(P10_PARENT_IPC_DIAGNOSTICS)) {
        if (!id.equals(parent.path("executionId").asText())) {
          continue;
        }
        ObjectNode safe = out.addObject();
        for (String key :
            List.of(
                "ordinal",
                "pid",
                "case",
                "c6",
                "receivedCount",
                "results",
                "killCount",
                "exitCode",
                "loopDelayMaxMs",
                "loopDelayP99Ms",
                "controlReadCount")) {
          JsonNode value = parent.path(key);
          if (value.isIntegralNumber() && value.canConvertToLong()) {
            safe.put(key, value.longValue());
          }
        }
        for (String key : List.of("resultOk", "resultSchemaValid")) {
          if (parent.path(key).isBoolean()) {
            safe.put(key, parent.path(key).booleanValue());
          }
        }
        if (parent.path("resultErrorCode").isTextual()) {
          safe.put("resultErrorCode", p10Code(parent.path("resultErrorCode")));
        }
        if (parent.path("exitSignal").isTextual()
            && parent.path("exitSignal").asText().matches("SIG[A-Z0-9]{1,12}")) {
          safe.put("exitSignal", parent.path("exitSignal").asText());
        }
        ArrayNode received = safe.putArray("received");
        for (JsonNode kind : parent.path("received")) {
          received.add(
              closedDiagnostic(
                  kind.asText(), "ready", "result", "step-started", "step-finished", "other"));
        }
        ArrayNode kills = safe.putArray("kills");
        for (JsonNode site : parent.path("kills")) {
          kills.add(
              closedDiagnostic(site.asText(), "violation-catch", "performStop-grace", "other"));
        }
        ArrayNode reads = safe.putArray("controlReads");
        for (JsonNode read : parent.path("controlReads")) {
          ArrayNode numbers = reads.addArray();
          for (JsonNode number : read) {
            numbers.add(number.isIntegralNumber() ? number.longValue() : -1);
          }
        }
        long pid = parent.path("pid").asLong(-1);
        for (JsonNode child : children) {
          if (child.path("pid").asLong(-2) != pid
              || !(child.path("executionId").isNull()
                  || id.equals(child.path("executionId").asText()))) {
            continue;
          }
          ObjectNode childSafe = safe.putObject("child");
          for (String key : List.of("readySent", "resultSent", "resultOk")) {
            if (child.path(key).isBoolean()) {
              childSafe.put(key, child.path(key).booleanValue());
            }
          }
          if (child.path("resultErrorCode").isTextual()) {
            childSafe.put("resultErrorCode", p10Code(child.path("resultErrorCode")));
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (IOException | RuntimeException unavailable) {
      out.addObject().put("diagnostic", "UNAVAILABLE");
    }
    return out;
  }

  private static List<JsonNode> p10DiagnosticLines(String name) throws IOException {
    Path file = TEMP.resolve(name);
    if (!Files.exists(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
      return List.of();
    }
    if (Files.isSymbolicLink(file)
        || !Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
        || Files.size(file) > 16 * 1024 * 1024) {
      throw new IOException("IPC diagnostic confinement failed");
    }
    List<JsonNode> lines = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    for (String line : Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
      try {
        JsonNode parsed = JSON.readTree(line);
        if (parsed != null && parsed.isObject() && parsed.path("v").asInt() == 1) {
          lines.add(parsed);
        }
      } catch (IOException partialLine) {
        // A line still being appended is skipped, never repaired.
      }
    }
    return lines;
  }

  // C6 adds four concrete cuts to the existing fixture. All observers forward original methods;
  // receipts below establish observation/ownership only, never execution or usage authority.
  private void p8C6Case(int kind) throws Exception {
    String value = "C6-PRIVATE-" + UUID.randomUUID() + "-+/界";
    C5Capture capture = new C5Capture(List.of(value), false);
    capture.cacheNonemptyRequired = kind <= 2;
    capture.c6Timing = kind == 2 || kind == 3 ? new P8PendingTiming() : null;
    c5Capture = capture;
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      capture.writeProfileValues();
      p3Case(() -> p8C6Run(kind, value, capture));
    } catch (Exception | Error failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      P4RevocationState owned = p4Revocation;
      if (owned != null && owned.c6Kind == 3) {
        // p3Case already attempted original owned-context cleanup. Residual actual writers
        // remain pinned by start identity; attempt every release without masking the primary.
        for (P6OwnedPid member : owned.c6BrowserTree) {
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              failures,
              () -> {
                if (member.handle().isAlive()) {
                  p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
                  if (p8KillRefusedWhileAlive(member.handle())) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException("C6 residual owned writer kill refused");
                  }
                }
              });
        }
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              assertThat(owned.c6BrowserTree.isEmpty()).isFalse();
              p6AwaitProcesses(owned.c6BrowserTree, true);
              owned.c6BrowserTreeGone = true;
            });
      }
      // Independently attempt the EXISTING selected producer restoration/seal command even
      // when original owner close failed. It cannot clear sticky failures or quarantine.
      if (owned != null && owned.c6Kind != 0 && !owned.c6ProducerSealed) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              p4SealPrivateProducersAfterOwnerCleanup(owned);
              owned.c6ProducerSealed = true;
            });
      }
      if (!cleanupUnconfirmed) {
        if (owned != null
            && owned.c6Kind != 0
            && (!owned.restored || (owned.c6Kind != 3 && !owned.active.isEmpty()))) {
          cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
          failures.add(
              new IllegalStateException(
                  "C6 exact restoration or original native drain remains unresolved"));
        }
        // Retain capture/hooks until actual owner closure and final producer acknowledgements.
        if (!cleanupUnconfirmed) {
          p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> p3Scan(List.of(value)));
          p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::writeNumericEvidence);
          p3AttemptTeardown(failures, capture::close);
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              failures,
              () ->
                  Files.writeString(
                      TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"),
                      "{\"active\":false}",
                      StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
          c5Capture = null;
          p4Revocation = null;
        }
      } else {
        failures.add(new IllegalStateException("C6 original owner cleanup remains unconfirmed"));
      }
      if (capture.c6Timing != null) {
        if (capture.c6Timing.diagnosticFailed) {
          failures.add(new IllegalStateException("C6 numeric timing observation failed"));
        }
        try {
          p8WritePendingTiming(kind, capture, cleanupUnconfirmed);
        } catch (Exception | Error diagnosticWriteFailure) {
          // No raw throwable/cause/value is exported; preserve original primary and quarantine.
          failures.add(new IllegalStateException("C6 numeric timing receipt persistence failed"));
        }
      }
      if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
        if (primary != null) {
          failures.forEach(primary::addSuppressed);
        } else {
          throwTeardownFailures(failures);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private void p8C6Run(int kind, String value, C5Capture capture) throws Exception {
    assertThat(kind).isBetween(1, 4);
    assertThat(p4Revocation).as("prior owned observer is genuinely closed").isNull();
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant("CSI-456", "fixture-other");
    UUID test = p3CreateSource("CSI-456", "fixture-other", true);
    // Legitimate test configuration precedes learning/publication/admission, never alters a job.
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT timeout_seconds FROM ui_tests WHERE id=?", Integer.class, test))
        .isEqualTo(30);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of("CSI-456", "fixture-other", test);
    p3ExpectedPassword = value;
    p3Seed(scope, value);
    P3Publication publication = null;
    if (kind <= 2) {
      publication = p4PositiveNativeControl(test);
    }
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    long ordinaryLogStart = kind <= 2 ? Files.size(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log")) : -1;
    P4RevocationState state =
        new P4RevocationState(P4_REVOCATION_CASE.incrementAndGet(), kind <= 2 ? 2 : 1, test, jdbc);
    state.c6Kind = kind;
    state.c6Capture = capture;
    state.c6Timing = capture.c6Timing;
    if (state.c6Timing != null) {
      state.c6Timing.selected = true;
    }
    state.c6Phase = p4StartObserverPhase(1);
    p4Revocation = state;
    p4WriteRevocationMode(state);
    if (kind >= 3) {
      state.guidedSession = p3Start(test, "fixture-other");
      capture.selectedGuidedSession = state.guidedSession;
    } else {
      state.execution = p3Execute(test);
      p4WriteRevocationMode(state);
      p3AssertBinding(state.execution, scope, publication);
      capture.watchExecution(state.execution, p3Cookie);
      assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
    }
    List<P6OwnedPid> browserTree = List.of();
    List<P6OwnedPid> ordinaryTree = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    if (kind < 4) {
      boolean pending =
          state.c6Timing == null
              ? state.c6Pending.await(15, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)
              : p8CaptureFirstPendingWait(state);
      if (!pending && kind <= 2) {
        // R10-rev2 I3 before R10b: a runner control-window self-stop explains a missed wait.
        p10AssertNoControlWindowSelfStop(state, ordinaryLogStart);
      }
      assertThat(pending)
          .as(
              "actual sole CDP invocation plus contemporaneous original Node pending"
                  + " acknowledgement"
                  + (pending || kind > 2 ? "" : "; " + p10PendingWaitState(state.execution)))
          .isTrue();
      synchronized (state) {
        assertThat(state.failed).isFalse();
        assertThat(state.c6PrimitiveAt).isPositive();
        assertThat(state.c6PendingAt).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(state.c6PrimitiveAt);
        assertThat(state.c6FillEntries).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(state.active).containsOnlyKeys(state.inFlightOrdinal);
        assertThat(state.active.get(state.inFlightOrdinal)).isEqualTo(1);
        if (kind == 1) {
          assertThat(state.c6DeadlineMarginMs)
              .as(
                  "actual admitted overall deadline remains strictly later than actual step"
                      + " deadline")
              .isPositive();
        }
        if (kind == 1) {
          state.c6CutAt = System.nanoTime();
        }
      }
      if (kind <= 2) {
        List<P6OwnedPid> children = p3OrdinaryChildren(ordinaryWorker);
        assertThat(children.size()).isEqualTo(1);
        P6OwnedPid child = children.getFirst();
        ordinaryTree.add(child);
        ordinaryTree.addAll(
            child
                .handle()
                .descendants()
                .limit(129)
                .map(GuidedPublicationReplayIT::p6SnapshotPid)
                .toList());
        assertThat(ordinaryTree.size()).isBetween(1, 128);
      }
      P6OwnedPid primaryBrowser = kind == 3 ? p8OwnedPrimaryBrowser(state) : null;
      if (kind == 3) {
        assertThat(primaryBrowser).isNotNull();
        browserTree =
            java.util.stream.Stream.concat(
                    java.util.stream.Stream.of(primaryBrowser),
                    primaryBrowser
                        .handle()
                        .descendants()
                        .map(GuidedPublicationReplayIT::p6SnapshotPid))
                .toList();
        assertThat(browserTree.size()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(128);
        state.c6BrowserTree = browserTree;
      }
      if (kind == 2 || kind == 3) {
        // Reconfirm genuine original pending after ownership discovery and before the real cut.
        p8WritePendingProbe(state, 2);
        assertThat(state.c6CutPending.await(2, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS))
            .as("contemporaneous original Node pending acknowledgement at actual fault cut")
            .isTrue();
        synchronized (state) {
          assertThat(state.active).containsOnlyKeys(state.inFlightOrdinal);
          assertThat(state.active.get(state.inFlightOrdinal)).isEqualTo(1);
          state.c6CutAt = System.nanoTime();
          assertThat(state.c6CutAt - state.c6PendingAt)
              .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofMillis(250).toNanos());
        }
      }
      if (kind == 2) {
        p3Call(
            p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/executions/" + state.execution + "/cancel", C.object(), 202);
      } else if (kind == 3) {
        assertThat(primaryBrowser).isNotNull();
        // Inventory was captured before the final real pending ACK, not after its cut clock.
        p6SameProcess(primaryBrowser.handle(), primaryBrowser.started());
        assertThat(primaryBrowser.handle().destroyForcibly()).isTrue();
        assertThat(System.nanoTime() - state.c6PendingAt)
            .as("actual verified-primary signal stayed contemporaneous with original pending ACK")
            .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofMillis(250).toNanos());
        assertThat(guidedWorker.isAlive())
            .as("genuine browser loss leaves owning worker alive")
            .isTrue();
        var residualFailures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
        for (P6OwnedPid member : browserTree) {
          if (member.handle().pid() == primaryBrowser.handle().pid()) {
            continue;
          }
          p3AttemptTeardown(
              residualFailures,
              () -> {
                if (member.handle().isAlive()) {
                  try {
                    p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
                    P6ProcessStatus actual = p6Status(member.handle());
                    if ("X".equals(actual.state()) && !member.handle().isAlive()) {
                      return;
                    }
                    assertThat(actual.group()).isEqualTo(member.group());
                    p6SameProcess(member.handle(), member.started());
                  } catch (Throwable originalCheckFailure) {
                    boolean confirmedGone = false;
                    try {
                      confirmedGone = !member.handle().isAlive();
                    } catch (Throwable unknownLiveness) {
                      // Unknown liveness preserves the original identity/status failure.
                    }
                    if (confirmedGone) {
                      return;
                    }
                    throw originalCheckFailure;
                  }
                  if (p8KillRefusedWhileAlive(member.handle())) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException("C6 captured browser descendant kill refused");
                  }
                }
              });
        }
        assertThat(guidedWorker.isAlive())
            .as("captured browser descendant release leaves owning worker alive")
            .isTrue();
        if (!residualFailures.isEmpty()) {
          var unresolved =
              new IllegalStateException("C6 captured browser descendant release unconfirmed");
          residualFailures.forEach(unresolved::addSuppressed);
          throw unresolved;
        }
      }
      // kind1 has no synthetic timeout: the unchanged original step timer supplies the cut.
    }
    if (kind <= 2) {
      JsonNode result;
      try {
        result = p3AwaitExecution(state.execution, "ERROR");
      } catch (AssertionError terminal) {
        try {
          p10AssertNoControlWindowSelfStop(state, ordinaryLogStart);
        } catch (AssertionError selfStop) {
          selfStop.addSuppressed(terminal);
          throw selfStop;
        }
        throw terminal;
      }
      // R10-rev2 I3: precondition before any outcome assertion; the test still fails on a miss.
      p10AssertNoControlWindowSelfStop(state, ordinaryLogStart);
      JsonNode report = p3RunnerReport(state.execution);
      assertThat(report.path("outcome").path("errorCode").asText())
          .isEqualTo(kind == 1 ? "RUN_ERROR" : "CANCELLED");
      if (kind == 1) {
        assertThat(report.path("outcome").path("errorMessage").asText())
            .isEqualTo("OUTCOME_UNKNOWN");
      }
      if (kind == 2) {
        assertThat(result.path("errorCode").asText()).isEqualTo("CANCELLED");
      }
      assertThat(report.path("steps").get(1).path("failure").asText())
          .isEqualTo(kind == 1 ? "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN" : "CANCELLED");
      assertThat(report.path("steps").get(2).path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("SKIPPED");
      assertThat(report.path("steps").get(2).path("startedAtMs").isNull()).isTrue();
      assertThat(report.path("secretUse").has("PASS")).as("unknown effects stay sparse").isFalse();
      assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
      p3AssertBinding(state.execution, scope, publication);
    } else {
      awaitState(state.guidedSession, "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME");
      List<Map<String, Object>> consumed =
          jdbc.queryForList(
              "SELECT document::text AS ledger FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND"
                  + " step_id=? AND proposal->'operation'->'value'->>'kind'='secretRef'",
              state.guidedSession,
              TWO);
      assertThat(consumed).hasSize(1);
      JsonNode ledger = C.parse(String.valueOf(consumed.getFirst().get("ledger")));
      assertThat(ledger.path("state").asText()).isEqualTo("UNKNOWN_OUTCOME");
      assertThat(ledger.path("outcomeCode").asText()).isEqualTo("ACK_LOST");
      assertThat(ledger.path("dispatchCount").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND step_id=?",
                  Long.class,
                  state.guidedSession,
                  THREE))
          .isZero();
      p3AwaitTeardown(state.guidedSession);
    }
    assertThat(
            COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                - reads)
        .isEqualTo(1);
    String ownerColumn = kind <= 2 ? "execution_id" : "session_id";
    Object owner = kind <= 2 ? state.execution : state.guidedSession;
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                    + ownerColumn
                    + "=? AND row_kind='RELEASE' AND outcome='RESOLVED' AND secret_id='PASS'",
                Long.class,
                owner))
        .isEqualTo(1);
    // Generic wire ACK_LOST is not evidence of acknowledgement-loss usage, USED or REFUSED.
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                    + ownerColumn
                    + "=? AND row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome IN"
                    + " ('USED','REFUSED','NOT_USED','ACK_LOST') AND secret_id='PASS'",
                Long.class,
                owner))
        .isZero();
    p3AssertAuditScope(state.guidedSession, state.execution, scope);
    if (kind == 3) {
      // Existing seal command is authorized only after actual original stop and captured tree exit.
      p6AwaitProcesses(browserTree, true);
      state.c6BrowserTreeGone = true;
      p4SealPrivateProducersAfterOwnerCleanup(state);
      state.c6ProducerSealed =
          true; // Observer output closed; its raw SDK sealed field remains false.
    }
    p3CleanupOwnedResources();
    if (!ordinaryTree.isEmpty()) {
      p6AwaitProcesses(ordinaryTree, true);
    }
    boolean parentValuesWipedObserved = false;
    if (kind <= 2) {
      JsonNode receipt =
          C.parse(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT observed_admission_receipt::text FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?",
                  String.class,
                  state.execution));
      assertThat(receipt.path("kind").asText()).isEqualTo("observed");
      assertThat(
              receipt
                  .path("request")
                  .path("executionId")
                  .asText()
                  .equals(state.execution.toString()))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(p3ParentEvents(ordinaryLogStart, List.of(receipt), false)[4]).isEqualTo(1);
      parentValuesWipedObserved = true;
    }
    p8AssertUnknownUsageLineage(state);
    if (!browserTree.isEmpty()) {
      p6AwaitProcesses(browserTree, true);
    }
    p8AwaitC6Closed(state);
    if (kind == 2) {
      assertThat(state.cutoffAt - state.c6CutAt)
          .as("real public cancel to valid original child Stop includes network and queues")
          .isPositive()
          .isLessThanOrEqualTo(Duration.ofSeconds(2).toNanos());
    }
    if (!state.c6ProducerSealed) {
      p4SealPrivateProducersAfterOwnerCleanup(state);
      state.c6ProducerSealed = true;
    }
    p8AssertManagedNativeState(state);
    assertThat(state.c6FillEntries).isEqualTo(kind == 4 ? 0 : 1);
    assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).isEmpty();
    assertThat(P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()).isZero();
    if (kind == 4) {
      p4AssertRegisteredAndNoPasswordEffects();
      assertThat(state.c6RawCalls).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(state.c6LiteralHits).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(state.c6EncodedHits).isEqualTo(2);
      assertThat(state.c6CallbackRedacted).isEqualTo(1);
      p8AssertInjectedProducedSinks(state, value);
    } else {
      // No absent page fence is promoted into a zero-write assertion.
      assertThat(state.c6NativeClose).isTrue();
      if (!state.c6FenceAvailable) {
        assertThat(P8_C6_CLOSED_UNKNOWN).contains(state.c6Phase);
      }
      if (kind == 3) {
        // This hook may observe managed CONTEXT_LOST; it supplies no genuine native-error credit.
        assertThat(state.c6RawCalls).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(state.c6LiteralHits).isZero();
        assertThat(state.c6EncodedHits).isZero();
      }
    }
    p3Scan(List.of(value));
    ObjectNode acceptance =
        C.object()
            .put("kind", kind)
            .put("realFocusIngress", state.c6FocusAt > 0)
            .put("originalPendingAck", state.c6PendingAt > 0)
            .put(
                "sameJavaClockPrimitiveToAckNanos",
                state.c6PendingAt == 0 || state.c6PrimitiveAt == 0
                    ? 0
                    : state.c6PendingAt - state.c6PrimitiveAt)
            .put("actualStepOverallMarginMs", state.c6DeadlineMarginMs)
            .put("originalNativeFillEntries", state.c6FillEntries)
            .put("originalCloseConfirmed", state.c6NativeClose)
            .put("pageEffectFenceAvailable", state.c6FenceAvailable)
            .put("effectKnowledgeUnknown", !state.c6FenceAvailable)
            .put("selectedInterruptedNotification", state.c6InterruptedNotification)
            .put("pendingSamples", state.c6PendingSamples)
            .put("rawCaptured", state.c6RawCalls)
            .put("rawLiteralHits", state.c6LiteralHits)
            .put("rawEncodedHits", state.c6EncodedHits)
            .put("producedRedactedCallback", state.c6CallbackRedacted)
            .put("realReleasedReads", 1)
            .put("ownedBrowserTreeGone", !browserTree.isEmpty())
            .put("parentValuesWipedObserved", parentValuesWipedObserved)
            .put("ownedOrdinaryChildTreeGone", !ordinaryTree.isEmpty())
            .put("ownedOrdinaryChildTreeCount", ordinaryTree.size());
    if (capture.c6Timing != null) {
      capture.c6Acceptance = acceptance.deepCopy();
    }
    p3WriteEvidence("c6-" + kind, acceptance);
  }

  private void p8AssertUnknownUsageLineage(P4RevocationState state) {
    String ownerColumn = state.path == 1 ? "session_id" : "execution_id";
    Object owner = state.path == 1 ? state.guidedSession : state.execution;
    List<Map<String, Object>> releases =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                + ownerColumn
                + "=? AND secret_id='PASS'"
                + " AND row_kind='RELEASE' AND decision_stage='DISPATCH' AND outcome='RESOLVED'",
            owner);
    List<Map<String, Object>> usages =
        jdbc.queryForList(
            "SELECT * FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE "
                + ownerColumn
                + "=? AND secret_id='PASS'"
                + " AND row_kind='USAGE'",
            owner);
    assertThat(releases.size()).as("one real resolved release").isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(usages.size()).as("one actual terminal usage summary").isEqualTo(1);
    Map<String, Object> release = releases.getFirst(), usage = usages.getFirst();
    assertThat("UNKNOWN_OUTCOME".equals(usage.get("usage_outcome")))
        .as("actual terminal unknown effect, never missing summary")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat("RESOLVED".equals(usage.get("outcome")) && release.get("vault_version") != null)
        .as("actual released version lineage")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(
            java.util.Objects.equals(release.get("id"), release.get("release_id"))
                && java.util.Objects.equals(release.get("id"), usage.get("release_id"))
                && release.get("affected_release_id") == null
                && usage.get("affected_release_id") == null)
        .as("actual immutable release usage linkage")
        .isTrue();
    p4CompareAuditScope(release, usage);
    assertThat(p4AuditOwnerIdentity(state, release))
        .as("actual consumed admission or accepted observed attempt lineage")
        .isTrue();
  }

  private static void p8WritePendingProbe(P4RevocationState state, int probe) throws IOException {
    Path root = TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-producers");
    p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
    int producer = state.path == 1 ? 1 : 3;
    Path target = root.resolve(state.ordinal + "-" + producer + "-seal.json");
    Path temporary = root.resolve(state.ordinal + "-" + producer + "-probe-" + probe + ".tmp");
    Files.createFile(
        temporary,
        java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
            java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")));
    Files.writeString(
        temporary,
        C.object()
            .put("case", state.ordinal)
            .put("epoch", state.ordinal)
            .put("producer", producer)
            .put("probe", probe)
            .toString(),
        StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    Files.move(
        temporary,
        target,
        java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,
        java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
  }

  private void p8AwaitC6Closed(P4RevocationState state) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(15).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      synchronized (state) {
        if (state.c6NativeClose
            && state.restored
            && (state.c6Kind == 3 || state.active.isEmpty())
            && (state.path == 2 || state.closed)) {
          break;
        }
      }
      Thread.sleep(20);
    }
    assertThat(state.c6NativeClose).as("original SDK close receipt").isTrue();
    if (state.c6FenceAvailable) {
      assertThat(p4OriginalCloseSettled(state.c6Phase) && P4_CLOSED_PHASES.contains(state.c6Phase))
          .as("actual original healthy page fence and close receipt")
          .isTrue();
    } else {
      assertThat(
              P8_C6_CLOSED_UNKNOWN.contains(state.c6Phase)
                  && !P4_CLOSED_PHASES.contains(state.c6Phase))
          .as("actual original close with unavailable realm effects, never healthy by absence")
          .isTrue();
    }
    assertThat(state.restored)
        .as("exact descriptors restored after original dispatch lifetime")
        .isTrue();
    p8AssertManagedNativeState(state);
    if (state.path == 1) {
      assertThat(state.closed).isTrue();
    }
    assertThat(state.failed).isFalse();
    if (state.c6InterruptedNotification) {
      assertThat(
              state.c6Kind > 0
                  && state.c6Kind < 4
                  && state.c6PendingSamples > 0
                  && state.c6NativeClose
                  && !state.c6FenceAvailable
                  && P8_C6_CLOSED_UNKNOWN.contains(state.c6Phase))
          .as(
              "provisional focus response loss requires actual native pending and original realm"
                  + " closure")
          .isTrue();
    }
    assertThat(P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.get()).as("no unexplained page observer loss").isFalse();
    assertThat(Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer-failed")))
        .as("malformed ACK/fence and original close failures remain sticky")
        .isFalse();
  }

  private static void p8AssertManagedNativeState(P4RevocationState state) {
    if (state.c6Kind == 3) {
      // The retained actual native map is raw-settlement evidence, never reset by managed close.
      assertThat(state.active.size()).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1);
      if (!state.active.isEmpty()) {
        assertThat(state.active).containsOnlyKeys(state.inFlightOrdinal);
        assertThat(state.active.get(state.inFlightOrdinal)).isEqualTo(1);
      }
      assertThat(state.closed && state.restored && state.c6NativeClose && state.c6BrowserTreeGone)
          .isTrue();
    } else {
      assertThat(state.active).isEmpty();
    }
  }

  private P6OwnedPid p8OwnedPrimaryBrowser(P4RevocationState state) throws Exception {
    // A strict original SDK/runtime inode binding exists before any signal. Paths remain transient.
    Path root = TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-producers");
    p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(root);
    Path samplePath = root.resolve(state.ordinal + "-1.json");
    JsonNode sample = null;
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(2).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      if (Files.exists(samplePath, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
        assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(samplePath)).isFalse();
        assertThat(Files.isRegularFile(samplePath, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS))
            .isTrue();
        assertThat(Files.size(samplePath)).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1024);
        assertThat(Files.getPosixFilePermissions(samplePath))
            .isEqualTo(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"));
        sample = C.parse(Files.readString(samplePath));
        if (sample.path("contextBound").asBoolean()) {
          break;
        }
      }
      Thread.sleep(10);
    }
    assertThat(sample).as("original SDK launch and selected runtime identity sample").isNotNull();
    assertThat(sample.path("case").asInt()).isEqualTo(state.ordinal);
    assertThat(sample.path("producer").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(sample.path("epoch").asInt()).isEqualTo(state.ordinal);
    assertThat(sample.path("contextBound").asBoolean()).isTrue();
    assertThat(sample.path("failed").isBoolean() && !sample.path("failed").asBoolean()).isTrue();
    assertThat(sample.size()).isEqualTo(state.c6Kind == 3 ? 19 : 13);
    if (state.c6Kind == 3) {
      for (String field :
          List.of("highPending", "cdpCreationPending", "nativeActive", "restoreAttempt")) {
        assertThat(p4Number(sample, field)).isLessThanOrEqualTo(1000000);
      }
      assertThat(p4Number(sample, "busy")).isBetween(0, 2);
      assertThat(p4Number(sample, "restoreRefusal")).isBetween(0, 5);
    }
    long device = sample.path("profileDev").asLong(-1),
        inode = sample.path("profileIno").asLong(-1);
    assertThat(inode).isPositive();
    JsonNode runtime =
        C.parse(Files.readString(Path.of("../studio-runner/dist/runtime-versions.json")));
    JsonNode provenance = runtime.path("observedBrowserProvenance");
    assertThat(provenance.path("channel").asText()).isEqualTo("chromium");
    assertThat(provenance.path("headless").asBoolean()).isTrue();
    assertThat(provenance.path("chromiumSandbox").asBoolean()).isTrue();
    assertThat(provenance.path("args").size()).isZero();
    assertThat(provenance.path("revision").asText())
        .isEqualTo(runtime.path("observedChromiumBuild").asText());
    Path browsers =
        Path.of(
            System.getenv()
                .getOrDefault(
                    "PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH",
                    Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"), "Library", "Caches", "ms-playwright")
                        .toString()));
    Path pinned =
        browsers
            .resolve("chromium-" + provenance.path("revision").asText())
            .resolve(provenance.path("executableRelativePath").asText())
            .toRealPath();
    assertThat(
            HexFormat.of()
                .formatHex(MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(Files.readAllBytes(pinned))))
        .isEqualTo(provenance.path("executableSha256").asText());
    Instant workerStarted = guidedWorker.info().startInstant().orElseThrow();
    List<P6OwnedPid> matches = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    for (ProcessHandle candidate : guidedWorker.descendants().toList()) {
      P6OwnedPid actual = p6SnapshotPid(candidate);
      String command = candidate.info().command().orElse("");
      String[] arguments = candidate.info().arguments().orElse(new String[0]);
      if (command.isEmpty()
          || !Path.of(command).toRealPath().equals(pinned)
          || java.util.Arrays.stream(arguments)
              .anyMatch(argument -> argument.startsWith("--type="))) {
        continue;
      }
      List<String> profiles =
          java.util.Arrays.stream(arguments)
              .filter(argument -> argument.startsWith("--user-data-dir="))
              .toList();
      if (profiles.size() != 1) {
        continue;
      }
      Path profile = Path.of(profiles.getFirst().substring("--user-data-dir=".length()));
      if (Files.isSymbolicLink(profile)
          || !Files.isDirectory(profile, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
          || !profile.toRealPath().equals(profile)) {
        continue;
      }
      if (((Number) Files.getAttribute(profile, "unix:dev")).longValue() != device
          || ((Number) Files.getAttribute(profile, "unix:ino")).longValue() != inode) {
        continue;
      }
      assertThat(Files.getOwner(profile)).isEqualTo(Files.getOwner(TEMP));
      assertThat(Files.getPosixFilePermissions(profile))
          .isEqualTo(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rwx------"));
      p6SameProcess(candidate, actual.started());
      p6SameProcess(guidedWorker.toHandle(), workerStarted);
      assertThat(guidedWorker.descendants().anyMatch(child -> child.pid() == candidate.pid()))
          .isTrue();
      matches.add(actual);
    }
    assertThat(matches)
        .as("exact selected-context pinned full Chromium primary; never whole-worker substitute")
        .hasSize(1);
    return matches.getFirst();
  }

  private static void p8CaptureInjectedCallback(P4RevocationState state, byte[] bytes, int status) {
    try {
