      if (status < 200 || status >= 300 || bytes.length < 1 || bytes.length > 262144) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 actual callback acceptance/bound mismatch");
      }
      C5Capture capture = c5Capture;
      if (capture == null) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 original sink capture unavailable");
      }
      for (String value : capture.values) {
        assertJoinedSinkAbsent(Map.of("c6-actual-callback", bytes), value);
      }
      JsonNode outcome = C.parseWire(bytes);
      if (!outcome.path("errorMessage").isTextual()) {
        return;
      } // Earlier successes are not positives.
      if (!outcome.path("sessionId").asText().equals(state.guidedSession)
          || !outcome.path("ledger").path("state").asText().equals("UNKNOWN_OUTCOME")
          || !outcome.path("ledger").path("outcomeCode").asText().equals("ACK_LOST")
          || outcome.path("ledger").path("dispatchCount").asInt() != 1) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 consumed failure identity mismatch");
      }
      String message = outcome.path("errorMessage").asText();
      if (!message.contains("C6_RELEASED")
          || !message.contains("REDACTED")
          || message.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length > 2048
          || message.length() > 2048) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 real callback is not bounded/redacted");
      }
      synchronized (state) {
        state.c6CallbackRedacted++;
      }
    } catch (RuntimeException | AssertionError failure) {
      state.failed = true; // Sticky even if the original HTTP endpoint returns an error or retries.
    }
  }

  private void p8AssertInjectedProducedSinks(P4RevocationState state, String value)
      throws Exception {
    Path diagnostic =
        TEMP.resolve("journal")
            .resolve(C.hash(JSON.valueToTree(state.guidedSession)) + ".diagnostic.jsonl");
    assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(diagnostic)).isFalse();
    assertThat(Files.isRegularFile(diagnostic, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.size(diagnostic)).isPositive().isLessThanOrEqualTo(262144);
    byte[] diagnosticBytes = Files.readAllBytes(diagnostic);
    byte[] stderr = Files.readAllBytes(TEMP.resolve("guided.log"));
    // Scan privately before parse/content checks; assertion failures only show fixed scalars.
    assertJoinedSinkAbsent(
        Map.of("c6-original-stderr", stderr, "c6-real-diagnostic", diagnosticBytes), value);
    List<String> lines = new String(diagnosticBytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8).lines().toList();
    assertThat(lines.size()).as("actual bounded diagnostic record count").isEqualTo(1);
    JsonNode line;
    try {
      line = C.parse(lines.getFirst());
    } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
      throw new AssertionError("C6 privately scanned diagnostic parse failed");
    }
    assertThat(
            line.path("sessionId").asText().equals(state.guidedSession)
                && line.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO))
        .as("actual selected diagnostic identity")
        .isTrue();
    String message = line.path("errorMessage").asText();
    assertThat(message.contains("C6_RELEASED") && message.contains("REDACTED"))
        .as("actual diagnostic marker and redacted value")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(message.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length)
        .as("actual diagnostic UTF8 bytes")
        .isLessThanOrEqualTo(2048);
    assertThat(message.length()).as("actual diagnostic character count").isLessThanOrEqualTo(2048);
    assertThat(new String(stderr, StandardCharsets.UTF_8).contains(message))
        .as("actual original stderr contains the privately scanned redacted diagnostic")
        .isTrue();
  }

  private P3Publication p4PositiveNativeControl(UUID test) throws Exception {
    int phase = p4StartObserverPhase(1);
    String control = p3Start(test, "fixture-other");
    awaitFinalizing(control);
    assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).hasSize(1);
    assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isTrue();
    int beforePublicationReads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size();
    P3Publication publication = p3Publish(control);
    p4PublicationValueReads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size() - beforePublicationReads;
    assertThat(p4PublicationValueReads).isZero();
    p3CleanupOwnedResources();
    p4AssertObserverPhaseCompleted(phase);
    assertThat(P4_PASSWORD_WRITES.get()).isPositive();
    assertThat(P4_NATIVE_SUBMITS.get()).isEqualTo(1);
    p3AssertUsed(control, null);
    p3WriteEvidence(
        "native-positive-control-" + phase,
        C.object()
            .put("phase", phase)
            .put("realPasswordWrites", P4_PASSWORD_WRITES.get())
            .put("realNativeSubmits", P4_NATIVE_SUBMITS.get())
            .put("allNotificationsAcked", true)
            .put("originalOwnerCleanupConfirmed", true));
    p3OwnedGuidedCookies.clear();
    p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.clear();
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    return publication;
  }

  private void p4GuidedAdversarialPasswordCount(int count) throws Exception {
    Throwable primary = null;
    try {
      p4AssertRepositoryGraph();
      P4_WITNESS_FAILED.set(false);
      p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
      p4AdversarialPasswordSelection = false;
      P4_FIRST_GUIDED_REQUEST.set(null);
      P4_FIRST_GUARD.set(null);
      p3Grant("CSI-456", "fixture-other");
      UUID test = p3CreateSource("CSI-456", "fixture-other", true);
      VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of("CSI-456", "fixture-other", test);
      String value = "P4-GUIDED-COUNT-" + UUID.randomUUID();
      p3ExpectedPassword = value;
      p3Seed(scope, value);
      p4PositiveNativeControl(test);
      p4StartObserverPhase(count);
      p4AdversarialPasswordSelection = true;
      int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
      String id = p3Start(test, "fixture-other");
      awaitState(id, "FAILED");
      JsonNode publicResult =
          p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/result", null, 200);
      assertThat(publicResult.path("errorCode").asText())
          .isEqualTo(count == 2 ? "LOGIN_REQUIRED" : "INVALID_CONTRACT");
      if (count == 2) {
        assertThat(publicResult.path("failure").path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("LOGIN_REQUIRED");
        assertThat(publicResult.path("detail").path("stepId").asText()).isEqualTo(TWO);
        assertThat(publicResult.path("detail").path("loginStepsPresent").asBoolean()).isFalse();
      } else {
        assertThat(
                publicResult.path("detail").isMissingNode() || publicResult.path("detail").isNull())
            .isTrue();
      }
      boolean actualFailureStep =
          sessions.transaction(
              () -> {
                var current = sessions.lock(id);
                return current
                    .document()
                    .path("sourceSteps")
                    .get(current.progress().path("stepIndex").asInt())
                    .path("stepId")
                    .asText()
                    .equals(TWO);
              });
      assertThat(actualFailureStep).isTrue();
      ObjectNode request = P4_FIRST_GUIDED_REQUEST.get();
      assertThat(request).as("genuine USER-stage NEXT_ACTION reached").isNotNull();
      assertThat(request.path("actualNextAction").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(request.path("availableUserOrdinal").asInt(-1)).isZero();
      assertThat(request.path("selectedPasswordOrdinal").asInt(-1)).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(request.path("passwordCount").asInt(-1)).isEqualTo(count);
      assertThat(request.path("actualSameStepSecretInput").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(request.path("actualPublicUserAvailable").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(P4_WITNESS_FAILED.get())
          .as("actual exception observer stayed available")
          .isFalse();
      ObjectNode refusal = P4_FIRST_GUARD.get();
      assertThat(refusal).as("FIRST original validator count predicate refused").isNotNull();
      assertThat(refusal.path("policyDenied").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(refusal.path("status").asInt()).isEqualTo(422);
      assertThat(refusal.path("validatorCountPredicate").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(refusal.path("validatorLine").asInt()).isEqualTo(363);
      assertThat(refusal.path("actualCallbackRollbackObserved").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(refusal.path("passwordCount").asInt(-1)).isEqualTo(count);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                  - reads)
          .isZero();
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_guided_actions WHERE session_id=? AND step_id=?",
                  Long.class,
                  id,
                  TWO))
          .isZero();
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE session_id=? AND"
                      + " row_kind='RELEASE'",
                  Long.class,
                  id))
          .isZero();
      JsonNode progress = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).progress().deepCopy());
      assertThat(progress.path("stepIndex").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(progress.path("observation").path("coverage").path("passwordControls").asInt(-1))
          .isEqualTo(count);
      p3AwaitTeardown(id);
      p4AssertRegisteredAndNoPasswordEffects();
      p3Scan(List.of(value));
      p3WriteEvidence(
          "guided-adversarial-count-" + count,
          C.object()
              .put("adversarialValidatorOnly", true)
              .put("passwordCount", count)
              .put("publicLoginRequired", count == 2)
              .put("publicInvalidContract", count == 0)
              .put("actualFailingStepMatched", actualFailureStep)
              .put("firstCountPredicate", true)
              .put("originalStatus", refusal.path("status").asInt())
              .put("originalLine", refusal.path("validatorLine").asInt())
              .put("actualSecretReads", 0)
              .put("secretAdmissions", 0)
              .put("passwordWrites", P4_PASSWORD_WRITES.get())
              .put("nativePosts", P3_RECEIPTS.size()));
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      primary = failure;
      throw failure;
    } finally {
      try {
        p4WriteFirstGuardDiagnostic(count);
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError diagnosticFailure) {
        if (primary != null) {
          primary.addSuppressed(diagnosticFailure);
        } else {
          throw diagnosticFailure;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private void p4WriteFirstGuardDiagnostic(int count) throws Exception {
    JsonNode guard = P4_FIRST_GUARD.get(), request = P4_FIRST_GUIDED_REQUEST.get();
    ObjectNode diagnostic =
        C.object()
            .put("schemaVersion", 1)
            .put("requestedCount", count)
            .put("fixturePhase", P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get())
            .put("actualFirstGuardCaptured", guard != null)
            .put("actualRequestCaptured", request != null)
            .put("observerFailed", P4_WITNESS_FAILED.get())
            .put("expectedGuardLine", 363);
    if (guard != null) {
      diagnostic
          .put("actualActionLine", guard.path("validatorLine").asInt())
          .put("actualStatus", guard.path("status").asInt())
          .put("actualCodeOrdinal", guard.path("policyDenied").asBoolean() ? 1 : 0)
          .put("actualPasswordCount", guard.path("passwordCount").asInt(-1))
          .put("actualGuardPhase", guard.path("phaseOrdinal").asInt())
          .put("actualRollbackObserved", guard.path("actualCallbackRollbackObserved").asBoolean())
          .put("actualPredicateMatched", guard.path("validatorCountPredicate").asBoolean());
    }
    if (request != null) {
      diagnostic
          .put("actualObservationCount", request.path("passwordCount").asInt(-1))
          .put("actualUserOrdinal", request.path("availableUserOrdinal").asInt(-1))
          .put("actualPasswordOrdinal", request.path("selectedPasswordOrdinal").asInt(-1));
    }
    Path source =
        Path.of(
            "src/main/java/com/citi/put/checks/studio/guided/translation/GuidedProposalValidator.java");
    diagnostic.put(
        "validatorSourceSha256",
        HexFormat.of()
            .formatHex(
                java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
                    .digest(Files.readAllBytes(source))));
    try (var bytes =
        com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.translation.GuidedProposalValidator.class
            .getResourceAsStream("GuidedProposalValidator.class")) {
      if (bytes == null) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Actual validator class identity unavailable");
      }
      diagnostic.put(
          "validatorClassSha256",
          HexFormat.of()
              .formatHex(
                  java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(bytes.readAllBytes())));
    }
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve("p4-first-guard-diagnostic-" + count + ".json"),
        diagnostic.toPrettyString(),
        StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
  }

  private void p4OrdinaryPasswordCount(int count) throws Exception {
    p4AssertRepositoryGraph();
    p3Mode = P3Mode.LOGIN;
    p3Grant("CSI-456", "fixture-other");
    UUID test = p3CreateSource("CSI-456", "fixture-other", true);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of("CSI-456", "fixture-other", test);
    String value = "P4-ORDINARY-COUNT-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    p3ExpectedPassword = value;
    p3Seed(scope, value);
    P3Publication publication = p4PositiveNativeControl(test);
    // p3Publish inside the genuine positive control asserts zero publication value/model reads.
    int publicationValueReads = p4PublicationValueReads;
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    p4StartObserverPhase(count);
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
    p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, publication);
    assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
    p3AwaitExecution(execution, "ERROR");
    JsonNode report = p3RunnerReport(execution);
    assertThat(report.path("outcome").path("result").asText()).isEqualTo("ERROR");
    assertThat(report.path("steps").size()).isEqualTo(3);
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(0).path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("PASS");
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(1).path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("FAIL");
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(1).path("failure").asText()).isEqualTo("POLICY_REFUSED");
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(2).path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("SKIPPED");
    assertThat(report.path("steps").get(2).path("startedAtMs").isNull()).isTrue();
    assertThat(report.path("secretUse").path("PASS").asText()).isEqualTo("REFUSED");
    assertThat(
            COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                - reads)
        .isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=? AND"
                    + " row_kind='RELEASE' AND outcome='RESOLVED' AND secret_id='PASS'",
                Long.class,
                execution))
        .isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=? AND"
                    + " row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome='REFUSED' AND secret_id='PASS'",
                Long.class,
                execution))
        .isEqualTo(1);
    p3AssertAuditScope(null, execution, scope);
    p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, publication);
    p3CleanupOwnedResources();
    p4AssertRegisteredAndNoPasswordEffects();
    p3Scan(List.of(value));
    p3WriteEvidence(
        "ordinary-native-count-" + count,
        C.object()
            .put("passwordCount", count)
            .put("publicationValueReads", publicationValueReads)
            .put(
                "freshSecretReads",
                COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                    - reads)
            .put("ordinaryModelCalls", MODEL_WIRE.size() - models)
            .put("artifactUnchanged", true)
            .put("nativeRefusal", true)
            .put("passwordWrites", P4_PASSWORD_WRITES.get())
            .put("nativePosts", P3_RECEIPTS.size()));
  }

  private void p3GuidedLogin(P3Mode mode, boolean password) throws Exception {
    p3Mode = mode;
    p3ReportsProtected = mode == P3Mode.REPORTS;
    UUID test = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", password);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", test);
    String wrong = "P3-WRONG-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    if (password) {
      p3Seed(scope, wrong);
    }
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    String id = p3Start(test, "fixture");
    if (mode == P3Mode.REGISTRATION) {
      awaitFinalizing(id);
      JsonNode observed =
          sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).progress().path("observation").deepCopy());
      assertThat(
              com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.translation.LoginRequiredSignature.matches(
                  observed))
          .isTrue();
      p3Publish(id);
      var result = p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/result", null, 200);
      assertThat(result.path("failure").path("code").asText()).isNotEqualTo("LOGIN_REQUIRED");
      assertThat(result.path("detail").isMissingNode() || result.path("detail").isNull()).isTrue();
    } else {
      awaitState(id, "FAILED");
      var result = p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/result", null, 200);
      assertThat(result.path("failure").path("code").asText()).isEqualTo("LOGIN_REQUIRED");
      assertThat(result.path("detail").path("stepId").asText()).isEqualTo(password ? THREE : TWO);
      assertThat(result.path("detail").path("loginStepsPresent").asBoolean()).isEqualTo(password);
      JsonNode observation =
          sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).progress().path("observation").deepCopy());
      assertThat(
              com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.translation.LoginRequiredSignature.matches(
                  observation))
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(observation.path("coverage").path("passwordControls").asInt()).isEqualTo(1);
      if (password) {
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isFalse();
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().password().equals(wrong)).isTrue();
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().username().equals("p3-user")).isTrue();
        p3AssertUsed(id, null);
        p3AssertAuditScope(id, null, scope);
      } else {
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).isEmpty();
      }
    }
    assertThat(
            COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                - reads)
        .isEqualTo(password ? 1 : 0);
    assertThat(P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()).isZero();
    p3Scan(password ? List.of(wrong, p3ExpectedPassword) : List.of(p3ExpectedPassword));
    p3WriteEvidence(
        "guided-login-" + mode.ordinal(),
        C.object()
            .put("branch", mode.ordinal())
            .put("loginStepsPresent", password)
            .put(
                "valueReads",
                COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                    - reads)
            .put(
                "nativeRejectedPosts",
                P3_RECEIPTS.stream().filter(receipt -> !receipt.accepted()).count())
            .put("protectedRequests", P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()));
  }

  private void p3PublishedLogin(P3Mode mode, boolean password) throws Exception {
    p3Mode = mode;
    UUID test = p3CreateSource(primaryCsi, "fixture", password);
    VaultScope scope = VaultScope.of(primaryCsi, "fixture", test);
    String correct = p3ExpectedPassword;
    if (password) {
      p3Seed(scope, correct);
    }
    String id = p3Start(test, "fixture");
    awaitFinalizing(id);
    P3Publication publication = p3Publish(id);
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size();
    int reads = COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS");
    P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.clear();
    P3_RECEIPTS.clear();
    P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.set(0);
    p3ReportsProtected = true;
    String wrong = "P3-ROTATED-WRONG-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    if (password) {
      p3Seed(scope, wrong);
    }
    int invocationStart = P3_INVOCATIONS.size();
    UUID execution = p3Execute(test);
    p3AssertBinding(execution, scope, publication);
    assertThat(dispatcher.dispatchOnce()).isEqualTo(1);
    JsonNode report =
        p3AwaitExecution(execution, mode == P3Mode.REGISTRATION ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
    assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
    assertThat(
            COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                - reads)
        .isEqualTo(password ? 1 : 0);
    if (mode == P3Mode.REGISTRATION) {
      assertThat(report.path("errorCode").asText()).isNotEqualTo("LOGIN_REQUIRED");
      assertThat(report.path("detail").isMissingNode() || report.path("detail").isNull()).isTrue();
      JsonNode runner = p3RunnerReport(execution);
      assertThat(runner.path("outcome").path("result").asText()).isEqualTo("PASSED");
      assertThat(runner.path("outcome").path("errorCode").isNull()).isTrue();
      assertThat(
              runner.path("outcome").path("detail").isMissingNode()
                  || runner.path("outcome").path("detail").isNull())
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(runner.path("steps").size()).isEqualTo(3);
      for (int index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
        JsonNode step = runner.path("steps").get(index);
        assertThat(step.path("stepIndex").asInt()).isEqualTo(index);
        assertThat(step.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("PASS");
        assertThat(step.path("failure").isNull()).isTrue();
      }
    } else {
      // FAILED runner causes remain on the authenticated callback, not API-owned error_code.
      assertThat(report.path("errorCode").isNull() || report.path("errorCode").isMissingNode())
          .isTrue();
      JsonNode runner = p3RunnerReport(execution);
      assertThat(runner.path("outcome").path("errorCode").asText()).isEqualTo("LOGIN_REQUIRED");
      assertThat(runner.path("outcome").path("detail").path("stepId").asText())
          .isEqualTo(password ? THREE : TWO);
      assertThat(runner.path("outcome").path("detail").path("loginStepsPresent").asBoolean())
          .isEqualTo(password);
      assertThat(runner.path("steps").size()).isEqualTo(3);
      int failedIndex = password ? 2 : 1;
      for (int index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
        JsonNode step = runner.path("steps").get(index);
        assertThat(step.path("stepIndex").asInt()).isEqualTo(index);
        if (index < failedIndex) {
          assertThat(step.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("PASS");
          assertThat(step.path("failure").isNull()).isTrue();
        } else if (index == failedIndex) {
          assertThat(step.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("FAIL");
          assertThat(step.path("failure").asText()).isEqualTo("LOGIN_REQUIRED");
        } else {
          assertThat(step.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("SKIPPED");
          assertThat(step.path("startedAtMs").isNull()).isTrue();
          assertThat(step.path("finishedAtMs").isNull()).isTrue();
        }
      }
      if (password) {
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().accepted()).isFalse();
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().password().equals(wrong)).isTrue();
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS.getFirst().username().equals("p3-user")).isTrue();
        p3AssertUsed(null, execution);
        p3AssertAuditScope(null, execution, scope);
        assertThat(runner.path("secretUse").path("PASS").asText()).isEqualTo("USED");
      } else {
        assertThat(P3_RECEIPTS).isEmpty();
      }
    }
    if (mode != P3Mode.REGISTRATION) {
      List<String> calls = P3_INVOCATIONS.subList(invocationStart, P3_INVOCATIONS.size());
      if (password) {
        assertThat(calls).containsExactly("fill", "click");
      } else {
        assertThat(calls).isEmpty();
      }
      assertThat(P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.get()).isZero();
    }
    long decodedFrames = p3DecodeVideo(execution, report);
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT artifact_hash FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?",
                String.class,
                publication.revision()))
        .isEqualTo(publication.artifactHash());
    p3Scan(password ? List.of(correct, wrong) : List.of(correct));
    p3WriteEvidence(
        "ordinary-login-" + mode.ordinal(),
        C.object()
            .put("branch", mode.ordinal())
            .put("loginStepsPresent", password)
            .put(
                "valueReads",
                COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS")
                    - reads)
            .put("modelCalls", MODEL_WIRE.size() - models)
            .put(
                "nativeRejectedPosts",
                P3_RECEIPTS.stream().filter(receipt -> !receipt.accepted()).count())
            .put("decodedFrames", decodedFrames)
            .put(
                "artifactUnchanged",
                jdbc.queryForObject(
                        "SELECT artifact_hash FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?",
                        String.class,
                        publication.revision())
                    .equals(publication.artifactHash())));
  }

  private UUID p3CreateSource(String csi, String environment, boolean password) throws Exception {
    String destination =
        ORIGIN
            + (p3Mode == P3Mode.LOGIN
                ? "/p3/login"
                : p3Mode == P3Mode.REPORTS ? "/p3/reports" : "/p3/registration");
    List<String> texts =
        password
            ? List.of(
                "Open " + destination + ".",
                "Enter <var USER> in User and <secret PASS> as password.",
                "Click Sign in, click the Reports link and check protected Reports heading.")
            : List.of(
                "Open " + destination + ".",
                "Check "
                    + (p3Mode == P3Mode.REGISTRATION ? "Registration" : "Reports")
                    + " heading.",
                "Check "
                    + (p3Mode == P3Mode.REGISTRATION ? "Registration" : "Reports")
                    + " heading remains visible.");
    UUID test = UUID.randomUUID();
    var authoring =
        new AuthoringV2Request(
            2,
            null,
            List.of(
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(ONE), texts.get(0), null),
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(TWO), texts.get(1), null),
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(THREE), texts.get(2), null)));
    String variables =
        password
            ? ("[{\"name\":\"USER\",\"kind\":\"plain\",\"value\":\"p3-user\"},"
                + "{\"name\":\"PASS\",\"kind\":\"secretRef\"}]")
            : "[]";
    jdbc.update(
        ("INSERT INTO ui_tests(id,name,csi_id,environment,browser,steps,"
                + "variables,authoring,authoring_revision_inputs,created_by,"
                + "created_at,updated_at)")
            + (" VALUES (?,'P3 controlled "
                + "join',?,?,'chromium',?::jsonb,?::jsonb,?::jsonb,?::jsonb,"
                + "'fixture@example.invalid',now(),now())"),
        test,
        csi,
        environment,
        JSON.writeValueAsString(texts),
        variables,
        JSON.writeValueAsString(authoring),
        JSON.writeValueAsString(AuthoringRevisionIdentity.create(authoring, Map.of())));
    return test;
  }

  private static boolean p3Entitled(String principal, String csi, String environment, String name) {
    P4RevocationState current = p4Revocation;
    if (current != null
        && current.revoked.get()
        && List.of("user-1", "fixture@example.invalid").contains(principal)
        && csi.equals("CSI-456")
        && environment.equals("fixture-other")
        && name.equals("PASS")) {
      return false;
    }
    return P3_PERMITTED.contains(new P3Entitlement(principal, csi, environment, name));
  }

  private static void p3Grant(String csi, String environment) {
    for (String actor : List.of("user-1", "fixture@example.invalid")) {
      P3_PERMITTED.add(new P3Entitlement(actor, csi, environment, "PASS"));
    }
  }

  private static String p3CoreCookie(String principal, String email) {
    String cookie = "p3-core-" + UUID.randomUUID();
    P3_CORE.put(
        "put_session=" + cookie,
        new P3CoreIdentity(principal, email, List.of(primaryCsi, "CSI-456")));
    return cookie;
  }

  private static void p3Seed(VaultScope scope, String value) {
    VAULT.rotate(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS", value);
    VAULT.bindOrigins(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.SECRET, scope, "PASS", ORIGIN);
    if (p3Mode == P3Mode.LOGIN) {
      p3ReportsProtected = true;
    }
  }

  private ObjectNode p3StartCommand(UUID test, String environment) throws Exception {
    var command =
        C.object()
            .put("schemaVersion", 3)
            .put("testId", test.toString())
            .put("sourceRevision", "1")
            .put("configurationRevision", "1")
            .put("environmentId", environment)
            .put("applicationId", "orders");
    command.set("versions", versions());
    return command;
  }

  private String p3Start(UUID test, String environment) throws Exception {
    return p3Call(
            p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", p3StartCommand(test, environment), 202)
        .path("session")
        .path("sessionId")
        .asText();
  }

  private JsonNode p3Call(String cookie, String path, JsonNode body, int status) throws Exception {
    var request =
        HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("https://localhost:" + port + path))
            .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15))
            .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + cookie);
    if (body == null) {
      request.GET();
    } else {
      refreshFixtureCredentialsBeforeDispatch(path);
      request
          .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
          .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
          .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(body.toString()));
    }
    var response =
        HttpClient.newBuilder()
            .sslContext(TLS)
            .build()
            .send(request.build(), HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
    JsonNode parsed = null;
    if (response.statusCode() == 202) {
      parsed = C.parse(response.body());
      if (path.equals("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions")) {
        String id = parsed.path("session").path("sessionId").asText();
        UUID.fromString(id);
        p3OwnedGuidedCookies.put(id, cookie);
        if (cookie.equals(p3Cookie)) {
          testOwnedSessions.put(id, parsed.path("session").path("testId").asText());
        }
      } else if (path.startsWith("/api/v1/studio/tests/") && path.endsWith("/execute")) {
        UUID execution = UUID.fromString(parsed.path("executionId").asText());
        p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.put(execution, cookie);
        P3_EXECUTIONS.add(execution);
      }
    }
    C5Capture capture = c5Capture;
    if (capture != null) {
      capture.recordHttp(response); // Observe the original returned response; no extra HTTP call.
    }
    // Track genuine success, including an unexpected negative-test 202, before refusing its status.
    assertThat(response.statusCode()).as("actual P3 public route status").isEqualTo(status);
    return parsed == null ? C.parse(response.body()) : parsed;
  }

  private P3Publication p3Publish(String id) throws Exception {
    int models = MODEL_WIRE.size(), reads = COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size();
    JsonNode published =
        p3Call(
            p3Cookie,
            "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + id + "/finalize",
            C.object()
                .put("schemaVersion", 3)
                .put("expectedSourceRevision", "1")
                .put("expectedConfigurationRevision", "1"),
            200);
    UUID revision = UUID.fromString(published.path("revisionId").asText());
    String artifact =
        jdbc.queryForObject(
            "SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?", String.class, revision);
    String hash =
        jdbc.queryForObject(
            "SELECT artifact_hash FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?", String.class, revision);
    assertThat(independentArtifactHash(C.parse(artifact))).isEqualTo(hash);
    assertThat(MODEL_WIRE.size()).isEqualTo(models);
    assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.reads.size()).isEqualTo(reads);
    p3AwaitTeardown(id);
    return new P3Publication(revision, hash, artifact);
  }

  private UUID p3Execute(UUID test) throws Exception {
    UUID execution =
        UUID.fromString(
            p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/tests/" + test + "/execute", C.object(), 202)
                .path("executionId")
                .asText());
    P3_EXECUTIONS.add(execution);
    return execution;
  }

  private void p3AssertBinding(UUID execution, VaultScope scope, P3Publication publication)
      throws Exception {
    JsonNode binding = observedBinding(execution);
    assertThat(binding.path("artifactHash").asText()).isEqualTo(publication.artifactHash());
    assertThat(binding.path("revisionId").asText()).isEqualTo(publication.revision().toString());
    assertThat(binding.path("csiId").asText()).isEqualTo(scope.csiId());
    assertThat(binding.path("environment").asText()).isEqualTo(scope.environment());
    assertThat(binding.path("testId").asText()).isEqualTo(scope.testId());
    assertThat(
            jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT artifact::text FROM ui_test_revisions WHERE id=?",
                String.class,
                publication.revision()))
        .isEqualTo(publication.artifact());
  }

  private JsonNode p3AwaitExecution(UUID execution, String expected) throws Exception {
    return p3AwaitExecution(
        execution, expected, System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(60).toNanos());
  }

  private JsonNode p3AwaitExecution(UUID execution, String expected, long deadline)
      throws Exception {
    assertThat(System.nanoTime() < deadline).as("original terminal observation deadline").isTrue();
    String actual = "";
    do {
      actual =
          jdbc.queryForObject(
              "SELECT status FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?", String.class, execution);
      if (List.of("PASSED", "FAILED", "ERROR").contains(actual)) {
        break;
      }
      Thread.sleep(30);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    assertThat(actual).as("actual P3 ordinary terminal status").isEqualTo(expected);
    return p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/executions/" + execution, null, 200);
  }

  private JsonNode p3RunnerReport(UUID execution) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(5).toNanos();
    JsonNode report = P3_FINAL_REPORTS.get(execution);
    while (report == null && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      Thread.sleep(20);
      report = P3_FINAL_REPORTS.get(execution);
    }
    assertThat(report != null)
        .as("actual authenticated P3 FINAL was observed by the forwarding filter")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(report.path("executionId").asText().equals(execution.toString())).isTrue();
    var ledger =
        jdbc.queryForMap(
            "SELECT attempt_id,admission_id,runner_instance_id,last_runner_sequence"
                + " FROM studio_dispatch_ledger WHERE execution_id=?",
            execution);
    assertThat(report.path("attemptId").asText().equals(String.valueOf(ledger.get("attempt_id"))))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(
            report.path("admissionId").asText().equals(String.valueOf(ledger.get("admission_id"))))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(
            report
                .path("runnerInstanceId")
                .asText()
                .equals(String.valueOf(ledger.get("runner_instance_id"))))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(report.path("runnerSequence").asLong())
        .isEqualTo(((Number) ledger.get("last_runner_sequence")).longValue());
    p3WriteCaptureDiagnostics(execution);
    return report;
  }

  private void p3AssertUsed(String session, UUID execution) {
    if (session != null) {
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE session_id=?"
                      + " AND row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome='USED' AND secret_id='PASS'",
                  Long.class,
                  session))
          .isEqualTo(1);
    } else {
      assertThat(
              jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT count(*) FROM studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=?"
                      + " AND row_kind='USAGE' AND usage_outcome='USED' AND secret_id='PASS'",
                  Long.class,
                  execution))
          .isEqualTo(1);
    }
  }

  private void p3AssertAuditScope(String session, UUID execution, VaultScope scope)
      throws Exception {
    List<Map<String, Object>> rows;
    String principal;
    if (session != null) {
      principal =
          sessions.transaction(
              () -> sessions.lock(session).document().path("scope").path("principalId").asText());
      rows =
          jdbc.queryForList(
              ("SELECT principal_id,csi_id,environment,test_id,secret_id FROM "
                  + "studio_secret_use_audit WHERE session_id=?"),
              session);
    } else {
      principal = observedBinding(execution).path("principalId").asText();
      rows =
          jdbc.queryForList(
              ("SELECT principal_id,csi_id,environment,test_id,secret_id FROM "
                  + "studio_secret_use_audit WHERE execution_id=?"),
              execution);
    }
    assertThat(rows.size()).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(2);
    for (var row : rows) {
      assertThat(String.valueOf(row.get("principal_id")).equals(principal)).isTrue();
      assertThat(String.valueOf(row.get("csi_id")).equals(scope.csiId())).isTrue();
      assertThat(String.valueOf(row.get("environment")).equals(scope.environment())).isTrue();
      assertThat(String.valueOf(row.get("test_id")).equals(scope.testId())).isTrue();
      assertThat(String.valueOf(row.get("secret_id")).equals("PASS")).isTrue();
    }
    assertThat(COUNTED_VAULT.readCount(StudioVaultProvider.ReferenceKind.VARIABLE, scope, "USER"))
        .isZero();
  }

  private void p3AwaitTeardown(String id) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
    do {
      if (sessions
          .transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).progress().path("teardownStatus").asText())
          .equals("CONFIRMED")) {
        return;
      }
      Thread.sleep(30);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    throw new AssertionError("Actual P3 owner teardown acknowledgement missing");
  }

  private long p3DecodeVideo(UUID execution, JsonNode current) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
    JsonNode report = current;
    while (!report.path("streamComplete").asBoolean() && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      Thread.sleep(30);
      report = p3Call(p3Cookie, "/api/v1/studio/executions/" + execution, null, 200);
    }
    assertThat(report.path("streamComplete").asBoolean()).isTrue();
    assertThat(report.path("video").path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("AVAILABLE");
    String key =
        jdbc.queryForObject(
            "SELECT video_key FROM ui_test_executions WHERE id=?", String.class, execution);
    Path video = TEMP.resolve("objects").resolve(key).normalize();
    assertThat(video.startsWith(TEMP.resolve("objects"))).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.isRegularFile(video, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)).isTrue();
    assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(video)).isFalse();
    JsonNode decoded = decodeJoinedVideo(video, execution);
    assertThat(decoded.path("decodedFrames").asLong()).isPositive();
    return decoded.path("decodedFrames").asLong();
  }

  private void p3Scan(List<String> values) throws Exception {
    var sinks = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, byte[]>();
    collectJoinedSinks(sinks);
    sinks.put(
        "p3-actual-final-callbacks",
        P3_FINAL_REPORTS.values().stream()
            .map(JsonNode::toString)
            .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.joining("\n"))
            .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    for (String value : values) {
      assertJoinedSinkAbsent(sinks, value);
      assertThat(
              COUNTED_VAULT.reads.stream()
                  .filter(read -> read.value != null)
                  .allMatch(read -> allErased(read.value)))
          .isTrue();
    }
  }

  private static JsonNode p3LaunchCounter() throws Exception {
    return C.parse(Files.readString(TEMP.resolve("p3-guided-launch-count.json")));
  }

  private static void p3WriteEvidence(String name, ObjectNode evidence) throws Exception {
    requireNumericBudgetEvidence(evidence);
    Files.writeString(TEMP.resolve("p3-" + name + ".json"), evidence.toPrettyString());
  }

  private static String p4RevocationScalarSupport(int producer) throws Exception {
    return """
import * as revFs from 'node:fs';
const revStateFile=%s,revFailedFile=%s,revOrigin=%s,revProducer=%d,revLedgerRoot=%s;
let revCase=0,revSeq=0,revTail=Promise.resolve(),revStarted=false,revNativeOrdinal=0;
let revPending=0,revOriginalPending=0,revAcked=0,revFailed=false,revSealing=false,revSealed=false;
let revC6HighPending=0,revC6CdpPending=0;
let revLossRuntime=null,revLossBusy=2,revLossRestoreAttempt=0,revLossRestoreRefusal=0;
const revLossSelected=()=>revProducer===1&&revLocalMode?.c6===3;
const revLossRefuse=code=>{if(revLossSelected()&&revLossRestoreRefusal===0)revLossRestoreRef\
usal=code;};
let revLocalMode=null,revWatching=false,revC6Probed=0;
let revC6ProfileDev=0,revC6ProfileIno=0,revC6ContextBound=false;
let revC6RunDeadline=0,revC6StepMs=0,revC6Restored=false,revC6RetryRestore=null;
let revActive=new Map();const revGroups=new Set();
let revLossLifetime=null;
const revManagedLoss=()=>revLossSelected()&&revLossLifetime?.retired===true;
const revMode=()=>{try{return revFs.existsSync(revStateFile)?JSON.parse(revFs.readFileSync(r\
evStateFile,'utf8')):null;}catch{return null;}};
const %s=()=>{try{const fd=revFs.openSync(revFailedFile,
  revFs.constants.O_WRONLY|revFs.constants.O_CREAT|revFs.constants.O_APPEND|revFs.constants.\
O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
  try{revFs.writeSync(fd,'1\\n');}finally{revFs.closeSync(fd);}}catch{}};
const revCheckRoot=()=>{
  const root=revFs.lstatSync(revLedgerRoot);
  if(root.isSymbolicLink()||!root.isDirectory()||revFs.realpathSync(revLedgerRoot)!==revLedgerRoot
    ||(root.mode&0o777)!==0o700||root.uid!==process.getuid())throw new Error('ROOT');
};
const %s=(mode,sealed)=>{
  try{
    revCheckRoot();
    const target=revLedgerRoot+'/'+mode.case+'-'+revProducer+'.json',tmp=target+'.tmp';
    if(revFs.existsSync(target)){const old=revFs.lstatSync(target);
      if(old.isSymbolicLink()||!old.isFile()||(old.mode&0o777)!==0o600)throw new Error('FILE');}
    if(mode.c6===3&&revProducer===1&&revLossRuntime){
      const actualBusy=revLossRuntime.busy;revLossBusy=typeof \
actualBusy==='boolean'?Number(actualBusy):2;
    }
    // Retired raw status is the last actual observation, never fake settlement/zero.
    const raw=revManagedLoss()?revLossLifetime.snapshot:null;
    const body=JSON.stringify({case:mode.case,producer:revProducer,epoch:mode.epoch,role:revProducer,
      sent:revSeq,acked:revAcked,pending:revPending+revOriginalPending+(raw?.high??revC6High\
Pending)+revC6CdpPending,originalPending:revOriginalPending,
      failed:revFailed,sealed,...(mode.c6?{profileDev:revC6ProfileDev,
        profileIno:revC6ProfileIno,contextBound:revC6ContextBound}:{}),
      ...(mode.c6===3&&revProducer===1?{highPending:raw?.high??revC6HighPending,cdpCreationP\
ending:revC6CdpPending,
        nativeActive:raw?.active??revActive.size,busy:revLossBusy,restoreAttempt:revLossRest\
oreAttempt,
        restoreRefusal:revLossRestoreRefusal}:{})});
    if(Buffer.byteLength(body)>1024)throw new Error('BOUND');
    revCheckRoot();
    const fd=revFs.openSync(tmp,revFs.constants.O_WRONLY|revFs.constants.O_CREAT|revFs.const\
ants.O_EXCL|
      revFs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
    try{revFs.writeFileSync(fd,body);revFs.fsyncSync(fd);}finally{revFs.closeSync(fd);}
    revCheckRoot();
    revFs.renameSync(tmp,target);
  }catch{revFailed=true;revMarker();}
};
const revFail=()=>{revFailed=true;revMarker();if(revLocalMode)revPublish(revLocalMode,revSealed);};
const revMaybeSeal=()=>{
  if(revManagedLoss())return; // This epoch explicitly has no normal raw SDK seal.
  if(revSealing&&revPending===0&&revOriginalPending===0&&revC6HighPending===0&&revC6CdpPendi\
ng===0){revSealed=true;revPublish(revLocalMode,true);}
};
const revInit=mode=>{
  if(revCase===mode.case)return;
  if(revLocalMode&&!revSealed&&!revManagedLoss()){revFail();return;}
  // The old map remains captured by its original pending callbacks; it is not erased.
  // The retired loss lifetime also owns its unsettled high-level fill count (its
  // snapshot already published it), so the next case must not inherit that count.
  if(revManagedLoss()){revActive=new Map();revC6HighPending=0;}
  revCase=mode.case;revSeq=0;revPending=0;revAcked=0;revOriginalPending=0;
  if(mode.c6){revNativeOrdinal=0;revC6Probed=0;revC6Restored=false;}
  if(mode.c6===3&&revProducer===1){revLossRuntime=null;revLossBusy=2;revLossRestoreAttempt=0\
;revLossRestoreRefusal=0;
    revLossLifetime={mode,active:revActive,high:0,operations:0,callbacks:0,cleanupAuthorized\
:false,retired:false,snapshot:null};}
  else revLossLifetime=null;
  if(mode.c6>=1&&mode.c6<=3){revStarted=false;revC6HighPending=0;revC6CdpPending=0;}
  revTail=Promise.resolve();revLocalMode=mode;revSealing=false;revSealed=false;
  if((revProducer!==3||mode.c6)&&!revWatching){
    %s
    const command=revLedgerRoot+'/'+mode.case+'-'+revProducer+'-seal.json';revWatching=true;
    revFs.watchFile(command,{interval:25,persistent:false},()=>{
      try{revCheckRoot();if(!revFs.existsSync(command))return;
        const stat=revFs.lstatSync(command);
        if(stat.isSymbolicLink()||!stat.isFile()||stat.size>1024||(stat.mode&0o777)!==0o600)
          throw new Error('COMMAND');
        revCheckRoot();
        const seal=JSON.parse(revFs.readFileSync(command,'utf8'));
        if(mode.c6&&Object.keys(seal).sort().join(',')==='case,epoch,probe,producer'
          &&seal.case===mode.case&&seal.epoch===mode.epoch&&seal.producer===revProducer&&Num\
ber.isInteger(seal.probe)&&seal.probe>=1&&seal.probe<=2){
          if(seal.probe<=revC6Probed)return;
          if(seal.probe!==revC6Probed+1)throw new Error('C6_PROBE_SEQUENCE');revC6Probed=seal.probe;
          const pending=[...revActive].filter(([,operation])=>operation===1);
          const margin=mode.path===2?Math.floor(revC6RunDeadline-Date.now()-revC6StepMs):0;
          revEmit(mode,10,pending.length===1?pending[0][0]:0,1,0,
            pending.length===1&&revActive.size===1,Math.max(0,margin));return;
        }
        if(Object.keys(seal).sort().join(',')!=='case,epoch,producer,seal'||seal.case!==mode.case
          ||seal.epoch!==mode.epoch||seal.producer!==revProducer||seal.seal!==true)throw \
new Error('EPOCH');
        if(mode.c6===3&&revProducer===1&&revLossLifetime)revLossLifetime.cleanupAuthorized=true;
        if(mode.c6&&revC6RetryRestore){
          // Existing teardown seal retries the retained selected restoration task.
          // No action/stop/native authority is created by this observation command.
          const retry=revC6RetryRestore;
          retry().then(restored=>{if(!restored){revFail();return;}
            revSealing=true;revFs.unwatchFile(command);revWatching=false;
            // Managed output closure is not normal raw-SDK settlement/sealing.
            if(revManagedLoss())revPublish(mode,false);else revMaybeSeal();
          },()=>{revFail();}).catch(()=>{revFail();});return;
        }
        revSealing=true;revFs.unwatchFile(command);revWatching=false;revMaybeSeal();
      }catch{revFail();revSealing=true;revFs.unwatchFile(command);revWatching=false;revMaybeSeal();}
    });
  }
};
const revEmit=(mode,event,ordinal=0,operation=0,reason=0,ok=true,deltaMicros=0)=>{
  if(!mode?.active)return;
  try{revInit(mode);}catch{revFail();return;}
  if(revSealing||revSealed){revFail();return;}
  revPending++;
  const body={case:mode.case,producer:revProducer,seq:++revSeq,event,ordinal,operation,reaso\
n,ok,deltaMicros};
  const acknowledgedAt=performance.now();
  revTail=revTail.then(async()=>{
    const response=await fetch(revOrigin+'/p3/revocation-observer',{method:'POST',
      headers:{'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body),signal:AbortSign\
al.timeout(1000)});
    const ack=await response.json();
    if(response.status!==200||Object.keys(ack).sort().join(',')!=='accepted,flipped,seq'
      ||ack.accepted!==true||ack.seq!==body.seq||typeof ack.flipped!=='boolean')throw new \
Error('OBSERVER');
    revAcked++;
    if(event===4&&ack.flipped)revEmit(mode,8,0,0,0,true,Math.ceil((performance.now()-acknowl\
edgedAt)*1000));
  }).catch(()=>{revFail();}).finally(()=>{
    revPending--;revPublish(mode,revSealed);revMaybeSeal();
  });
};
const revRestore=()=>{
  if(revLossSelected())revLossRestoreAttempt++;
  let restored=true;
  for(const group of [...revGroups]){
    for(const [name,descriptor,wrapped] of [...group.methods]){
      const current=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(group.cap,name);
      let success=false;
      try{if(current?.value===wrapped){success=descriptor?
        Reflect.defineProperty(group.cap,name,descriptor):Reflect.deleteProperty(group.cap,name);
        if(!success)revLossRefuse(3);
      }else revLossRefuse(2);}catch{revLossRefuse(3);}
      if(success)group.methods=group.methods.filter(item=>item[0]!==name);else{restored=fals\
e;revFail();}
    }
    if(!group.methods.length){group.cap=null;revGroups.delete(group);}
  }
  return restored;
};

  if(!mode?.active)return;
  const cdpSelected=mode.c6>=1&&mode.c6<=3;
  if(!cap||(!cdpSelected&&revGroups.size)||[...revGroups].some(group=>group.cap===cap)
    ||typeof cap.fillPassword!=='function'||typeof cap.invoke!=='function'){revFail();return;}
  const group={cap,methods:[]};revGroups.add(group);
  for(const name of ['fillPassword','invoke']){
    const descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(cap,name),original=cap[name];
    const wrapped=function(...args){
      if(cdpSelected&&name==='fillPassword'){
        revC6HighPending++;let result;const lifetime=revLossSelected()?revLossLifetime:null;
        if(lifetime)lifetime.high++;
        const settled=()=>{
          if(lifetime)lifetime.high--;
          if(lifetime&&lifetime!==revLossLifetime)return;
          revC6HighPending--;
          if(lifetime?.retired)return; // Value-free observer retired, actual promise unchanged.
          revPublish(mode,revSealed);revMaybeSeal();};
        try{result=Reflect.apply(original,this,args);}catch(error){settled();throw error;}
        try{result.then(settled,settled).catch(()=>{revFail();});}catch{revFail();}
        return result;
      }
      const operation=name==='fillPassword'?1:args[0]?.operation?.kind==='click'?2:3;
      const ordinal=++revNativeOrdinal;revActive.set(ordinal,operation);revEmit(mode,2,ordin\
al,operation);
      const settled=ok=>{revActive.delete(ordinal);revEmit(mode,3,ordinal,operation,0,ok);};
      let result;try{result=Reflect.apply(original,this,args);}catch(error){settled(false);t\
hrow error;}

      return result;
    };
    try{if(!Reflect.defineProperty(cap,name,{...(descriptor??{configurable:true,writable:tru\
e,enumerable:true}),value:wrapped}))throw new Error('DESCRIPTOR');
      group.methods.push([name,descriptor,wrapped]);}catch{revFail();}
  }
};
"""
        .formatted(
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-observer-failed").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(ORIGIN),
            producer,
            JSON.writeValueAsString(
                TEMP.toRealPath().resolve("p4-revocation-producers").toString()),
            // R10-rev2 I1: producer 2 runs in the runner PARENT. Its marker, publish and first
            // root check are asynchronous, defined in p4OrdinaryRevocationHttp(); these
            // synchronous copies stay unused there. Producers 1 and 3 are byte-identical.
            producer == 2 ? "revMarkerUnused" : "revMarker",
            producer == 2 ? "revPublishUnused" : "revPublish",
            producer == 2 ? "revCheckRootLater();" : "revCheckRoot();");
  }

  private static String p4GuidedRevocationObservers() throws Exception {

  revFs.constants.O_CREAT|revFs.constants.O_APPEND|revFs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600).then(async fd=>{
    try{await fd.writeFile('1');}finally{await fd.close();}}).catch(()=>undefined);};
