  private static String p4OrdinaryRevocationHttp() throws Exception {
    String root = Path.of("../studio-runner/dist").toAbsolutePath().normalize().toUri().toString();
    return p4RevocationScalarSupport(2)
        + """
import {createFetchHttpClient as revRealClient} from %s;
import {validSecretControl as revValidControl,ackRequestsCancel as revRequestsCancel} from %s;
import * as revFsAsync from 'node:fs/promises';
// R10-rev2 I1: this producer runs on the runner PARENT loop, so it makes no synchronous
// file-system call there. One ordered chain carries every producer-2 file write.
let revWriteTail=Promise.resolve(),revWritesQueued=0,revWritesDone=0,revKillCalls=0;
const revEnqueue=task=>{revWritesQueued++;
  revWriteTail=revWriteTail.then(task).catch(()=>{}).finally(()=>{revWritesDone++;});};
const revCheckRootAsync=async()=>{
  const info=await revFsAsync.lstat(revLedgerRoot);
  if(info.isSymbolicLink()||!info.isDirectory()
    ||await revFsAsync.realpath(revLedgerRoot)!==revLedgerRoot
    ||(info.mode&0o777)!==0o700||info.uid!==process.getuid())throw new Error('ROOT');
};
const revAppendAsync=async(file,text)=>{
  const handle=await revFsAsync.open(file,revFs.constants.O_WRONLY|revFs.constants.O_CREAT
    |revFs.constants.O_APPEND|revFs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
  try{await handle.writeFile(text);}finally{await handle.close();}
};
const revMarkerAsync=async()=>{try{await revAppendAsync(revFailedFile,'1\\n');}catch{}};
const revMarker=()=>{revEnqueue(revMarkerAsync);};
const revCheckRootLater=()=>{revEnqueue(async()=>{
  try{await revCheckRootAsync();}catch{revFailed=true;await revMarkerAsync();}});};
// R10-rev2 I1(c): one cached mode, refreshed asynchronously (watchFile, 25 ms).
let revModeCache=null,revModeGeneration=0;
const revModeRefresh=()=>{const generation=++revModeGeneration;
  revFsAsync.readFile(revStateFile,'utf8').then(text=>JSON.parse(text)).then(
    mode=>{if(generation===revModeGeneration)revModeCache=mode;},
    ()=>{if(generation===revModeGeneration)revModeCache=null;});};
const revModeCached=()=>revModeCache;
// The one existing synchronous read per spawn stays; it also refreshes the cache.
const revModeNow=()=>{const mode=revMode();revModeGeneration++;revModeCache=mode;return mode;};
revFs.watchFile(revStateFile,{interval:25,persistent:false},()=>{revModeRefresh();});
revModeRefresh();
// R10-rev2 I2: per selected control read, send-to-settle ms and the largest loop stall
// seen by a 10 ms drift sampler that runs only from send to settle. Numbers only.
let revReadCase=0,revReadCount=0,revReadMaxMs=0,revReadStallMaxMs=0,revLastRead=null;
const revReadLog=new Map();
const revReadStart=mode=>{
  if(revReadCase!==mode.case){revReadCase=mode.case;revReadCount=0;revReadMaxMs=0;
    revReadStallMaxMs=0;revLastRead=null;}
  const now=performance.now(),read={case:mode.case,executionId:mode.executionId,sentAt:now,
    last:now,stall:0,writes:revWritesQueued,writing:revWritesQueued!==revWritesDone,
    kills:revKillCalls,timer:null,settled:false};
  read.timer=setInterval(()=>{const tick=performance.now();
    read.stall=Math.max(read.stall,tick-read.last-10);read.last=tick;},10);
  return read;
};
const revReadSettle=read=>{
  if(!read||read.settled)return;read.settled=true;
  const now=performance.now();clearInterval(read.timer);
  const entry={ms:Math.ceil(now-read.sentAt),
    stall:Math.ceil(Math.max(0,read.stall,now-read.last-10)),
    publish:read.writing||revWritesQueued!==read.writes||revWritesQueued!==revWritesDone,
    kill:revKillCalls!==read.kills};
  if(read.case===revReadCase){revReadCount++;revReadMaxMs=Math.max(revReadMaxMs,entry.ms);
    revReadStallMaxMs=Math.max(revReadStallMaxMs,entry.stall);revLastRead=entry;}
  let log=revReadLog.get(read.executionId);
  if(!log){log=[];revReadLog.set(read.executionId,log);}
  if(log.length<512)log.push([entry.ms,entry.stall,entry.publish?1:0,entry.kill?1:0]);
};
const revReadSample=mode=>{const own=mode.case===revReadCase;
  return {reads:own?revReadCount:0,readMaxMs:own?revReadMaxMs:0,
    readStallMaxMs:own?revReadStallMaxMs:0,lastReadMs:own?revLastRead?.ms??0:0,
    lastReadStallMs:own?revLastRead?.stall??0:0,
    lastReadPublishOverlap:own&&revLastRead?.publish===true,
    lastReadKillOverlap:own&&revLastRead?.kill===true};};
// Same sample content as the synchronous producer, taken at call time; written in order.
const revPublish=(mode,sealed)=>{
  let sample;
  try{
    const raw=revManagedLoss()?revLossLifetime.snapshot:null;
    sample={case:mode.case,producer:revProducer,epoch:mode.epoch,role:revProducer,
      sent:revSeq,acked:revAcked,pending:revPending+revOriginalPending+(raw?.high??revC6Hig\
hPending)+revC6CdpPending,originalPending:revOriginalPending,
      failed:revFailed,sealed,...(mode.c6?{profileDev:revC6ProfileDev,
        profileIno:revC6ProfileIno,contextBound:revC6ContextBound}:{}),...revReadSample(mode)};
  }catch{revFailed=true;revMarker();return;}
  revEnqueue(async()=>{
    try{
      await revCheckRootAsync();
      const target=revLedgerRoot+'/'+mode.case+'-'+revProducer+'.json',tmp=target+'.tmp';
      let exists=true;try{await revFsAsync.stat(target);}catch{exists=false;}
      if(exists){const old=await revFsAsync.lstat(target);
        if(old.isSymbolicLink()||!old.isFile()||(old.mode&0o777)!==0o600)
          throw new Error('FILE');}
      // A failure is sticky: a later write never reports less than its call-time snapshot.
      sample.failed=sample.failed||revFailed;
      const body=JSON.stringify(sample);
      if(Buffer.byteLength(body)>1024)throw new Error('BOUND');
      await revCheckRootAsync();
      const handle=await revFsAsync.open(tmp,revFs.constants.O_WRONLY|revFs.constants.O_CREAT
        |revFs.constants.O_EXCL|revFs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
      try{await handle.writeFile(body);await handle.sync();}finally{await handle.close();}
      await revCheckRootAsync();
      await revFsAsync.rename(tmp,target);
    }catch{revFailed=true;await revMarkerAsync();}
  });
};
const revHttp=revRealClient(),revSend=revHttp.send;
// A settle reaction only stops the sampler; the observer work runs after setImmediate, so
// nothing synchronous runs ahead of the runner's own continuation on its request.
const revLater=work=>{setImmediate(()=>{try{work();}catch{try{revFail();}catch{}}});};
revHttp.send=function(...args){const mode=revModeCached();
  const selected=mode?.active&&mode.path===2&&args[0]?.method==='GET'
    &&args[0]?.url===mode.controlUrl;
  let read=null;
  if(selected){try{revInit(mode);}catch{revFail();}revOriginalPending++;
    try{read=revReadStart(mode);}catch{read=null;}}
  let result;try{result=Reflect.apply(revSend,this,args);}catch(error){
    if(selected){revReadSettle(read);revOriginalPending--;revFail();revPublish(mode,revSealed);
      revMaybeSeal();}throw error;}
  if(selected){result.then(outcome=>{revReadSettle(read);revLater(()=>{try{
    if(outcome.kind==='response'&&outcome.status===200
      &&revValidControl(outcome.body,mode.executionId)){
      if(!revRequestsCancel(outcome.body))revEmit(mode,4,1);
    }
  }catch{revFail();}finally{revOriginalPending--;revPublish(mode,revSealed);revMaybeSeal();}});},
    ()=>{revReadSettle(read);revLater(()=>{revOriginalPending--;revFail();
      revPublish(mode,revSealed);revMaybeSeal();});})
    .catch(()=>{revFail();});}
  return result;};
"""
            .formatted(
                JSON.writeValueAsString(root + "reporting/httpClient.js"),
                JSON.writeValueAsString(root + "reporting/reportClient.js"));
  }

  private static String p4OrdinaryRevocationChild() throws Exception {
    String root = Path.of("../studio-runner/dist").toAbsolutePath().normalize().toUri().toString();
    return p4RevocationScalarSupport(3)
        + """
import {validateObserved as revValidateObserved} from %s;
let revReady=false;
// R10-rev2-2 diagnostic: outgoing messages are only appended in memory. The one write
// happens in the exit wrapper and is synchronous there, because process.exit never returns.
const revChildDiagnosticFile=%s,revChildSent=[];
const revChildDiagnostic=()=>{try{
  const result=revChildSent.find(m=>m?.kind==='result')??null,code=result?.errorCode;
  const executionId=revChildSent.map(m=>m?.runId).find(id=>typeof id==='string'
    &&/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/.test(id))??null;
  const line=JSON.stringify({v:1,pid:process.pid,executionId,
    readySent:revChildSent.some(m=>m?.kind==='ready'),resultSent:result!==null,
    resultOk:typeof result?.ok==='boolean'?result.ok:null,
    resultErrorCode:result===null||code===null||code===undefined?null
      :typeof code==='string'&&/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,63}$/.test(code)?code:'OTHER'})+'\\n';
  const fd=revFs.openSync(revChildDiagnosticFile,revFs.constants.O_WRONLY|revFs.constants.O_CREAT
    |revFs.constants.O_APPEND|revFs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
  try{revFs.writeSync(fd,line);}finally{revFs.closeSync(fd);}
}catch{/* Diagnostic only; the original exit continues. */}};
const revProcess={
  on(event,listener){return Reflect.apply(process.on,process,[event,function(...args){
    const mode=revMode(),message=args[0];
    if(mode?.c6&&mode.path===2){
      if(message?.kind==='launch')revC6RunDeadline=Math.min(Date.parse(message.admission?.ru\
nDeadlineAt),Date.now()+message.totalRuntimeMs);
      if(message?.kind==='execute')revC6StepMs=message.stepTimeoutMs;
    }
    const result=Reflect.apply(listener,this,args);
    if(mode?.active&&mode.path===2&&args[0]?.kind==='stop'){
      try{revValidateObserved('replay-ipc#/definitions/Stop',args[0],128);
        if(!revReady||!revStarted)throw new Error('READY');
        if(!mode.c6)revEmit(mode,6,1,0,0,revRestore());
        revEmit(mode,5,1,0,4);
      }catch{revFail();}}return result;}]);},
  send(...args){const result=Reflect.apply(process.send,process,args),mode=revMode();
    if(revChildSent.length<1024)revChildSent.push(args[0]);
    if(mode?.active&&mode.path===2&&args[0]?.kind==='ready'){
      try{const ready=revValidateObserved('replay-ipc#/definitions/Ready3',args[0],16384);
        if(result!==true||ready.runId!==mode.executionId)throw new Error('BINDING');
        revReady=true;revStarted=true;revEmit(mode,1,1);
      }catch{revFail();}}return result;},
  exit(...args){const mode=revMode();try{
    if(mode?.active&&mode.path===2){revInit(mode);
      if(mode.c6&&!revC6Restored)revFail();
      revSealing=true;revSealed=true;revPublish(mode,true);}
  }catch{revFail();}finally{revChildDiagnostic();return Reflect.apply(process.exit,process,args);}}
};
"""
            .formatted(
                JSON.writeValueAsString(root + "validator/observedReferencePolicy.js"),
                JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve(P10_CHILD_IPC_DIAGNOSTICS).toString()));
  }

  private static String c5ProfileScannerSupport() {
    return """
const c5Variants=value=>{
  const utf8=Buffer.from(value,'utf8'),le=Buffer.from(value,'utf16le'),be=Buffer.from(le);be.swap16();
  const percent=[...utf8].map(byte=>String.fromCharCode(37)+byte.toString(16).padStart(2,'0'\
).toUpperCase()).join('');
  const unicode=[...Array(value.length)].map((_,index)=>String.fromCharCode(92)+'u'+value.ch\
arCodeAt(index).toString(16).padStart(4,'0')).join('');
  const json=JSON.stringify(value),form=new URLSearchParams({v:value}).toString().slice(2);
  return [utf8,le,be,...[utf8.toString('base64'),utf8.toString('hex'),utf8.toString('hex').t\
oUpperCase(),
    percent,percent.toLowerCase(),encodeURIComponent(value),form,form.replaceAll('+',String.\
fromCharCode(37)+'20'),
    unicode,unicode.toUpperCase().replaceAll('U','u'),json,json.slice(1,-1)].map(text=>Buffe\
r.from(text,'utf8'))];
};
const c5ProfileHitCategory=relative=>{
  const parts=relative.split('/'),name=parts.at(-1);
  if(parts.some(part=>['Cache','Cache_Data'].includes(part)))return 0;
  if(parts.some(part=>['Code Cache','GPUCache','DawnCache','ShaderCache','GrShaderCache'].in\
cludes(part)))return 1;
  if(parts.some(part=>['Local Storage','Session \
Storage','IndexedDB','WebStorage','CacheStorage'].includes(part)))return 2;
  if(parts.includes('Sessions'))return 3;
  if(['History','History-journal'].includes(name))return 4;
  if(['Preferences','Secure Preferences','Local State'].includes(name))return 5;
  return 6;
};
const c5ProfileScan=directory=>{
  let files=0,bytes=0,hits=0,links=0,failed=false,stage=0,errno=0;
  const hitCategories=Array(8).fill(0);
  let linkName=0,linkRoot=false,linkType=0,linkBytes=0,linkTargetRead=false,linkTargetIsVersion=false;
  try{
    const root=p4Fs.realpathSync(directory),stat=p4Fs.lstatSync(directory);
    const canonical=p4Fs.lstatSync(root);
    if(stat.isSymbolicLink()||!stat.isDirectory()||stat.dev!==canonical.dev||stat.ino!==canonical.ino
      ||stat.uid!==process.getuid())throw new Error('ROOT');
    const valuesStat=p4Fs.lstatSync(c5ValuesFile);
    if(valuesStat.isSymbolicLink()||!valuesStat.isFile()||(valuesStat.mode&0o777)!==0o600||v\
aluesStat.size>32768)throw new Error('VALUES');
    const values=JSON.parse(p4Fs.readFileSync(c5ValuesFile,'utf8'));
    if(!Array.isArray(values)||values.length<1||values.length>16||values.some(value=>typeof \
value!=='string'||value.length>4096))throw new Error('VALUES');
    const variants=values.flatMap(c5Variants),walk=path=>{
      for(const entry of p4Fs.readdirSync(path,{withFileTypes:true})){
        const child=path+'/'+entry.name,info=p4Fs.lstatSync(child);
        if(info.isSymbolicLink()){
          if(path!==root||!['SingletonLock','SingletonCookie','SingletonSocket','RunningChro\
meVersion'].includes(entry.name)
            ||info.size>1024){
            linkName=['SingletonLock','SingletonCookie','SingletonSocket','RunningChromeVers\
ion'].indexOf(entry.name)+1;
            linkRoot=path===root;linkType=1;linkBytes=Number.isFinite(info.size)?info.size:0;
            if(linkRoot&&linkName===4&&info.size<=1024){try{const target=p4Fs.readlinkSync(child);
              linkTargetRead=true;
              linkTargetIsVersion=target.length<=128&&/^[0-9]{1,4}(?:\\.[0-9]{1,6}){3}$/.test(target);
            }catch{/* Metadata loss cannot change the original SYMLINK rejection. */}}
            throw new Error('SYMLINK');
          }
          const text=Buffer.from(entry.name+' '+p4Fs.readlinkSync(child),'utf8');
          if(text.length>2048)throw new Error('LINKBOUND');
          links++;bytes+=text.length;
          for(const variant of variants)if(text.includes(variant)){hits++;hitCategories[7]++;}
          continue;
        }
        if(info.isDirectory()){walk(child);continue;}
        if(!info.isFile()||info.size>67108864||bytes+info.size>268435456)throw new Error('BOUND');
        const actual=p4Fs.realpathSync(child);if(!actual.startsWith(root+'/'))throw new Error('PATH');
        const fd=p4Fs.openSync(child,p4Fs.constants.O_RDONLY|p4Fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW);
        try{
          const opened=p4Fs.fstatSync(fd);
          if(!opened.isFile()||opened.dev!==info.dev||opened.ino!==info.ino
            ||opened.size>67108864||bytes+opened.size>268435456)throw new Error('FD');
          const buffer=Buffer.alloc(opened.size+1);let length=0;
          while(length<buffer.length){const \
read=p4Fs.readSync(fd,buffer,length,buffer.length-length,null);
            if(read===0)break;length+=read;}
          const after=p4Fs.fstatSync(fd);
          if(length>opened.size||after.size!==length||after.dev!==opened.dev||after.ino!==opened.ino)
            throw new Error('CHANGED');
          const body=buffer.subarray(0,length);files++;bytes+=body.length;
          for(const variant of variants)if(body.includes(variant)){hits++;hitCategories[c5Pr\
ofileHitCategory(child.slice(root.length+1))]++;}
        }finally{p4Fs.closeSync(fd);}
      }
    };walk(root);
  }catch(error){
    failed=true;
    stage=['ROOT','VALUES','SYMLINK','LINKBOUND','BOUND','PATH','FD','CHANGED'].indexOf(erro\
r?.message)+1;
    errno=['EACCES','EPERM','ENOENT','ELOOP','ENOTDIR','EISDIR','EIO','EBADF','EMFILE','ENFI\
LE'].indexOf(error?.code)+1;
    if(stage===0){const operation=['open','read','scandir','lstat','realpath','fstat','close\
','readlink'].indexOf(error?.syscall);
      if(operation>=0)stage=9+operation;}
  }
  try{const fd=p4Fs.openSync(c5CountsFile,p4Fs.constants.O_WRONLY|p4Fs.constants.O_CREAT|
    p4Fs.constants.O_APPEND|p4Fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
    try{p4Fs.writeSync(fd,JSON.stringify({files,bytes,hits,links,failed,stage,errno,linkName\
,linkRoot,linkType,linkBytes,linkTargetRead,linkTargetIsVersion,hitCategories})+'\\n');}fina\
lly{p4Fs.closeSync(fd);}}
  catch{try{const fd=p4Fs.openSync(c5CountsFile+'-failed',p4Fs.constants.O_WRONLY|p4Fs.const\
ants.O_CREAT|
    p4Fs.constants.O_APPEND|p4Fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);try{p4Fs.writeSync(fd,'1');}fin\
ally{p4Fs.closeSync(fd);}}catch{}}
};
"""
        .stripTrailing();
  }

  private static String p4NativeCloseObserverSdk() throws Exception {
    String playwright =
        Path.of("../studio-runner/node_modules/playwright/index.mjs")
            .toAbsolutePath()
            .normalize()
            .toUri()
            .toString();
    return """
import {chromium as p4Chromium} from %s;import * as p4Fs from 'node:fs';
const p4ModeFile=%s,p4FailedFile=%s,p4SettledFile=%s,p4Origin=%s;
const c5ValuesFile=%s,c5CountsFile=%s;
import {installObservationCollector as c6TrustedCollector} from %s;
%s
// Selected C6 observes the original SDK primitive; current-document authority stays in \
production guards.
const c6CdpHolders=new WeakMap();
const c6FillSource='function(target, origin, allowedOrigins, value) { return \
this.fillPasswordResult(target, origin, allowedOrigins, value); }';
const c6InstallSource=c6TrustedCollector.toString();
const c6Drop=state=>{state.objectId=null;state.world=null;};
const c6InstallSend=(holder,session,page)=>{
  if(holder.sessions.has(session)){revFail();return;}holder.sessions.set(session,{page,frame\
:null,world:null,objectId:null});
  const state=holder.sessions.get(session),descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(sessio\
n,'send'),original=session.send;
  if(typeof original!=='function'){revFail();return;}
  const group={cap:session,methods:[]};revGroups.add(group);
  const wrapped=function(...args){
    // Only fixed source/opaque receiver metadata is read. The arguments array and \
result.value are untouched.
    const method=args[0],metadata=args[1],source=method==='Runtime.callFunctionOn'?metadata?\
.functionDeclaration:null;
    const receiver=method==='Runtime.callFunctionOn'?metadata?.objectId:null;
    const install=source===c6InstallSource,fill=source===c6FillSource;
    const probe=source==='function() { return true; }'&&receiver===state.objectId;
    const worldRequest=method==='Page.createIsolatedWorld'&&typeof metadata?.worldName==='string'
      &&metadata.worldName.startsWith('checks-observation-');
    const frame=worldRequest?metadata.frameId:null,world=install?metadata?.executionContextId:null;
    if(worldRequest||source==='function() { this.dispose(); \
}'||method==='Runtime.releaseObjectGroup')c6Drop(state);
    let result;try{result=Reflect.apply(original,this,args);}catch(error){if(probe)c6Drop(st\
ate);throw error;}
    if(method==='Page.getFrameTree'||worldRequest||install||probe){
      try{result.then(reply=>{try{
        if(this!==session||holder.closed)throw new Error('C6_CDP_IDENTITY');
        if(method==='Page.getFrameTree'){
          const id=reply?.frameTree?.frame?.id;if(typeof id!=='string')throw new \
Error('C6_CDP_FRAME');state.frame=id;
        }else if(worldRequest){
          if(frame!==state.frame||!Number.isSafeInteger(reply?.executionContextId))throw \
new Error('C6_CDP_WORLD');
          state.world=reply.executionContextId;
        }else if(install){
          const id=reply?.result?.objectId;
          if(world!==state.world||state.frame===null||typeof \
id!=='string'||reply?.exceptionDetails!==undefined)
            throw new Error('C6_CDP_INSTALL');
          state.objectId=id;
        }
      }catch{c6Drop(state);revFail();}},()=>{if(probe||install||worldRequest)c6Drop(state);})
      .catch(()=>{c6Drop(state);revFail();});}catch{c6Drop(state);revFail();}
    }
    if(fill){
      const current=revMode();
      const owned=this===session&&!holder.closed&&receiver===state.objectId&&state.objectId!==null
        &&state.world!==null&&state.frame!==null&&current?.active===true
        &&current.case===holder.mode.case&&current.epoch===holder.mode.epoch
        &&current.path===holder.mode.path&&current.c6===holder.mode.c6
        &&holder.dev===revC6ProfileDev&&holder.ino===revC6ProfileIno&&revStarted
        &&(current.path===1?revC6ContextBound:revReady);
      if(!owned){revFail();return result;}
      const ordinal=++revNativeOrdinal,active=revActive,lifetime=revLossSelected()?revLossLi\
fetime:null;
      active.set(ordinal,1);revEmit(holder.mode,2,ordinal,1);
      const settled=fulfilled=>{
        const removed=active.delete(ordinal);
        if(lifetime?.retired)return; // Exact actual settlement remains private; no late \
observer output.
        if(!removed)revFail();revEmit(holder.mode,3,ordinal,1,0,fulfilled);};
      try{result.then(()=>settled(true),()=>settled(false)).catch(()=>{revFail();});}catch{revFail();}
    }
    return result;
  };
  try{if(!Reflect.defineProperty(session,'send',{...(descriptor??{configurable:true,writable\
:true,enumerable:true}),value:wrapped}))
    throw new Error('C6_CDP_DESCRIPTOR');group.methods.push(['send',descriptor,wrapped]);}ca\
tch{revFail();}
};
const c6BindContext=(context,mode)=>{
  revInit(mode);if(c6CdpHolders.has(context)){revFail();return;}
  const holder={context,mode,dev:revC6ProfileDev,ino:revC6ProfileIno,closed:false,sessions:new Map()};
  c6CdpHolders.set(context,holder);
  const descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(context,'newCDPSession'),original=context\
.newCDPSession;
  if(typeof original!=='function'){revFail();return;}
  const group={cap:context,methods:[]};revGroups.add(group);
  const wrapped=function(...args){const page=args[0];revC6CdpPending++;let result;
    try{result=Reflect.apply(original,this,args);}catch(error){revC6CdpPending--;revFail();t\
hrow error;}
    try{result.then(session=>{try{
      if(this!==context||holder.closed||!page||!session)throw new Error('C6_CDP_CONTEXT');
      // The original SDK creation validates this Page under this same actual context.
      c6InstallSend(holder,session,page);
    }catch{revFail();}finally{revC6CdpPending--;revPublish(mode,revSealed);revMaybeSeal();}},
    ()=>{revC6CdpPending--;revFail();revPublish(mode,revSealed);revMaybeSeal();}).catch(()=>\
{revFail();});}
    catch{revFail();}return result;};
  try{if(!Reflect.defineProperty(context,'newCDPSession',{...(descriptor??{configurable:true\
,writable:true,enumerable:true}),value:wrapped}))
    throw new Error('C6_CDP_DESCRIPTOR');group.methods.push(['newCDPSession',descriptor,wrap\
ped]);}catch{revFail();}
};
const c6ClosedContext=context=>{const holder=c6CdpHolders.get(context);if(holder){holder.closed=true;
  for(const state of holder.sessions.values())c6Drop(state);}};
const p4NativeLaunch=p4Chromium.launchPersistentContext;
p4Chromium.launchPersistentContext=function(...args){
  const launched=Reflect.apply(p4NativeLaunch,this,args);let mode=null;
  try{if(p4Fs.existsSync(p4ModeFile))mode=JSON.parse(p4Fs.readFileSync(p4ModeFile,'utf8'));}catch{}
  const c5Active=p4Fs.existsSync(c5ValuesFile);
  if(mode?.active!==true&&!c5Active)return launched;
  const phase=mode?.phase;
  return launched.then(context=>{
    const selected=revMode();
    if(selected?.c6){try{
      const actual=p4Fs.lstatSync(args[0]),canonical=p4Fs.realpathSync(args[0]);
      if(actual.isSymbolicLink()||!actual.isDirectory()||canonical!==args[0]
        ||actual.uid!==process.getuid()||(actual.mode&0o777)!==0o700
        ||!Number.isSafeInteger(actual.dev)||!Number.isSafeInteger(actual.ino))throw new \
Error('C6_PROFILE');
      revC6ProfileDev=actual.dev;revC6ProfileIno=actual.ino;
    }catch(error){revFail();return Promise.resolve(Reflect.apply(context.close,context,[]))
      .then(()=>{throw error;},closeFailure=>{revFail();throw closeFailure;});}}
    if(selected?.c6>=1&&selected.c6<=3)c6BindContext(context,selected);
    const originalClose=context.close;
    context.close=function(...closeArgs){const receiver=this;
      return (async()=>{
        const fail=()=>{try{p4Fs.appendFileSync(p4FailedFile,'1\\n',{mode:0o600});}catch{}};
        let fenceSucceeded=false;
        try{
          if(c5Active){c5ProfileScan(args[0]);if(mode?.active!==true)return;}
          if(!Number.isInteger(phase)||phase<1)throw new Error('PHASE');
          const deadline=performance.now()+3000;let timer;
          const fence=async()=>{
            for(const page of context.pages()){
              const location=new URL(page.url());
              if(location.origin===p4Origin&&location.pathname.startsWith('/p3/')){
                const state=await page.evaluate(async()=>{
                  const observer=globalThis.__p4NativeObserver;
                  return observer?await observer.seal():null;
                });
                if(state!==null&&(Object.keys(state).sort().join(',')!=='failed,pending,sequence'
                  ||state.failed!==false||state.pending!==0||!Number.isInteger(state.sequence)
                  ||state.sequence<1))throw new Error('FENCE');
              }
            }
            const remaining=deadline-performance.now();if(remaining<=0)throw new Error('TIME');
            const response=await fetch(p4Origin+'/p3/password-effects?kind=closed&phase='+phase,
              {method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json'},body:'{"seq":0}',
               signal:AbortSignal.timeout(Math.max(1,Math.floor(remaining)))});
            const ack=await response.json();
            if(response.status!==200||Object.keys(ack).sort().join(',')!=='accepted,seq'
              ||ack.accepted!==true||ack.seq!==0)throw new Error('ACK');
          };
          const holder=selected?.c6===3&&selected.path===1?c6CdpHolders.get(context):null;
          const current=holder?revMode():null,browser=holder?context.browser():null;
          const unavailable=holder?.context===context&&holder.closed===false&&selected.active===true
            &&Number.isInteger(selected.case)&&selected.case>0&&selected.epoch===selected.case
            &&current?.active===true&&current.c6===3&&current.path===1
            &&current.case===selected.case&&current.epoch===selected.epoch
            &&holder.mode.case===selected.case&&holder.mode.epoch===selected.epoch
            &&holder.mode.c6===3&&holder.mode.path===1&&revLocalMode?.case===selected.case
            &&revLocalMode?.epoch===selected.epoch&&revC6ContextBound&&revStarted&&revC6Probed===2
            &&holder.dev===revC6ProfileDev&&holder.ino===revC6ProfileIno
            &&browser!==null&&typeof browser?.isConnected==='function'&&browser.isConnected()===false;
          if(!unavailable){
            try{await Promise.race([fence(),new Promise((_,reject)=>{
              timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(new \
Error('TIME')),Math.max(1,deadline-performance.now()));})]);}
            finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
            fenceSucceeded=true;
          } // Genuine bound disconnection supplies UNKNOWN, never a healthy page fence.
        }catch(error){
          // Only the deliberate in-flight cut may leave a destroyed/stalled realm.
          // Malformed ACK/fence failures remain sticky even in C6.
          const unavailable=error instanceof Error&&(error.message==='TIME'
            ||error.message.includes('Target page, context or browser has been closed'));
          if(!selected?.c6||selected.c6===4||!unavailable)fail();
        }
        finally{
          let result;
          try{result=await Reflect.apply(originalClose,receiver,closeArgs);}
          catch(originalFailure){fail();throw originalFailure;}
          if(selected?.c6>=1&&selected.c6<=3)c6ClosedContext(receiver);
          if(selected?.c6){
            // Original close has settled, and the original native promise must also
            // settle before descriptor removal. Neither timeout nor Stop grants this.
            const observerDeadline=performance.now()+1000;
            if(selected.path===2){
              while((revActive.size||revC6HighPending||revC6CdpPending)&&performance.now()<o\
bserverDeadline)
                await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,10));
              if(revActive.size||revC6HighPending||revC6CdpPending)revFail();
              else{revC6Restored=revRestore();revEmit(selected,6,1,0,0,revC6Restored);}
            }
            revEmit(selected,13,phase,fenceSucceeded?1:0,0,true);
            // Reuse the same finite post-close observer budget for final ACK drain.
            let timer;try{const remaining=observerDeadline-performance.now();
              if(remaining<=0)throw new Error('C6_ACK_DRAIN');
              await Promise.race([revTail,new Promise((_,reject)=>{
                timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error('C6_ACK_DRAIN')),remaining);})]);}
            catch{revFail();}finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
          }
          if(fenceSucceeded){try{p4Fs.appendFileSync(p4SettledFile,phase+'\\n',{mode:0o600});}
            catch{fail();}}
          return result;
        }
      })();
    };
    return context;
  });
};
"""
        .formatted(
            JSON.writeValueAsString(playwright),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer.json").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer-failed").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("p4-native-observer-settled").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(ORIGIN),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("c5-private-values.json").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("c5-profile-counts.jsonl").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(
                Path.of("../studio-runner/dist/run/observationCollector.js")
                    .toAbsolutePath()
                    .normalize()
                    .toUri()
                    .toString()),
            c5ProfileScannerSupport());
  }

  private static String p3GuidedLaunchCounter() throws Exception {
    String playwright =
        Path.of("../studio-runner/node_modules/playwright/index.mjs")
            .toAbsolutePath()
            .normalize()
            .toUri()
            .toString();
    String main =
        Path.of("../studio-runner/dist/run/guidedMain.js")
            .toAbsolutePath()
            .normalize()
            .toUri()
            .toString();
    String file = JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("p3-guided-launch-count.json").toString());
    return "import {chromium} from '"
        + playwright
        + "';import fs from 'node:fs';const original=chromium.launchPersistentContext;const"
        + " registered=typeof original==='function';fs.writeFileSync("
        + file
        + ",JSON.stringify({registered,count:0}),{mode:0o600});"
        // Keep the constant in bounded chunks across formatter reflow.
        + "if(registered){chromium.launchPersistentContext=function(...args){const"
        + " state=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync("
        + file
        + ",'utf8'));"
        + "state.count++;const recording=args[1]&&args[1].recordVideo;"
        // STUDIO-OBS-013 TEST-001: the guided recording directory, for the recorder-fault test.
        + "if(recording&&typeof recording.dir==='string'){state.lastRecordVideoDir=recording.dir;}"
        + "fs.writeFileSync("
        + file
        + ",JSON.stringify(state),{mode:0o600});return Reflect.apply(original,this,args);};}"
        + p4NativeCloseObserverSdk()
        + p4GuidedRevocationObservers()
        + incrementalNativeFaultObserver()
        + "await import('"
        + main
        + "');";
  }

  /** Fault injection wraps the actual owning runtime; it never supplies a receipt or outcome. */
  private static String incrementalNativeFaultObserver() throws Exception {
    return """
const i15ModeFile=%s,i15Events=%s;

const i15CaptureRoot=%s;
const {canonical:i15Canonical,hash:i15Hash,validate:i15Validate}=await import(i15CaptureRoot);
const i15Known=new Set('schemaVersion sessionId workerId ownerFence scopeHash contextId contextRevision sourceRevision configurationRevision versions contract ledger availability errorMessage coordinate stepId ordinal idempotencyKey requestDigest operationHash generation documentId admission admissionId authorizationId admittedAt policyDecisionHash state dispatchCount outcomeCode assertionPassed executionEvidence evidenceHash authoredInputsHash preparationId attemptOrdinal kind creationId bootstrapId url origin observationHash bindingId producerAdmissionId'.split(' '));
let i15Cohort=null,i15CaptureEvents=0,i15CaptureBytes=0;
function i15Capture(original){try{
 const sessionHash=i15Hash(original.sessionId);if(i15Cohort===null)i15Cohort=sessionHash;if(i15Cohort!==sessionHash||i15CaptureEvents>=200||i15CaptureBytes>=1040000)return;
 const shape=[];const visit=(node,path)=>{if(shape.length>=200)return;shape.push({path,type:node===null?'NULL':Array.isArray(node)?'ARRAY':typeof node==='object'?'OBJECT':typeof node==='number'?'NUMBER':typeof node==='boolean'?'BOOLEAN':'STRING'});
 if(node&&typeof node==='object')for(const key of Object.keys(node)){const next=Array.isArray(node)?key:i15Known.has(key)?key:'sha256:'+i15Hash(key);visit(node[key],path+'/'+next);}};visit(original,'');
 let nodeValid=false;try{i15Validate('bridge',original,'outcome');nodeValid=true;}catch{}
 const states=new Set('ADMITTED DISPATCHING SUCCEEDED FAILED UNKNOWN_OUTCOME REVOKED OK PENDING TARGET_CHANGED POLICY_DENIED ASSERTION_FAILED TIMEOUT CANCELLED ACK_LOST'.split(' '));
 const canonical=i15Canonical(original),row={kind:'original-outcome-return',canonicalBodySha256:i15Hash(original),canonicalBodyBytes:Buffer.byteLength(canonical),sessionHash,admissionHash:i15Hash(original.ledger?.admission?.admissionId??null),shape,nodeOutcomeValid:nodeValid,state:states.has(original.ledger?.state)?original.ledger.state:'OTHER',outcomeCode:states.has(original.ledger?.outcomeCode)?original.ledger.outcomeCode:'OTHER',instrumentationError:false,truncated:shape.length>=200};
 let line=JSON.stringify(i15CaptureEvents>=199?{truncated:true,instrumentationError:false}:row)+'\\n';if(i15CaptureBytes+Buffer.byteLength(line)>1040000)line=JSON.stringify({truncated:true,instrumentationError:false})+'\\n';fs.appendFileSync(%s,line,{mode:0o600});i15CaptureEvents++;i15CaptureBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(line);
}catch{try{if(i15CaptureEvents<200&&i15CaptureBytes<1040000){fs.appendFileSync(%s,JSON.stringify({instrumentationError:true,stage:'capture'})+'\\n',{mode:0o600});i15CaptureEvents++;}}catch{}}}

const i15Start=RevRuntime.prototype.start;
RevRuntime.prototype.start=async function(...args){const result=await Reflect.apply(i15Start,this,args);
  let mode={};try{mode=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(i15ModeFile,'utf8'));}catch{}
  if(['drift-auth','drift-auth-pre'].includes(mode.mode)){
    const callback=this.options.callback;
    this.options.callback=async function(...cb){if(cb[0]==='outcomes'&&cb[1]?.kind==='prefixDrift'){
      const response=await fetch(%s,{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(cb[1])});
      if(!response.ok)throw new Error('UNKNOWN_OUTCOME');}
      return Reflect.apply(callback,this,cb);};}
  return result;};
const i15Update=RevRuntime.prototype.update;
RevRuntime.prototype.update=async function(...args){const result=await Reflect.apply(i15Update,this,args);
  let mode={};try{mode=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(i15ModeFile,'utf8'));}catch{}
  if(mode.mode==='append-input'&&args[0]?.contextRevision===2&&typeof args[1]==='string'){
    const response=await fetch(%s,{method:'POST',body:args[0].sessionId});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error('CONTEXT_LOST');}
  return result;};
const i15Prepare=RevRuntime.prototype.preparePrefix;
RevRuntime.prototype.preparePrefix=async function(...args){const result=await Reflect.apply(i15Prepare,this,args);
  let mode={};try{mode=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(i15ModeFile,'utf8'));}catch{}
  if(result.kind==='PREPARED'&&args[0]?.request?.coordinate?.stepId===mode.stepId)
    fs.appendFileSync(i15Events,JSON.stringify({phase:'PREPARED',sessionId:args[0].request.sessionId,
      preparationId:result.preparation.preparationId,expiresAt:result.preparation.expiresAt,evidenceHash:result.proposal.evidenceHash})+'\\n',{mode:0o600});
  return result;};
const i15Dispatch=RevRuntime.prototype.dispatch;
RevRuntime.prototype.dispatch=async function(request){
  let mode={};try{mode=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(i15ModeFile,'utf8'));}catch{}
  const selected=this.context?.prefixProgram&&request?.proposal?.coordinate?.stepId===mode.stepId;
  if(!selected){const original=await Reflect.apply(i15Dispatch,this,[request]);i15Capture(original);return original;}
  const event=phase=>fs.appendFileSync(i15Events,JSON.stringify({sessionId:request.sessionId,
    admissionId:request.ledger.admission.admissionId,attemptOrdinal:request.ledger.attemptOrdinal,
    stepId:request.proposal.coordinate.stepId,ordinal:request.proposal.coordinate.ordinal,phase})+'\\n',{mode:0o600});
  const observations=this.browser.observations,originalInvoke=observations.invoke,originalMatches=this.matches;
  const inject=!this.i15Injected&&['admitted-drift2','admitted-literal2','same-pair-literal','drift-auth','unknown2'].includes(mode.mode)&&request.proposal.coordinate.ordinal===mode.ordinal;
  if(inject)this.i15Injected=true;
  observations.invoke=async function(...args){event('NATIVE_ENTER');const result=await Reflect.apply(originalInvoke,this,args);
    event('NATIVE_COMPLETED');if(inject&&mode.mode==='unknown2')throw new Error('TARGET_CHANGED');return result;};
  if(inject&&['admitted-drift2','admitted-literal2','same-pair-literal','drift-auth'].includes(mode.mode))this.matches=async()=>false;
  event('DISPATCH_ENTER');
  try{const original=await Reflect.apply(i15Dispatch,this,[request]);i15Capture(original);return original;}
  finally{observations.invoke=originalInvoke;this.matches=originalMatches;event('DISPATCH_END');}
};
""".formatted(JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-mode.json").toString()),
        JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("obs015-native-events.jsonl").toString()),

        JSON.writeValueAsString(ORIGIN + "/obs015-drift"), JSON.writeValueAsString(ORIGIN + "/obs015-ack"));
  }

  private static void p3Application(com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange exchange)
      throws IOException {
    P6ChildCut heldCut = null;
    P3ConcurrentHold heldConcurrent = null;
    int concurrentOrdinal = -1;
    try {
      if (p3Mode == null) {
        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(404, -1);
        return;
      }
      String path = exchange.getRequestURI().getPath();
      if (path.equals("/p3/revocation-observer") && p4Revocation != null) {
        p4RevocationNotice(exchange);
        return;
      }
      if (path.equals("/p3/password-effects") && p4PasswordControls >= 0) {
        p4HandleObserver(exchange);
        return;
      }
      String document;
      if (path.equals("/p3/login") && exchange.getRequestMethod().equals("POST")) {
        String raw = new String(exchange.getRequestBody().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        var values = new java.util.HashMap<String, String>();
        for (String pair : raw.split("&")) {
          String[] parts = pair.split("=", 2);
          values.put(
              java.net.URLDecoder.decode(parts[0], StandardCharsets.UTF_8),
              java.net.URLDecoder.decode(
                  parts.length == 2 ? parts[1] : "", StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
        String password = values.getOrDefault("password", "");
        boolean accepted = password.equals(p3ExpectedPassword);
        P3_RECEIPTS.add(new P3Receipt(password, values.getOrDefault("username", ""), accepted));
        P3ConcurrentHold concurrent = p3ConcurrentHold;
        if (concurrent != null) {
          heldConcurrent = concurrent;
          concurrentOrdinal = concurrent.calls.getAndIncrement();
          concurrent.hold(accepted, concurrentOrdinal);
        }
        P6ChildCut selectedCut = p6ChildCut;
        if (selectedCut != null) {
          heldCut = selectedCut;
          if (!accepted || selectedCut.signInCalls.incrementAndGet() != 1) {
            selectedCut.failed = true;
          }
          selectedCut.signInEntered.countDown();
          try {
            if (!selectedCut.releaseSignIn.await(30, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
              selectedCut.failed = true;
            }
          } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
            selectedCut.failed = true;
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
          }
        }
        P4RevocationState held = p4Revocation;
        if (held != null) {
          synchronized (held) {
            held.signInEntered = accepted;
            if (!accepted) {
              held.failed = true;
            }
          }
          try {
            if (!held.releaseSignIn.await(20, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
              held.failed = true;
            }
          } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            held.failed = true;
          }
        }
        if (p4PasswordControls >= 0) {
          p4AwaitNativeSubmitFence(exchange.getRequestURI().getRawQuery());
        }
        if (accepted) {
          String session = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
          P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS.add(session);
          exchange
              .getResponseHeaders()
              .set(
                  "Set-Cookie", "p3_session=" + session + "; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/p3/");
          exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Location", "/p3/home");
          exchange.sendResponseHeaders(302, -1);
          return;
        }
        document = p3LoginDocument();
      } else if (path.equals("/p3/registration")) {
        document = p3LoginDocument() + "<h1>Registration</h1>";
      } else if (path.equals("/p3/login")) {
        document = p3LoginDocument();
      } else {
        String cookies = exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("Cookie");
        boolean signedIn =
            cookies != null
                && java.util.Arrays.stream(cookies.split(";"))
                    .map(String::trim)
                    .filter(cookie -> cookie.startsWith("p3_session="))
                    .map(cookie -> cookie.substring("p3_session=".length()))
                    .anyMatch(P3_NATIVE_SESSIONS::contains);
        if ((!path.equals("/p3/reports") || p3ReportsProtected) && !signedIn) {
          exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Location", "/p3/login");
          exchange.sendResponseHeaders(302, -1);
          return;
        }
        if (path.equals("/p3/home")) {
          document = "<main><h1>Home</h1><a href='/p3/reports'>Reports</a></main>";
        } else if (path.equals("/p3/reports")) {
          P3_REPORT_REQUESTS.incrementAndGet();
          document = "<main><h1>Reports</h1><p>Protected report</p></main>";
        } else {
          exchange.sendResponseHeaders(404, -1);
          return;
        }
      }
      byte[] body =
          ("<!doctype html><html><body>" + document + "</body></html>")
              .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8");
      exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
      exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, body.length);
      exchange.getResponseBody().write(body);
    } finally {
      try {
        exchange.close();
      } finally {
        if (heldCut != null) {
          heldCut.signInExited.countDown();
        }
        if (heldConcurrent != null && concurrentOrdinal >= 0 && concurrentOrdinal < 2) {
          heldConcurrent.exited[concurrentOrdinal].countDown();
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static Map<String, String> p4Query(String query) {
    Map<String, String> parsed = new java.util.HashMap<>();
    if (query != null) {
      for (String pair : query.split("&")) {
        String[] parts = pair.split("=", 2);
        if (parts.length != 2 || parsed.putIfAbsent(parts[0], parts[1]) != null) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid private observer query");
        }
      }
    }
    return parsed;
  }

  private static int p4PositiveOrdinal(String value) {
    if (value == null || !value.matches("[1-9][0-9]{0,8}")) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid private observer ordinal");
    }
    return Integer.parseInt(value);
  }

  private static int p4Number(JsonNode body, String name) {
    JsonNode number = body.path(name);
    if (!number.isIntegralNumber() || !number.canConvertToInt() || number.intValue() < 0) {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid private observer count");
    }
    return number.intValue();
  }

  private static void p4HandleObserver(com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange exchange)
      throws IOException {
    boolean accepted = false;
    int sequence = 0;
    int timingSite = 200;
    boolean timingScopeKnown = false;
    P4DocumentWitness completion = null;
    try {
      if (!exchange.getRequestMethod().equals("POST")) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid private observer method");
      }
      Map<String, String> query = p4Query(exchange.getRequestURI().getRawQuery());
      String kind = query.get("kind");
      byte[] bytes = exchange.getRequestBody().readNBytes(1025);
      if (bytes.length > 1024) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Private observer body bound");
      }
      JsonNode body = C.parse(new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      if (!body.isObject()) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid private observer body");
      }
      if ("closed".equals(kind)) {
        int phase = p4PositiveOrdinal(query.get("phase"));
        if (query.size() != 2 || body.size() != 1 || p4Number(body, "seq") != 0) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid original close fence");
        }
        List<P4DocumentWitness> documents =
            P4_DOCUMENTS.values().stream().filter(witness -> witness.phase == phase).toList();
        if (documents.isEmpty() || P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.get()) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Missing actual page observer fence");
        }
        for (P4DocumentWitness witness : documents) {
          synchronized (witness) {
            if (!witness.fenced
                || !witness.fenceCompleted
                || witness.events.size() != witness.acknowledged.size()) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("Incomplete actual page observer fence");
            }
          }
        }
        P4_CLOSED_PHASES.add(phase);
      } else {
        int document = p4PositiveOrdinal(query.get("document"));
        P4DocumentWitness witness = P4_DOCUMENTS.get(document);
        if (query.size() != 2 || witness == null || P4_CLOSED_PHASES.contains(witness.phase)) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Missing actual observer document");
        }
        timingScopeKnown = p4Revocation != null && witness.phase == p4Revocation.c6Phase;
        sequence = p4Number(body, "seq");
        if (sequence < 1 || sequence > 1024) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("Private observer sequence bound");
        }
        synchronized (witness) {
          if (witness.fenced && !"fence-complete".equals(kind)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Notification after real observer fence");
          }
          if ("fence-complete".equals(kind)) {
            if (body.size() != 1
                || !witness.fenced
                || witness.fenceCompleted
                || sequence != witness.events.size()
                || sequence != witness.acknowledged.size()) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid actual final fence ACK completion");
            }
            completion = witness;
          } else if (List.of("registered", "write", "submit").contains(kind)
              || (p4Revocation != null
                  && p4Revocation.c6Kind > 0
                  && p4Revocation.c6Kind < 4
                  && witness.phase == p4Revocation.c6Phase
                  && List.of("focus-enter", "focus-exit").contains(kind))) {
            if (body.size() != 1 || witness.events.putIfAbsent(sequence, kind) != null) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid original event notification");
            }
            switch (kind) {
              case "registered" -> P4_EFFECT_REGISTRATIONS.incrementAndGet();
              case "write" -> P4_PASSWORD_WRITES.incrementAndGet();
              case "submit" -> P4_NATIVE_SUBMITS.incrementAndGet();
              case "focus-enter" -> {
                timingSite = 230;
                P4RevocationState selected = p4Revocation;
                if (selected.c6FocusAt != 0 || selected.c6Kind == 4) {
                  throw new IllegalArgumentException("Duplicate C6 native focus");
                }
                selected.c6FocusAt = System.nanoTime();
                if (selected.c6Timing != null) {
                  selected.c6Timing.mark(3);
                }
                // DOM focus is effect support only; the real CDP entry requests the probe.
              }
              case "focus-exit" -> {
                timingSite = 231;
                if (p4Revocation.c6FocusAt == 0) {
                  throw new IllegalArgumentException("C6 focus exit without entry");
                }
              }
              default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid original event kind");
            }
          } else if ("ack".equals(kind)) {
            timingSite = 250;
            if (body.size() != 1
                || !witness.events.containsKey(sequence)
                || !witness.acknowledged.add(sequence)) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid actual event ACK");
            }
          } else if ("fence".equals(kind)) {
            timingSite = 260;
            if (body.size() != 5
                || p4Number(body, "pending") != 0
                || !body.path("failed").isBoolean()
                || body.path("failed").booleanValue()
                || sequence != witness.events.size()
                || sequence != witness.acknowledged.size()
                || !"registered".equals(witness.events.get(1))
                || p4Number(body, "writes")
                    != witness.events.values().stream().filter("write"::equals).count()
                || p4Number(body, "submits")
                    != witness.events.values().stream().filter("submit"::equals).count()) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid authoritative event completion fence");
            }
            for (int ordinal = 1; ordinal <= sequence; ordinal++) {
              if (!witness.acknowledged.contains(ordinal)) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Missing actual ordered event ACK");
              }
            }
            witness.fenced = true;
          } else {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid private observer phase");
          }
          witness.notifyAll();
        }
      }
      accepted = true;
    } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
      p8TimingRefusal(p4Revocation, timingSite, timingScopeKnown);
      P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
    } catch (IOException failure) {
      p8TimingRefusal(p4Revocation, timingSite, timingScopeKnown);
      P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
      throw failure;
    }
    byte[] response =
        C.object()
            .put("accepted", accepted)
            .put("seq", sequence)
            .toString()
            .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    if (accepted && completion != null) {
      synchronized (completion) {
        p4WriteObserverResponse(exchange, accepted, response);
        completion.fenceCompleted = true;
        completion.notifyAll();
      }
    } else {
      p4WriteObserverResponse(exchange, accepted, response);
    }
  }

  private static void p4WriteObserverResponse(
      com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange exchange, boolean accepted, byte[] response)
      throws IOException {
    try {
      exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", "application/json");
      exchange.sendResponseHeaders(accepted ? 200 : 409, response.length);
      exchange.getResponseBody().write(response);
      exchange.getResponseBody().close();
    } catch (IOException | RuntimeException failure) {
      P4RevocationState selected = p4Revocation;
      if (failure instanceof RuntimeException) {
        p8TimingRefusal(selected, 300);
        P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
      } else {
        Map<String, String> query = Map.of();
        P4DocumentWitness actual = null;
        try {
          query = p4Query(exchange.getRequestURI().getRawQuery());
          actual = P4_DOCUMENTS.get(p4PositiveOrdinal(query.get("document")));
        } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
          p8TimingRefusal(selected, 301);
          P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
        }
        if (accepted
            && selected != null
            && selected.c6Kind > 0
            && selected.c6Kind < 4
            && "focus-enter".equals(query.get("kind"))
            && actual != null
            && actual.phase == selected.c6Phase) {
          // Provisional transport unavailability, not a healthy ACK or no-effect receipt.
          // Terminal proof below must independently confirm the realm was genuinely closed.
          selected.c6InterruptedNotification = true;
          if (selected.c6Timing != null) {
            synchronized (selected.c6Timing) {
              selected.c6Timing.provisionalFocusLossObserved = true;
            }
          }
        } else {
          p8TimingRefusal(selected, 302);
          P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
        }
      }
      throw failure;
    }
  }

  private static void p4AwaitNativeSubmitFence(String query) {
    try {
      List<P4DocumentWitness> documents =
          P4_DOCUMENTS.values().stream()
              .filter(witness -> witness.phase == P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get())
              .toList();
      if (query != null || documents.size() != 1) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Missing unique actual native submit document");
      }
      P4DocumentWitness witness = documents.getFirst();
      long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofMillis(2500).toNanos();
      synchronized (witness) {
        while (!witness.fenceCompleted
            && !P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.get()
            && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
          long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
          if (remaining <= 0) {
            break;
          }
          java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.timedWait(witness, remaining);
        }
        if (!witness.fenced
            || !witness.fenceCompleted
            || !witness.events.containsValue("submit")
            || witness.events.size() != witness.acknowledged.size()) {
          P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
        }
      }
    } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
      P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
    } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
      Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
      P4_NOTIFICATION_FAILED.set(true);
    }
  }

  private static String p4PageObserverScript(int document) {
    return """
<script>(()=>{
  const s={sequence:0,pending:new Set(),failed:false,sealed:false,writes:0,submits:0};
  const endpoint=kind=>'/p3/password-effects?document=%d&kind='+kind;
  const request=async(kind,payload,deadline)=>{
    const remaining=deadline-performance.now();if(remaining<=0)throw new Error('FENCE');
    const response=await fetch(endpoint(kind),
      {method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(payload),
       signal:AbortSignal.timeout(Math.max(1,Math.floor(remaining)))});
    const ack=await response.json();
    if(response.status!==200||Object.keys(ack).sort().join(',')!=='accepted,seq'
      ||ack.accepted!==true||ack.seq!==payload.seq)throw new Error('ACK');
  };
  const notify=kind=>{
    if(s.sealed)s.failed=true;
    const seq=++s.sequence;if(kind==='write')s.writes++;if(kind==='submit')s.submits++;
    const deadline=performance.now()+1000;
    const p=(async()=>{
      await request(kind,{seq},deadline);
      await request('ack',{seq},deadline);
    })()
      .catch(()=>{s.failed=true;});
    s.pending.add(p);void p.finally(()=>{s.pending.delete(p);});return p;
  };
  let closing=null;
  const seal=()=>closing??=(async()=>{
    s.sealed=true;const deadline=performance.now()+2500;let timer;
    try{
      await Promise.race([Promise.all([...s.pending]),new Promise((_,reject)=>{
        timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(new \
Error('PENDING')),Math.max(1,deadline-performance.now()));})]);
      if(s.pending.size||s.failed)throw new Error('FAILED');
      await request('fence',{seq:s.sequence,pending:s.pending.size,failed:s.failed,
        writes:s.writes,submits:s.submits},deadline);
      // Sent only after the page received and validated the real final fence ACK.
      await request('fence-complete',{seq:s.sequence},deadline);
      return {pending:0,failed:false,sequence:s.sequence};
    }catch{ s.failed=true;throw new Error('FENCE'); }finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
  })();
  globalThis.__p4NativeObserver={seal};
  document.addEventListener('input',event=>{
    if(event.target instanceof HTMLInputElement&&event.target.type==='password')void \
notify('write');});
  document.addEventListener('submit',()=>{void notify('submit');void \
seal().catch(()=>{s.failed=true;});});
  %s
  void notify('registered');
})();</script>
"""
        .formatted(
            document,
            p4Revocation != null && p4Revocation.c6Kind > 0 && p4Revocation.c6Kind < 4
                ? "document.addEventListener('focusin',event=>{if(event.target instanceof"
                    + " HTMLInputElement&&event.target.type==='password'){const"
                    + " end=performance.now()+"
                    + (p4Revocation.c6Kind == 1 ? 40000 : 8000)
                    + ";"
                    + "while(performance.now()<end){}}});"
                : "");
  }

  private static String p3LoginDocument() {
    if (p4PasswordControls >= 0) {
      int ordinal = P4_DOCUMENT_ORDINAL.incrementAndGet();
      P4_DOCUMENTS.put(ordinal, new P4DocumentWitness(P4_PHASE_ORDINAL.get()));
      String passwords = "<input name='password' type='password'>".repeat(p4PasswordControls);
      return "<form method='post' action='/p3/login'><label>User<input name='username'></label>"
          + passwords
          + "<button type='submit'>Sign in</button></form><button type='button'>Reports</button>"
          + p4PageObserverScript(ordinal);
    }
    return "<form method='post' action='/p3/login'><label>User<input name='username'></label>"
        + "<input name='password' type='password'><button type='submit'>Sign in</button></form>"
        + "<button type='button'>Reports</button>";
  }

  private static ObjectNode incrementalSourcePlanWindow(ObjectNode result, JsonNode context) {
    if (!context.has("learningWindow")) return result;
    var allowed = new java.util.HashSet<String>();
    context.path("learningWindow").path("learnableStepIds").forEach(step -> allowed.add(step.asText()));
    JsonNode originalReferences = result.path("sourceReferences").deepCopy();
    JsonNode originalObligations = result.path("obligations").deepCopy();
    var references = result.putArray("sourceReferences"); var obligations = result.putArray("obligations");
    var indexes = new java.util.HashMap<Integer, Integer>();
    for (JsonNode original : originalObligations) {
      if (!allowed.contains(original.path("stepId").asText())) continue;
      ObjectNode copied = original.deepCopy();
      if (!original.path("referenceIndex").isNull()) {
        int old = original.path("referenceIndex").asInt();
        int index = indexes.computeIfAbsent(old, key -> { int next = references.size(); references.add(originalReferences.get(key)); return next; });
        copied.put("referenceIndex", index);
      }
      obligations.add(copied);
    }
    return result;
  }

  private static ObjectNode p3Model(ObjectNode result, JsonNode context, String kind) {
    if (kind.equals("SOURCE_PLAN")) {
      String destination =
          ORIGIN
              + (p3Mode == P3Mode.LOGIN
                  ? "/p3/login"
                  : p3Mode == P3Mode.REPORTS ? "/p3/reports" : "/p3/registration");
      var references = result.putArray("sourceReferences");
      references
          .addObject()
          .put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan")
          .put("sourceRevision", "1")
          .put("stepId", ONE)
          .put("startUtf16", 5)
          .put("endUtf16", 5 + destination.length())
          .put("kind", "destination")
          .put("intendedSlot", "navigate.destination");
      var obligations = result.putArray("obligations");
      obligations.add(obligation(ONE, 0, "navigate", "page", 0, null));
      if (p3Mode == P3Mode.LOGIN) {
        for (String name : List.of("USER", "PASS")) {
          JsonNode declaring = null;
          for (JsonNode configured : context.path("configurationReferences")) {
            if (configured.path("referenceName").asText().equals(name)
                && configured.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
                && configured.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("fill.value")) {
              declaring = configured;
            }
          }
          if (declaring == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Authentic P3 declaration missing");
          }
          var reference =
              ((ObjectNode) declaring)
                  .deepCopy()
                  .put("referenceKind", "configuration")
                  .put("configurationRevision", "1");
          if (name.equals("USER")) {
            reference.remove("referenceName");
          }
          references.add(reference);
        }
        obligations.add(obligation(TWO, 0, "fill", "User", 1, null));
        obligations.add(obligation(TWO, 1, "fill", "password", 2, null));
        obligations.add(obligation(THREE, 0, "click", "Sign in", null, null));
        obligations.add(obligation(THREE, 1, "click", "Reports", null, null));
        obligations.add(
            obligation(THREE, 2, "assert", "Reports", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
      } else {
        String target = p3Mode == P3Mode.REGISTRATION ? "Registration" : "Reports";
        obligations.add(
            obligation(TWO, 0, "assert", target, null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
        obligations.add(
            obligation(THREE, 0, "assert", target, null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
      }
      result.putArray("needs");
      return incrementalSourcePlanWindow(result, context);
    }
    JsonNode obligation = context.path("obligations").get(0);
    if (p4AdversarialPasswordSelection
        && kind.equals("NEXT_ACTION")
        && obligation.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)) {
      if (!obligation.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
          || obligation.path("occurrence").asInt(-1) != 0
          || !obligation.path("targetIntent").asText().equals("User")) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Actual USER-stage inference was not reached");
      }
      JsonNode password = null;
      for (JsonNode remaining : context.path("obligations")) {
        if (remaining.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)
            && remaining.path("occurrence").asInt(-1) == 1
            && remaining.path("operationKind").asText().equals("fill")
            && remaining.path("targetIntent").asText().equals("password")) {
          if (password != null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Actual password obligation is not unique");
          }
          password = remaining;
        }
      }
      if (password == null) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Actual accepted password obligation is missing");
      }
      JsonNode input = context.path("inputs").get(password.path("referenceIndex").asInt());
      if (input == null
          || !input.path("kind").asText().equals("secretRef")
          || !input.path("sourceStepId").asText().equals(TWO)
          || !input.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("fill.value")) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Actual accepted password input identity is missing");
      }
      P4_FIRST_GUIDED_REQUEST.compareAndSet(
          null,
          C.object()
              .put("actualNextAction", true)
              .put("availableUserOrdinal", obligation.path("occurrence").asInt())
              .put("selectedPasswordOrdinal", password.path("occurrence").asInt())
              .put(
                  "passwordCount",
                  context.path("observation").path("coverage").path("passwordControls").asInt(-1))
              .put("actualSameStepSecretInput", true)
              .put(
                  "actualPublicUserAvailable",
                  java.util.stream.StreamSupport.stream(
                          context.path("observation").path("nodes").spliterator(), false)
                      .anyMatch(
                          node ->
                              node.path("name").asText().equals("User")
                                  && node.path("role").asText().equals("textbox")
                                  && node.path("visible").asBoolean()
                                  && node.path("actionable").asBoolean())));
      obligation = password;
    }
    String operationKind = obligation.path("operationKind").asText();
    String targetName = obligation.path("targetIntent").asText();
    if (operationKind.equals("navigate")) {
      result
          .put("status", "ACTION")
          .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
          .putNull("question");
      result
          .putObject("action")
          .putObject("operation")
          .put("kind", "navigate")
          .put("destinationInputId", context.path("inputs").get(0).path("inputId").asText());
      result.withObject("/action").putObject("target").put("kind", "page");
      return result;
    }
    if (targetName.equals("password")) {
      result
          .put("status", "ACTION")
          .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
          .putNull("question");
      var action = result.putObject("action");
      action
          .putObject("operation")
          .put("kind", "fill")
          .putObject("value")
          .put("kind", "secretRef")
          .put(
              "sourceInputId",
              context
                  .path("inputs")
                  .get(obligation.path("referenceIndex").asInt())
                  .path("inputId")
                  .asText());
      action.putObject("target").put("kind", "passwordControl");
      return result;
    }
    String role =
        operationKind.equals("assert")
            ? "heading"
            : targetName.equals("Reports")
                ? "link"
                : operationKind.equals("fill") ? "textbox" : "button";
    JsonNode selected = null;
    for (JsonNode node : context.path("observation").path("nodes")) {
      if (node.path("name").asText().equals(targetName)
          && node.path("role").asText().equals(role)) {
        if (selected != null) {
          throw new IllegalStateException("P3 target is not unique");
        }
        selected = node;
      }
    }
    if (selected == null) {
      result.put("status", "UNSUPPORTED").put("reasonCode", "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE");
      return result;
    }
    result
        .put("status", "ACTION")
        .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
        .putNull("question");
    var action = result.putObject("action");
    var operation = action.putObject("operation").put("kind", operationKind);
    action
        .putObject("target")
        .put("nodeId", selected.path("nodeId").asText())
        .putObject("locator")
        .put("kind", "role")
        .put("role", role)
        .put("name", targetName)
        .put("exact", true);
    if (operationKind.equals("fill")) {
      operation
          .putObject("value")
          .put("kind", "authoredLiteral")
          .put(
              "sourceInputId",
              context
                  .path("inputs")
                  .get(obligation.path("referenceIndex").asInt())
                  .path("inputId")
                  .asText());
    }
    if (operationKind.equals("assert")) {
      operation.put("predicate", "visible").putNull("expected");
    }
    return result;
  }

  private static final class CountedLoginVault implements StudioVaultProvider {
    final List<OwnedVaultRead> reads = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    final List<VaultScope> lists = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

    @Override
    public String name() {
      return VAULT.name();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<VaultEntry> read(ReferenceKind kind, VaultScope scope, String name) {
      if (secretLoginScenario && kind == ReferenceKind.SECRET) {
        S2TraceBinding binding = s2TraceBinding;
        if (binding == null
            || !binding.scope().equals(scope)
            || !name.equals("PASS")
            || c5Capture != binding.capture()
            || binding.capture().selectedGuidedSession == null) {
          throw new IllegalStateException("S2 exact pre-read capture binding mismatch");
        }
        binding.capture().armTrace();
        assertThat(binding.capture().rootLogger.getLevel())
            .isEqualTo(ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.TRACE);
        assertThat(binding.capture().appLogger.getLevel())
            .isEqualTo(ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.TRACE);
      }
      P4RevocationState selected = p4Revocation;
      if (selected != null
          && selected.c6Kind >= 1
          && selected.c6Kind <= 4
          && kind == ReferenceKind.SECRET
          && name.equals("PASS")
          && VaultScope.of("CSI-456", "fixture-other", selected.test).equals(scope)) {
        C5Capture active = c5Capture;
        if (active == null || active != selected.c6Capture) {
          selected.failed = true;
          if (selected.c6Capture != null) {
            synchronized (selected.c6Capture) {
              selected.c6Capture.failed = true;
            }
          }
        } else {
          try {
            active.armTrace();
          } catch (RuntimeException | AssertionError failure) {
            selected.failed = true;
            synchronized (active) {
              active.failed = true;
            }
          }
        }
      }
      VaultScope disappearing = P3_FAIL_READ.get();
      if (kind == ReferenceKind.SECRET
          && name.equals("PASS")
          && scope.equals(disappearing)
          && P3_FAIL_READ.compareAndSet(disappearing, null)) {
        // The current entry vanishes only at its real value-read boundary; another scope remains.
        VAULT.delete(kind, scope, name);
      }
      Optional<VaultEntry> result;
      VaultScope unavailableScope = P7_OUTAGE_SCOPE.get();
      if (kind == ReferenceKind.SECRET
          && name.equals("PASS")
          && scope.equals(unavailableScope)
          && P7_OUTAGE_SCOPE.compareAndSet(unavailableScope, null)) {
        VAULT.makeUnavailable();
        try {
          result = VAULT.read(kind, scope, name); // Original provider increments and throws itself.
        } finally {
          VAULT.makeAvailable(); // Preserve every counter, entry, version and returned array.
        }
      } else {
        result = VAULT.read(kind, scope, name);
      }
      reads.add(
          new OwnedVaultRead(
              com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.FakeStudioVaultProvider.key(kind, scope, name),
              result.map(VaultEntry::versionIdentifier).orElse(null),
              result.map(VaultEntry::value).orElse(null)));
      return result;
    }

    @Override
    public String create(VaultScope scope, String name, char[] value, List<String> origins) {
      return VAULT.create(scope, name, value, origins);
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> listSecretNames(VaultScope scope) {
      lists.add(scope);
      return VAULT.listSecretNames(scope);
    }

    int readCount(ReferenceKind kind, VaultScope scope, String name) {
      String key =
          com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.FakeStudioVaultProvider.key(kind, scope, name);
      return Math.toIntExact(reads.stream().filter(read -> read.key.equals(key)).count());
    }
  }

  /** No printable representation of the actual delivered array, even on assertion failure. */
  private static final class OwnedVaultRead {
    final String key;
    final String version;
    final char[] value;

    OwnedVaultRead(String key, String version, char[] value) {
      this.key = key;
      this.version = version;
      this.value = value;
    }
  }

  private static boolean allErased(char[] value) {
    for (char character : value) {
      if (character != '\0') {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  private JsonNode awaitJoinedVideo(UUID execution) throws Exception {
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(45).toNanos();
    JsonNode snapshot;
    do {
      snapshot = publicCall("/api/v1/studio/executions/" + execution, null, 200, null);
      if (snapshot.path("streamComplete").asBoolean()) {
        break;
      }
      Thread.sleep(30);
    } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
    assertThat(snapshot.path("streamComplete").asBoolean()).isTrue();
    assertThat(snapshot.path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("PASSED");
    assertThat(snapshot.path("video").path("status").asText()).isEqualTo("AVAILABLE");
    return snapshot;
  }

  /** Independent recursive key sorting; does not call production hash/canonical helpers. */
  private static String independentArtifactHash(JsonNode artifact) throws Exception {
    return HexFormat.of()
        .formatHex(
            MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
                .digest(independentCanonical(artifact).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
  }

  private static String independentCanonical(JsonNode value) throws Exception {
    if (value.isObject()) {
      var names = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
      value.fieldNames().forEachRemaining(names::add);
      names.sort(String::compareTo);
      var fields = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
      for (String name : names) {
        fields.add(JSON.writeValueAsString(name) + ":" + independentCanonical(value.get(name)));
      }
      return "{" + String.join(",", fields) + "}";
    }
    if (value.isArray()) {
      var items = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
      for (JsonNode item : value) {
        items.add(independentCanonical(item));
      }
      return "[" + String.join(",", items) + "]";
    }
    return JSON.writeValueAsString(value);
  }

  private static ObjectNode joinedWorkerIdentity() throws Exception {
    var identity = C.object();
    for (String relative :
        List.of(
            "run/child.js",
            "guided/runtime.js",
            "versions.js",
            "runtime-versions.json",
            "validator/observedSchemas.json")) {
      identity.put(
          relative,
          HexFormat.of()
              .formatHex(
                  MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
                      .digest(
                          Files.readAllBytes(Path.of("../studio-runner/dist").resolve(relative)))));
    }
    return identity;
  }

  private static ObjectNode decodeJoinedVideo(Path video, UUID execution) throws Exception {
    Path decoder = null;
    for (String directory : System.getenv("PATH").split(java.io.File.pathSeparator)) {
      Path candidate = Path.of(directory).resolve("ffmpeg").toAbsolutePath().normalize();
      if (Files.isRegularFile(candidate) && Files.isExecutable(candidate)) {
        decoder = candidate.toRealPath();
        break;
      }
    }
    assertThat(decoder)
        .as("installed native decoder, also used by runner fixture gates")
        .isNotNull();
    Path output = TEMP.resolve("native-decode-" + execution + ".log");
    Process decode =
        new ProcessBuilder(
                decoder.toString(),
                "-nostdin",
                "-i",
                video.toString(),
                "-map",
                "0:v:0",
                "-f",
                "null",
                "-")
            .redirectErrorStream(true)
            .redirectOutput(output.toFile())
            .start();
    awaitJoinedProcess(decode, 30, "native application video decode");
    String report = Files.readString(output);
    var dimensions = java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("Video:.*? (\\d+)x(\\d+)").matcher(report);
    assertThat(dimensions.find()).as("decoder returned actual video dimensions").isTrue();
    int width = Integer.parseInt(dimensions.group(1));
    int height = Integer.parseInt(dimensions.group(2));
    assertThat(width).isPositive();
    assertThat(height).isPositive();
    var frames = java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("frame=\\s*(\\d+)").matcher(report);
    int count = 0;
    while (frames.find()) {
      count = Integer.parseInt(frames.group(1));
    }
    assertThat(count).as("actual native frames decoded through EOF").isPositive();
    return C.object()
        .put("executionId", execution.toString())
        .put("width", width)
        .put("height", height)
        .put("decodedFrames", count)
        .put(
            "decoderSha256",
            HexFormat.of()
                .formatHex(
                    MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(Files.readAllBytes(decoder))));
  }

  private static void awaitJoinedProcess(Process process, int seconds, String label)
      throws Exception {
    try {
      boolean finished = process.waitFor(seconds, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS);
      assertThat(finished).as("bounded %s", label).isTrue();
      assertThat(process.exitValue()).as("%s exit", label).isZero();
    } finally {
      if (process.isAlive()) {
        process.destroyForcibly();
        process.waitFor(5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS);
      }
    }
  }

  /** Private capture of actual existing sinks; usable around the required C4 sub-runs too. */
  private static final class C5ClaimSampler implements AutoCloseable {
    private enum Segment {
      OWNER_LOCK,
      JDBC_PATH,
      CURRENT_AUTH,
      SOURCE_CHECK,
      SCHEMA,
      JSON_RENDER,
      LOG_ENCODER,
      COMMIT,
      OTHER,
      FIXTURE_SCAN
    }

    private enum Status {
      OFF,
      UNOBSERVED,
      OBSERVED,
      FAILED,
      NON_QUIESCENT
    }

    private static final int MAX_THREADS = 8;
    private final Object gate = new Object();
    private final java.util.Set<Thread> executing =
        java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>());
    private final long[][] counts = new long[Segment.values().length][Thread.State.values().length];
    private final java.util.Map<Thread, Long> entered = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();

    private record PgClaimBinding(int pid) {}

    private final Map<Thread, PgClaimBinding> bindings = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();

    private record SourceSample(PgClaimBinding binding, int family, int phase) {}

    private record SourceClassification(int family, int phase) {}

    private final Map<Thread, SourceSample> sourceSamples = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();
    private volatile boolean bindingUnconfirmed, pgExactClaimAttribution;
    private final java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean pgOnce =
