        new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean();
    private volatile Thread pgObserver;

    private enum PgStatus {
      NOT_TRIGGERED,
      RUNNING,
      OBSERVED,
      UNOBSERVED,
      FAILED,
      AMBIGUOUS,
      TRUNCATED,
      NON_QUIESCENT
    }

    private volatile PgStatus pgStatus = PgStatus.NOT_TRIGGERED;
    // Separate fixed scalar; the original forty aggregate bins retain their meanings.
    private int pgBlockerCallerPoint = -1;
    private final long[][] pgStagesAndWaits = new long[7][10];
    // Independent nonadditive partitions: SQL 0..6, source family 7..12, caller 13..39.
    // Never sum partitions: the same confirmed blocker can contribute to each.
    // Source codes: 7 claim, 8 translation, 9 observation/materialization, 10 consume/outcome,
    // 11 read/start/command (including publication), 12 stop/reconcile. Exact Translation.call
    // phases:
    // 13 reserve, 14 send, 15 apply, 16 duplicate, 17 reject accounting, 18 late accounting,
    // 19 preparation capture. Family 9 method labels: 20 observe, 21 observeV2, 22 materialize,
    // 23 allocateBinding, 24 runnerContext, 25 storePreparation, 26 bootstrap, 27 beginRefresh,
    // 28 committedRefresh. Family 10: 29 recordSecretDecision, 30 refuseResolvedSecret, 31 admit,
    // 32 advance, 33 outcome, 34 outcomeV2, 35 completeStep, 36 recordEvidence, 37 consume,
    // 38 lifecycle, 39 existingAdmission. Caller labels do not assert guard completion or cost.
    private final long[] pgBlockerStages = new long[40],
        pgRelations = new long[3],
        pgWaitEvents = new long[8];
    private long pgRows, pgCandidates, pgBlockers;
    private volatile boolean stopping;
    private volatile Status status = Status.OFF;
    private long empty, unattributed, cycles, claimSessionValidationSamples;
    private final Thread sampler;
    private final long end;
    private Thread finalizer;
    private final long publicationRemainingNanos;
    // Disjoint rows: 59 unknown/canonical99/schema104, then 65 encoding/69 budget/82 send-call.
    private final long[][] publicationPhases = new long[6][3];

    C5ClaimSampler(boolean selected, Thread finalizer, long origin, long remainingNanos) {
      publicationRemainingNanos = remainingNanos;
      for (long[] row : publicationPhases) {
        row[1] = -1;
        row[2] = -1;
      }
      if (!selected) {
        sampler = null;
        end = 0;
        return;
      }
      status = Status.UNOBSERVED;
      end = origin + java.time.Duration.ofSeconds(30).toNanos();
      this.finalizer = finalizer;
      sampler =
          Thread.ofPlatform().daemon().name("private-c5-claim-sampler").unstarted(this::sample);
      try {
        sampler.start();
      } catch (Throwable unavailable) {
        status = Status.FAILED;
      }
    }

    boolean observingCallbacks() {
      try {
        synchronized (gate) {
          return sampler != null && !stopping && System.nanoTime() < end;
        }
      } catch (Throwable unavailable) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    PgClaimBinding bindPrequery(
        org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager manager, JdbcTemplate jdbc) {
      try {
        if (!observingCallbacks()
            || !(manager instanceof org.springframework.orm.jpa.JpaTransactionManager jpa)
            || jpa.getDataSource() != jdbc.getDataSource()
            || jpa.getEntityManagerFactory() == null) {
          return null;
        }
        Object resource =
            org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.getResource(
                jpa.getEntityManagerFactory());
        if (!(resource instanceof org.springframework.orm.jpa.EntityManagerHolder holder)
            || !(holder.getEntityManager()
                instanceof org.hibernate.engine.spi.SessionImplementor session)) {
          return null;
        }
        var logical = session.getJdbcCoordinator().getLogicalConnection();
        if (!(logical
                instanceof
                org.hibernate.resource.jdbc.internal.LogicalConnectionManagedImpl managed)
            || !managed.isOpen()
            || !managed.isPhysicallyConnected()
            || managed.getConnectionHandlingMode().getReleaseMode()
                == org.hibernate.ConnectionReleaseMode.AFTER_STATEMENT) {
          return null;
        }
        // Never obtain/borrow a connection when the native logical connection is not connected.
        var existing = managed.getPhysicalConnection();
        if (existing == null || existing.isClosed()) {
          return null;
        }
        var pg = existing.unwrap(org.postgresql.PGConnection.class);
        int pid = pg.getBackendPID(); // Already-connected handshake metadata; no statement/SQL.
        if (pid <= 0) {
          return null;
        }
        synchronized (gate) {
          Thread current = Thread.currentThread();
          if (stopping || System.nanoTime() >= end || bindings.containsKey(current)) {
            return null;
          }
          if (!executing.contains(current) && unionSizeLocked() >= MAX_THREADS) {
            bindingUnconfirmed = true;
            return null;
          }
          var binding = new PgClaimBinding(pid);
          bindings.put(current, binding);
          return binding;
        }
      } catch (Throwable unavailable) {
        return null; // Diagnostic availability cannot replace original callback/status/error.
      }
    }

    void retirePrequery(PgClaimBinding binding) {
      if (binding == null) {
        return;
      }
      try {
        synchronized (gate) {
          Thread current = Thread.currentThread();
          if (bindings.get(current) == binding) {
            bindings.remove(current);
            sourceSamples.remove(current);
          }
        }
      } catch (Throwable unavailable) {
        bindingUnconfirmed = true;
        pgExactClaimAttribution = false;
      }
    }

    private boolean activeTriggerBinding(Thread target, PgClaimBinding expected) {
      try {
        synchronized (gate) {
          return target != null
              && expected != null
              && !stopping
              && System.nanoTime() < end
              && !bindingUnconfirmed
              && executing.contains(target)
              && bindings.get(target) == expected;
        }
      } catch (Throwable unavailable) {
        bindingUnconfirmed = true;
        return false;
      }
    }

    void enterCurrent() {
      if (sampler == null || stopping || System.nanoTime() >= end) {
        return;
      }
      try {
        synchronized (gate) {
          if (stopping) {
            return;
          }
          Thread current = Thread.currentThread();
          if (!executing.contains(current)
              && !bindings.containsKey(current)
              && unionSizeLocked() >= MAX_THREADS) {
            status = Status.FAILED;
            return;
          }
          executing.add(current); // Only original selected filter's executing thread.
          entered.putIfAbsent(current, System.nanoTime());
        }
      } catch (Throwable unsupported) {
        status = Status.FAILED;
      }
    }

    void leaveCurrent() {
      try {
        synchronized (gate) {
          Thread current = Thread.currentThread();
          executing.remove(current);
          entered.remove(current);
          bindings.remove(current);
          sourceSamples.remove(current);
        }
      } catch (Throwable unsupported) {
        status = Status.FAILED;
      }
    }

    private void sample() {
      try {
        while (!stopping && System.nanoTime() < end) {
          Thread[] snapshot;
          Thread finalizerSnapshot;
          var claimSnapshot =
              java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(new java.util.IdentityHashMap<Thread, Boolean>());
          Map<Thread, PgClaimBinding> callbackSnapshot = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();
          Map<Thread, Long> claimEntries = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();
          synchronized (gate) {
            claimSnapshot.addAll(executing);
            for (Thread target : claimSnapshot) {
              claimEntries.put(target, entered.get(target));
            }
            callbackSnapshot.putAll(bindings);
            var union =
                java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(
                    new java.util.IdentityHashMap<Thread, Boolean>());
            union.addAll(executing);
            union.addAll(bindings.keySet());
            finalizerSnapshot = finalizer;
            if (finalizerSnapshot != null) {
              union.add(finalizerSnapshot);
            }
            snapshot = union.toArray(Thread[]::new); // Combined TOTAL <= 8, not eight per set.
            union.clear();
          }
          try {
            for (Thread target : snapshot) {
              if (stopping || System.nanoTime() >= end) {
                break;
              }
              Long identity = claimEntries.get(target);
              boolean activeClaim;
              synchronized (gate) {
                activeClaim =
                    identity != null
                        && entered.get(target) == identity
                        && executing.contains(target)
                        && !stopping
                        && System.nanoTime() < end;
              }
              StackTraceElement[] stack = target.getStackTrace();
              try {
                Thread.State state = claimSnapshot.contains(target) ? target.getState() : null;
                if (stack.length == 0 || stack.length > 512) {
                  if (claimSnapshot.contains(target)) {
                    empty++;
                  }
                  sampleSource(target, callbackSnapshot.get(target), null);
                  continue;
                }
                if (target == finalizerSnapshot) {
                  samplePublication(target, stack);
                }
                SourceClassification classification = sourceFamily(stack);
                sampleSource(target, callbackSnapshot.get(target), classification);
                if (claimSnapshot.contains(target)) {
                  Segment segment = classify(stack);
                  if (segment == Segment.OTHER) {
                    unattributed++;
                  }
                  counts[segment.ordinal()][state.ordinal()]++;
                  if (segment == Segment.JDBC_PATH && state == Thread.State.WAITING) {
                    triggerPgOnce(target);
                  }
                  if (segment != Segment.OTHER
                      && status != Status.FAILED
                      && status != Status.NON_QUIESCENT) {
                    status = Status.OBSERVED;
                  }
                  if (activeClaim && claimSessionValidationPresence(stack)) {
                    synchronized (gate) {
                      if (entered.get(target) == identity
                          && executing.contains(target)
                          && !stopping
                          && System.nanoTime() < end) {
                        if (claimSessionValidationSamples == Long.MAX_VALUE) {
                          status = Status.FAILED;
                        } else {
                          claimSessionValidationSamples++;
                        }
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }
              } finally {
                java.util.Arrays.fill(stack, null);
              }
            }
          } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(snapshot, null);
            claimSnapshot.clear();
            callbackSnapshot.clear();
            claimEntries.clear();
          }
          cycles++;
          Thread.sleep(20); // Diagnostic cadence only; no authority poll/action changed.
        }
      } catch (InterruptedException stopped) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
      } catch (Throwable unsupported) {
        status = Status.FAILED; // No exception/message/raw stack retained or emitted.
      } finally {
        synchronized (gate) {
          executing.clear();
          entered.clear();
          bindings.clear();
          sourceSamples.clear();
          finalizer = null;
        }
      }
    }

    private int unionSizeLocked() {
      int size = executing.size();
      for (Thread owner : bindings.keySet()) {
        if (!executing.contains(owner)) {
          size++;
        }
      }
      if (finalizer != null && !executing.contains(finalizer) && !bindings.containsKey(finalizer)) {
        size++;
      }
      return size;
    }

    void retireFinalizer(Thread expected) {
      synchronized (gate) {
        if (finalizer == expected) {
          finalizer = null;
        }
      }
    }

    private void samplePublication(Thread target, StackTraceElement[] stack) {
      int client = -1, publisher = -1, canonical = -1, schema = -1, line = -1;
      for (int i = 0; i < stack.length; i++) {
        StackTraceElement frame = stack[i];
        if (frame
                .getClassName()
                .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.publication.ObservedValidationClient")
            && frame.getMethodName().equals("validate")
            && "ObservedValidationClient.java".equals(frame.getFileName())) {
          if (client >= 0) {
            return;
          }
          client = i;
          line = frame.getLineNumber();
        } else if (frame
                .getClassName()
                .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.publication.GuidedPlanPublisher")
            && frame.getMethodName().equals("finalizeForUser")
            && "GuidedPlanPublisher.java".equals(frame.getFileName())
            && frame.getLineNumber() == 135) {
          publisher = i;
        } else if (frame
                .getClassName()
                .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedContracts")
            && frame.getMethodName().equals("validatedCanonical")
            && "GuidedContracts.java".equals(frame.getFileName())) {
          if (frame.getLineNumber() == 99) {
            canonical = i;
          } else if (frame.getLineNumber() == 104) {
            schema = i;
          }
        }
      }
      if (client < 0 || publisher <= client) {
        return;
      }
      int phase =
          line == 59
              ? canonical >= 0 && canonical < client ? 1 : schema >= 0 && schema < client ? 2 : 0
              : line == 65 ? 3 : line == 69 ? 4 : line == 82 ? 5 : -1;
      if (phase < 0) {
        return;
      }
      synchronized (gate) {
        long now = System.nanoTime();
        if (stopping || finalizer != target || now >= end) {
          return;
        }
        long offset = now - (end - java.time.Duration.ofSeconds(30).toNanos());
        long[] row = publicationPhases[phase];
        row[0]++;
        if (row[1] < 0) {
          row[1] = offset;
        }
        row[2] = offset;
        if (status != Status.FAILED && status != Status.NON_QUIESCENT) {
          status = Status.OBSERVED;
        }
      }
    }

    private void sampleSource(
        Thread target, PgClaimBinding expected, SourceClassification classification) {
      synchronized (gate) {
        if (expected == null
            || bindings.get(target) != expected
            || stopping
            || System.nanoTime() >= end) {
          return;
        }
        if (classification != null
            && classification.family() >= 7
            && classification.family() <= 12) {
          sourceSamples.put(
              target, new SourceSample(expected, classification.family(), classification.phase()));
        } else {
          sourceSamples.remove(target);
        }
      }
    }

    private boolean activeHolderBinding(Thread target, SourceSample sample) {
      synchronized (gate) {
        return target != null
            && sample != null
            && !stopping
            && System.nanoTime() < end
            && !bindingUnconfirmed
            && bindings.get(target) == sample.binding();
      }
    }

    private long[] blockerStageEvidence() {
      synchronized (gate) {
        long[] exported = pgBlockerStages.clone();
        if (!pgJoined()
            || sampler != null && sampler.isAlive()
            || pgStatus != PgStatus.OBSERVED
            || !pgExactClaimAttribution
            || bindingUnconfirmed
            || status == Status.FAILED
            || status == Status.NON_QUIESCENT) {
          java.util.Arrays.fill(exported, 7, exported.length, 0);
        }
        return exported;
      }
    }

    private int blockerCallerPointEvidence() {
      synchronized (gate) {
        // Reuse the original joined/clean/exact source-evidence guards, including failed sampling.
        return blockerStageEvidence()[11] > 0 ? pgBlockerCallerPoint : -1;
      }
    }

    private static boolean claimSessionValidationPresence(StackTraceElement[] stack) {
      int validation = -1, renewal = -1;
      for (int index = 0; index < stack.length; index++) {
        StackTraceElement frame = stack[index];
        if (frame.getClassName().equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedContracts")
            && frame.getMethodName().equals("validate")
            && "GuidedContracts.java".equals(frame.getFileName())
            && frame.getLineNumber() == 90) {
          if (validation >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          validation = index;
        } else if (frame
                .getClassName()
                .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionRepository")
            && frame.getMethodName().equals("saveRenewalAndCommand")
            && "GuidedSessionRepository.java".equals(frame.getFileName())
            && frame.getLineNumber() == 79) {
          if (renewal >= 0) {
            return false;
          }
          renewal = index;
        } else if (frame
                .getClassName()
                .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService")
            && frame.getMethodName().equals("claim")
            && "GuidedSessionService.java".equals(frame.getFileName())
            && frame.getLineNumber() == 751) {
          return validation >= 0 && validation < renewal && renewal < index;
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

    private static SourceClassification sourceFamily(StackTraceElement[] stack) {
      int boundary = -1;
      for (int i = 0; i < stack.length; i++) {
        if (stack[i]
                .getClassName()
                .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionRepository")
            && stack[i].getMethodName().equals("transaction")) {
          boundary = i; // Outermost repository callback; nested joins do not rebind it.
        }
      }
      if (boundary < 0) {
        return null;
      }
      for (int i = boundary + 1; i < stack.length; i++) {
        String type = stack[i].getClassName(), method = stack[i].getMethodName();
        if (type.equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService")
            && (method.equals("internal") || method.equals("translationTransaction"))) {
          continue;
        }
        if (type.equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService")) {
          return switch (method) {
            case "claim" -> new SourceClassification(7, -1);
            case "translationQuestion",
                    "reserveModel",
                    "settleModel",
                    "annotateLoginFailure",
                    "translationActions" ->
                new SourceClassification(8, -1);
            case "observe",
                    "observeV2",
                    "materialize",
                    "allocateBinding",
                    "runnerContext",
                    "storePreparation",
                    "bootstrap",
                    "beginObservationRefresh",
                    "committedObservationRefresh" ->
                new SourceClassification(9, sourceFamily9Method(stack[i], method));
            case "recordSecretDecision",
                    "refuseResolvedSecret",
                    "admit",
                    "advance",
                    "outcome",
                    "outcomeV2",
                    "completeStep",
                    "recordEvidence",
                    "consume",
                    "lifecycle",
                    "existingAdmission" ->
                new SourceClassification(10, sourceFamily10Method(stack[i], method));
            case "secretSession",
                    "start",
                    "read",
                    "clarify",
                    "clarificationSelection",
                    "publicCommand",
                    "prepareAssignment",
                    "acknowledgeAssignment",
                    "assignmentLifecycle" ->
                new SourceClassification(11, -1);
            case "secretRevoked",
                    "cancel",
                    "failTranslation",
                    "expire",
                    "lostAcknowledgment",
                    "stop",
                    "finalizeSession",
                    "publicationCommitted",
                    "reconcileLateModelUsage" ->
                new SourceClassification(12, -1);
            default -> new SourceClassification(-1, -1);
          };
        }
        if (type.equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.translation.GuidedTranslationService")) {
          return switch (method) {
            case "plan",
                    "bootstrap",
                    "beginBootstrapDelivery",
                    "finishBootstrap",
                    "advance",
                    "context",
                    "replan",
                    "call",
                    "question",
                    "recover",
                    "admitAndDispatch",
                    "complete",
                    "completeCurrent",
                    "acquireBeforeProposal",
                    "observeAcquisition",
                    "refresh" ->
                new SourceClassification(8, sourcePhase(stack[i], method));
            case "reconcile", "stopRejected" -> new SourceClassification(12, -1);
            default -> new SourceClassification(-1, -1);
          };
        }
        if (type.equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.publication.GuidedPlanPublisher")
            && "GuidedPlanPublisher.java".equals(stack[i].getFileName())) {
          // Exact compiled call sites only; these private phase values are not aggregate indices.
          if (method.equals("prepareLowering") && stack[i].getLineNumber() == 388) {
            return new SourceClassification(11, 40);
          }
          if (method.equals("finalizeForUser") && stack[i].getLineNumber() == 100) {
            return new SourceClassification(11, 41);
          }
        }
        return null; // Unsupported immediate caller: never borrow a more distant broad class.
      }
      return null;
    }

    private static int sourcePhase(StackTraceElement frame, String method) {
      if (!method.equals("call") || !"GuidedTranslationService.java".equals(frame.getFileName())) {
        return -1;
      }
      // Same selected immediate caller only; exact postcompile mapping is bound externally.
      return switch (frame.getLineNumber()) {
        case 796 -> 13;
        case 949 -> 14;
        case 984 -> 15;
        case 932 -> 16;
        case 1083 -> 17;
        case 1113 -> 18;
        case 774 -> 19;
        default -> -1;
      };
    }

    private static int sourceFamily9Method(StackTraceElement frame, String method) {
      if (!frame
              .getClassName()
              .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService")
          || !"GuidedSessionService.java".equals(frame.getFileName())
          || frame.getLineNumber() <= 0) {
        return -1;
      }
      return switch (method) {
        case "observe" -> 20;
        case "observeV2" -> 21;
        case "materialize" -> 22;
        case "allocateBinding" -> 23;
        case "runnerContext" -> 24;
        case "storePreparation" -> 25;
        case "bootstrap" -> 26;
        case "beginObservationRefresh" -> 27;
        case "committedObservationRefresh" -> 28;
        default -> -1;
      };
    }

    private static int sourceFamily10Method(StackTraceElement frame, String method) {
      if (!frame
              .getClassName()
              .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService")
          || !"GuidedSessionService.java".equals(frame.getFileName())
          || frame.getLineNumber() <= 0) {
        return -1;
      }
      return switch (method) {
        case "recordSecretDecision" -> 29;
        case "refuseResolvedSecret" -> 30;
        case "admit" -> 31;
        case "advance" -> 32;
        case "outcome" -> 33;
        case "outcomeV2" -> 34;
        case "completeStep" -> 35;
        case "recordEvidence" -> 36;
        case "consume" -> 37;
        case "lifecycle" -> 38;
        case "existingAdmission" -> 39;
        default -> -1;
      };
    }

    private void triggerPgOnce(Thread target) {
      synchronized (gate) {
        Long start = entered.get(target);
        if (stopping
            || start == null
            || !executing.contains(target)
            || System.nanoTime() - start < Duration.ofMillis(500).toNanos()
            || !pgOnce.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
          return;
        }
        PgClaimBinding expected = bindings.get(target);
        Map<Thread, SourceSample> expectedHolders = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>(sourceSamples);
        pgStatus = PgStatus.RUNNING;
        pgObserver =
            Thread.ofPlatform()
                .daemon()
                .name("private-c5-pg-once")
                .unstarted(() -> snapshotPg(target, expected, expectedHolders));
        try {
          pgObserver.start();
        } catch (Throwable failed) {
          expectedHolders.clear();
          pgStatus = PgStatus.FAILED;
        }
      }
    }

    private static int pgStage(String original) {
      if (original == null || original.length() > 4096) {
        return 6;
      }
      String sql = original.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).replaceAll("\\s+", " ").trim();
      if (sql.startsWith("select state,revoked_at from studio_guided_sessions")
          && sql.contains("for update")) {
        return 0;
      }
      if (sql.startsWith("select document::text,progress::text from studio_guided_sessions")
          && sql.contains("for update")) {
        return 1;
      }
      if (sql.contains(" from ui_tests ")
          && (sql.contains("for update") || sql.contains("for no key update"))) {
        return 2;
      }
      if (sql.startsWith("select m.manifest::text from studio_guided_translation_state")) {
        return 3;
      }
      if (sql.startsWith("with updated as (update studio_guided_sessions")) {
        return 4;
      }
      if (sql.startsWith("update studio_guided_sessions")) {
        return 5;
      }
      return 6;
    }

    private static int pgWait(String type) {
      if (type == null) {
        return 0;
      }
      int index =
          List.of("Lock", "LWLock", "IO", "Client", "IPC", "Timeout", "Activity", "BufferPin")
              .indexOf(type);
      return index < 0 ? 9 : index + 1;
    }

    private static int pgEvent(String event) {
      if (event == null) {
        return 0;
      }
      int index =
          List.of("transactionid", "tuple", "relation", "ClientRead", "ClientWrite", "DataFileRead")
              .indexOf(event);
      return index < 0 ? 7 : index + 1;
    }

    private void snapshotPg(
        Thread target, PgClaimBinding expected, Map<Thread, SourceSample> expectedHolders) {
      // Only the original slow-trigger identity; no other registered or later binding can qualify.
      boolean activeBefore = activeTriggerBinding(target, expected);
      Map<Thread, SourceSample> activeHolders = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();
      Map<Thread, SourceSample> matchedHolders = new java.util.IdentityHashMap<>();
      for (var holder : expectedHolders.entrySet()) {
        if (holder.getKey() != target && activeHolderBinding(holder.getKey(), holder.getValue())) {
          activeHolders.put(holder.getKey(), holder.getValue());
        }
      }
      boolean targetRowMatched = false;
      int targetBlockerEdges = 0;
      pgExactClaimAttribution = false;
      var privatePids = new java.util.HashSet<Integer>();
      var candidates = new java.util.HashSet<Integer>();
      var blockers = new java.util.HashSet<Integer>();
      var settings = new java.util.Properties();
      settings.setProperty("user", DB.getUsername());
      settings.setProperty("password", DB.getPassword());
      settings.setProperty("connectTimeout", "1");
      settings.setProperty("socketTimeout", "1");
      settings.setProperty("ApplicationName", "c5-private-pg-observer");
      try (var connection = java.sql.DriverManager.getConnection(DB.getJdbcUrl(), settings)) {
        connection.setReadOnly(true);
        connection.setAutoCommit(false);
        try {
          try (var statement = connection.createStatement()) {
            statement.setQueryTimeout(1);
            statement.execute("SET LOCAL statement_timeout='250ms'");
            statement.setMaxRows(64);
            String catalog =
                "SELECT a.pid,a.query,a.wait_event_type,a.wait_event,b.pid AS blocker_pid,b.query"
                    + " AS blocker_query,"
                    + (" EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM pg_locks l WHERE l.pid=a.pid AND "
                        + "l.relation='studio_guided_sessions'::regclass) AS owner_relation,")
                    + (" EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM pg_locks l WHERE l.pid=a.pid AND "
                        + "l.relation='ui_tests'::regclass) AS source_relation")
                    + " FROM pg_stat_activity a LEFT JOIN LATERAL unnest(pg_blocking_pids(a.pid))"
                    + " blocked(pid) ON true LEFT JOIN pg_stat_activity b ON b.pid=blocked.pid"
                    + " WHERE a.datname=current_database() AND a.backend_type='client backend' AND"
                    + " a.pid<>pg_backend_pid() AND a.state<>'idle'";
            try (var rows = statement.executeQuery(catalog)) {
              while (rows.next()) {
                if (++pgRows >= 64) {
                  pgStatus = PgStatus.TRUNCATED;
                  break;
                }
                String original = rows.getString("query"),
                    blockerQuery = rows.getString("blocker_query");
                if (original != null && original.length() > 4096
                    || blockerQuery != null && blockerQuery.length() > 4096) {
                  pgStatus = PgStatus.TRUNCATED;
                  break;
                }
                int pid = rows.getInt("pid"),
                    stage = pgStage(original),
                    wait = pgWait(rows.getString("wait_event_type"));
                if (privatePids.add(pid)) {
                  pgStagesAndWaits[stage][wait]++;
                  pgWaitEvents[pgEvent(rows.getString("wait_event"))]++;
                  if (stage < 6) {
                    candidates.add(pid);
                  }
                  boolean owner = rows.getBoolean("owner_relation"),
                      source = rows.getBoolean("source_relation");
                  if (owner) {
                    pgRelations[0]++;
                  }
                  if (source) {
                    pgRelations[1]++;
                  }
                  if (!owner && !source) {
                    pgRelations[2]++;
                  }
                }
                int blocker = rows.getInt("blocker_pid");
                if (!rows.wasNull() && blockers.add(blocker)) {
                  pgBlockerStages[pgStage(blockerQuery)]++;
                }
                if (activeBefore
                    && stage < 6
                    && pid == expected.pid()
                    && activeTriggerBinding(target, expected)) {
                  targetRowMatched = true;
                  // Supplement ONLY the actual triggering target's authentic blocker edge.
                  if (!rows.wasNull()) {
                    targetBlockerEdges++;
                    Thread matchedThread = null;
                    SourceSample matchedSample = null;
                    int matches = 0;
                    for (var holder : activeHolders.entrySet()) {
                      if (holder.getValue().binding().pid() == blocker
                          && activeHolderBinding(holder.getKey(), holder.getValue())) {
                        matches++;
                        matchedThread = holder.getKey();
                        matchedSample = holder.getValue();
                      }
                    }
                    if (matches == 1) {
                      matchedHolders.put(matchedThread, matchedSample);
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        } finally {
          connection.rollback();
        }
        pgCandidates = candidates.size();
        pgBlockers = blockers.size();
        pgExactClaimAttribution =
            activeBefore
                && targetRowMatched
                && !bindingUnconfirmed
                && candidates.size() == 1
                && candidates.contains(expected.pid())
                && activeTriggerBinding(target, expected)
                && pgStatus != PgStatus.TRUNCATED
                && pgStatus != PgStatus.NON_QUIESCENT;
        if (pgStatus != PgStatus.TRUNCATED && pgStatus != PgStatus.NON_QUIESCENT) {
          pgStatus =
              pgCandidates > 1
                  ? PgStatus.AMBIGUOUS
                  : pgCandidates == 1 ? PgStatus.OBSERVED : PgStatus.UNOBSERVED;
        }
      } catch (Throwable failed) {
        if (pgStatus != PgStatus.NON_QUIESCENT) {
          pgStatus = PgStatus.FAILED;
        }
        // Never retain/log raw SQL/IDs/credentials/exception.
      } finally {
        synchronized (gate) {
          if (pgStatus == PgStatus.OBSERVED
              && pgExactClaimAttribution
              && !bindingUnconfirmed
              && activeTriggerBinding(target, expected)) {
            if (targetBlockerEdges == 1 && matchedHolders.size() == 1) {
              var holder = matchedHolders.entrySet().iterator().next();
              SourceSample sample = holder.getValue();
              if (activeHolderBinding(holder.getKey(), sample)
                  && sample.family() == 11
                  && (sample.phase() == 40 || sample.phase() == 41)) {
                pgBlockerCallerPoint = sample.phase();
              }
            }
            for (var holder : matchedHolders.entrySet()) {
              if (activeHolderBinding(holder.getKey(), holder.getValue())) {
                pgBlockerStages[holder.getValue().family()]++;
                int phase = holder.getValue().phase();
                if (holder.getValue().family() == 8 && phase >= 13 && phase <= 19) {
                  pgBlockerStages[phase]++;
                }
                if (holder.getValue().family() == 9 && phase >= 20 && phase <= 28) {
                  pgBlockerStages[phase]++;
                }
                if (holder.getValue().family() == 10 && phase >= 29 && phase <= 39) {
                  pgBlockerStages[phase]++;
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
        // Finalization follows original JDBC resource close. Normal later retirement retains
        // genuine latched historical matches; failed/truncated/ambiguous snapshots never latch.
        privatePids.clear();
        candidates.clear();
        blockers.clear();
        expectedHolders.clear();
        activeHolders.clear();
        matchedHolders.clear();
        settings.clear();
      }
    }

    private boolean pgJoined() {
      Thread observer = pgObserver;
      return observer == null || !observer.isAlive();
    }

    private static Segment classify(StackTraceElement[] stack) {
      boolean claim =
          java.util.Arrays.stream(stack)
              .anyMatch(
                  frame ->
                      frame
                              .getClassName()
                              .equals(
                                  "com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionService")
                          && (frame.getMethodName().equals("claim")
                              || frame.getMethodName().startsWith("lambda$claim$")));
      if (!claim) {
        return Segment.OTHER;
      }
      String streamType = C5CanaryStream.class.getName();
      String captureNestedType = C5Capture.class.getName() + "$";
      for (StackTraceElement frame : stack) {
        String type = frame.getClassName(), method = frame.getMethodName();
        if ((type.equals(streamType) && (method.equals("write") || method.equals("consume")))
            || (type.startsWith(captureNestedType) && method.equals("write"))) {
          return Segment.FIXTURE_SCAN;
        }
      }
      // Fixed source categories only; no arbitrary class/method/file/line/name/ID export.
      for (StackTraceElement frame : stack) {
        String type = frame.getClassName(), method = frame.getMethodName();
        if (type.startsWith("ch.qos.logback.") || type.startsWith("net.logstash.logback.")) {
          return Segment.LOG_ENCODER;
        }
        if (type.startsWith("com.networknt.") || type.endsWith("GuidedContracts")) {
          return Segment.SCHEMA;
        }
        if (type.startsWith("com.fasterxml.jackson.")) {
          return Segment.JSON_RENDER;
        }
        if (type.startsWith("org.postgresql.") || type.startsWith("org.springframework.jdbc.")) {
          return Segment.JDBC_PATH;
        }
        if (type.endsWith("GuidedSessionRepository") && method.equals("lock")) {
          return Segment.OWNER_LOCK;
        }
        if (type.endsWith("GuidedProductionSessionAccess") && method.equals("checkCurrent")) {
          return Segment.CURRENT_AUTH;
        }
        if (type.endsWith("GuidedProductionSessionAccess") && method.equals("checkSourceCurrent")
            || type.endsWith("GuidedSourceSnapshotRepository")) {
          return Segment.SOURCE_CHECK;
        }
        if (type.startsWith("org.springframework.transaction.") && method.equals("commit")) {
          return Segment.COMMIT;
        }
      }
      return Segment.OTHER;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
      stopping = true;
      synchronized (gate) {
        executing.clear();
        bindings.clear();
        sourceSamples.clear();
        finalizer = null;
      }
      if (sampler == null) {
        return;
      }
      boolean restoreInterrupt = Thread.interrupted();
      long deadline = System.nanoTime() + java.time.Duration.ofSeconds(1).toNanos();
      try {
        sampler.interrupt();
        while (sampler.isAlive()) {
          long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
          if (remaining <= 0) {
            break;
          }
          try {
            sampler.join(java.time.Duration.ofNanos(remaining));
          } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
            restoreInterrupt = true;
          }
        }
        if (sampler.isAlive()) {
          status = Status.NON_QUIESCENT;
        }
        Thread observer = pgObserver;
        if (observer != null) {
          observer.interrupt();
          long pgDeadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(3).toNanos();
          while (observer.isAlive()) {
            long remaining = pgDeadline - System.nanoTime();
            if (remaining <= 0) {
              break;
            }
            try {
              observer.join(Duration.ofNanos(remaining));
            } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
              restoreInterrupt = true;
            }
          }
          if (observer.isAlive()) {
            pgStatus = PgStatus.NON_QUIESCENT;
          }
        }
      } finally {
        synchronized (gate) {
          executing.clear();
          entered.clear();
          bindings.clear();
          sourceSamples.clear();
        }
        if (restoreInterrupt) {
          Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
      }
      // Export counts ONLY if sampler joined. NON_QUIESCENT never claims all refs discarded:
      // public virtual-thread getStackTrace can retry/yield without honoring interruption.
      // Future caller uses fixed status/segment/thread-state ordinal and counts only.
    }
  }

  private final class C5Capture implements AutoCloseable {
    private static final int MAX_BYTES = 128 * 1024 * 1024;
    private volatile C5ClaimSampler claimSampler;
    private final boolean claimSamplerSelected;
    private boolean claimSamplerArmed, claimSamplerClosed, finalizerSelected;
    private Thread selectedFinalizer;
    private ObjectNode claimSamplerEvidence;
    private final List<String> values;
    private volatile String selectedGuidedSession;
    private int lifecycleOrdinal, claimOrdinal;
    private volatile boolean lifecycleDiagnosticFailed;
    private final Map<String, byte[]> produced = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final List<C5Stream> streams = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    private final C5CanaryStream encoded;
    private final Object encodedMonitor = new Object();
    private final ch.qos.logback.classic.Logger rootLogger;
    private final ch.qos.logback.classic.Logger appLogger;
    private final ch.qos.logback.classic.Level rootLevel, appLevel;
    private final ch.qos.logback.core.OutputStreamAppender<ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>
        appender;
    private final ch.qos.logback.core.encoder.Encoder<ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>
        originalEncoder;
    private final java.io.OutputStream originalStream;
    // STUDIO-OBS-014 AC-10: the Studio audit logger's own appender (logback AUDIT_JSON,
    // additivity=false) is mapped explicitly and scanned by its own canary stream, so the audit
    // output never bypasses this capture.
    private final ch.qos.logback.core.OutputStreamAppender<ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>
        auditAppender;
    private final java.io.OutputStream auditOriginalStream;
    private final C5CanaryStream auditEncoded;
    private final Object auditMonitor = new Object();
    private final ch.qos.logback.classic.LoggerContext loggerContext;
    private final long statusSince;
    private boolean rootTraceObserved,
        appTraceObserved,
        streamedLogsCollected,
        streamedLogsFinalChecked;
    private boolean failed, c4WindowsExecuted, traceArmed;
    private final boolean traceFromStart;
    private P8PendingTiming c6Timing;
    private ObjectNode c6Acceptance;
    private int s2Epoch;
    private boolean cacheNonemptyRequired = true;
    private JsonNode poolRefusalTarget;
    private ObjectNode poolRefusalDiagnostic;
    private int httpBodies,
        httpErrors,
        jsonBodies,
        problemBodies,
        cacheEntries,
        metricBytes,
        profileFiles,
        profileLinks;

    C5Capture(List<String> values) {
      this(values, true);
    }

    C5Capture(List<String> values, boolean traceFromStart) {
      this.values = values;
      encoded = new C5CanaryStream(values);
      this.traceFromStart = traceFromStart;
      var context =
          (ch.qos.logback.classic.LoggerContext) org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getILoggerFactory();
      loggerContext = context;
      statusSince = System.currentTimeMillis();
      rootLogger = context.getLogger(org.slf4j.Logger.ROOT_LOGGER_NAME);
      appLogger = context.getLogger("com.citi.put.checks");
      rootLevel = rootLogger.getLevel();
      appLevel = appLogger.getLevel();
      var installed = rootLogger.getAppender("JSON_CONSOLE");
      assertThat(installed).isInstanceOf(ch.qos.logback.core.ConsoleAppender.class);
      var iterator = rootLogger.iteratorForAppenders();
      assertThat(iterator.next()).isSameAs(installed);
      assertThat(iterator.hasNext()).as("all original root appenders mapped").isFalse();
      var auditLogger =
          context.getLogger(com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.audit.StudioActionAudit.CATEGORY);
      for (var logger : context.getLoggerList()) {
        if (logger != rootLogger && logger != auditLogger) {
          assertThat(logger.iteratorForAppenders().hasNext())
              .as("no unmapped independently attached appender")
              .isFalse();
        }
      }
      var auditInstalled = auditLogger.getAppender("AUDIT_JSON");
      assertThat(auditInstalled).isInstanceOf(ch.qos.logback.core.ConsoleAppender.class);
      for (var auditAppenders = auditLogger.iteratorForAppenders(); auditAppenders.hasNext(); ) {
        assertThat(auditAppenders.next())
            .as("audit logger carries only the mapped AUDIT_JSON and this class's audit capture")
            .isIn(auditInstalled, AUDIT_EVENTS);
      }
      @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
      var actualAudit =
          (ch.qos.logback.core.OutputStreamAppender<ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>)
              auditInstalled;
      auditAppender = actualAudit;
      auditOriginalStream = auditAppender.getOutputStream();
      assertThat(auditOriginalStream).isNotNull();
      auditEncoded = new C5CanaryStream(values);
      auditAppender.setOutputStream(
          new java.io.OutputStream() {
            @Override
            public void write(int value) {
              synchronized (auditMonitor) {
                try {
                  auditEncoded.write(value);
                } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
                  auditEncoded.invalid();
                }
              }
            }

            @Override
            public void write(byte[] bytes, int offset, int length) {
              synchronized (auditMonitor) {
                try {
                  auditEncoded.write(bytes, offset, length);
                } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
                  auditEncoded.invalid();
                }
              }
            }

            @Override
            public void close() {
              synchronized (auditMonitor) {
                auditEncoded.closeInput();
              }
            }
          });
      @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
      var actual =
          (ch.qos.logback.core.OutputStreamAppender<ch.qos.logback.classic.spi.ILoggingEvent>)
              installed;
      appender = actual;
      originalEncoder = appender.getEncoder();
      assertThat(appender.isStarted() && originalEncoder.isStarted()).isTrue();
      assertThat(((ch.qos.logback.core.ConsoleAppender<?>) installed).getTarget())
          .isEqualTo("System.out");
      originalStream = appender.getOutputStream();
      assertThat(originalStream).isNotNull();
      appender.setOutputStream(
          new java.io.OutputStream() {
            @Override
            public void write(int value) {
              synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
                try {
                  encoded.write(value);
                } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
                  encoded.invalid();
                }
              }
            }

            @Override
            public void write(byte[] bytes, int offset, int length) {
              synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
                try {
                  encoded.write(bytes, offset, length);
                } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
                  encoded.invalid();
                }
              }
            }

            @Override
            public void close() {
              synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
                encoded.closeInput();
              }
              // Original replacement/encoder footer closes this target; no raw history retained.
            }
          });
      assertThat(appender.getEncoder()).isSameAs(originalEncoder);
      if (traceFromStart) {
        armTrace();
      }
      claimSamplerSelected = "true".equals(System.getenv("STUDIO_C5_CLAIM_DIAGNOSTIC"));
    }

    synchronized boolean selectFinalizer(jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest request) {
      String selected = selectedGuidedSession;
      if (!claimSamplerSelected
          || claimSamplerArmed
          || claimSamplerClosed
          || finalizerSelected
          || selectedFinalizer != null
          || !secretLoginScenario
          || selected == null
          || !request.getMethod().equals("POST")
          || !request
              .getRequestURI()
              .equals("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/" + selected + "/finalize")) {
        return false;
      }
      finalizerSelected = true;
      selectedFinalizer = Thread.currentThread();
      return true;
    }

    synchronized boolean pendingFinalizer() {
      return claimSamplerSelected
          && !claimSamplerArmed
          && !claimSamplerClosed
          && selectedFinalizer == Thread.currentThread();
    }

    synchronized void retireFinalizer() {
      Thread current = Thread.currentThread();
      if (selectedFinalizer == current) {
        selectedFinalizer = null;
      }
      if (claimSampler != null) {
        claimSampler.retireFinalizer(current);
      }
    }

    synchronized void afterPublicationClaim(Instant deadline) {
      if (!pendingFinalizer()) {
        return;
      }
      StackTraceElement[] stack = Thread.currentThread().getStackTrace();
      try {
        if (stack.length == 0 || stack.length > 512) {
          return;
        }
        int boundary = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < stack.length; i++) {
          if (stack[i]
                  .getClassName()
                  .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.session.GuidedSessionRepository")
              && stack[i].getMethodName().equals("transaction")) {
            boundary = i;
          }
        }
        if (boundary < 0 || boundary + 1 >= stack.length) {
          return;
        }
        StackTraceElement caller = stack[boundary + 1];
        if (!caller
                .getClassName()
                .equals("com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.publication.GuidedPlanPublisher")
            || !caller.getMethodName().equals("finalizeForUser")
            || !"GuidedPlanPublisher.java".equals(caller.getFileName())
            || caller.getLineNumber() != 110) {
          return;
        }
        claimSamplerArmed = true;
        var field = GuidedPlanPublisher.class.getDeclaredField("clock");
        field.setAccessible(true);
        var clock =
            (java.time.Clock) field.get(ownedApplicationContext.getBean(GuidedPlanPublisher.class));
        long origin = System.nanoTime();
        long remaining = Duration.between(clock.instant(), deadline).toNanos();
        claimSampler = new C5ClaimSampler(true, selectedFinalizer, origin, remaining);
        selectedFinalizer =
            null; // Transfer only the thread, never the attempt or clock, to the bounded recorder.
      } catch (ReflectiveOperationException | RuntimeException unavailable) {
        lifecycleDiagnosticFailed = true;
      } finally {
        java.util.Arrays.fill(stack, null);
      }
    }

    void armTrace() {
      assertThat(appender.getEncoder()).isSameAs(originalEncoder);
      assertThat(appender.isStarted() && originalEncoder.isStarted()).isTrue();
      rootLogger.setLevel(ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.TRACE);
      appLogger.setLevel(ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.TRACE);
      traceArmed = true;
    }

    void beginS2(int epoch) throws Exception {
      assertThat(epoch > 0 && s2Epoch == 0).isTrue();
      s2Epoch = epoch;
      Path directory = TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-producers");
      if (!Files.exists(directory, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
        Files.createDirectory(
            directory,
            java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
                java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rwx------")));
      }
      p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(directory);
      writeS2Mode(null);
    }

    void bindS2Session(String id) throws Exception {
      assertThat(s2Epoch > 0 && selectedGuidedSession == null).isTrue();
      selectedGuidedSession = id;
      writeS2Mode(id);
    }

    private void writeS2Mode(String id) throws Exception {
      ObjectNode mode = C.object().put("active", false).put("s2", true).put("case", s2Epoch);
      if (id == null) {
        mode.putNull("sessionId");
      } else {
        mode.put("sessionId", id);
      }
      Files.writeString(
          TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-state.json"), mode.toString(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    void awaitS2Completion() throws Exception {
      assertThat(s2Epoch > 0 && selectedGuidedSession != null).isTrue();
      Path directory = TEMP.resolve("p4-revocation-producers");
      Path receipt = directory.resolve("s2-" + s2Epoch + "-1.json");
      long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos();
      JsonNode sample = null;
      do {
        p4RequirePrivateProducerRoot(directory);
        if (Files.exists(receipt, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
          assertThat(
                  !Files.isSymbolicLink(receipt)
                      && Files.isRegularFile(receipt, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
                      && Files.size(receipt) <= 1024
                      && receipt.toRealPath().getParent().equals(directory.toRealPath())
                      && Files.getPosixFilePermissions(receipt)
                          .equals(
                              java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")))
              .isTrue();
          JsonNode current = C.parse(Files.readString(receipt, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
          assertThat(
                  current.isObject()
                      && current.size() == 13
                      && current.path("case").asInt(-1) == s2Epoch
                      && current.path("producer").asInt(-1) == 1
                      && current.path("epoch").asInt(-1) == s2Epoch)
              .isTrue();
          for (String field :
              List.of("failed", "closed", "restored", "stopFulfilled", "active", "busy")) {
            assertThat(current.path(field).isBoolean()).isTrue();
          }
          assertThat(current.path("failed").booleanValue()).isFalse();
          if (current.path("closed").booleanValue()) {
            sample = current;
            break;
          }
        }
        Thread.sleep(20);
      } while (System.nanoTime() < deadline);
      assertThat(sample != null)
          .as("actual selected guided operation/callback completion")
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(
              sample.path("restored").booleanValue() && sample.path("stopFulfilled").booleanValue())
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(sample.path("active").booleanValue() || sample.path("busy").booleanValue())
          .isFalse();
      assertThat(p4Number(sample, "operations")).isZero();
      assertThat(p4Number(sample, "callbacks")).isZero();
      assertThat(p4Number(sample, "matches")).isEqualTo(1);
      assertThat(p4Number(sample, "starts")).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    void writeProfileValues() throws Exception {
      Path file = TEMP.resolve("c5-private-values.json");
      if (!Files.exists(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
        Files.createFile(
            file,
            java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
                java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")));
      }
      assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(file)).isFalse();
      assertThat(Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file))
          .isEqualTo(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"));
      Path temporary = TEMP.resolve("c5-private-values.tmp");
      Files.createFile(
          temporary,
          java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
              java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")));
      try {
        Files.writeString(temporary, JSON.writeValueAsString(values), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        Files.move(
            temporary,
            file,
            java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,
            java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
      } finally {
        Files.deleteIfExists(temporary);
      }
    }

    boolean selectedLifecycle(jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest request) {
      String selected = selectedGuidedSession;
      return selected != null
          && request.getMethod().equals("POST")
          && request
              .getRequestURI()
              .equals("/internal/v2/studio/guided-sessions/" + selected + "/lifecycle");
    }

    boolean selectedClaim(jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest request) {
      String selected = selectedGuidedSession;
      return selected != null
          && request.getMethod().equals("POST")
          && request
              .getRequestURI()
              .equals("/internal/v2/studio/guided-sessions/" + selected + "/claim");
    }

    synchronized void recordClaim(byte[] bytes, int status, long handlerNanos) {
      try {
        ObjectNode sample =
            C.object()
                .put("ordinal", ++claimOrdinal)
                .put("status", status)
                .put("handlerNanos", handlerNanos)
                .put("consumed", bytes.length > 0 && bytes.length < 262144);
        Path file = TEMP.resolve("c5-claim-server-diagnostic.jsonl");
        if (!Files.exists(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
          Files.createFile(
              file,
              java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
                  java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")));
        }
        if (Files.isSymbolicLink(file)
            || !Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
            || !Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file)
                .equals(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"))) {
          throw new IOException("Unsafe private claim diagnostic target");
        }
        Files.writeString(
            file,
            sample.toString() + "\n",
            StandardCharsets.UTF_8,
            java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.APPEND);
      } catch (Exception failure) {
        lifecycleDiagnosticFailed = true;
      }
    }

    synchronized void recordLifecycle(byte[] bytes, int status) {
      String kind = "UNCLASSIFIED", reason = "UNCLASSIFIED";
      boolean consumed = bytes.length > 0 && bytes.length < 262144;
      try {
        if (consumed) {
          JsonNode original = C.parseWire(bytes);
          String session =
              original.path("kind").asText().equals("assignment")
                  ? original.path("assignment").path("sessionId").asText()
                  : original.path("sessionId").asText();
          if (session.equals(selectedGuidedSession)) {
            kind =
                original.path("kind").asText().equals("assignment")
                    ? "ASSIGNMENT"
                    : original.has("contextId") ? "RUNTIME" : "UNCLASSIFIED";
            String actualReason = original.path("reason").asText();
            if (List.of(
                    "CANCELLED",
                    "EXPIRED",
                    "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME",
                    "LEASE_LOST",
                    "CONTEXT_LOST",
                    "REVOKED")
                .contains(actualReason)) {
              reason = actualReason;
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
        // The original chain already owns parsing/errors; export no diagnostic parser exception.
      }
      try {
        ObjectNode sample =
            C.object()
                .put("ordinal", ++lifecycleOrdinal)
                .put("status", status)
                .put("kind", kind)
                .put("reason", reason)
                .put("consumed", consumed);
        Path file = TEMP.resolve("c5-lifecycle-diagnostic.jsonl");
        if (!Files.exists(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)) {
          Files.createFile(
              file,
              java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(
                  java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------")));
        }
        if (Files.isSymbolicLink(file)
            || !Files.isRegularFile(file, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
            || !Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file)
                .equals(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"))) {
          throw new IOException("Unsafe private lifecycle diagnostic target");
        }
        Files.writeString(
            file,
            sample.toString() + "\n",
            StandardCharsets.UTF_8,
            java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.APPEND);
      } catch (Exception failure) {
        lifecycleDiagnosticFailed = true;
      }
    }

    void recordHttp(HttpResponse<String> response) {
      byte[] bytes = response.body().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      for (String value : values) {
        assertJoinedSinkAbsent(Map.of("c5-private-http-response", bytes), value);
      }
      synchronized (this) {
        if (bytes.length > MAX_BYTES) {
          failed = true;
          throw new AssertionError("C5 actual HTTP response exceeds the capture bound");
        }
        produced.put("c5-http-" + ++httpBodies, bytes);
        if (response.statusCode() >= 400) {
          httpErrors++;
        }
        String media = response.headers().firstValue("Content-Type").orElse("");
        if (media.startsWith("application/problem+json")) {
          problemBodies++;
        } else if (media.startsWith("application/json")) {
          jsonBodies++;
        }
      }
    }

    void watchExecution(UUID execution) throws Exception {
      watchExecution(execution, COOKIE);
    }

    void watchExecution(UUID execution, String cookie) throws Exception {
      var response =
          HttpClient.newBuilder()
              .sslContext(TLS)
              .build()
              .send(
                  HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                          URI.create(
                              "https://localhost:"
                                  + port
                                  + "/api/v1/studio/executions/"
                                  + execution
                                  + "/events"))
                      .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15))
                      .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + cookie)
                      .header("Accept", "text/event-stream")
                      .GET()
                      .build(),
                  HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofInputStream());
      C5Stream stream = null;
      try {
        assertThat(response.statusCode()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(response.headers().firstValue("Content-Type").orElse(""))
            .startsWith("text/event-stream");
        stream = new C5Stream(response.body());
        streams.add(stream);
      } catch (Exception | Error original) {
        try {
          if (stream == null) {
            response.body().close();
          } else {
            stream.close();
          }
        } catch (Exception | Error cleanup) {
          original.addSuppressed(cleanup);
        }
        throw original;
      }
    }

    void collect(Map<String, byte[]> sinks) throws Exception {
      for (int index = 0; index < streams.size(); index++) {
        sinks.put("c5-sse-" + index, streams.get(index).completed());
      }
      var metrics =
          HttpClient.newBuilder()
              .sslContext(TLS)
              .build()
              .send(
                  HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                          URI.create("https://localhost:" + port + "/actuator/prometheus"))
                      .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15))
                      .GET()
                      .build(),
                  HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofByteArray());
      assertThat(metrics.statusCode()).isEqualTo(200);
      assertThat(metrics.body().length).isPositive().isLessThanOrEqualTo(MAX_BYTES);
      metricBytes = metrics.body().length;
      sinks.put("c5-prometheus", metrics.body());
      @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
      var cache =
          (com.github.benmanes.caffeine.cache.Cache<
                  String, com.citi.put.checks.config.CachedResponse>)
              ownedApplicationContext.getBean(
                  "idempotencyCache", com.github.benmanes.caffeine.cache.Cache.class);
      cacheEntries = cache.asMap().size();
      if (cacheNonemptyRequired) {
        assertThat(cacheEntries).as("real accepted mutation cache entries").isPositive();
      }
      int ordinal = 0;
      for (var entry : cache.asMap().values()) {
        sinks.put("c5-cache-body-" + ordinal, entry.body());
        sinks.put("c5-cache-headers-" + ordinal++, JSON.writeValueAsBytes(entry.headers()));
      }
      synchronized (this) {
        assertThat(failed).as("actual capture bound/observer integrity").isFalse();
        assertThat(appender.getEncoder()).isSameAs(originalEncoder);
        assertThat(appender.isStarted() && originalEncoder.isStarted()).isTrue();
        for (var status : loggerContext.getStatusManager().getCopyOfStatusList()) {
          if (status.getDate() >= statusSince) {
            assertThat(status.getEffectiveLevel()).as("original appender status").isZero();
          }
        }
        rootTraceObserved = rootLogger.getEffectiveLevel() == ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.TRACE;
        appTraceObserved = appLogger.getEffectiveLevel() == ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.TRACE;
        assertThat(traceArmed && rootTraceObserved && appTraceObserved)
            .as("actual original root/app TRACE levels during collection")
            .isTrue();
        synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
          encoded.assertClean(false);
        }
        streamedLogsCollected = true;
        sinks.putAll(produced);
        // Original encoded logs were fully streamed; no fake raw-log Map entry.
      }
      Path profiles = TEMP.resolve("c5-profile-counts.jsonl");
      assertThat(Files.exists(profiles)).as("actual profile before-close scan produced").isTrue();
      assertThat(Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("c5-profile-counts.jsonl-failed"))).isFalse();
      assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(profiles)).isFalse();
      assertThat(Files.getPosixFilePermissions(profiles))
          .isEqualTo(java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"));
      profileFiles = 0;
      profileLinks = 0;
      List<String> profileSamples = Files.readAllLines(profiles);
      assertThat(profileSamples.size())
          .as("guided and completed ordinary profile scans")
          .isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(streams.size() + 1);
      for (String line : profileSamples) {
        JsonNode sample = C.parse(line);
        assertThat(sample.size()).isEqualTo(14);
        assertThat(sample.path("failed").isBoolean()).isTrue();
        for (String field :
            List.of(
                "files",
                "bytes",
                "hits",
                "links",
                "stage",
                "errno",
                "linkName",
                "linkType",
                "linkBytes")) {
          assertThat(sample.path(field).isIntegralNumber()).isTrue();
          assertThat(sample.path(field).asLong()).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(0);
        }
        assertThat(sample.path("linkRoot").isBoolean()).isTrue();
        assertThat(sample.path("linkTargetRead").isBoolean()).isTrue();
        assertThat(sample.path("linkTargetIsVersion").isBoolean()).isTrue();
        assertThat(sample.path("linkName").asInt()).isBetween(0, 4);
        assertThat(sample.path("linkType").asInt()).isBetween(0, 1);
        assertThat(sample.path("stage").asInt()).isBetween(0, 16);
        assertThat(sample.path("errno").asInt()).isBetween(0, 10);
        assertThat(sample.path("failed").asBoolean(true)).isFalse();
        JsonNode hitCategories = sample.path("hitCategories");
        assertThat(hitCategories.isArray() && hitCategories.size() == 8)
            .as("fixed private profile hit categories")
            .isTrue();
        long categoryTotal = 0;
        for (JsonNode count : hitCategories) {
          assertThat(count.isIntegralNumber() && count.canConvertToLong())
              .as("bounded private profile category count")
              .isTrue();
          assertThat(count.asLong()).isBetween(0L, sample.path("hits").asLong());
          categoryTotal = Math.addExact(categoryTotal, count.asLong());
        }
        assertThat(categoryTotal)
            .as("original profile hit coverage")
            .isEqualTo(sample.path("hits").asLong());
        assertThat(sample.path("hits").asLong(-1)).isZero();
        assertThat(sample.path("files").asInt()).isPositive();
        assertThat(sample.path("bytes").asLong()).isPositive();
        profileFiles += sample.path("files").asInt();
        profileLinks += sample.path("links").asInt();
      }
    }

    synchronized void recordPoolRefusal(
        JsonNode binding,
        RuntimeException failure,
        String mappedCode,
        java.time.Instant dispatchTime) {
      JsonNode expected = poolRefusalTarget;
      if (expected == null || !binding.path("executionId").equals(expected.path("executionId"))) {
        return;
      }
      if (poolRefusalDiagnostic != null) {
        return;
      }
      var codes =
          List.of(
              "AUTHORIZATION_EXPIRED",
              "FORBIDDEN_SCOPE",
              "SECRET_USE_REVOKED",
              "SECRET_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED",
              "CANCELLED",
              "REFERENCE_UNAVAILABLE");
      String originalMessage = failure.getMessage();
      int code = originalMessage == null ? 0 : codes.indexOf(originalMessage) + 1;
      originalMessage = null;
      boolean deniedReferenceObserved =
          failure
              instanceof
              com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioReferenceResolver.SecretUseRevokedException;
      boolean deniedReferenceIsPass =
          deniedReferenceObserved
              && "PASS"
                  .equals(
                      ((com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioReferenceResolver
                                  .SecretUseRevokedException)
                              failure)
                          .getReferenceName());
      if (deniedReferenceObserved) {
        code = 3;
      }
      int source = 0, line = 0;
      StackTraceElement[] frames = failure.getStackTrace();
      try {
        for (StackTraceElement frame : frames) {
          int candidate =
              List.of(
                          "com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.publication.ObservedReplayAuthorization",
                          "com.citi.put.checks.studio.secrets.VaultStudioReferenceResolver",
                          "com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.publication.GuidedPublicationAuthorization")
                      .indexOf(frame.getClassName())
                  + 1;
          if (candidate > 0) {
            source = candidate;
            line = frame.getLineNumber();
            break;
          }
        }
      } finally {
        java.util.Arrays.fill(frames, null);
      }
      JsonNode grant = binding.path("replayGrant");
      long issued = java.time.Instant.parse(grant.path("issuedAt").asText()).toEpochMilli();
      long expires = java.time.Instant.parse(grant.path("expiresAt").asText()).toEpochMilli();
      poolRefusalDiagnostic =
          C.object()
              .put("originalCodeOrdinal", code)
              .put("codeObserved", code > 0)
              .put("sourceClassOrdinal", source)
              .put("sourceLine", line)
              .put("mappedAuthorizationRevoked", "AUTHORIZATION_REVOKED".equals(mappedCode))
              .put("sameAttempt", binding.path("attemptId").equals(expected.path("attemptId")))
              .put("sameScope", binding.path("scope").equals(expected.path("scope")))
              .put("deniedReferenceObserved", deniedReferenceObserved)
              .put("deniedReferenceIsPass", deniedReferenceIsPass)
              .put("environmentObserved", binding.path("environment").isTextual())
              .put(
                  "sameEnvironment",
                  binding.path("environment").isTextual()
                      && binding.path("environment").equals(expected.path("environment")))
              .put("testObserved", binding.path("testId").isTextual())
              .put(
                  "sameTest",
                  binding.path("testId").isTextual()
                      && binding.path("testId").equals(expected.path("testId")))
              .put(
                  "samePrincipal", binding.path("principalId").equals(expected.path("principalId")))
              .put("sameCsi", binding.path("csiId").equals(expected.path("csiId")))
              .put(
                  "samePolicy",
                  binding.path("currentPolicyHash").equals(expected.path("currentPolicyHash")))
              .put("grantIssuedAtMs", issued)
              .put("grantExpiresAtMs", expires)
              .put("dispatchStartedAtMs", dispatchTime.toEpochMilli())
              .put("refusalObservedAtMs", System.currentTimeMillis())
              .put("exactCheckTimestampObserved", false);
    }

    void writeNumericEvidence() throws Exception {
      long encodedBytes, encodedWriteCalls, encodedPatternHits, encodedInvalidWrites;
      boolean encodedObserverFailed, captureTargetClosed;
      int patternCount, patternBytes;
      synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
        encodedBytes = encoded.encodedBytes;
        encodedWriteCalls = encoded.writeCalls;
        encodedPatternHits = encoded.hits;
        encodedInvalidWrites = encoded.invalidWrites;
        encodedObserverFailed = encoded.failed;
        captureTargetClosed = encoded.inputClosed;
        patternCount = encoded.patterns.size();
        patternBytes = encoded.patternBytes;
      }
      ObjectNode evidence =
          C.object()
              .put("mappedAppenders", 1)
              .putNull("rootEvents")
              .putNull("appEvents")
              .put("eventCountsStatus", "SUPPORT_UNOBSERVED")
              .put("logCoverage", "STREAMED_ORIGINAL_ENCODED_OUTPUT")
              .put("encodedBytes", encodedBytes)
              .put("encodedWriteCalls", encodedWriteCalls)
              .put("encodedPatternHits", encodedPatternHits)
              .put("encodedInvalidWrites", encodedInvalidWrites)
              .put("encodedObserverFailed", encodedObserverFailed)
              .put("streamedLogsCollected", streamedLogsCollected)
              .put("streamedLogsFinalChecked", streamedLogsFinalChecked)
              .put("captureTargetClosed", captureTargetClosed)
              .put("patternCount", patternCount)
              .put("patternBytes", patternBytes)
              .put("rootTraceObserved", rootTraceObserved)
              .put("appTraceObserved", appTraceObserved)
              .put("httpBodies", httpBodies)
              .put("httpErrors", httpErrors)
              .put("jsonBodies", jsonBodies)
              .put("problemBodies", problemBodies)
              .put("sseStreams", streams.size())
              .put("metricBytes", metricBytes)
              .put("cacheEntries", cacheEntries)
              .put("cacheNonemptyRequired", cacheNonemptyRequired)
              .put("cacheProduced", cacheEntries > 0)
              .put(
                  "cacheUnproducedOutsideFilterRoutes", !cacheNonemptyRequired && cacheEntries == 0)
              .put("profileFiles", profileFiles)
              .put("knownRootLinks", profileLinks)
              .put("externalLinkTargetBytesScanned", false)
              .put("c4WindowsExecuted", c4WindowsExecuted)
              .put("traceFromStart", traceFromStart)
              .put("traceArmed", traceArmed);
      if (claimSamplerEvidence != null) {
        evidence.setAll(claimSamplerEvidence.deepCopy());
      }
      if (poolRefusalDiagnostic != null) {
        evidence.set("poolOriginalRefusal", poolRefusalDiagnostic.deepCopy());
      }
      Files.writeString(TEMP.resolve("c5-numeric-evidence.json"), evidence.toString());
    }

    private void closeClaimSampler() throws Exception {
      try {
        claimSampler.close();
      } finally {
        if (claimSampler.sampler.isAlive() || !claimSampler.pgJoined()) {
          cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
        }
      }
      boolean joined = !claimSampler.sampler.isAlive();
      boolean pgJoined = claimSampler.pgJoined();
      Path path = TEMP.resolve("c5-numeric-evidence.json");
      ObjectNode evidence =
          Files.exists(path, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
              ? (ObjectNode) C.parse(Files.readString(path, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
              : C.object();
      evidence
          .put("claimSamplerStatus", claimSampler.status.ordinal())
          .put("claimSamplerJoined", joined)
          .put("claimSamplerCleanupConfirmed", joined && pgJoined)
          .put("claimSamplerDiagnosticOnly", true);
      boolean publicationObserved =
          joined
              && pgJoined
              && !cleanupUnconfirmed
              && claimSampler.status != C5ClaimSampler.Status.FAILED
              && claimSampler.status != C5ClaimSampler.Status.NON_QUIESCENT;
      evidence.put("publicationBudgetObserved", publicationObserved);
      evidence.put(
          "publicationRemainingNanos",
          publicationObserved ? claimSampler.publicationRemainingNanos : 0L);
      if (publicationObserved) {
        evidence.set("publicationPhaseSamples", JSON.valueToTree(claimSampler.publicationPhases));
      } else {
        evidence.remove("publicationPhaseSamples");
      }
      if (!joined) {
        evidence.remove(
            List.of(
                "claimSamplerCycles",
                "claimSamplerEmpty",
                "claimSamplerUnattributed",
                "claimSamplerCounts"));
      }
      if (joined) {
        evidence
            .put("claimSamplerCycles", claimSampler.cycles)
            .put("claimSamplerEmpty", claimSampler.empty)
            .put("claimSamplerUnattributed", claimSampler.unattributed);
        evidence.set("claimSamplerCounts", JSON.valueToTree(claimSampler.counts));
      }
      evidence.remove(
          List.of(
              "pgSnapshotRows",
              "pgSnapshotCandidates",
              "pgSnapshotBlockers",
              "pgSnapshotStageWaitCounts",
              "pgSnapshotBlockerStageCounts",
              "pgSnapshotBlockerCallerPoint",
              "pgSnapshotRelationCounts",
              "pgSnapshotWaitEventCounts"));
      evidence
          .put("pgSnapshotStatus", claimSampler.pgStatus.ordinal())
          .put("pgSnapshotJoined", pgJoined)
          .put(
              "pgSnapshotExactClaimAttribution",
              pgJoined
                  && claimSampler.pgStatus == C5ClaimSampler.PgStatus.OBSERVED
                  && !claimSampler.bindingUnconfirmed
                  && claimSampler.pgExactClaimAttribution);
      evidence.put("pgSnapshotBlockerCallerPoint", claimSampler.blockerCallerPointEvidence());
      if (pgJoined) {
        evidence
            .put("pgSnapshotRows", claimSampler.pgRows)
            .put("pgSnapshotCandidates", claimSampler.pgCandidates)
            .put("pgSnapshotBlockers", claimSampler.pgBlockers);
        if (claimSampler.pgStatus == C5ClaimSampler.PgStatus.OBSERVED
            || claimSampler.pgStatus == C5ClaimSampler.PgStatus.AMBIGUOUS) {
          evidence.set(
