              "pgSnapshotStageWaitCounts", JSON.valueToTree(claimSampler.pgStagesAndWaits));
          evidence.set(
              "pgSnapshotBlockerStageCounts",
              JSON.valueToTree(claimSampler.blockerStageEvidence()));
          evidence.set("pgSnapshotRelationCounts", JSON.valueToTree(claimSampler.pgRelations));
          evidence.set("pgSnapshotWaitEventCounts", JSON.valueToTree(claimSampler.pgWaitEvents));
        }
      }
      evidence.put(
          "claimSessionValidationSamples",
          joined
                  && pgJoined
                  && !cleanupUnconfirmed
                  && !claimSampler.bindingUnconfirmed
                  && claimSampler.status == C5ClaimSampler.Status.OBSERVED
              ? claimSampler.claimSessionValidationSamples
              : -1L);
      claimSamplerEvidence = C.object();
      for (String key :
          List.of(
              "claimSamplerStatus",
              "claimSamplerJoined",
              "claimSamplerCleanupConfirmed",
              "claimSamplerDiagnosticOnly",
              "publicationBudgetObserved",
              "publicationRemainingNanos",
              "publicationPhaseSamples",
              "claimSamplerCycles",
              "claimSamplerEmpty",
              "claimSamplerUnattributed",
              "claimSamplerCounts",
              "claimSessionValidationSamples",
              "pgSnapshotStatus",
              "pgSnapshotJoined",
              "pgSnapshotExactClaimAttribution",
              "pgSnapshotRows",
              "pgSnapshotCandidates",
              "pgSnapshotBlockers",
              "pgSnapshotStageWaitCounts",
              "pgSnapshotBlockerStageCounts",
              "pgSnapshotBlockerCallerPoint",
              "pgSnapshotRelationCounts",
              "pgSnapshotWaitEventCounts")) {
        if (evidence.has(key)) {
          claimSamplerEvidence.set(key, evidence.get(key).deepCopy());
        }
      }
      Files.writeString(path, evidence.toString(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      if (!joined || !pgJoined) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Private claim sampler cleanup remains unconfirmed");
      }
    }

    @Override
    public void close() throws Exception {
      synchronized (this) {
        // Shares the arm monitor: close-before-finalize permanently prevents a new sampler.
        claimSamplerClosed = true;
        selectedFinalizer = null;
      }
      var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
      boolean producersDrained = false;
      try {
        if (claimSampler != null) {
          p3AttemptTeardown(failures, this::closeClaimSampler);
        }
        for (C5Stream stream : streams) {
          p3AttemptTeardown(failures, stream::close);
        }
        producersDrained = failures.isEmpty() && !cleanupUnconfirmed;
        final boolean actualProducersDrained = producersDrained;
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> rootLogger.setLevel(rootLevel));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> appLogger.setLevel(appLevel));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> appender.setOutputStream(originalStream));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> auditAppender.setOutputStream(auditOriginalStream));
        p3AttemptTeardown(
            failures,
            () -> {
              synchronized (this) {
                assertThat(lifecycleDiagnosticFailed)
                    .as("private lifecycle diagnostic integrity")
                    .isFalse();
                assertThat(
                        Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("c5-grant-validation-diagnostic.jsonl-failed")))
                    .as("private grant diagnostic integrity")
                    .isFalse();
                assertThat(failed).as("final private appender capture integrity").isFalse();
                assertThat(actualProducersDrained)
                    .as("existing producers genuinely drained")
                    .isTrue();
                assertThat(appender.getOutputStream() == originalStream)
                    .as("original output target actually restored")
                    .isTrue();
                synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
                  encoded.assertClean(true);
                }
                assertThat(auditAppender.getOutputStream() == auditOriginalStream)
                    .as("original audit output target actually restored")
                    .isTrue();
                synchronized (auditMonitor) {
                  auditEncoded.assertClean(true);
                }
                streamedLogsFinalChecked = true;
                var finalBytes = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>(produced);
                for (String value : values) {
                  assertJoinedSinkAbsent(finalBytes, value);
                }
              }
            });
        // Persist only actual fixed counters, including late/restoration bytes or sticky hits.
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, this::writeNumericEvidence);
      } finally {
        synchronized (this) {
          boolean targetClosed = false;
          if (producersDrained) {
            synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
              targetClosed = encoded.inputClosed;
            }
          }
          if (producersDrained && targetClosed && appender.getOutputStream() == originalStream) {
            p3AttemptTeardown(
                failures, () -> Files.deleteIfExists(TEMP.resolve("c5-private-values.json")));
            synchronized (encodedMonitor) {
              encoded.wipe();
            }
            synchronized (auditMonitor) {
              auditEncoded.wipe();
            }
            produced.clear();
          } else {
            cleanupUnconfirmed = true;
            failures.add(
                new IllegalStateException("C5 encoded stream closure remains unconfirmed"));
            // Retain bounded patterns/state and original evidence while writers are unresolved.
          }
        }
      }
      throwTeardownFailures(failures);
    }
  }

  private static final class C5Stream implements AutoCloseable {
    private final java.io.InputStream input;
    private final java.util.concurrent.FutureTask<byte[]> result;
    private final Thread reader;
    private byte[] bytes;

    C5Stream(java.io.InputStream input) {
      this.input = input;
      result = new java.util.concurrent.FutureTask<>(() -> input.readNBytes(4 * 1024 * 1024 + 1));
      reader = Thread.ofVirtual().name("private-c5-sse").start(result);
    }

    byte[] completed() throws Exception {
      if (bytes == null) {
        try {
          bytes = result.get(15, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (Exception primary) {
          try {
            close();
          } catch (Exception cleanup) {
            primary.addSuppressed(cleanup);
          }
          throw primary;
        }
        close();
        assertThat(bytes.length).isPositive().isLessThanOrEqualTo(4 * 1024 * 1024);
        String framing = new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        assertThat(framing.contains("event:execution.snapshot")).isTrue();
        assertThat(framing.contains("\"streamComplete\":true")).isTrue();
      }
      return bytes;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() throws Exception {
      try {
        input.close();
      } finally {
        result.cancel(true);
        reader.join(1000);
      }
      assertThat(reader.isAlive()).as("owned SSE reader released").isFalse();
    }
  }

  private void collectJoinedSinks(Map<String, byte[]> sinks) throws Exception {
    // Values never become SQL parameters or query text, including when JDBC TRACE is enabled.
    for (String table :
        List.of(
            "ui_test_revisions",
            "ui_test_executions",
            "studio_guided_source_manifests",
            "studio_guided_materializations",
            "studio_guided_actions",
            "studio_secret_use_audit",
            "studio_guided_commands",
            "studio_guided_translation_plans",
            "studio_guided_translation_state",
            "studio_guided_model_attempts",
            "studio_observed_secret_read_entries",
            "studio_guided_publications")) {
      List<String> rows =
          jdbc.queryForList("SELECT row_to_json(t)::text FROM " + table + " t", String.class);
      sinks.put("postgres-" + table, String.join("\n", rows).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }
    sinks.put("gateway", String.join("\n", MODEL_WIRE).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    for (String file : List.of("guided.log", "ordinary.log")) {
      sinks.put(file, Files.readAllBytes(TEMP.resolve(file)));
    }
    for (String directory : List.of("journal", "scratch", "objects")) {
      Path root = TEMP.resolve(directory);
      if (Files.isDirectory(root)) {
        try (var paths = Files.walk(root)) {
          for (Path file :
              paths
                  .filter(
                      path -> Files.isRegularFile(path, java.nio.file.LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS))
                  .toList()) {
            assertThat(Files.isSymbolicLink(file)).isFalse();
            sinks.put("owned-" + TEMP.relativize(file), Files.readAllBytes(file));
          }
        }
      }
    }
    C5Capture capture = c5Capture;
    if (capture != null) {
      capture.collect(sinks);
    }
    // Fixture /__receipts deliberately contains the password; it is not a production sink.
  }

  private static List<byte[]> canaryByteVariants(String value) {
    var variants = new java.util.ArrayList<byte[]>();
    byte[] utf8 = value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    variants.add(utf8);
    variants.add(value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_16LE));
    variants.add(value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_16BE));
    variants.add(Base64.getEncoder().encode(utf8));
    variants.add(HexFormat.of().formatHex(utf8).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    variants.add(HexFormat.of().withUpperCase().formatHex(utf8).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    StringBuilder unicode = new StringBuilder();
    for (char character : value.toCharArray()) {
      unicode.append(String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "\\u%04x", (int) character));
    }
    variants.add(unicode.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    variants.add(
        unicode
            .toString()
            .toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT)
            .replace("\\U", "\\u")
            .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    StringBuilder percent = new StringBuilder();
    StringBuilder component = new StringBuilder();
    for (byte octet : utf8) {
      int unsigned = Byte.toUnsignedInt(octet);
      String escape = String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%%%02X", unsigned);
      percent.append(escape);
      // JavaScript encodeURIComponent leaves these ASCII bytes unescaped.
      if (unsigned >= 'a' && unsigned <= 'z'
          || unsigned >= 'A' && unsigned <= 'Z'
          || unsigned >= '0' && unsigned <= '9'
          || "-_.!~*'()".indexOf(unsigned) >= 0) {
        component.append((char) unsigned);
      } else {
        component.append(escape);
      }
    }
    variants.add(percent.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    variants.add(
        percent.toString().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    variants.add(component.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    String form = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    variants.add(form.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    variants.add(form.replace("+", "%20").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    try {
      String json = JSON.writeValueAsString(value);
      variants.add(json.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      variants.add(json.substring(1, json.length() - 1).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    } catch (com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException failure) {
      throw new IllegalStateException("Synthetic sink JSON encoding unavailable");
    }
    return variants;
  }

  private static void assertJoinedSinkAbsent(Map<String, byte[]> sinks, String value) {
    List<byte[]> variants = canaryByteVariants(value);
    for (var sink : sinks.entrySet()) {
      for (byte[] variant : variants) {
        assertThat(containsBytes(sink.getValue(), variant))
            .as("released value absent from actual sink %s", sink.getKey())
            .isFalse();
      }
    }
  }

  /** Bounded patterns/prefix state only; never retains original encoded log history. */
  private static final class C5CanaryStream {
    private static final int MAX_VALUES = 16, MAX_VALUE_CHARS = 4096, VARIANTS = 15;
    private static final int MAX_PATTERN_BYTES = MAX_VALUE_CHARS * 12;
    private static final int MAX_TOTAL_PATTERN_BYTES = MAX_VALUES * VARIANTS * MAX_PATTERN_BYTES;
    private final List<byte[]> patterns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
    private int[][] prefixes = new int[0][];
    private int[] states = new int[0];
    private final int[][] firstByteIndices = new int[256][];
    private int[] activeIndices = new int[0], scratchCandidates = new int[0];
    private boolean indicesReady;
    private final boolean[] startBytes = new boolean[256];
    private int activeStates;
    private long encodedBytes, writeCalls, hits, invalidWrites;
    private int patternBytes;
    private boolean failed, inputClosed, wiped;

    C5CanaryStream(List<String> values) {
      try {
        if (values == null || values.isEmpty() || values.size() > MAX_VALUES) {
          throw new IllegalArgumentException("C5 stream value inventory bound");
        }
        for (String value : values) {
          if (value == null || value.isEmpty() || value.length() > MAX_VALUE_CHARS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("C5 stream value bound");
          }
          List<byte[]> variants = canaryByteVariants(value);
          if (variants.size() != VARIANTS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("C5 stream variant count");
          }
          for (byte[] variant : variants) {
            if (variant.length == 0
                || variant.length > MAX_PATTERN_BYTES
                || patternBytes > MAX_TOTAL_PATTERN_BYTES - variant.length) {
              throw new IllegalArgumentException("C5 stream pattern bound");
            }
            patterns.add(variant);
            patternBytes += variant.length;
          }
        }
        prefixes = new int[patterns.size()][];
        states = new int[patterns.size()];
        for (int index = 0; index < patterns.size(); index++) {
          byte[] pattern = patterns.get(index);
          startBytes[Byte.toUnsignedInt(pattern[0])] = true;
          int[] prefix = new int[pattern.length];
          for (int offset = 1, matched = 0; offset < pattern.length; offset++) {
            while (matched > 0 && pattern[offset] != pattern[matched]) {
              matched = prefix[matched - 1];
            }
            if (pattern[offset] == pattern[matched]) {
              matched++;
            }
            prefix[offset] = matched;
          }
          prefixes[index] = prefix;
        }
        activeIndices = new int[patterns.size()];
        scratchCandidates = new int[patterns.size()];
        int[] bucketOffsets = new int[256];
        try {
          for (byte[] pattern : patterns) {
            bucketOffsets[Byte.toUnsignedInt(pattern[0])]++;
          }
          for (int octet = 0; octet < firstByteIndices.length; octet++) {
            firstByteIndices[octet] = new int[bucketOffsets[octet]];
          }
          java.util.Arrays.fill(bucketOffsets, 0);
          for (int index = 0; index < patterns.size(); index++) {
            int octet = Byte.toUnsignedInt(patterns.get(index)[0]);
            firstByteIndices[octet][bucketOffsets[octet]++] = index;
          }
          indicesReady = true;
        } finally {
          java.util.Arrays.fill(bucketOffsets, 0);
        }
      } catch (RuntimeException invalid) {
        // Failure is sticky and closed; never export a payload-containing cause.
        failed = true;
      }
    }

    private long increment(long counter) {
      if (counter == Long.MAX_VALUE) {
        failed = true;
        return counter;
      }
      return counter + 1;
    }

    private void invalid() {
      failed = true;
      invalidWrites = increment(invalidWrites);
    }

    void write(int octet) {
      writeCalls = increment(writeCalls);
      if (inputClosed || wiped) {
        invalid();
      }
      consume((byte) octet); // Original OutputStream.write(int) consumes its low eight bits.
    }

    void write(byte[] bytes, int offset, int length) {
      writeCalls = increment(writeCalls);
      if (inputClosed || wiped) {
        invalid();
      }
      if (bytes == null || offset < 0 || length < 0 || offset > bytes.length - length) {
        invalid();
        return;
      }
      // No truncation/drop after a hit; every later valid byte is independently consumed.
      for (int index = offset; index < offset + length; index++) {
        consume(bytes[index]);
      }
    }

    private void consume(byte octet) {
      encodedBytes = increment(encodedBytes);
      if (wiped
          || !indicesReady
          || firstByteIndices.length != 256
          || activeIndices.length != patterns.size()
          || scratchCandidates.length != patterns.size()
          || states.length != patterns.size()
          || prefixes.length != patterns.size()
          || activeStates < 0
          || activeStates > patterns.size()) {
        failed = true;
        return;
      }
      if (activeStates == 0 && !startBytes[Byte.toUnsignedInt(octet)]) {
        return;
      }
      int[] starts = firstByteIndices[Byte.toUnsignedInt(octet)];
      if (starts == null || starts.length > patterns.size()) {
        failed = true;
        return;
      }
      // Merge the ordered OLD-state inventories completely before any KMP state changes.
      int candidateCount = 0, activeOffset = 0, startOffset = 0, previousIndex = -1;
      while (activeOffset < activeStates || startOffset < starts.length) {
        int index;
        boolean wasActive;
        if (startOffset == starts.length
            || activeOffset < activeStates && activeIndices[activeOffset] < starts[startOffset]) {
          index = activeIndices[activeOffset++];
          wasActive = true;
        } else if (activeOffset == activeStates
            || starts[startOffset] < activeIndices[activeOffset]) {
          index = starts[startOffset++];
          wasActive = false;
        } else {
          index = activeIndices[activeOffset++];
          startOffset++;
          wasActive = true;
        }
        if (index <= previousIndex
            || index >= states.length
            || candidateCount >= scratchCandidates.length
            || wasActive != (states[index] > 0)) {
          failed = true;
          return;
        }
        scratchCandidates[candidateCount++] = index;
        previousIndex = index;
      }
      int nextActive = 0;
      for (int candidate = 0; candidate < candidateCount; candidate++) {
        int index = scratchCandidates[candidate];
        byte[] pattern = patterns.get(index);
        int[] prefix = prefixes[index];
        int previous = states[index];
        int matched = previous;
        while (matched > 0 && pattern[matched] != octet) {
          matched = prefix[matched - 1];
        }
        if (pattern[matched] == octet) {
          matched++;
        }
        if (matched == pattern.length) {
          hits = increment(hits);
          matched = prefix[matched - 1];
        }
        if (previous == 0 && matched > 0) {
          activeStates++;
        } else if (previous > 0 && matched == 0) {
          activeStates--;
        }
        states[index] = matched;
        if (matched > 0) {
          activeIndices[nextActive++] = index;
        }
      }
      if (activeStates < 0 || activeStates > patterns.size() || nextActive != activeStates) {
        failed = true;
      }
    }

    void closeInput() {
      inputClosed = true;
    }

    void assertClean(boolean requireClosed) {
      assertThat(indicesReady || wiped || failed)
          .as("complete original-index dispatch metadata")
          .isTrue();
      assertThat(activeIndices.length).isEqualTo(states.length);
      assertThat(scratchCandidates.length).isEqualTo(states.length);
      int previousIndex = -1;
      for (int offset = 0; offset < activeStates; offset++) {
        int index = activeIndices[offset];
        assertThat(index > previousIndex && index < states.length && states[index] > 0)
            .as("ordered exact positive original-pattern index inventory")
            .isTrue();
        previousIndex = index;
      }
      assertThat(activeStates)
          .as("exact positive KMP state inventory")
          .isEqualTo((int) java.util.Arrays.stream(states).filter(state -> state > 0).count());
      assertThat(failed).as("original encoded stream observer/counter integrity").isFalse();
      assertThat(invalidWrites).as("original encoded stream invalid or late writes").isZero();
      assertThat(encodedBytes).as("actual original encoded bytes consumed").isPositive();
      assertThat(writeCalls).as("actual original encoded write calls consumed").isPositive();
      assertThat(hits)
          .as("released literal/encoded values absent from original encoded stream")
          .isZero();
      if (requireClosed) {
        assertThat(inputClosed).as("original capture target was actually closed").isTrue();
      }
    }

    void wipe() {
      for (byte[] pattern : patterns) {
        java.util.Arrays.fill(pattern, (byte) 0);
      }
      for (int[] prefix : prefixes) {
        if (prefix != null) {
          java.util.Arrays.fill(prefix, 0);
        }
      }
      java.util.Arrays.fill(states, 0);
      java.util.Arrays.fill(activeIndices, 0);
      java.util.Arrays.fill(scratchCandidates, 0);
      for (int[] bucket : firstByteIndices) {
        if (bucket != null) {
          java.util.Arrays.fill(bucket, 0);
        }
      }
      java.util.Arrays.fill(firstByteIndices, null);
      activeIndices = new int[0];
      scratchCandidates = new int[0];
      indicesReady = false;
      java.util.Arrays.fill(startBytes, false);
      activeStates = 0;
      patterns.clear();
      prefixes = new int[0][];
      states = new int[0];
      wiped = true;
    }
  }

  private static boolean containsBytes(byte[] bytes, byte[] needle) {
    for (int start = 0; start <= bytes.length - needle.length; start++) {
      int offset = 0;
      while (offset < needle.length && bytes[start + offset] == needle[offset]) {
        offset++;
      }
      if (offset == needle.length) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  private UUID createSource(List<String> texts) throws Exception {
    return createSource(texts, 30);
  }

  private UUID createSource(List<String> texts, int timeoutSeconds) throws Exception {
    UUID id = UUID.randomUUID();
    var authoring =
        new AuthoringV2Request(
            2,
            null,
            List.of(
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(ONE), texts.get(0), null),
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(TWO), texts.get(1), null),
                new AuthoringStep(UUID.fromString(THREE), texts.get(2), null)));
    jdbc.update(
        "INSERT INTO ui_tests(id,name,csi_id,environment,browser,steps,variables,authoring,"
            + "authoring_revision_inputs,timeout_seconds,created_by,created_at,updated_at) VALUES"
            + " (?,'Delayed observed',?,'fixture','chromium',?::jsonb,'[]'::jsonb,?::jsonb,"
            + "?::jsonb,?,'fixture@example.invalid',now(),now())",
        id,
        primaryCsi,
        JSON.writeValueAsString(texts),
        JSON.writeValueAsString(authoring),
        JSON.writeValueAsString(AuthoringRevisionIdentity.create(authoring, Map.of())),
        timeoutSeconds);
    return id;
  }

  private String startLearning(UUID id) throws Exception {
    ObjectNode start =
        C.object()
            .put("schemaVersion", 3)
            .put("testId", id.toString())
            .put("sourceRevision", "1")
            .put("configurationRevision", "1")
            .put("environmentId", "fixture")
            .put("applicationId", "orders");
    start.set("versions", versions());
    return publicCall("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions", start, 202, UUID.randomUUID().toString())
        .path("session")
        .path("sessionId")
        .asText();
  }

  private void awaitFinalizing(String id) throws Exception {
    long end = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(90).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < end) {
      String state =
          c5Capture != null
              ? jdbc.queryForObject(
                  "SELECT state FROM studio_guided_sessions WHERE session_id=?", String.class, id)
              : sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().path("state").asText());
      if (List.of("FINALIZING", "FAILED", "EXPIRED", "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME", "CANCELLED")
          .contains(state)) {
        assertThat(state).as("second explicit learning; logs %s", TEMP).isEqualTo("FINALIZING");
        return;
      }
      Thread.sleep(50);
    }
    throw new AssertionError("Second explicit learning did not finalize; logs " + TEMP);
  }

  private UUID arrangeActivation(UUID schedule, UUID testId, UUID revision, long version) {
    UUID activation = UUID.randomUUID();
    Instant due =
        Instant.now()
            .truncatedTo(java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit.MINUTES)
            .minusSeconds(version == 2 ? 60 : 30);
    sessions.transaction(
        () -> {
          jdbc.update(
              "INSERT INTO studio_schedule_activations(id, schedule_id, test_id, csi_id,"
                  + " schedule_version, revision_id, environment, execution_principal,"
                  + " activated_by) VALUES"
                  + " (?,?,?,'CSI-123',?,?,'fixture','user-1','fixture@example.invalid')",
              activation,
              schedule,
              testId,
              version,
              revision);
          if (version == 4) {
            jdbc.update(
                "UPDATE studio_schedules SET cron_expression='30 * * * * *' WHERE id=?", schedule);
          }
          jdbc.update(
              "UPDATE studio_schedules SET version=?, state='ENABLED', requested_enabled=true,"
                  + " blocked_code=NULL, activated_revision_id=?, activation_id=?, next_due_at=?,"
                  + " poll_after_at=? WHERE id=?",
              version,
              revision,
              activation,
              java.sql.Timestamp.from(due),
              java.sql.Timestamp.from(due),
              schedule);
          return null;
        });
    return activation;
  }

  private Map<String, Object> activationIdentity(UUID schedule) {
    return jdbc.queryForMap(
        "SELECT a.* FROM studio_schedule_activations a JOIN studio_schedules s ON"
            + " s.activation_id=a.id WHERE s.id=?",
        schedule);
  }

  private org.springframework.context.ConfigurableApplicationContext observedWorkerReplica(
      AtomicInteger closedExecutors) {
    Map<String, Object> settings = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
    properties((name, value) -> settings.put(name, value.get()));
    settings.put("spring.flyway.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("server.port", "0");
    settings.put("server.ssl.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("management.server.port", "-1");
    settings.put("spring.jmx.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("spring.main.banner-mode", "off");
    settings.put("logging.level.root", "WARN");
    settings.put("checks.scheduling.dispatcher-enabled", "false");
    settings.put("checks.scheduling.retry-enabled", "false");
    settings.put("checks.scheduling.partition-scheduler-enabled", "false");
    settings.put("checks.scheduling.retention-pruner-enabled", "false");
    settings.put("checks.reports.delivery-enabled", "false");
    settings.put("studio.scheduling.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("studio.execution.dispatcher.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("studio.guided.publication.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("studio.gateway.provider", "disabled");
    var replica =
        new org.springframework.boot.builder.SpringApplicationBuilder(ChecksApplication.class)
            .profiles("test", "worker")
            .initializers(
                context -> {
                  context
                      .getEnvironment()
                      .getPropertySources()
                      .addFirst(
                          new org.springframework.core.env.MapPropertySource(
                              "observed-worker-fixture", settings));
                  context.addBeanFactoryPostProcessor(
                      factory -> {
                        var local =
                            (org.springframework.beans.factory.support.DefaultListableBeanFactory)
                                factory;
                        // Suppress timers before registration; every domain bean remains
                        // production.
                        local.removeBeanDefinition(
                            org.springframework.scheduling.config.TaskManagementConfigUtils
                                .SCHEDULED_ANNOTATION_PROCESSOR_BEAN_NAME);
                        local.addBeanPostProcessor(
                            new org.springframework.beans.factory.config.BeanPostProcessor() {
                              @Override
                              public Object postProcessAfterInitialization(
                                  Object bean, String name) {
                                if (java.util.Set.of(
                                        "applicationTaskExecutor",
                                        "checkExecutor",
                                        "dispatchExecutor")
                                    .contains(name)) {
                                  assertThat(bean)
                                      .isInstanceOf(
                                          org.springframework.core.task.support.TaskExecutorAdapter
                                              .class);
                                  Object bounded =
                                      org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(
                                          bean, "concurrentExecutor");
                                  assertThat(bounded)
                                      .isInstanceOf(
                                          com.citi.put.checks.config.SemaphoreBoundedExecutor
                                              .class);
                                  var delegate =
                                      (java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService)
                                          org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils
                                              .getField(bounded, "delegate");
                                  assertThat(delegate).isNotNull();
                                  // The adapter has no destroy method. Register its real owned
                                  // delegate
                                  // before a later startup failure can discard the context
                                  // reference.
                                  local.registerDisposableBean(
                                      name,
                                      () -> {
                                        delegate.shutdown();
                                        awaitOwnedExecutorTermination(
                                            delegate,
                                            System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(10).toNanos());
                                        closedExecutors.incrementAndGet();
                                      });
                                }
                                return bean;
                              }
                            });
                      });
                })
            .run("--spring.profiles.active=test,worker", "--checks.local-dev-auth.enabled=false");
    ensureAuditCapture();
    return replica;
  }

  private void assertObservedWorker(
      org.springframework.context.ConfigurableApplicationContext worker, UUID testId) {
    assertThat(worker.getEnvironment().getActiveProfiles()).containsExactly("test", "worker");
    assertThat(
            worker.containsBean(
                org.springframework.scheduling.config.TaskManagementConfigUtils
                    .SCHEDULED_ANNOTATION_PROCESSOR_BEAN_NAME))
        .isFalse();
    assertThat(
            worker.getBeansOfType(
                com.citi.put.checks.studio.dispatch.StudioDispatchScheduler.class))
        .isEmpty();
    assertThat(worker.getBeansOfType(com.citi.put.checks.studio.StudioController.class)).isEmpty();
    assertThat(
            worker.getBeansOfType(
                com.citi.put.checks.studio.internal.StudioInternalController.class))
        .isEmpty();
    assertThat(worker.getBeansOfType(GuidedPublicationController.class)).isEmpty();
    assertThat(
            worker
                .getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.StudioExecutionProperties.class)
                .callbackOrDefault()
                .serviceToken()
                .equals(CALLBACK))
        .isTrue();
    var actual = worker.getBean(StudioDispatcher.class);
    var client = worker.getBean(HttpRunnerDispatchClient.class);
    var verifier = worker.getBean(ObservedRevisionVerifier.class);
    var authorization = worker.getBean(ObservedReplayAuthorization.class);
    var readiness =
        worker.getBean(com.citi.put.checks.studio.readiness.ExecutionReadinessService.class);
    assertThat(actual.getClass()).isEqualTo(StudioDispatcher.class);
    assertThat(client.getClass()).isEqualTo(HttpRunnerDispatchClient.class);
    assertThat(verifier.getClass()).isEqualTo(ObservedRevisionVerifier.class);
    assertThat(org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(actual, "runnerClient"))
        .isSameAs(client);
    assertThat(org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(actual, "observed"))
        .isSameAs(authorization);
    assertThat(
            org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(authorization, "verifier"))
        .isSameAs(verifier);
    assertThat(org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(readiness, "observed"))
        .isSameAs(verifier);
    assertThat(readiness.evaluate(testId).ready()).isTrue();
  }

  private void historicalReplica(
      String id, JsonNode receipt, boolean replay, boolean support, String readinessCode)
      throws Exception {
    Map<String, Object> settings = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
    settings.put("spring.datasource.driver-class-name", "org.postgresql.Driver");
    settings.put("spring.datasource.url", DB.getJdbcUrl());
    settings.put("spring.datasource.username", DB.getUsername());
    settings.put("spring.datasource.password", DB.getPassword());
    settings.put("server.port", "0");
    settings.put("server.ssl.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("spring.jmx.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("spring.main.banner-mode", "off");
    settings.put("logging.level.root", "WARN");
    settings.put("checks.core-service.base-url", ORIGIN);
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.enabled", "true");
    settings.put(
        "studio.guided-authoring.service-public-key",
        Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(ISSUER.getPublic().getEncoded()));
    settings.put("studio.guided-authoring.translation.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("studio.guided.publication.enabled", "false");
    settings.put("studio.observed-replay.enabled", replay);
    settings.put(
        "studio.observed-replay.catalog-json",
        support ? C.object().putArray("catalog").add(CATALOG).toString() : "[]");
    settings.put(
        "studio.observed-replay.policies-json",
        C.object().putArray("policies").add(POLICY).toString());
    settings.put("studio.gateway.provider", "disabled");
    settings.put("studio.gateway.model", "benign-new-learning-default");
    settings.put("studio.execution.dispatcher.enabled", "false");
    try (var replica =
        new org.springframework.boot.builder.SpringApplicationBuilder(ChecksApplication.class)
            .profiles("test")
            .initializers(
                context ->
                    context
                        .getEnvironment()
                        .getPropertySources()
                        .addFirst(
                            new org.springframework.core.env.MapPropertySource(
                                "observed-history-fixture", settings)))
            .run("--spring.profiles.active=test", "--checks.local-dev-auth.enabled=false")) {
      ensureAuditCapture();
      assertThat(replica.getEnvironment().getActiveProfiles()).containsExactly("test");
      int localPort =
          ((org.springframework.boot.web.servlet.context.ServletWebServerApplicationContext)
                  replica)
              .getWebServer()
              .getPort();
      var request =
          HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                  URI.create(
                      "http://localhost:"
                          + localPort
                          + "/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions/"
                          + id
                          + "/result"))
              .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE)
              .GET()
              .build();
      int before = CORE_CALLS.get();
      String expectedVideo = replicaExpectedVideoStatus(id);
      var response = HttpClient.newHttpClient().send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
      assertThat(response.statusCode()).as(response.body()).isEqualTo(200);
      assertThat(CORE_CALLS.get()).isGreaterThan(before);
      JsonNode result = C.parse(response.body());
      assertThat(result.path("publication").path("receipt")).isEqualTo(receipt);
      assertThat(result.path("currentReadiness").path("code").asText()).isEqualTo(readinessCode);
      assertThat(result.path("learnedFromThisRun").asBoolean()).isTrue();
      assertThat(result.path("replayVerified").asBoolean()).isFalse();
      // STUDIO-OBS-013/014 S3: C2b tightened result.evidence.video to a VideoEvidence
      // object (null is no longer valid) and B13's projection always answers one. This
      // replica serves no recording store, so a recorded run reads UNAVAILABLE
      // (NO_RECORDING_STORE) and an unrecorded one is PROCESSING inside the settle window
      // or UNAVAILABLE after it; never AVAILABLE here.
      JsonNode video = result.path("evidence").path("video");
      assertThat(video.isObject()).as(video.toString()).isTrue();
      // AR-QA TEST001 line16036: exact, from the stored recording row on the shared database and
      // the DB-clock window, judged before and after the replica read. Not AC-6 evidence.
      assertThat(replicaExpectedVideoStatus(id))
          .as("no recording fact landed and no DB-clock window edge passed during the read")
          .isEqualTo(expectedVideo);
      assertThat(video.path("status").asText()).as(video.toString()).isEqualTo(expectedVideo);
      if ("AVAILABLE".equals(guidedRecordingRowState(id))) {
        assertThat(video.path("error").asText())
            .isEqualTo(com.citi.put.checks.studio.ExecutionSnapshotMapper.NO_RECORDING_STORE);
      }
    }
  }

  private void startWorkers() throws Exception {
    Files.writeString(
        TEMP.resolve("worker.jwt"), token("owning-worker", "worker-1", "studio-guided-controller"));
    ObjectNode config =
        C.object()

            .put("tlsCertFile", TEMP.resolve("tls.crt").toString())

            .put("controllerSubject", "controller-1")

            .put("callbackBaseUrl", "https://localhost:" + port)
            .put("callbackTokenFile", TEMP.resolve("worker.jwt").toString());
    // STUDIO-OBS-014 S3 (RK-2, ar-mounted-rules): the worker mounts neither `rules` nor
    // `applicationPolicy`. The trusted policy's application policy becomes the governed, approved
    // policy of every learning scope this class uses (both CSIs, both environments).
    for (String csi : List.of("CSI-123", "CSI-456")) {
      seedGovernedPolicies(csi);
    }
    Files.writeString(TEMP.resolve("worker.json"), config.toString());
    Path guidedMain = TEMP.resolve("p3-guided-launch-counter.mjs");

    var guided = new ProcessBuilder("node", guidedMain.toString());
    guided.environment().put("STUDIO_GUIDED_CONFIG_FILE", TEMP.resolve("worker.json").toString());
    // STUDIO-OBS-013/014 S3: the worker records into the same local store the backend serves
    // video from (studio.execution.video-store.directory = TEMP/objects).
    guided.environment().put("STUDIO_RUNNER_OBJECT_STORE_DIR", TEMP.resolve("objects").toString());
    guided.redirectErrorStream(true).redirectOutput(TEMP.resolve("guided.log").toFile());
    guidedWorker = guided.start();
    String root = Path.of("../studio-runner/dist").toAbsolutePath().normalize().toUri().toString();

        System.getenv()

            .put("STUDIO_RUNNER_OBSERVED_IMAGE_DIGEST", "c".repeat(64))

    String initialContextCheck =
        ("const checkInitial=async(handle)=>{const fs=await import('node:fs');const state=()=>J"

            + JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-state.json").toString())
            + ",'utf8'));fs.writeFileSync("
            + JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("initial-context-check-entered").toString())
            + ",'entered');while(state().pause)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));"
            + "if(state().fail)return false;return handle();};";
    Path child = TEMP.resolve("recorded-child.mjs");

        child,
        "import {startChild} from '"

            + JSON.writeValueAsString(child.toString())

            + env
            + (",STUDIO_RUNNER_POOL_SIZE:process.env.OBS007_ORDINARY_POOL_SIZE});"
                + "const app=createApplication(cutConfig,{launcher,http:revHttp,"
                + "scratch:cutScratchStore(cutConfig.scratchDir),"
                + "runtimeVersions:loadRuntimeVersions(),observed:{build,policies:[")
            + POLICY
