            + " app.start();process.on('SIGTERM',()=>{void"
            + " app.drain().then(()=>process.exit(0));});");
    startOrdinaryWorker(false);
    for (int wanted : new int[] {GUIDED_PORT, NORMAL_PORT}) {
      long until = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
      boolean ready = false;
      while (System.nanoTime() < until) {
        try (var socket = new java.net.Socket("127.0.0.1", wanted)) {
          ready = true;
          break;
        } catch (java.io.IOException pending) {
          Thread.sleep(30);
        }
      }
      assertThat(ready).as("worker port %s logs at %s", wanted, TEMP).isTrue();
    }
  }

  private void startOrdinaryWorker(boolean preserveExistingLog) throws Exception {
    startOrdinaryWorker(preserveExistingLog, 1);
  }

  private void startOrdinaryWorker(boolean preserveExistingLog, int poolSize) throws Exception {
    assertThat(poolSize).isBetween(1, 2);
    String browsers =
        System.getenv()
            .getOrDefault(
                "PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH",
                Path.of(System.getProperty("user.home"), "Library", "Caches", "ms-playwright")
                    .toString());
    var ordinary = new ProcessBuilder("node", TEMP.resolve("ordinary.mjs").toString());
    ordinary.environment().put("NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS", TEMP.resolve("tls.crt").toString());
    ordinary.environment().put("PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH", browsers);
    ordinary.environment().put("OBS007_ORDINARY_POOL_SIZE", Integer.toString(poolSize));
    ordinary
        .redirectErrorStream(true)
        .redirectOutput(
            preserveExistingLog
                ? ProcessBuilder.Redirect.appendTo(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log").toFile())
                : ProcessBuilder.Redirect.to(TEMP.resolve("ordinary.log").toFile()));
    ordinaryWorker = ordinary.start();
    if (!preserveExistingLog) {
      return; // Original startWorkers owns its unchanged initial two-port readiness check.
    }
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(20).toNanos();
    while (System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      try (var socket = new java.net.Socket("127.0.0.1", NORMAL_PORT)) {
        assertThat(ordinaryWorker.isAlive()).isTrue();
        return;
      } catch (IOException pending) {
        Thread.sleep(30);
      }
    }
    throw new IllegalStateException("Actual ordinary worker restart readiness unconfirmed");
  }

  private static String p6ChildCutSpawner(String root) throws Exception {
    return """
import {validateObserved as cutValidate,OBSERVED_LIMITS as cutLimits} from %s;
import {monitorEventLoopDelay as cutLoopDelay} from 'node:perf_hooks';
const cutRealSpawner=createForkSpawner(),cutReady=new Map(),cutStops=new Map();
// R10-rev2-2 permanent diagnostic, through the existing spawner wrappers only. Per message
// it only appends to an in-memory array; one asynchronous write happens at child exit.
const cutDiagnosticFile=%s,cutDiagnosticKinds=['ready','result','step-started','step-finished'];
let cutSpawnOrdinal=0;
const cutCode=value=>value===null||value===undefined?null
  :typeof value==='string'&&/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,63}$/.test(value)?value:'OTHER';
const cutUuid=value=>typeof value==='string'
  &&/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/.test(value)?value:null;
// Fixed call-site label from the stack; no frame is retained or written.
const cutKillSite=stack=>{const frames=String(stack??'').split('\\n').slice(2);
  if(frames.some(frame=>/\\bperformStop\\b/.test(frame)))return 'performStop-grace';
  if(/launcher\\.js:\\d+:\\d+/.test(frames[0]??'')
    &&frames.some(frame=>/node:events|node:internal\\/child_process/.test(frame)))
    return 'violation-catch';
  return 'other';};
const cutWriteDiagnostic=record=>{
  let line;
  try{
    const messages=record.received,results=messages.filter(m=>m?.kind==='result');
    const result=results[0]??null;let schemaValid=null;
    if(result){try{cutValidate('replay-ipc#/definitions/Result3',result,cutLimits.result);
      schemaValid=true;}catch{schemaValid=false;}}
    const executionId=cutUuid(result?.runId)??cutUuid(messages.find(m=>cutUuid(m?.runId))?.runId)
      ??cutUuid(record.mode?.executionId);
    const reads=executionId?revReadLog.get(executionId)??[]:[];
    if(executionId)revReadLog.delete(executionId);
    const loop=record.loop,whole=value=>Number.isSafeInteger(value)?value:0;
    line=JSON.stringify({v:1,ordinal:record.ordinal,pid:whole(record.pid),
      case:whole(record.mode?.case),c6:whole(record.mode?.c6),executionId,
      received:messages.slice(0,256).map(m=>cutDiagnosticKinds.includes(m?.kind)?m.kind:'other'),
      receivedCount:messages.length,results:results.length,
      resultOk:typeof result?.ok==='boolean'?result.ok:null,
      resultErrorCode:result?cutCode(result.errorCode):null,resultSchemaValid:schemaValid,
      kills:record.kills.slice(0,8),killCount:record.kills.length,
      exitCode:Number.isSafeInteger(record.exit?.code)?record.exit.code:null,
      exitSignal:typeof record.exit?.signal==='string'&&/^SIG[A-Z0-9]{1,12}$/.test(record.exit.signal)
        ?record.exit.signal:null,
      loopDelayMaxMs:loop?Math.ceil(loop.max/1e6):null,
      loopDelayP99Ms:loop&&loop.count>0?Math.ceil(loop.percentile(99)/1e6):null,
      controlReads:reads,controlReadCount:reads.length})+'\\n';
  }catch{line=JSON.stringify({v:1,ordinal:record.ordinal,diagnosticFailed:true})+'\\n';}
  try{record.loop?.disable();}catch{}
  record.received=[];
  revEnqueue(async()=>{try{await revAppendAsync(cutDiagnosticFile,line);}catch{}});
};
const cutObserveChild=(handle,mode)=>{
  const record={ordinal:++cutSpawnOrdinal,pid:handle.pid,mode,received:[],kills:[],
    exit:null,loop:null};
  try{record.loop=cutLoopDelay({resolution:10});record.loop.enable();}catch{record.loop=null;}
  try{handle.onMessage(message=>{record.received.push(message);});}catch{}
  const kill=handle.kill;
  if(typeof kill==='function')handle.kill=function(...args){
    revKillCalls++;try{record.kills.push(cutKillSite(new Error().stack));}catch{}
    return Reflect.apply(kill,this,args);};
  try{handle.onExit((code,signal)=>{record.exit={code,signal};
    setImmediate(()=>{cutWriteDiagnostic(record);});});}catch{}
};
const cutSpawner={spawn(request){
  const handle=cutRealSpawner.spawn(request);
  let spawnMode=null;
  try{const selected=revModeNow();spawnMode=selected;
  if(selected?.childCut===true&&selected.c6===2&&selected.path===2){
    const stop={pid:handle.pid,mode:selected,observed:false,result:null};
    cutStops.set(handle,stop);
    const original=handle.quiesce;
    if(typeof original==='function'){
      handle.quiesce=function(...args){
        let result;try{result=Reflect.apply(original,this,args);}catch(error){stop.observed=\
true;stop.result=null;throw error;}
        try{result.then(value=>{stop.observed=true;stop.result=typeof value==='boolean'?value:null;},
          ()=>{stop.observed=true;stop.result=null;}).catch(()=>{stop.observed=true;stop.res\
ult=null;});}
        catch{stop.observed=true;stop.result=null;}return result;
      };
    }
  }}catch{/* Missing observation remains unknown; original spawn and listeners continue. */}
  try{cutObserveChild(handle,spawnMode);}catch{/* Diagnostic only. */}
  handle.onMessage(message=>{
    if(message?.kind!=='ready')return;
    const mode=revModeCached();
    if(mode?.childCut!==true||message.runId!==mode.executionId)return;
    try{
      const ready=cutValidate('replay-ipc#/definitions/Ready3',message,16384);
      if(!Number.isSafeInteger(handle.pid)||handle.pid<1||cutReady.has(ready.runId))
        throw new Error('IDENTITY');
      cutReady.set(ready.runId,{pid:handle.pid,ready,stop:cutStops.get(handle)});
    }catch{cutReady.set(mode.executionId,{failed:true});}
  });
  return handle;
}};
const cutOriginalSend=revHttp.send;
revHttp.send=function(...args){
  let request=args[0];
  const mode=request?.method==='POST'?revModeCached():null;
  if(mode?.childCut===true&&request?.method==='POST'){
    try{
      const body=JSON.parse(request.body);
      if(body.executionId===mode.executionId){
        const witness=cutReady.get(body.executionId);
        if(witness?.failed)request={...request,headers:{...request.headers,
          'X-Fixture-Child-Observer-Failed':'1'}};
        else if(witness){
          if(body.attemptId!==witness.ready.attemptId||body.admissionId!==witness.ready.admissionId)
            throw new Error('IDENTITY');
          const encoded=Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(witness.ready),'utf8').toString('base64url');
          if(encoded.length>4096)throw new Error('BOUND');
          request={...request,headers:{...request.headers,
            'X-Fixture-Child-Pid':String(witness.pid),'X-Fixture-Child-Ready':encoded}};
        }
      }
    }catch{request={...request,headers:{...request.headers,'X-Fixture-Child-Observer-Failed':'1'}};}
  }
  return Reflect.apply(cutOriginalSend,this,[request,...args.slice(1)]);
};

import {createFsScratchStore as cutFsScratchStore} from %s;
import {SCRATCH_SUBDIRECTORIES as cutScratchLayout} from %s;
import * as p4Fs from 'node:fs';import {join as cutJoin,dirname as cutDirname} from 'node:path';
const c5ValuesFile=%s,c5CountsFile=%s,cutProfileWitnessFile=%s,cutProducerRoot=%s;
%s
const cutCounts=()=>{
  const stat=p4Fs.lstatSync(c5CountsFile);
  if(stat.isSymbolicLink()||!stat.isFile()||(stat.mode&0o777)!==0o600||stat.size>1048576)thr\
ow new Error('COUNTS');
  return p4Fs.readFileSync(c5CountsFile,'utf8').trimEnd().split('\\n');
};
const cutRecord=record=>{try{
  if(p4Fs.existsSync(cutProfileWitnessFile)){const old=p4Fs.lstatSync(cutProfileWitnessFile);
    if(old.isSymbolicLink()||!old.isFile()||(old.mode&0o777)!==0o600)throw new Error('WITNESS');}
  const fd=p4Fs.openSync(cutProfileWitnessFile,p4Fs.constants.O_WRONLY|p4Fs.constants.O_CREAT|
    p4Fs.constants.O_TRUNC|p4Fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
  try{p4Fs.writeSync(fd,JSON.stringify(record));}finally{p4Fs.closeSync(fd);}
}catch{try{const fd=p4Fs.openSync(c5CountsFile+'-failed',p4Fs.constants.O_WRONLY|p4Fs.consta\
nts.O_CREAT|
  p4Fs.constants.O_APPEND|p4Fs.constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);try{p4Fs.writeSync(fd,'1');}final\
ly{p4Fs.closeSync(fd);}}catch{}}};
const cutScratchStore=root=>{
  const original=cutFsScratchStore(root);
  return {create(...args){const result=Reflect.apply(original.create,original,args),selected\
=revMode(),admission=args[0];
    if(selected?.childCut===true&&selected.c6===2&&selected.path===2){
      try{result.then(run=>{
        try{const descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(run,'clear'),clear=run.clear;
          if(typeof clear!=='function')throw new Error('CLEAR');
          const wrapped=function(...clearArgs){
            const record={case:selected.case,epoch:selected.epoch,postFaultScratchProfileObs\
erved:false,
              quiesceObserved:false,quiescentTrue:false,readyAllocationMatched:false,profile\
InodeMatched:false,
              observerFailed:false,profileSampleOrdinal:0};
            try{
              const mode=revMode(),witness=cutReady.get(selected.executionId),stop=witness?.stop;
              if(mode?.case!==selected.case||mode.epoch!==selected.epoch||mode.executionId!=\
=selected.executionId
                ||!witness?.ready||witness.failed||witness.ready.runId!==selected.executionId
                ||witness.ready.admissionId!==admission||stop?.pid!==witness.pid
                ||stop.mode.case!==selected.case||stop.mode.epoch!==selected.epoch||stop.mod\
e.executionId!==selected.executionId)throw new Error('IDENTITY');
              record.readyAllocationMatched=true;record.quiesceObserved=stop.observed===true;
              record.quiescentTrue=stop.observed===true&&stop.result===true;
              if(!record.quiescentTrue)throw new Error('QUIESCENCE');
              const allocated=p4Fs.realpathSync(run.path),rootPath=p4Fs.realpathSync(root),r\
unStat=p4Fs.lstatSync(run.path);
              if(allocated!==run.path||cutDirname(allocated)!==rootPath||runStat.isSymbolicLink()
                ||!runStat.isDirectory()||runStat.uid!==process.getuid()||(runStat.mode&0o777)!==0o700
                ||cutJoin(rootPath,admission)!==allocated)throw new Error('ALLOCATION');
              const receipt=cutJoin(cutProducerRoot,selected.case+'-3.json'),stat=p4Fs.lstat\
Sync(receipt);
              if(stat.isSymbolicLink()||!stat.isFile()||(stat.mode&0o777)!==0o600||stat.size\
>1024)throw new Error('PROFILE');
              const source=JSON.parse(p4Fs.readFileSync(receipt,'utf8'));
              if(source.case!==selected.case||source.epoch!==selected.epoch||source.producer!==3
                ||!Number.isSafeInteger(source.profileDev)||!Number.isSafeInteger(source.profileIno)
                ||source.profileIno<1)throw new Error('PROFILE');
              const profile=cutJoin(allocated,cutScratchLayout.profile),info=p4Fs.lstatSync(profile);
              if(info.isSymbolicLink()||!info.isDirectory()||p4Fs.realpathSync(profile)!==profile
                ||info.uid!==process.getuid()||(info.mode&0o777)!==0o700
                ||info.dev!==source.profileDev||info.ino!==source.profileIno)throw new \
Error('PROFILE');
              record.profileInodeMatched=true;
              const before=cutCounts().length;c5ProfileScan(profile);const after=cutCounts();
              if(after.length!==before+1)throw new Error('SCAN');
              const sample=JSON.parse(after[before]);
              if(sample.failed!==false||sample.hits!==0||!Number.isSafeInteger(sample.files)\
||sample.files<1
                ||!Number.isSafeInteger(sample.bytes)||sample.bytes<1)throw new Error('SCAN');
              record.profileSampleOrdinal=before+1;record.postFaultScratchProfileObserved=true;
            }catch{record.observerFailed=true;}
            cutRecord(record);
            // Observer refusal cannot prevent, retry or replace the original clear/error/promise.
            return Reflect.apply(clear,this,clearArgs);
          };
          if(!Reflect.defineProperty(run,'clear',{...(descriptor??{configurable:true,writabl\
e:true,enumerable:true}),value:wrapped}))throw new Error('CLEAR');
        }catch{cutRecord({case:selected.case,epoch:selected.epoch,postFaultScratchProfileObs\
erved:false,
          quiesceObserved:false,quiescentTrue:false,readyAllocationMatched:false,profileInod\
eMatched:false,
          observerFailed:true,profileSampleOrdinal:0});}
      },()=>{}).catch(()=>{cutRecord({case:selected.case,epoch:selected.epoch,postFaultScrat\
chProfileObserved:false,
        quiesceObserved:false,quiescentTrue:false,readyAllocationMatched:false,profileInodeM\
atched:false,
        observerFailed:true,profileSampleOrdinal:0});});}catch{}
    }return result;},clearAll(...args){return Reflect.apply(original.clearAll,original,args);},
    list(...args){return Reflect.apply(original.list,original,args);}};
};
"""
        .formatted(
            JSON.writeValueAsString(root + "validator/observedReferencePolicy.js"),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve(P10_PARENT_IPC_DIAGNOSTICS).toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(root + "server/scratch.js"),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(root + "run/scratchLayout.js"),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("c5-private-values.json").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("c5-profile-counts.jsonl").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("p3-c11-midfill-process.json").toString()),
            JSON.writeValueAsString(
                TEMP.toRealPath().resolve("p4-revocation-producers").toString()),
            c5ProfileScannerSupport());
  }

  private static String ordinaryAdmissionRefusalTap() throws Exception {
    String witnessFile =
        JSON.writeValueAsString(TEMP.resolve("ordinary-admission-refusals.jsonl").toString());
    return "import {ServerResponse as FixtureServerResponse} from 'node:http';"
        + "import {appendFileSync as appendRefusalWitness} from 'node:fs';"
        + "const originalResponseEnd=FixtureServerResponse.prototype.end;"
        + "const refusalClockOrigin=process.hrtime.bigint();"
        + "const permittedRefusalCodes=['PROFILE_MISMATCH','AUTHORIZATION_EXPIRED',"
        + "'POLICY_REFUSED','REFERENCE_REFUSED'];"
        + "FixtureServerResponse.prototype.end=function(...args){"
        + "const status=this.statusCode;const result=Reflect.apply(originalResponseEnd,this,args);"
        + "try{if(status===409){let code='OTHER';const chunk=args[0];"
        + "for(const allowed of permittedRefusalCodes){"
        + "const expected='{\"code\":\"'+allowed+'\"}';"
        + "if(typeof chunk==='string'&&chunk===expected||Buffer.isBuffer(chunk)"
        + "&&chunk.equals(Buffer.from(expected,'utf8'))){code=allowed;break;}}"
        + "const ms=Number(process.hrtime.bigint()-refusalClockOrigin)/1e6;"
        + "appendRefusalWitness("
        + witnessFile
        + ",'{\"status\":409,\"code\":\"'+code+'\",\"monotonicMs\":'+ms+'}\\n',"
        + "{mode:0o600});}}catch{}return result;};";
  }

  private JsonNode invoke(String id, String route) throws Exception {
    JsonNode session = sessions.transaction(() -> sessions.lock(id).document().deepCopy());
    ObjectNode body =
        C.object()
            .put("schemaVersion", 3)
            .put("sessionId", id)
            .put("expectedSourceRevision", "1")
            .put("expectedConfigurationRevision", "1")
            .put("expectedPlanRevision", plans.state(id).path("planRevision").asInt());
    HttpRequest request =
        HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                URI.create(
                    "https://localhost:"
                        + port
                        + "/internal/v2/studio/guided-sessions/"
                        + id
                        + "/"
                        + route))
            .header(
                "Authorization",
                "Bearer " + token("backend-controller", "controller-1", "studio-guided-controller"))
            .header("X-Owner-Fence", session.path("owner").path("fence").asText())
            .header("Idempotency-Key", UUID.randomUUID().toString())
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(body.toString()))
            .build();
    var response =
        HttpClient.newBuilder()
            .sslContext(TLS)
            .build()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
    assertThat(response.statusCode()).as("%s; logs %s", response.body(), TEMP).isEqualTo(202);
    return C.parse(response.body());
  }

  private static String c5FixedHttpFailureCode(String body) {
    try {
      JsonNode envelope = C.parse(body);
      JsonNode version = envelope.path("schemaVersion");
      JsonNode code = envelope.path("code");
      JsonNode messageCode = envelope.path("messageCode");
      if (!envelope.isObject()
          || !version.isIntegralNumber()
          || !version.canConvertToInt()
          || version.intValue() != 3
          || !code.isTextual()
          || !messageCode.isTextual()
          || !code.asText().equals(messageCode.asText())) {
        return "UNKNOWN";
      }
      return switch (code.asText()) {
        case "INVALID_CONTRACT" -> "INVALID_CONTRACT";
        case "UNAUTHENTICATED" -> "UNAUTHENTICATED";
        case "FORBIDDEN_SCOPE" -> "FORBIDDEN_SCOPE";
        case "REVISION_CONFLICT" -> "REVISION_CONFLICT";
        case "STALE_GENERATION" -> "STALE_GENERATION";
        case "TARGET_MISMATCH" -> "TARGET_MISMATCH";
        case "AMBIGUOUS_TARGET" -> "AMBIGUOUS_TARGET";
        case "MISSING_SOURCE" -> "MISSING_SOURCE";
        case "BINDING_ORDER" -> "BINDING_ORDER";
        case "SENSITIVE_SOURCE" -> "SENSITIVE_SOURCE";
        case "ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED" -> "ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED";
        case "UNSUPPORTED_SURFACE" -> "UNSUPPORTED_SURFACE";
        case "BUDGET_EXCEEDED" -> "BUDGET_EXCEEDED";
        case "STALE_FENCE" -> "STALE_FENCE";
        case "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT" -> "IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT";
        case "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME" -> "UNKNOWN_OUTCOME";
        case "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT" -> "PUBLICATION_CONFLICT";
        case "RUNTIME_INCOMPATIBLE" -> "RUNTIME_INCOMPATIBLE";
        case "ASSERTION_FAILED" -> "ASSERTION_FAILED";
        case "CANCELLED" -> "CANCELLED";
        case "EXPIRED" -> "EXPIRED";
        case "SECRET_UNAVAILABLE" -> "SECRET_UNAVAILABLE";
        case "SECRET_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED" -> "SECRET_USE_NOT_AUTHORIZED";
        case "SECRET_USE_REVOKED" -> "SECRET_USE_REVOKED";
        case "VAULT_UNAVAILABLE" -> "VAULT_UNAVAILABLE";
        case "LOGIN_REQUIRED" -> "LOGIN_REQUIRED";
        case "SECRET_TOO_LONG" -> "SECRET_TOO_LONG";
        case "PUBLICATION_DISABLED" -> "PUBLICATION_DISABLED";
        case "PUBLICATION_PENDING" -> "PUBLICATION_PENDING";
        case "REPLAY_DISABLED" -> "REPLAY_DISABLED";
        case "AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" -> "AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE";
        case "EVIDENCE_MISMATCH" -> "EVIDENCE_MISMATCH";
        case "PROFILE_MISMATCH" -> "PROFILE_MISMATCH";
        default -> "UNKNOWN";
      };
    } catch (RuntimeException malformed) {
      return "UNKNOWN";
    }
  }

  private JsonNode publicCall(String path, JsonNode body, int status, String key) throws Exception {
    var builder =
        HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create("https://localhost:" + port + path))
            .header("Cookie", "put_session=" + COOKIE);
    if (body == null) {
      builder.GET();
    } else {
      refreshFixtureCredentialsBeforeDispatch(path);
      builder
          .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
          .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString(body.toString()));
      if (key != null) {
        builder.header("Idempotency-Key", key);
      }
    }
    var response =
        HttpClient.newBuilder()
            .sslContext(TLS)
            .build()
            .send(builder.build(), HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
    C5Capture capture = c5Capture;
    JsonNode s2Accepted = null;
    if (capture != null
        && s2TraceBinding != null
        && s2TraceBinding.capture() == capture
        && response.statusCode() == 202) {
      s2Accepted = C.parse(response.body());
      if (path.equals("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions")) {
        String id = s2Accepted.path("session").path("sessionId").asText();
        UUID.fromString(id);
        p3OwnedGuidedCookies.put(id, COOKIE);
        testOwnedSessions.put(id, s2Accepted.path("session").path("testId").asText());
        assertThat(
                s2Accepted
                    .path("session")
                    .path("testId")
                    .asText()
                    .equals(s2TraceBinding.scope().testId()))
            .isTrue();
        capture.bindS2Session(id);
      } else if (path.equals(
          "/api/v1/studio/tests/" + s2TraceBinding.scope().testId() + "/execute")) {
        UUID id = UUID.fromString(s2Accepted.path("executionId").asText());
        p3OwnedOrdinaryCookies.put(id, COOKIE);
        P3_EXECUTIONS.add(id);
      }
    }
    if (capture != null) {
      capture.recordHttp(response);
    }
    if (capture != null) {
      String label = "C5 actual HTTP response status";
      if (response.statusCode() != status) {
        label += " code=" + c5FixedHttpFailureCode(response.body());
      }
      assertThat(response.statusCode()).as(label).isEqualTo(status);
    } else {
      assertThat(response.statusCode())
          .as("%s %s; logs %s", path, response.body(), TEMP)
          .isEqualTo(status);
    }
    JsonNode parsed = s2Accepted == null ? C.parse(response.body()) : s2Accepted;
    if (path.equals("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions") && response.statusCode() == 202) {
      String sessionId = parsed.path("session").path("sessionId").asText();
      String testId = parsed.path("session").path("testId").asText();
      assertThat(sessionId).isNotBlank();
      assertThat(testId).isEqualTo(body.path("testId").asText());
      testOwnedSessions.put(sessionId, testId);
      if (capture != null) {
        capture.selectedGuidedSession = sessionId;
      }
    }
    return parsed;
  }

  private static void persistFinalizeDiagnostic(
      GuidedSessionException failure,
      ObservedReplayProperties replay,
      GuidedPublicationProperties publication,
      ObservedReplayPins pins) {
    var diagnostic =
        C.object()
            .put("schemaVersion", 1)
            .put("exceptionCode", safeDiagnosticCode(failure.getMessage()))
            .put("publicationEnabled", publication.enabled())
            .put("replayEnabled", replay.enabled())
            .put(
                "resolverTransactionActive",
                org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager
                    .isActualTransactionActive());
    var frames = diagnostic.putArray("stackFrames");
    for (StackTraceElement frame : failure.getStackTrace()) {
      frames
          .addObject()
          .put("class", frame.getClassName())
          .put("method", frame.getMethodName())
          .put("line", frame.getLineNumber());
    }
    try {
      diagnostic.set("selectedRuntime", pins.selected());
    } catch (RuntimeException pinFailure) {
      diagnostic.put("selectedRuntimeFailureClass", pinFailure.getClass().getName());
      if (pinFailure instanceof GuidedSessionException guarded) {
        diagnostic.put("selectedRuntimeFailureCode", safeDiagnosticCode(guarded.getMessage()));
      }
    }
    try {
      Files.writeString(
          TEMP.resolve("finalize-exception-" + FINALIZE_DIAGNOSTICS.incrementAndGet() + ".json"),
          diagnostic.toPrettyString());
    } catch (IOException writeFailure) {
      failure.addSuppressed(
          new IllegalStateException("Safe finalize diagnostic could not be saved"));
    }
  }

  private static JsonNode versions() throws Exception {
    return C.parse(
            Files.readString(
                Path.of("../docs/specs/studio-observed-authoring/contracts/v3/fixtures.json")))
        .path("documents")
        .path("versions");
  }

  private static ObjectNode model(JsonNode request) {
    String kind = request.path("kind").asText();
    JsonNode context = request.path("context");
    ObjectNode result =
        C.object()
            .put("schemaVersion", 3)
            .put("kind", kind)
            .put("requestId", request.path("requestId").asText());
    if (incrementalTenScenario) {
      INCREMENTAL_MODEL_CONTEXTS.add(request.deepCopy());
      return incrementalTenModel(result, context, kind);
    }
    if (p14Recipe != P14Recipe.NONE) {
      return p14Model(result, context, kind);
    }
    if (p3Mode != null) {
      return p3Model(result, context, kind);
    }
    if (secretLoginScenario) {
      return secretLoginModel(result, context, kind);
    }
    if (kind.equals("SOURCE_PLAN")) {
      if (askTimingSource) {
        askTimingSource = false;
        var choices =
            result.putObject("question").put("reason", "AMBIGUOUS_BINDING").putArray("choices");
        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
          String destination = i == 0 ? URL : URL + "?alternative=two";
          int start = i == 0 ? 5 : timingSourceText.indexOf(destination, 6);
          ObjectNode reference =
              C.object()
                  .put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan")
                  .put("sourceRevision", "1")
                  .put("stepId", ONE)
                  .put("startUtf16", start)
                  .put("endUtf16", start + destination.length())
                  .put("kind", "destination")
                  .put("intendedSlot", "navigate.destination");
          ObjectNode meaning = C.object();
          meaning.set("reference", reference);
          meaning.set(
              "need",
              C.object()
                  .put("stepId", ONE)
                  .put("occurrence", 0)
                  .put("operationKind", "navigate")
                  .put("argumentSlot", "navigate.destination"));
          choices
              .addObject()
              .put("choiceId", "timing-" + i)
              .put(
                  "safeLabel",
                  i == 0 ? "Primary authored destination" : "Alternative authored destination")
              .set("meaning", meaning);
        }
        return result;
      }
      result
          .putArray("sourceReferences")
          .addObject()
          .put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan")
          .put("sourceRevision", "1")
          .put("stepId", ONE)
          .put("startUtf16", 5)
          .put("endUtf16", 5 + URL.length())
          .put("kind", "destination")
          .put("intendedSlot", "navigate.destination");
      if (!context.path("clarificationSelections").isEmpty()) {
        result
            .withArray("sourceReferences")
            .set(0, context.path("clarificationSelections").get(0).path("reference"));
      }
      var items = result.putArray("obligations");
      items.add(obligation(ONE, 0, "navigate", "page", 0, null));
      items.add(obligation(ONE, 1, "read", "Order reference", null, "order"));
      items.add(obligation(TWO, 0, "fill", "Destination", null, "order"));
      items.add(obligation(THREE, 0, "click", "Submit", null, null));
      items.add(
          obligation(THREE, 1, "assert", "Confirmation", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
      result
          .putArray("needs")
          .addObject()
          .put("needId", "order")
          .put("producerStepId", ONE)
          .putArray("consumers")
          .addObject()
          .put("stepId", TWO)
          .put("argumentSlot", "fill.value");
      return result;
    }
    if (delayTimingModel) {
      delayTimingModel = false;
      try {
        Thread.sleep(1250);
      } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new IllegalStateException("Fixture interrupted");
      }
    }
    JsonNode obligation = context.path("obligations").get(0);
    String op = obligation.path("operationKind").asText();
    if (askObservedChoice && op.equals("fill")) {
      askObservedChoice = false;
      try {
        Thread.sleep(1250);
      } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new IllegalStateException("Fixture interrupted");
      }
      result.put("status", "CLARIFICATION").putNull("obligationId").putNull("action");
      var choices =
          result.putObject("question").put("reason", "AMBIGUOUS_BINDING").putArray("choices");
      for (String name : List.of("Destination", "Alternate destination")) {
        JsonNode node = null;
        for (JsonNode candidate : context.path("observation").path("nodes")) {
          if (candidate.path("name").asText().equals(name)) {
            node = candidate;
          }
        }
        if (node == null) {
          throw new IllegalStateException("Expected observed choice missing");
        }
        ObjectNode meaning =
            C.object()
                .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
                .put("nodeId", node.path("nodeId").asText())
                .putNull("bindingId");
        meaning.set(
            "locator",
            C.object()
                .put("kind", "role")
                .put("role", "textbox")
                .put("name", name)
                .put("exact", true));
        choices
            .addObject()
            .put("choiceId", "provider-" + choices.size())
            .put("safeLabel", name)
            .set("meaning", meaning);
      }
      var hook = beforeObservedQuestion;
      if (hook != null) {
        hook.accept(context);
      }
      return result;
    }
    result
        .put("status", "ACTION")
        .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
        .putNull("question");
    ObjectNode action = result.putObject("action"),
        operation = action.putObject("operation").put("kind", op),
        target = action.putObject("target");
    if (op.equals("navigate")) {
      target.put("kind", "page");
      operation.put("destinationInputId", context.path("inputs").get(0).path("inputId").asText());
    } else {
      JsonNode node = null;
      for (JsonNode candidate : context.path("observation").path("nodes")) {
        if (candidate.path("name").equals(obligation.path("targetIntent"))) {
          node = candidate;
        }
      }
      if (node == null) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("Expected controlled node missing");
      }
      target
          .put("nodeId", node.path("nodeId").asText())
          .putObject("locator")
          .put("kind", "role")
          .put("role", node.path("role").asText())
          .put("name", node.path("name").asText())
          .put("exact", true);
      if (op.equals("read")) {
        operation
            .put("bindingId", context.path("needs").get(0).path("bindingId").asText())
            .putObject("recipe")
            .put("kind", "visibleText")
            .put("classification", "permittedBusinessData")
            .put("policyRuleId", "business-read");
      }
      if (op.equals("fill")) {
        operation
            .putObject("value")
            .put("kind", "binding")
            .put("bindingId", context.path("needs").get(0).path("bindingId").asText());
      }
      if (op.equals("assert")) {
        operation.put("predicate", "visible").putNull("expected");
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  private static ObjectNode incrementalTenModel(ObjectNode result, JsonNode context, String kind) {
    if (kind.equals("SOURCE_PLAN")) {
      if ("drift-plan-fail".equals(incrementalDomVariant) && context.has("learningWindow"))
        return result.put("status", "UNSUPPORTED").put("reasonCode", "INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE");
      var source = new java.util.ArrayList<JsonNode>(); context.path("sourceSteps").forEach(source::add);
      int first = 0;
      if (context.path("learningWindow").has("firstLearnStepId")) {
        String id = context.path("learningWindow").path("firstLearnStepId").asText();
        first = java.util.stream.IntStream.range(0, source.size()).filter(at -> source.get(at).path("stepId").asText().equals(id)).findFirst().orElseThrow();
      }
      var references = result.putArray("sourceReferences");
      var obligations = result.putArray("obligations");
      var needs = result.putArray("needs");
      boolean literalReplacement = first > 0 && List.of("deleted-consumers", "admitted-literal2", "pre-literal2", "same-pair-literal").contains(incrementalDomVariant);
      var bindingConsumers = new java.util.ArrayList<JsonNode>();
      for (int at = first; at < source.size(); at++) {
        String stepId = source.get(at).path("stepId").asText();
        if (!literalReplacement && stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(1))) bindingConsumers.add(C.object().put("stepId", stepId).put("argumentSlot", "fill.value"));
        if (!literalReplacement && stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(2)) && source.get(at).path("text").asText().contains("that order reference")) bindingConsumers.add(C.object().put("stepId", stepId).put("argumentSlot", "assert.expected"));
      }
      if (!bindingConsumers.isEmpty()) {
        var need = needs.addObject().put("needId", "order").put("producerStepId", INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(0));
        var consumers = need.putArray("consumers"); bindingConsumers.forEach(consumers::add);
      }
      for (int at = first; at < source.size(); at++) {
        String stepId = source.get(at).path("stepId").asText();
        if (stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(0))) {
          boolean navigated = false;
          for (JsonNode completed : context.path("learningWindow").path("completedCoordinates"))
            navigated |= completed.path("stepId").asText().equals(stepId) && completed.path("ordinal").asInt() == 0;
          if (!navigated) {
            int reference = references.size();
            references.addObject().put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan")
                .put("sourceRevision", context.path("sourceRevision").asText()).put("stepId", stepId)
                .put("startUtf16", 5).put("endUtf16", 5 + URL.length()).put("kind", "destination").put("intendedSlot", "navigate.destination");
            obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 0, "navigate", "page", reference, null));
          }
          if (!bindingConsumers.isEmpty()) obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 1, "read", "Order reference", null, "order"));
        } else if (stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(1))) {
          if (literalReplacement) {
            int offset = source.get(at).path("text").asText().indexOf("literal-control");
            if (offset < 0) throw new IllegalStateException("Replacement is not authored");
            int reference = references.size();
            references.addObject().put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan").put("sourceRevision", context.path("sourceRevision").asText())
                .put("stepId", stepId).put("startUtf16", offset).put("endUtf16", offset + "literal-control".length())
                .put("kind", "nonsecretText").put("intendedSlot", "fill.value");
            int ordinal = context.path("learningWindow").path("firstLearnStepId").asText().equals(stepId)
                ? context.path("learningWindow").path("nextSemanticOrdinal").asInt() : 0;
            obligations.add(obligation(stepId, ordinal, "fill", "Destination", reference, null));
          } else {
            obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 0, "fill", "Destination", null, "order"));
            if (source.get(at).path("text").asText().contains("twice")) obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 1, "fill", "Destination", null, "order"));
            if (source.get(at).path("text").asText().contains("click Submit")) obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 1, "click", "Submit", null, null));
          }
        } else if (stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(2)) && source.get(at).path("text").asText().contains("Confirmation")) {
          obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 0, "assert", "Confirmation", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
        } else if (stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(2)) && literalReplacement) {
          obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 0, "assert", "Destination", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
        } else if (stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(2))) {
          boolean output = source.get(at).path("text").asText().contains("Order reference");
          obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 0, "assert", output ? "Order reference" : "Destination", null, "order")
              .put("predicate", output ? "containsText" : "hasValue"));
        } else if (stepId.equals(INCREMENTAL_STEPS.get(4)) && source.get(at).path("text").asText().contains("Click Submit")) {
          obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 0, "click", "Submit", null, null));
          obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 1, "assert", "Confirmation", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
        } else {
          String target = source.get(at).path("text").asText().contains("Destination") ? "Destination" : "Confirmation";
          obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 0, "assert", target, null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
          String text = source.get(at).path("text").asText();
          if (text.contains("Check page title is Operations.")) {
            int offset = text.indexOf("Operations");
            int reference = references.size();
            references.addObject().put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan").put("sourceRevision", context.path("sourceRevision").asText())
                .put("stepId", stepId).put("startUtf16", offset).put("endUtf16", offset + "Operations".length())
                .put("kind", "nonsecretText").put("intendedSlot", "assert.expected");
            obligations.add(obligation(stepId, 1, "assert", "page", reference, null).put("predicate", "title"));
            int unused = text.indexOf("unused-input");
            if (unused >= 0) references.addObject().put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan").put("sourceRevision", context.path("sourceRevision").asText())
                .put("stepId", stepId).put("startUtf16", unused).put("endUtf16", unused + "unused-input".length())
                .put("kind", "nonsecretText").put("intendedSlot", "assert.expected");
          }
        }
      }
      return result;
    }
    JsonNode obligation = context.path("obligations").get(0);
    String op = obligation.path("operationKind").asText();
    result.put("status", "ACTION").put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText()).putNull("question");
    var action = result.putObject("action");
    var operation = action.putObject("operation").put("kind", op);
    var target = action.putObject("target");
    if (op.equals("navigate")) {
      target.put("kind", "page");
      operation.put("destinationInputId", context.path("inputs").get(0).path("inputId").asText());
      return result;
    }
    if (op.equals("assert") && obligation.path("predicate").asText().equals("title")) {
      target.put("kind", "page"); operation.put("predicate", "title");
      JsonNode input = context.path("inputs").get(obligation.path("referenceIndex").asInt());
      if (input == null || !input.path("sourceStepId").equals(obligation.path("stepId"))
          || !input.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("assert.expected")) throw new IllegalStateException("Controlled suffix title input missing");
      operation.putObject("expected").put("kind", "authoredLiteral").put("sourceInputId", input.path("inputId").asText());
      return result;
    }
    JsonNode node = null;
    for (JsonNode candidate : context.path("observation").path("nodes")) {
      if (candidate.path("name").equals(obligation.path("targetIntent"))) node = candidate;
    }
    if (node == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Controlled incremental target missing");
    target.put("nodeId", node.path("nodeId").asText()).putObject("locator").put("kind", "role")
        .put("role", node.path("role").asText()).put("name", node.path("name").asText()).put("exact", true);
    if (op.equals("read")) {
      operation.put("bindingId", context.path("needs").get(0).path("bindingId").asText()).putObject("recipe")
          .put("kind", "visibleText").put("classification", "permittedBusinessData").put("policyRuleId", "business-read");
    } else if (op.equals("fill") && obligation.path("needId").isNull()) {
      JsonNode input = null;
      for (JsonNode candidate : context.path("inputs"))
        if (candidate.path("sourceStepId").equals(obligation.path("stepId")) && candidate.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("fill.value")) input = candidate;
      if (input == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Controlled authored replacement input missing");
      operation.putObject("value").put("kind", "authoredLiteral").put("sourceInputId", input.path("inputId").asText());
    } else if (op.equals("fill") || op.equals("assert") && !obligation.path("needId").isNull()) {
      operation.putObject(op.equals("fill") ? "value" : "expected").put("kind", "binding")
          .put("bindingId", context.path("needs").get(0).path("bindingId").asText());
      if (op.equals("assert")) operation.set("predicate", obligation.path("predicate"));
    } else if (op.equals("assert")) {
      operation.put("predicate", "visible").putNull("expected");
    }
    return result;
  }

  private static ObjectNode secretLoginModel(ObjectNode result, JsonNode context, String kind) {
    if (kind.equals("SOURCE_PLAN")) {
      String destination = SECRET_ORIGIN + "/login";
      var references = result.putArray("sourceReferences");
      references
          .addObject()
          .put("referenceKind", "sourceSpan")
          .put("sourceRevision", "1")
          .put("stepId", ONE)
          .put("startUtf16", 5)
          .put("endUtf16", 5 + destination.length())
          .put("kind", "destination")
          .put("intendedSlot", "navigate.destination");
      for (String name : List.of("USER", "PASS")) {
        JsonNode declared = null;
        for (JsonNode reference : context.path("configurationReferences")) {
          if (reference.path("referenceName").asText().equals(name)
              && reference.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("fill.value")
              && reference.path("stepId").asText().equals(TWO)) {
            declared = reference;
          }
        }
        if (declared == null) {
          throw new IllegalStateException("Missing authentic login declaration");
        }
        ObjectNode sourceReference =
            ((ObjectNode) declared)
                .deepCopy()
                .put("referenceKind", "configuration")
                .put("configurationRevision", "1");
        if (name.equals("USER")) {
          // The closed nonsecret branch identifies the declaration by its authentic referenceId.
          sourceReference.remove("referenceName");
        }
        references.add(sourceReference);
      }
      var obligations = result.putArray("obligations");
      obligations.add(obligation(ONE, 0, "navigate", "page", 0, null));
      obligations.add(obligation(TWO, 0, "fill", "User", 1, null));
      obligations.add(obligation(TWO, 1, "fill", "password", 2, null));
      obligations.add(obligation(THREE, 0, "click", "Sign in", null, null));
      obligations.add(
          obligation(THREE, 1, "assert", "Home", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
      obligations.add(obligation(THREE, 2, "click", "Reports", null, null));
      obligations.add(
          obligation(THREE, 3, "assert", "Reports", null, null).put("predicate", "visible"));
      result.putArray("needs");
      return result;
    }
    JsonNode obligation = context.path("obligations").get(0);
    String operationKind = obligation.path("operationKind").asText();
    result
        .put("status", "ACTION")
        .put("obligationId", obligation.path("obligationId").asText())
        .putNull("question");
    var action = result.putObject("action");
    var operation = action.putObject("operation").put("kind", operationKind);
    var target = action.putObject("target");
    if (operationKind.equals("navigate")) {
      target.put("kind", "page");
      for (JsonNode input : context.path("inputs")) {
        if (input.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("navigate.destination")) {
          operation.put("destinationInputId", input.path("inputId").asText());
        }
      }
    } else {
      if (operationKind.equals("fill")
          && obligation.path("targetIntent").asText().equals("password")) {
        target.put("kind", "passwordControl");
        for (JsonNode input : context.path("inputs")) {
          if (input.path("kind").asText().equals("secretRef")) {
            operation
                .putObject("value")
                .put("kind", "secretRef")
                .put("sourceInputId", input.path("inputId").asText());
          }
        }
      } else {
        JsonNode selected = null;
        for (JsonNode node : context.path("observation").path("nodes")) {
          if (node.path("name").equals(obligation.path("targetIntent"))) {
            selected = node;
          }
        }
        if (selected == null) {
          throw new IllegalStateException("Missing controlled login node");
        }
        target
            .put("nodeId", selected.path("nodeId").asText())
            .putObject("locator")
            .put("kind", "role")
            .put("role", selected.path("role").asText())
            .put("name", selected.path("name").asText())
            .put("exact", true);
        if (operationKind.equals("fill")) {
          for (JsonNode input : context.path("inputs")) {
            if (input.path("kind").asText().equals("nonsecretText")
                && input.path("sourceStepId").asText().equals(TWO)
                && input.path("intendedSlot").asText().equals("fill.value")) {
              operation
                  .putObject("value")
                  .put("kind", "authoredLiteral")
                  .put("sourceInputId", input.path("inputId").asText());
            }
          }
        }
        if (operationKind.equals("assert")) {
          operation.put("predicate", "visible").putNull("expected");
        }
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  private static ObjectNode obligation(
      String step, int occurrence, String op, String target, Integer reference, String need) {
    ObjectNode o =
        C.object()
            .put("stepId", step)
            .put("occurrence", occurrence)
            .put("operationKind", op)
            .put("targetIntent", target)
            .putNull("predicate")
            .put("needId", need);
    if (reference == null) {
      o.putNull("referenceIndex");
    } else {
      o.put("referenceIndex", reference);
    }
    return o;
  }

  private static String token(String role, String subject, String audience) throws Exception {
    var encoder = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding();
    String header =
        encoder.encodeToString(
            "{\"alg\":\"RS256\",\"typ\":\"JWT\"}".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    ObjectNode claims =
        C.object()
            .put("iss", "fixture-issuer")
            .put("sub", subject)
            .put("aud", audience)
            .put("role", role)
            .put("exp", Instant.now().plusSeconds(299).getEpochSecond())
            .put("iat", Instant.now().minusSeconds(1).getEpochSecond());
    String unsigned =
        header + "." + encoder.encodeToString(claims.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    Signature signature = Signature.getInstance("SHA256withRSA");
    signature.initSign(ISSUER.getPrivate());
    signature.update(unsigned.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    return unsigned + "." + encoder.encodeToString(signature.sign());
  }

  /**
   * Re-mints the fixture-owned credential files with the unchanged {@link #token} claims, issuer
   * key and 300-second lifetime. The bridge controller, translation coordinator and guided worker
   * re-read these files per request, so one long class run must not outlive a single issuance.
   */
  private static void refreshFixtureCredentials() throws Exception {
    replaceFixtureCredential(
        "controller.jwt", token("backend-controller", "controller-1", "studio-guided-runner"));
    replaceFixtureCredential(
        "coordinator.jwt", token("backend-controller", "controller-1", "studio-guided-controller"));
    if (Files.exists(TEMP.resolve("worker.jwt"))) { // Issued first by startWorkers().
      replaceFixtureCredential(
          "worker.jwt", token("owning-worker", "worker-1", "studio-guided-controller"));
    }
  }

  /** New guided session, guided session control and ordinary dispatch POSTs get fresh tokens. */
  private static void refreshFixtureCredentialsBeforeDispatch(String path) throws Exception {
    if (path.startsWith("/api/v1/studio/guided-sessions")
        || (path.startsWith("/api/v1/studio/tests/") && path.endsWith("/execute"))) {
      refreshFixtureCredentials();
    }
  }

  /** Concurrent readers observe either the previous or the new complete credential file. */
  private static void replaceFixtureCredential(String name, String credential) throws IOException {
    Path target = TEMP.resolve(name);
    Path temporary = TEMP.resolve(name + ".refresh.tmp");
    try {
      Files.writeString(temporary, credential);
      Files.move(
          temporary,
          target,
          java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,
          java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
    } finally {
      Files.deleteIfExists(temporary);
    }
  }

  private static String command(String... args) throws Exception {
    Process p = new ProcessBuilder(args).redirectErrorStream(true).start();
    String output = new String(p.getInputStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    if (!p.waitFor(30, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS) || p.exitValue() != 0) {
      throw new IllegalStateException(output);
    }
    return output;
  }

  private void stopOwnedApplicationJobs() throws Exception {
    assertThat(ownedApplicationContext.getBean(JdbcTemplate.class)).isSameAs(jdbc);
    assertThat(ownedApplicationContext.getBean(GuidedSessionRepository.class)).isSameAs(sessions);
    assertThat(
            ownedApplicationContext
                .getEnvironment()
                .getProperty("spring.datasource.url")
                .equals(DB.getJdbcUrl()))
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(
            ownedApplicationContext
                .getEnvironment()
                .getProperty("local.server.port", Integer.class))
        .isEqualTo(port);
    var factory = ownedApplicationContext.getBeanFactory();
    var scheduled =
        java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(
            new java.util.IdentityHashMap<java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService, Boolean>());
    var executors =
        java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(
            new java.util.IdentityHashMap<java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService, Boolean>());
    var simple =
        new java.util.ArrayList<
            org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.SimpleAsyncTaskScheduler>();
    int cancelledTasks = 0, fixedDelayTasks = 0, fixedRateTasks = 0, cronTasks = 0;
    // Only actual local singleton identities: never instantiate or inspect another context's beans.
    var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
    for (String name : factory.getSingletonNames()) {
      try {
        Object bean = factory.getSingleton(name);
        if (bean
            instanceof
            org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.ScheduledAnnotationBeanPostProcessor holder) {
          cancelledTasks += holder.getScheduledTasks().size();
          for (var scheduledTask : holder.getScheduledTasks()) {
            Object task = scheduledTask.getTask();
            if (task instanceof org.springframework.scheduling.config.FixedDelayTask) {
              fixedDelayTasks++;
            } else if (task instanceof org.springframework.scheduling.config.FixedRateTask) {
              fixedRateTasks++;
            } else if (task instanceof org.springframework.scheduling.config.CronTask) {
              cronTasks++;
            }
            scheduledTask.cancel(false);
          }
        }
        if (bean
            instanceof
            org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.SimpleAsyncTaskScheduler scheduler) {
          Object actual =
              org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(
                  scheduler, "scheduledExecutor");
          assertThat(actual instanceof java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService).isTrue();
          scheduled.add((java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService) actual);
          Object tracked =
              org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(
                  scheduler, "activeThreads");
          assertThat(tracked instanceof java.util.Set<?>)
              .as("before-init tracking of actual scheduler bodies")
              .isTrue();
          simple.add(scheduler);
        } else if (bean
            instanceof
            org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.ThreadPoolTaskScheduler scheduler) {
          scheduled.add(scheduler.getScheduledThreadPoolExecutor());
        }
        if (bean instanceof org.springframework.core.task.support.TaskExecutorAdapter adapter) {
          Object bounded =
              org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(
                  adapter, "concurrentExecutor");
          assertThat(bounded instanceof com.citi.put.checks.config.SemaphoreBoundedExecutor)
              .isTrue();
          Object actual =
              org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(bounded, "delegate");
          assertThat(actual instanceof java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService).isTrue();
          executors.add((java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService) actual);
        } else if (bean
            instanceof org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.ThreadPoolTaskExecutor executor) {
          executors.add(executor.getThreadPoolExecutor());
        } else if (bean instanceof java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService executor) {
          executors.add(executor);
        }
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError captureFailure) {
        failures.add(captureFailure);
      }
    }
    p3AttemptTeardown(
        failures,
        () -> assertThat(scheduled.size()).as("actual owned scheduler services").isPositive());
    p3AttemptTeardown(
        failures,
        () ->
            assertThat(executors.size())
                .as("actual owned async delegates")
                .isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(3));
    for (var services : java.util.List.of(scheduled, executors)) {
      for (var executor : new java.util.ArrayList<>(services)) {
        if (executor instanceof java.util.concurrent.ForkJoinPool) {
          failures.add(
              new IllegalStateException("Global or unsupported ForkJoin ownership refused"));
          services.remove(executor);
        }
      }
    }
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(90).toNanos();
    // All safe captured resources are attempted, even when another resource cannot be confirmed.
    for (var scheduler : scheduled) {
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, scheduler::shutdown);
    }
    for (var scheduler : scheduled) {
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> awaitOwnedExecutorTermination(scheduler, deadline));
    }
    for (var scheduler : simple) {
      p3AttemptTeardown(
          failures,
          () -> {
            Object tracked =
                org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils.getField(
                    scheduler, "activeThreads");
            assertThat(tracked instanceof java.util.Set<?>).isTrue();
            while (!((java.util.Set<?>) tracked).isEmpty() && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
              Thread.sleep(20);
            }
            assertThat(((java.util.Set<?>) tracked).isEmpty())
                .as("actual scheduler virtual bodies drained")
                .isTrue();
            scheduler.close();
          });
    }
    for (var executor : executors) {
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, executor::shutdown);
    }
    for (var executor : executors) {
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> awaitOwnedExecutorTermination(executor, deadline));
    }
    throwTeardownFailures(failures);
    // Context destruction (including managed private pools) happens only after observed jobs drain.
    assertThat(ownedFrameworkContext != null).isTrue();
    assertThat(ownedFrameworkContext.getTestClass()).isEqualTo(GuidedPublicationReplayIT.class);
    assertThat(ownedFrameworkContext.getApplicationContext()).isSameAs(ownedApplicationContext);
    var closer =
        java.util.concurrent.Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(
            task -> {
              Thread thread = new Thread(task, "obs007-owned-context-close");
              thread.setDaemon(true);
              return thread;
            });
    Throwable closePrimary = null;
    java.util.concurrent.Future<?> closeFuture = null;
    try {
      closeFuture =
          closer.submit(
              () ->
                  ownedFrameworkContext.markApplicationContextDirty(
                      org.springframework.test.annotation.DirtiesContext.HierarchyMode
                          .CURRENT_LEVEL));
      long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
      assertThat(remaining).isPositive();
      closeFuture.get(remaining, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      closePrimary = failure;
      if (closeFuture != null) {
        closeFuture.cancel(true);
      }
    } finally {
      try {
        closer.shutdownNow();
        assertThat(closer.awaitTermination(5, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS)).isTrue();
        assertThat(closer.isTerminated()).isTrue();
      } catch (Exception | AssertionError terminationFailure) {
        if (closePrimary == null) {
          closePrimary = terminationFailure;
        } else {
          closePrimary.addSuppressed(terminationFailure);
        }
      }
    }
    if (closePrimary != null) {
      throwTeardownFailures(java.util.List.of(closePrimary));
    }
    assertThat(ownedFrameworkContext.hasApplicationContext()).isFalse();
    assertThat(ownedApplicationContext.isActive()).isFalse();
    for (var scheduler : scheduled) {
      assertThat(scheduler.isTerminated()).isTrue();
    }
    for (var executor : executors) {
      assertThat(executor.isTerminated()).isTrue();
    }
    ownedApplicationQuiescent = true;
    var metadata =
        C.object()
            .put("owningContextIdentity", System.identityHashCode(ownedApplicationContext))
            .put("cancelledScheduledTasks", cancelledTasks)
            .put("fixedDelayTasks", fixedDelayTasks)
            .put("fixedRateTasks", fixedRateTasks)
            .put("cronTasks", cronTasks)
            .put("terminatedSchedulerServices", scheduled.size())
            .put("terminatedAsyncDelegates", executors.size())
            .put("trackedSchedulerBodiesEmpty", true)
            .put("contextClosed", true)
            .put("afterClassCacheEvictionConfigured", true)
            .put("actualFrameworkCacheEntryEvicted", !ownedFrameworkContext.hasApplicationContext())
            .put("commonPoolTerminationClaimed", false);
    p3WriteEvidence("owned-context-teardown", metadata);
  }

  private static void p3AttemptTeardown(java.util.List<Throwable> failures, P3Action operation) {
    try {
      operation.run();
    } catch (Exception | AssertionError failure) {
      failures.add(failure);
    }
  }

  private static void throwTeardownFailures(java.util.List<Throwable> failures) throws Exception {
    if (failures.isEmpty()) {
      return;
    }
    Throwable first = failures.getFirst();
    for (int index = 1; index < failures.size(); index++) {
      if (failures.get(index) != first) {
        first.addSuppressed(failures.get(index));
      }
    }
    if (first instanceof Exception exception) {
      throw exception;
    }
    throw (Error) first;
  }

  private static void awaitOwnedExecutorTermination(
      java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService executor, long deadline) throws InterruptedException {
    long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
    assertThat(remaining).as("bounded owning executor drain deadline").isPositive();
    assertThat(executor.awaitTermination(remaining, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS))
        .as("actual owning executor terminated before PostgreSQL teardown")
        .isTrue();
    assertThat(executor.isTerminated()).isTrue();
  }

  private static void stopOwnedProcessTree(Process process) throws Exception {
    if (process == null) {
      return;
    }
    var descendants = process.toHandle().descendants().toList();
    process.destroy();
    long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(30).toNanos();
    while ((process.isAlive() || descendants.stream().anyMatch(ProcessHandle::isAlive))
        && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
      Thread.sleep(30);
    }
    if (process.isAlive() || descendants.stream().anyMatch(ProcessHandle::isAlive)) {
      descendants.stream().filter(ProcessHandle::isAlive).forEach(ProcessHandle::destroyForcibly);
      if (process.isAlive()) {
        process.destroyForcibly();
      }
      deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(5).toNanos();
      while ((process.isAlive() || descendants.stream().anyMatch(ProcessHandle::isAlive))
          && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
        Thread.sleep(20);
      }
    }
    assertThat(process.isAlive()).as("actual owned fixture process exit acknowledged").isFalse();
    assertThat(descendants.stream().anyMatch(ProcessHandle::isAlive))
        .as("actual owned fixture descendant exits acknowledged")
        .isFalse();
  }

  void stop() throws Exception {
    retryRelease.countDown();
    var failures = new java.util.ArrayList<Throwable>();
    ownedApplicationQuiescent = false;
    try {
      p3AttemptTeardown(failures, this::stopOwnedApplicationJobs);
      int processFailuresBefore = failures.size();
      for (Process process : new Process[] {guidedWorker, ordinaryWorker, SECRET_FIXTURE}) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> stopOwnedProcessTree(process));
      }
      boolean processesQuiescent = failures.size() == processFailuresBefore;
      if (ownedApplicationQuiescent && processesQuiescent) {
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> PROVIDER.stop(0));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, () -> APP.stop(0));
        p3AttemptTeardown(failures, DB::stop);
      } else {
        failures.add(
            new IllegalStateException(
                "Endpoints/PostgreSQL retained because actual owned dependencies remain"
                    + " unconfirmed"));
      }
      throwTeardownFailures(failures);
    } finally {
      SSLContext.setDefault(PREVIOUS_TLS);
    }
  }

  /**
   * STUDIO-OBS-014 AC-10 (AR-2 e): across every real run of this class (including the OBS-007
   * secret-login end-to-end), the audit stream holds only the closed event shape, each event is at
   * most 1 KiB, and no planted canary (secret values, authored literal, step text, page labels)
   * appears anywhere in an event.
   */
  @Test
  @org.junit.jupiter.api.Order(39)
  void auditStreamCarriesIdsAndCodesOnlyAcrossTheWholeClass() {
    var events = new java.util.ArrayList<>(AUDIT_EVENTS.list);
    assertThat(AUDIT_CANARIES).as("the OBS-007 case registered its canaries").isNotEmpty();
    assertThat(events)
        .as("the joined runs emitted admitted-action and replay-dispatch events")
        .anyMatch(e -> e.getFormattedMessage().equals("GUIDED_ACTION_ADMITTED"))
        .anyMatch(e -> e.getFormattedMessage().equals("REPLAY_DISPATCHED"));
    for (var event : events) {
      var keys = new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
      var rendered = new StringBuilder(event.getFormattedMessage());
      for (Object argument : event.getArgumentArray()) {
        var field = (net.logstash.logback.marker.SingleFieldAppendingMarker) argument;
        keys.add(field.getFieldName());
        rendered.append(' ').append(argument);
      }
      assertThat(keys)
          .containsExactlyElementsOf(
              com.citi.put.checks.studio.guided.audit.StudioActionAudit.KEYS);
      assertThat(rendered.toString().getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length)
          .isLessThanOrEqualTo(1024);
      for (String canary : AUDIT_CANARIES) {
        assertThat(rendered.toString()).doesNotContain(canary);
      }
    }
  }
}
